Chapter 1201: The Future Of Dao Deviation

Yi Shu gradually recovered from his shock, yet he still chose not to take a clear stance.

However, his specific actions had already begun to change.

Originally planning to observe for a while and then return, he now made a request to Sun Erlang to stay long-term for further observation.

Sun Erlang appeared somewhat troubled: "I cannot decide this matter on my own. I need to ask my master for instructions."

Yi Shu nodded: "I believe your master will agree."

As expected, the Faceless Holy Emperor permitted the Taiyan Sect's request to remain. There was, however, one condition.

"Use simulation to help the cultivators of the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi grow rapidly?"

Yi Shu hesitated for a moment.

"My master said that even if the Taiyan Sect does not assist in this, he has other methods. It would merely be more troublesome. The core strength of the Taiyan Sect lies in deducing the future of the Xuanhuang Realm. Until the final outcome is set, my master agrees to allow your Taiyan Sect to maintain a certain degree of neutrality. Consider it a form of transaction." Sun Erlang repeated Li Ping's exact words.

Yi Shu fell silent for a moment. After discussion within the Taiyan Sect and with the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, he ultimately agreed.

For the Taiyan Sect, running a few extra simulations was merely utilizing idle computing power.

Yet it could open an additional path for the future. No matter how one looked at it, the deal was advantageous.

Just as before the great calamity arrived, when a portion of the sect's cultivators chose to flee. The Ten Great Immortal Sects had never placed all their options in a single basket.

From that point onward, the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi gained an additional observer.

Apart from the areas under the Holy Emperor's direct presence, the territory of the You Clan, and the Xuanhuang Realm's Fishing Pond, Yi Shu could observe freely without restrictions.

Li Ping harbored no concerns about this.

With the Holy Dynasty's fortune protecting him, and the Myriad Realms All-Spirits Grand Formation growing ever more perfect, every move Yi Shu made was firmly within his grasp.

Even the life and death of Yi Shu's current body depended on a single thought from him.

Meanwhile, in the distant Xuanhuang Realm, someone had already set their sights on the Taiyan Sect.

"Though I have no intention of actively harming others, I must still prepare a contingency."

"The Taiyan Sect has abandoned physical, material existence and collectively resides within the Dao Deviation Stone Mother. Information indestructibility is indeed remarkable. Even when the Immortal Ruins Immortal obliterated the world with a single glance, they managed to escape that terrifying calamity. But..."

"As the saying goes, every trade has its specialty!"

Within the Derivation Law Jade space, Li Fan appeared to be in closed-door cultivation with eyes closed.

In reality, thoughts surged ceaselessly through his mind.

Information indestructibility rendered physical means, and even ordinary immortal techniques, largely ineffective. But data pollution might produce an unexpected effect.

As far as Li Fan knew, among the orthodoxies currently circulating in the Xuanhuang Realm, no such method yet existed. His idea stemmed from that enigmatic Grand Formation of the Primordial Mystic Spirit.

A composite of nine immortal-grade formations, its might was unimaginable. It could even reestablish earth, fire, water, and wind in the void and recreate a world anew.

Fortunately, thanks to the Final Dissociation Disc and various strokes of fortune, Li Fan had already preliminarily mastered three of them.

The Astral Mechanism, All Spirits Return to Numbers; Desolate Fall, Flowers Bloom in an Empty Room; Boundless, Primal Chaos Reveals in Form.

Among them, the Astral Mechanism, All Spirits Return to Numbers formation possessed the wondrous ability to convert everything within its range into data that could be freely transformed.

"Though it remains theoretical, and the lack of suitable materials makes actually setting up the formation impossible. Fortunately, the Taiyan Sect has no physical body. Being pure information, they are instead more prone to exposing flaws."

"Primal Chaos Reveals in Form combined with All Spirits Return to Numbers—if these two formations cooperate..."

In his sea of consciousness, under the enhanced computing power of the Dao Deviation Stone, the Final Dissociation Disc was rapidly deducing a brand-new formation structure.

Over the past six months, Li Fan's clone had largely completed the analysis of the Primal Chaos Reveals in Form formation within the Boundless Mirror.

After the clone's repairs, the damaged Boundless Mirror's capabilities had seen a substantial improvement.

The corresponding cost was that the clone had grown even weaker once more.

However, Li Fan was not worried.

Sun Luyuan had been slow to complete the task assigned by Mirror Holder Fu. Even his deliberate perfunctory attitude had been noticed by the other party. The pressure he endured grew heavier by the day. It would probably not be long before he had no choice but to yield and once again use the Boundless Mirror to attempt spying on the Tianxuan Mirror.

After sensing the developments on the side of his clone Li Ping, what Li Fan pondered now was an entirely different matter.

He was thinking about the origin of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother.

When the Taiyan Sect first received the inheritance of the Heavenly Capital Great Mage, they should not have possessed the Dao Deviation Stone Mother.

It was only later, from some unknown source, that they obtained this treasure. It directly transformed the Taiyan Sect's cultivation method from one suitable only for a select few into something anyone could practice. This thoroughly solidified the Taiyan Sect's position among the Ten Great Immortal Sects.

"There are many treasures in the Xuanhuang Realm capable of deduction. Take the Boundless Mirror, for example. Though damaged, it can still predict certain future events. Yet the Boundless Mirror, and even the Tianxuan Mirror—their extraordinary powers seem to stem from being refined with immortal-grade strength. They are fundamentally artifacts."

"The Dao Deviation Stone Mother, however, feels more like a pure object."

"A legendary heavenly material and earthly treasure... The Xuanhuang Realm may be vast, but it does not seem the sort of place that could naturally give birth to such a divine item."

Li Fan suddenly recalled an anecdote he had once heard from someone in the Du'e Sect.

A foreign star fell into the Xuanhuang Realm, causing the complete effectiveness of spiritual energy throughout the realm.

"Could it be that this Dao Deviation Stone Mother also came from beyond the stars?"

Li Fan could not help but speculate in this way.

But it remained mere speculation. Unless one day he could invade the Dao Deviation Stone itself, the true answer would stay out of reach.

"The Stone Mother's deduction of the future relies purely on its own formidable computing power. Even so, it has already reached a certain extreme, which shows just how immense that computing power is. If supplemented with external aids to further optimize its deductive methods, who knows what heights it might attain."

For instance, the Dao Deviation Stone within Li Fan's sea of consciousness, though upgraded to high grade after several fusions, still struggled greatly if tasked with deducing formations on its own. Forget immortal-grade grand formations—even something like the Reversal of Life and Death Grand Formation would tax it heavily.

Likewise, relying solely on the Final Dissociation Disc, which after all was merely a mortal object, made deducing immortal-grade grand formations somewhat forced.

But when the two worked in tandem, the difference was vast.

"Mutually complementary, each enhancing the other."

Such a harmonious scene prompted Li Fan to imagine: if the Dao Deviation Stone Mother were paired with something like the Final Dissociation Disc—not limited to formations alone—what kind of spectacle might result?

The Derivation Law Jade's cultivation technique, the Boundless Mirror's foresight...

If all these were augmented by the terrifying computing power of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, a qualitative transformation would surely occur.

It might even be possible, through endless cycles of reincarnation across lifetimes, to infinitely stack enhancements upon the already peerless Dao Deviation Stone Mother.

Pushing it to an unimaginable realm.

The assistance the Final Dissociation Disc had provided Li Fan across his reincarnations needed no further explanation.

If other elements were added...

It made Li Fan's heart stir with excitement.

"Perhaps the way to break through the impasse lies right here."

"A treasure lies before me. It all depends on how I scheme for it."

Li Fan's eyes flickered.

Next

Chapter 1202: Chief Of The Guardian Hall

After all, the Dao Deviation Stone Mother was an existence that had successfully escaped calamities on the scale of the Xuanhuang Realm's extinction. Li Fan would not underestimate it.

"There is no need to rush for quick success. In this life, I can first become an absolutely reliable partner, earn the Taiyan Sect's trust, and thereby learn more of its hidden secrets."

"As for myself, I will first perfect this formation for data contamination. If the opportunity arises, I can test it with a small trial and see its effects."

The Derivation Jade Space provided Li Fan with sufficiently safe time that would not be disturbed by the outside world.

Aside from occasionally having to deal with the little loli's strikes, the rest of the time could be considered quite leisurely.

The exchanges among the three guarding elders had also grown increasingly rare. Each was immersed in their own world.

However, on this day, a special group arrived in the Derivation Jade Space.

They lacked the Attendee Jade Token, yet they had received special approval from the Immortal Alliance. Led by the chief elder of the Guardian Hall, they came here.

The usually somewhat languid Hua Yueye now appeared a little tense. He repeatedly greeted: "Greetings, Elder Xu."

Li Fan noticed that not only the other two guardians but even the typically carefree, fearless little girl of the Derivation Law Jade had become much more restrained.

While paying respects along with them, Li Fan looked at the white-haired old man before him, who seemed blind in both eyes. Information about this chief of the Guardian Hall flashed through his mind.

"Xu Yuanzian has served as chief of the Guardian Hall for more than three thousand years. Though he appears approachable on the surface, he is actually eccentric and rigid in temperament. He adheres to Immortal Alliance regulations to the point of pedantry and shows no mercy. Even when Dharma-Transmitters come and wish to bend the rules, it is impossible. He is highly regarded by Dharma-Transmitter Jiang. Though other Dharma-Transmitters may dislike him, none can do anything about it."

"As someone capable of holding the position of chief of the Guardian Hall, Xu Yuanzian's own strength is unfathomably deep and far beyond ordinary Dao Integration cultivators. The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's sole record of slaying a Dao Integration cultivator and recovering a leaked cultivation method was achieved by this person. It was clean and decisive, so swift that onlookers had no time to react."

Xu Yuanzian stated the purpose of his visit: "The Immortal Alliance's grand examination has only recently concluded. These young ones are among the outstanding participants. By special approval from the Dharma-Transmitter Council, they are permitted to use the Derivation Law Jade once to deduce a Dao Integration-grade cultivation method."

"Hmm?"

"How can this be?!"

The three guardians and the Derivation little girl were all somewhat surprised.

The little girl pouted, about to complain further.

But after catching a glimpse of Xu Yuanzian's expression, she cautiously swallowed her words.

Li Fan reacted the fastest. He nodded and said: "Please have the chief present the council's seal."

His face was serious, showing no leniency simply because the other was his superior.

Xu Yuanzian cooperated fully and projected a seal.

Sensing the aura of a Dharma-Transmitter surrounding it, Li Fan carefully verified the information within. Only then did he nod to indicate the inspection had passed.

He postponed the cultivators who had been waiting for deduction and arranged for the Derivation Law Jade to handle the tasks of these newly recruited Immortal Alliance cultivators first.

There were sixteen in total, varying in age and cultivation level.

The massive Derivation light sphere continuously rotated. The little girl worked on the deductions with puffed-up cheeks.

While this group waited in line for their cultivation methods to be deduced, Li Fan curiously transmitted a question to Xu Yuanzian: "Chief, forgive this junior's poor eyesight, but these cultivators... do not seem to possess particularly outstanding aptitude. Why is the Immortal Alliance treating them so preferentially?"

The transmission did not avoid the other two guardians. They too showed curiosity in their eyes.

Yet what came in response was only silence.

Xu Yuanzian said nothing, as if he had not heard Li Fan's transmission at all.

The two guardians tactfully withdrew their gazes.

Li Fan did not press further. The scene fell into an eerie silence.

Only when the sixteen had all completed their cultivation method deductions and were about to depart did Xu Yuanzian suddenly reply.

"This question is actually something I would also like to know."

"But there are too many unknowns in the world. There is no need to understand everything. It is enough to simply perform one's own duties well."

The three guardians all bowed slightly, indicating they had learned from this.

Only after Xu Yuanzian had completely left the Derivation Jade Space did they dare to converse.

"The chief truly lives up to his reputation for being rigid."

"Fellow Daoist Sun reacted very quickly this time. The chief likes precisely the kind of rule-following attitude you showed just now. It's settled; you've entered the chief's good graces. In five hundred years when you leave, he will surely choose you as the next chief." Hua Yueye teased with a laugh.

Li Fan ignored their banter. First, he recorded and reported the special use of the Derivation Law Jade this time in accordance with regulations.

Then he said lightly: "Being chief of the Guardian Hall is even more like imprisonment than our job here. I have no interest in it."

"I still have a lovely wife waiting for me to return home."

Half serious, half joking, it drew hearty laughter from the two guardians.

This matter was merely a small interlude in the process of guarding the Derivation Law Jade.

Soon enough, the others put it behind them.

Of course, except for Li Fan.

Because...

Among those sixteen people was unmistakably one planted by the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

His name was Yang Fei.

Li Fan knew him, but he did not know Li Fan.

That did not diminish the alertness in Li Fan's heart.

The Immortal Alliance never acted without meaning. For these sixteen, who had only just joined the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, to receive such preferential treatment made it hard not to suspect that some scheme lay behind it.

"It cannot be another case like the previous Chosen Immortal, used as sacrifices to awaken the power left by the Immortal Ancestor in stone statues scattered everywhere? No, that does not fit. If they were sacrifices, they were destined to die. There would be no need to waste the Derivation Law Jade's computational power..."

Li Fan keenly sensed the abnormality behind this.

Meanwhile, in the Sun family's underground secret chamber, his clone had finally waited for the right moment.

Sun Luyuan came to the chamber once more, his eyes bloodshot.

His face showed struggle, yet in the end he still took out a small Tianxuan Mirror.

Then, using his own essence blood to nourish Sun Luyao within the Boundless Mirror, he explained: "The pressure from above is truly too great. Forgive your brother."

"I originally thought I could swear to die rather than comply. But when I look at the faces of everyone in the Sun family..."

Sun Luyao manifested as a black shadow, taking form. Following Li Fan's clone's instructions, he comforted: "Brother, I understand. It's fine. Just leave it to me."

Sun Luyuan trembled slightly upon hearing this. His fists clenched tightly, nails nearly piercing deep into his flesh.

At this moment, the information he had previously uncovered about the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi surfaced again in his mind.

"Perhaps I could try making contact with them."

"Being a dog for a lifetime still carries the risk of being beheaded at any moment. Maybe this so-called Great Qi could truly bring me some surprises?"

The Boundless Mirror pretended to search within the Tianxuan Mirror for information related to the Tianxuan Mirror's clone.

In reality, it was probing the reason why those sixteen cultivators received such preferential treatment.

Next

Chapter 1203: A Secret Encounter In The Star Sea

It was clearly an absolute secret of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

Hidden extremely deeply. Even after repairing a portion of the [Primal Chaos Reveals in Form] grand formation, the Boundless Mirror still found the search extraordinarily difficult.

It had to remain constantly vigilant against detection by the Tianxuan Mirror. During the relentless probing, the Boundless Mirror gradually began to show signs of overload.

The mirror's body started shaking violently, and faint cracks even appeared on its surface.

Sun Luyuan's eyes turned bloodshot. Several times he nearly reached out to retract the small Tianxuan Mirror.

Yet each time, the firm conviction transmitted from Sun Luyao stopped him.

"Brother, I can hold on a little longer."

"I've already found some traces. We can't give up now!"

"Brother, I can do this!"

Hearing Sun Luyao's voice, barely able to suppress the intense pain, Sun Luyuan's earlier hesitation gradually solidified into resolve.

Time seemed to pass with agonizing slowness. Sun Luyuan felt as though his heart were being sliced by knives and needles were pricking his body.

Beyond the worry that his younger brother might not endure and would perish in body and soul, there was also the constant fear that they would be discovered by the Tianxuan Mirror.

Finally, the Boundless Mirror voluntarily severed the devouring black light.

The strange small mirror could no longer even maintain its floating state and fell to the ground with a clatter.

Sun Luyuan hurriedly took out the reserve essence blood and poured it in.

After quite a while, the Boundless Mirror tremblingly regained a little strength.

"How is it..." Sun Luyuan almost did not dare to ask.

"Brother, mission accomplished."

Fortunately, his younger brother did not disappoint him. The moment he heard those four words, Sun Luyuan felt an immense sense of relief.

And after examining the top-secret information his brother had uncovered, his expression kept shifting dramatically.

"Fu's suspicions were correct. Cang has indeed been counter-controlled."

"Weidu..."

"It seems the internal affairs among the Mirror Holders are far more complicated than we imagined," Sun Luyao's shadowy figure said weakly. "Brother, our Sun family is still too weak. One careless move, and we could be torn to pieces."

"Don't worry. Leave what comes next to me," Sun Luyuan said in a deep voice.

"By the way, Brother. Could we leave the small Tianxuan Mirror here this time?" Just as he was about to leave, he suddenly heard Sun Luyao ask.

Sun Luyuan could not help hesitating.

"Don't worry, Brother. I haven't lost control. After these recent extreme explorations, for some reason my mastery over the Boundless Mirror has deepened even further. I was thinking that when I have spare strength, I could try a few more times. In the future, it will be even smoother..."

In the end, Sun Luyuan was persuaded by his younger brother's sincere words.

Moreover, the other's current state was indeed much better than the out-of-control condition after previous explorations.

Thus, after repeatedly giving instructions, he still left the small Tianxuan Mirror behind.

Not long after Sun Luyuan hurriedly departed.

A phantom drifted out from the Boundless Mirror and entered the small Tianxuan Mirror.

Moments later, rays of light flashed continuously. A large quantity of soul-nourishing supplies had been purchased.

Li Fan's clone devoured them to his heart's content. His originally weakened condition rapidly reversed.

With the origin power of the Tianxuan Mirror within him, he could pilfer vast quantities of supplies through the small mirror without anyone noticing.

At that moment, Sun Luyao's figure also drifted out.

He gazed at Li Fan, his expression filled with fear.

These soul-nourishing treasures were of no use to him.

Yet this lord's casual guidance could make his fusion with the Boundless Mirror proceed far more smoothly.

This was something he and his brother had failed to achieve despite centuries of painstaking effort.

The terror of this lord was beyond doubt.

In Sun Luyao's eyes, he was far more fearsome than any Mirror Holder.

His own submission now seemed entirely natural.

"Brother, you can understand me, right?" Sun Luyao thought silently to himself.

The intense pain he had displayed in front of Sun Luyuan earlier had naturally all been an act.

Though it had involved quite a few twists and turns, everything had passed without real danger.

However, the information this lord had gone to such lengths to uncover left him somewhat puzzled.

"Sixteen cultivators, summoned by the Dharma-Transmitter, leaving the Xuanhuang Realm for the outer heavens."

To Sun Luyao, there was nothing particularly unusual about this.

But in Li Fan's eyes, the meaning was entirely different.

While his clone slowly recovered, Li Fan pondered the matter.

"The Dharma-Transmitter actually breaking precedent by summoning cultivators to leave the Xuanhuang Realm?"

"What exactly is going on?"

"With his strength, even if these sixteen were nurtured into Dao Integration realm cultivators by the Immortal Alliance in a short time, they would still be mere ants."

"And they were not selected from the Immortal Alliance's existing Dao Integration cultivators either."

Li Fan recalled the pawn he had arranged, Yang Fei.

There seemed to be nothing particularly special about him.

Li Fan was not worried that Yang Fei might accidentally expose him upon encountering the Dharma-Transmitter, because even he himself did not know he was a spy for the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi.

Yang Fei was genuinely a cultivator of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. It was only that, just before participating in the Immortal Alliance's grand examination, he had befriended a pair of friends who were quite knowledgeable about it and had correctly guessed a few questions.

"It can be confirmed that the Dharma-Transmitter summoning these sixteen cultivators must be related to the Echoing Star Force that is currently shaking the Xuanhuang Realm."

"But what exactly has the Immortal Ancestor encountered that he would need these sixteen cultivators?"

"Moreover, the Echoing Star Force near the Immortal Ruins has already vanished. It is unclear how this group will even leave the Xuanhuang Realm."

The matter was far too secretive. Even the records within the Tianxuan Mirror were scarce.

"It is a pity my Holy Embryo is far away on the other side of the Star Sea. Otherwise, I could follow behind them and investigate thoroughly."

At this thought, Li Fan's mind suddenly paused slightly.

He abruptly recalled that the Immortal Ancestor's purpose in the Darkest Star Sea was to collect fragments of various immortal cultivation worlds.

And the location of his Holy Embryo was precisely the Sea of Relics—a place where the mysterious Echoing Star Force had drawn and gathered all the relics of cultivators scattered across the Star Sea.

"Since the relics are gathering there, could the fragments of immortal cultivation worlds also be converging in a similar way?"

"Beyond the Sea of Relics, might there exist another Sea of Fragmented Realms?"

On the far side of the Star Sea, the Holy Embryo Li Fan, lurking and hidden outside the Immortal Ark, suddenly opened his eyes.

His gaze seemed to pierce through endless ruins and darkness, looking deep into the Sea of Relics.

"Could it be that right now, I am actually very close to the Immortal Ancestor?"

Li Fan could not help thinking this.

"If my guess is correct, then by waiting here like a hunter by the stump, I might even encounter those sixteen cultivators from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance."

"Going further, a being as powerful as the Immortal Ancestor has wandered the Darkest Star Sea for thousands of years. It is impossible for him to be unaware of the Immortal Ark civilization hidden here. The two sides may have already had contact long ago."

"It is only that the Immortal Ark perhaps does not know his true identity."

Although, from the information currently available, the Immortal Ark civilization appeared weak and fragile, it had endured in the Darkest Star Sea for tens of thousands of years and even possessed some understanding of the power of True Immortal Seal Script...

The top-tier strength of the Immortal Ark was absolutely not to be underestimated.

"Zhao Xian's strength is too low, and his knowledge of the Immortal Ark's past is limited."

"It seems a bit of forced growth is needed."

Chapter 1204: The Crippled Heaven Deficient Earth Sea

Drifting in the Star Sea for ten thousand years, even without any civilization rupture, the Immortal Ark’s cultivation method theoretical level had made essentially no progress.

All the worlds they had seen were destroyed, leaving only various destructive remnants of True Immortal Seal Script power echoing in the Star Sea. As the saying goes, rely on the mountain to eat the mountain and rely on the water to eat the water, the Immortal Ark thus placed all its future hopes on researching this mighty power. As for other aspects...

Compared with the calamity-ridden Xuanhuang Realm, it was weaker by more than a notch.

At the very least, in the extreme environment where laws could not be cultivated together, the various wondrous methods born in the Xuanhuang Realm were, in the eyes of Immortal Ark cultivators, all existences worthy of applause and admiration.

With the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s cultivation method repository as foundation, Li Fan’s clone quickly found the most suitable cultivation method for Zhao Xian. Of course, in Zhao Xian’s own view, this was merely a part of the vast number of cultivation methods derived by the future Academy of Dao Attainment through the Derivation Law Jade.

“Spirit-Soul Harmonization Art. A divine art suitable for cultivators even in conditions of extreme resource scarcity. It can achieve perfect coordination between divine sense and spiritual energy; no matter which side is stronger, it can convert it to supplement the weaker side.”

“Within the academy, spiritual energy provision is truly limited. Supplying me to advance to Golden Core is already the limit. Wanting to progress further is fundamentally impossible. Yet I can use secret arts that increase divine sense power to drive an increase in spiritual energy through the growth of divine sense...”

“In the academy’s exchange catalog, the value of the items needed to perform the secret arts is far less than spirit stones.”

Zhao Xian, who was just troubled by being stuck in a bottleneck, suddenly had a flash of inspiration. He recalled even more from that strange dream of the future. In his excitement, he immediately spent all the contributions he had accumulated over the years to exchange for the materials needed to temper his divine sense.

Black Spirit Resin, Crimson Ancient Essence, Mind-Nurturing Amber...

These ordinary mind-calming miscellaneous items in the Immortal Ark were refined by Zhao Xian using flames, calcined and melted into crystalline liquid. Then, like a whale sucking water, they continuously entered his sea of consciousness.

The scalding heat waves nearly incinerated Zhao Xian’s divine sense clean in an instant.

After a brief blank in his consciousness, Zhao Xian discovered that he had actually adapted to this scorching heat. At the same time, the intensity of his divine sense began to rise visibly.

“Truly an unparalleled miraculous art!” Zhao Xian praised in his heart.

However, what Zhao Xian did not know was that the reason his fragile Qi Condensation stage divine sense could survive such intense refining was entirely due to Li Fan’s clone.

Even the majority of the efficacy from the liquids refined from those treasures was absorbed and refined by Li Fan’s clone. After all, Zhao Xian only had late Qi Condensation cultivation; merely sharing a strand was enough to steadily elevate his divine sense.

When Zhao Xian awakened from cultivation, he felt that his divine sense intensity had nearly multiplied several times compared to before. It had already reached the level of mid Foundation Establishment.

Compared with this “vast” divine sense power, his spiritual power cultivation appeared somewhat frail.

It gave the feeling of a giant playing with a toy body.

Zhao Xian suppressed the wild joy in his heart and hurriedly circulated the [Spirit-Soul Harmonization Art]. In his sea of consciousness, an invisible millstone seemed to appear abruptly. It ground the divine sense power into pieces, transforming it into pure energy that nourished Zhao Xian’s body.

Without absorbing any spiritual energy, Zhao Xian’s cultivation advanced at a terrifying speed.

In the end, after three days, Zhao Xian’s divine sense and spiritual power balanced at the early Foundation Establishment stage.

“I’ve succeeded in Foundation Establishment just like that?”

Upon regaining his senses, Zhao Xian looked at his own hands in utter disbelief.

“This breakthrough was way too easy. I didn’t feel any of the heart demons or bottlenecks that Master and the academy seniors always talk about.”

“After Foundation Establishment, my permissions in the academy have jumped up a big notch. I can exchange for more resources now. My cultivation will probably progress much faster than before…”

After all, he was still just a young boy. Fantasizing about the future, Zhao Xian could hardly contain his excitement.

In the end, it was the scene from that dream of the future—the vision of the Immortal Ark’s destruction that pulled him back to reality.

“The road ahead is long. I still have a very long way to go. I cannot grow arrogant.”

Zhao Xian was pondering how to make greater use of his “foreknowledge” advantage to rapidly climb the ranks.

Meanwhile, Li Fan’s clone was savoring the scene from when Zhao Xian had broken through earlier.

Zhao Xian had not truly avoided any obstacles in breaking through the barrier. It was Li Fan who had blocked them for him.

“That sudden fluctuation that appeared out of nowhere and struck straight at the heart.”

“It was not True Immortal Seal Script. It was the Heavenly Dao of the Star Sea…”

At that moment, outside the Immortal Ark, Holy Embryo Li Fan suddenly felt something stir within him.

His body came into direct contact with this space filled with the aura of deathly stillness. During the journey across the Darkest Star Sea, Holy Embryo Li Fan had already realized that this Darkest Star Sea actually shared the same foundational set of Dao principles as the Xuanhuang Realm. In fact, its upper limit was even slightly higher than that of the Xuanhuang Realm.

The sword path, which had already reached its limit in the Xuanhuang Realm, had unknowingly broken through its original bottleneck during the time spent traversing the Star Sea and reached a new height.

The bottleneck manifested as Heaven’s Calamity in the Xuanhuang Realm took on a different form here in the Darkest Star Sea.

“Urging one to return to the mortal path, leaving all thoughts in ashes. For a mere breakthrough from the peak of Qi Condensation, it already possesses such power. If I had not experienced so much and possessed a will far tougher than ordinary people…”

“I’m afraid I would have had to resort to the Immortal Heart Mantra to resolve it.” Holy Embryo Li Fan felt a chill in his heart.

Amid the various illusions carried by that invisible fluctuation, Holy Embryo Li Fan seemed to see scenes of achieving breakthrough success, then enduring countless hardships, yet ultimately perishing in all sorts of tribulations.

No matter how hard one tried, the outcome of failure could not be escaped.

“Better to return to the Dao. Return to the Dao…”

The obsession echoed like demonic sounds in his sea of consciousness, persuading him to abandon cultivation.

“The Heavenly Dao of this Darkest Star Sea is rather interesting. It bears some resemblance to the ancient heart demons recorded in Xuanhuang Realm classics.”

“From this glimpse, one can see that although this Star Sea has been shattered and fragmented by the power of True Immortal Seal Script, its fundamental underlying operations remain intact.”

Holy Embryo Li Fan pondered deeply. His vision suddenly broadened, as if he saw a world far vaster than before, enveloping everything beyond the Xuanhuang Realm.

Even though this world was already heaven crippled and earth deficient, it still brought Li Fan new insights into comprehending the principles of the world.

He once again savored the contents of the Heavenly Doctor’s [Record of Mending Heaven].

“If the situation in the Xuanhuang Realm is like a patient in the terminal stage, stitched together and possessed by another using a corpse as a vessel, then this Darkest Star Sea is something that has long since died. The vast majority of its body has been crushed to pieces by immense force, leaving behind only a few scattered limbs and fragments…”

“The Xuanhuang Realm can still be saved to some extent, but this Darkest Star Sea is completely beyond rescue.”

“Of course, that is from the perspective of the Heavenly Doctor when he wrote the [Record of Mending Heaven].”

Chapter 1205: Breaking Through Star Sea Path With Force

"The only thing that can be done is to dissect and analyze the remnants of these worlds. Perhaps one might still be able to infer and reconstruct the appearance of the Darkest Star Sea when it was alive."

"However, after all, this is an existence several levels higher than the ordinary cultivation world of the Xuanhuang Realm. Even its corpse can yield tremendous benefits."

The aura on Holy Embryo Li Fan suddenly underwent a drastic change.

It erupted like molten lava surging forth. He, who was already at the peak of Soul Transformation, saw his aura instantly swell several times over.

Unfortunately, the Holy Embryo still could not escape the restrictive link between clone and main body.

It was as though an invisible shackle existed, brutally forcing the surging aura back down.

Holy Embryo Li Fan let out a muffled groan and actually spat out a mouthful of blood.

Within the small world evolved by the Black Sword in his body, countless black fissures appeared, on the verge of destruction at any moment.

"The heavens and earth are incomplete; the Dao principles are weakened."

"Indeed, in this Darkest Star Sea, it is possible for a clone to surpass the main body's realm!"

Holy Embryo Li Fan savored the sensation of that momentary explosive surge.

It was like a barbaric, colossal force forcibly shattering the shackles of heaven and earth.

"The driving force behind my breakthrough was still a little short. Halfway through, it collapsed, and I suffered backlash in return."

"If I had accumulated just a bit more, I could have pressed forward in one go and directly broken through that shackle."

Li Fan suddenly recalled the scene from before, when his clone had cultivated the Heaven-Devouring Earth-Swallowing Great Method and forcibly broken through the [Calamity of Heaven].

"This is what is called breaking the Dao with force . In a normal great world, without reaching True Immortal, one simply does not possess such overwhelming power. But in the Darkest Star Sea, with its crippled heavens and deficient earth, the requirements have been greatly lowered."

"Previously, after my clone forcibly shattered the bottleneck of the Calamity of Heaven, it suffered the counterattack from the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao—that giant palm. Yet in this Darkest Star Sea, even such backlash might not descend at all."

Holy Embryo Li Fan felt the same sudden clarity and joy that Zhao Xian had experienced moments ago.

"From one to ten thousand. Since this inherent Dao principle can be broken, can the others be achieved as well?"

"When one whale falls, myriad things are born. The death of the Star Sea may, for the cultivators who ultimately survive, represent an unprecedented grand opportunity spanning billions of years."

"The Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor's strength, far surpassing that of ordinary cultivators, stems not only from the fact that all new method cultivators throughout the Xuanhuang Realm serve as his foundation, but perhaps also has an inseparable connection to this."

"Having remained in the Star Sea for ten thousand years, he is likely infinitely close to the legendary Immortals."

...

The insights gained by the Holy Embryo in the Darkest Star Sea dealt a tremendous shock to Li Fan's main body as well.

The discovery that the crippled heavens and deficient earth of the Star Sea great world actually benefited cultivators by running counter to the Dao caused even his aura to waver slightly.

This drew the attention of the two Dao Integration elders, Hua Yueye and Zhang Xu.

"Fellow Daoist Sun?"

"It's nothing. I merely thought of my delicate wife at home and my mind wandered." Li Fan said with some embarrassment.

The two guardians exchanged strange looks and nodded in understanding.

After forcibly steadying his mind, Li Fan's main body suppressed the urge to immediately rush out of the Xuanhuang Realm.

"Opportunities and crises have always coexisted."

"Although the current Darkest Star Sea is a holy land for cultivation to cultivators, there is no doubt that it is equally fraught with danger."

"If not for the fact that the Holy Embryo was built with the foundation of a heavenly wonder, it would probably have perished midway long ago. Faced with the various strange and eerie phenomena in the Star Sea, even the world evolved from my Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven could not hold out for long."

The invisible voids in the dark emptiness, the deadly black plagues...

No matter which one it was, none could be handled by the current Li Fan.

"Using the Holy Embryo's insights is the same."

Li Fan could only comfort himself in this way.

"If those sixteen Dao Integration cultivators from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance leave the Xuanhuang Realm, they should gradually discover this secret as well."

"I just do not know whether they will be able to return alive."

Li Fan held a pessimistic view regarding the fate of these sixteen people.

In the eyes of the Immortal Ancestor, Xuanhuang Realm cultivators were undoubtedly nothing more than tools.

This summons was unlikely to escape that same fate.

"However, I can find a way to secretly learn the method of flying out of the Xuanhuang Realm without the Echoing Star Power."

"The appearance of Echoing Star Power in this life was purely coincidental. There is no guarantee it can be reproduced in the future. And now that I know the importance of the Darkest Star Sea, how could I possibly miss it?"

"Even if this time point is an absolute secret and cannot be probed right now, waiting a few decades, or until the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi threatens the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the two sides go to war, such secrets will no longer be guarded closely and will not be hard to inquire about."

...

Although that was what he said, Li Fan maintained constant attention on these sixteen individuals at all times.

Through the mini Tianxuan Mirror, the clone left in the Sun family had now stabilized its physical form. As the clone's strength recovered, Sun Luyao within the Boundless Mirror also grew increasingly powerful day by day, and his fusion with the Boundless Mirror became more and more pronounced.

On this day, while peeking into the Tianxuan Mirror, Sun Luyao suddenly mustered his courage and said to Li Fan: "Senior, I seem to have sensed the location

Previous

Next

Chapter 1206: The Ancient Boundless Mirror Covered In Dust

“Truly strange. Even an urgent matter like the Tianxuan Mirror possibly having transformed into a human clone and escaped has been set aside for processing...”

“If not for the fact that I can still make unilateral contact outward, I would almost think the Immortal Alliance had already fallen.” Sun Luyuan said with a frown.

Sun Luyao also put on a look of surprise at this moment: “There is such a thing? I actually did not detect anything related to it while peering into the Tianxuan Mirror?”

The two brothers exchanged a glance.

Sun Luyao said somewhat uncertainly: “I do not know whether my peering failed, or whether the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance is even concealing this matter from the Tianxuan Mirror itself?”

“The Immortal Alliance headquarters is an independent space inside the Tianxuan Mirror. If they want to shield it from the Tianxuan Mirror’s perception, I am afraid it would require the Dharma-Transmitters’ Council and the Mirror Holders to join hands and act together...” Sun Luyuan’s expression grew solemn, as if he had recalled something.

“The Immortal Alliance headquarters is inside the Tianxuan Mirror?” Sun Luyao was unaware of this and received a shock.

Sun Luyuan came back to his senses at this point and nodded, but he did not explain much further.

“That old fellow Fu cannot spare the time, which is good for us. We can take this opportunity to go and find the Boundless Mirror fragment you sensed.”

“Very well, Brother.” Sun Luyao naturally had no objections.

“I originally wanted to use this rare interval to take you to the Essence Blood Pool for some nourishment. We can only do that after we return.” Sun Luyuan shook his head slightly.

The Essence Blood Pool was a secret place of the Sun family, where the bloodline essence blood donated by the direct descendants of the clan was stored.

Refined with secret arts, its effect was even slightly better than the essence blood of Sun Luyuan, their closest relative.

However, collecting essence blood from the family members could not be done too frequently, or it would definitely arouse suspicion. Therefore, it took quite a long time to accumulate enough for nourishment.

Sun Luyao had tasted this benefit several times before, and it did have some effect.

But...

“Compared with our ancestors’ methods, it is far inferior.”

The family essence blood nourishment was, in the end, merely strengthening Sun Luyao’s remnant soul power.

The stronger his own power became, the easier it would be to erode the Boundless Mirror.

Li Fan, on the other hand, had directly displayed the method of controlling the Boundless Mirror before him.

Not only did he no longer need to erode it by brute force, but even the damaged and disordered Boundless Mirror showed signs of slow recovery, so that he no longer felt the previous pain when facing it.

Although he thought this in his heart, the surface work still had to be done.

Thus he put on a somewhat regretful expression.

Sun Luyuan saw this and comforted him: “Let us see how smoothly this trip goes. If we return quickly, there will still be plenty of time.”

After saying this, Sun Luyuan went back and made some preparations.

When he returned to the secret chamber once more, he had already taken on the appearance of a junior member of the Sun family.

This was Sun Luyuan’s usual disguise.

“Let us go.”

He stored the Boundless Mirror in his bosom, quietly left the Sun family, and headed toward Shilin Prefecture.

Perhaps because the damage was unstable, the Boundless Mirror could not use teleportation formations and could only fly with the physical body.

Fortunately, this distance was not far for a Dao Integration cultivator, and they soon reached the entrance of the Hundred Stones Cave-Heaven.

Following Sun Luyao’s sensing, they continuously delved deeper into the underground karst caves.

When they encountered dead ends, they forcibly broke through, getting closer and closer to the destination.

“This damned place.” But the deeper they went, the harder the cave walls became, and even Sun Luyuan started to feel somewhat strained.

“Brother, we are almost there.” Sun Luyao sensed the familiar aura ahead and could not help feeling a little excited.

Perhaps because it had been too deep underground before, it had affected his perception. Only when they truly arrived here did he realize that this fragment might be larger than he had imagined.

“Hmm? Someone behind us?”

Just when they were almost a wall away from the Boundless Mirror fragment, Sun Luyuan’s expression suddenly changed and he stopped in his tracks.

“We have been tracked.”

Sun Luyuan reacted quickly. He immediately changed direction and continued deeper into the cave.

He suddenly accelerated, and his figure instantly vanished among the maze-like passages.

Two cultivators wearing blue long robes now revealed themselves, their expressions somewhat ugly.

“Damn it. He still noticed us.”

“It seems Sun Luyuan’s cultivation has advanced once more.”

“No matter. We have already sealed the entrance. He cannot escape.”

...

However, what the two did not know was that, deeper in the cave, Sun Luyuan had already used the Boundless Mirror to watch all their actions.

“Tianquan, Chen family.”

Sun Luyuan snorted coldly.

He had long known about the various small moves of the Medicine Hall Chen family, which had always coveted the Boundless Mirror.

But the other side still had some foundation, and before they took concrete action, there was no reason to strike first.

“Yet they have taken the initiative to come to my door and given me an opportunity.”

An idea flashed in Sun Luyuan’s mind.

He deliberately revealed his own aura to draw the two Chen family members deeper underground.

Then he quietly hid himself and quickly returned to the location of the Boundless Mirror fragment.

Boom!

He opened a sealed cave. Sun Luyuan quickly locked onto the fragment’s position.

This seemed to be the ruins of an ancient sect. The Boundless Mirror fragment had actually fused into another mirror-shaped treasure.

It was a pity that the sect here had long been destroyed. This mirror-shaped treasure had also been covered in dust and buried underground for thousands of years.

Knowing that this place was not suitable to linger, Sun Luyuan had no time to examine it closely and simply stored it away.

After that he no longer bothered to hide himself. He even burned his essence blood, and his full strength erupted in an instant.

In the blink of an eye, he had already rushed to the exit of the Hundred Stones Cave-Heaven.

The two cultivators guarding the entrance were caught off guard and could not stop him for a moment. Sun Luyuan actually charged straight out.

“Chen family! If I do not avenge this, I swear I am not human!”

Clearly uninjured, Sun Luyuan suddenly spat out a mouthful of blood, and his aura rapidly weakened.

His voice boomed like thunder, echoing for a long time over Shilin Prefecture.

Then Sun Luyuan turned into a streak of light and continued flying toward the central region of the Immortal Alliance.

He left the two Chen family cultivators somewhat bewildered.

“Bad.” The four of them looked at one another, their expressions gloomy.

Since the matter had already been exposed, they could not pursue and kill him in broad daylight.

“This operation has failed. The Sun family will probably tear off all pretense now. We should return early to discuss how to respond.”

“Hmph. At worst we will give up on that immortal artifact and make the matter public. I refuse to believe that their small Sun family can still keep this immortal artifact!”

The four Chen family members vanished one after another.

Inside Shilin Prefecture, Sun Luyuan’s furious roar from earlier gradually sparked discussion among the people.

A disturbance slowly began to spread.

At the same time, Sun Luyuan had already returned home.

He went straight to the underground secret chamber and took out the Boundless Mirror.

Chapter 1207: Immortal Calamity, Wind Obliterating Manifests

On the way back, Sun Luyuan could clearly sense the restless stirring of the Boundless Mirror in his arms, along with his younger brother Sun Luyao's familiar, out-of-control roars. He forcibly suppressed it until he safely returned before releasing it.

"Brother, give it to me quickly! I can't stand it anymore!"

The mirror surface shook violently without cease. Sun Luyao's misty black shadow seemed to be ceaselessly battered by a hurricane, twisting and distorting.

"Calm down!"

Sun Luyuan gave a low shout. He skillfully offered a drop of his own essence blood, which eased his brother's frenzy.

Only then did he take out the mirror retrieved from the ruins of the Hundred Stones Winter.

The aura of the same origin and lineage confirmed that the main body of this mirror was a fragment of the Boundless Mirror. Yet it had been reforged into a separate treasure.

Sun Luyuan carefully broke the remaining restrictions on the mirror before tossing it to his brother.

With the bindings gone, the darkness greedily drifted above the mirror.

A beam of dim light spewed from Sun Luyao's mouth, corroding this ancient relic.

As the mirror gradually vanished within the black light, the mirror to which Sun Luyao was bound, that somewhat aged and damaged Boundless Mirror—began to change at a visible speed.

Traces left by the years slowly faded away. The sense of antiquity disappeared, replaced instead by an aura of having endured countless hardships and vicissitudes.

The surface of the Boundless Mirror surged from time to time with a luster that made one's heart palpitate.

Even Sun Luyuan felt a slight chill in his mind.

"So this is the might of an immortal artifact..." he thought to himself, growing even more expectant of his brother's performance once he awakened from the devouring process.

Meanwhile, the true master of the Boundless Mirror—Li Fan's clone hidden within it, could witness the transformation of the Boundless Mirror far more directly.

Or rather, its restoration.

The originally incomplete [Boundless, Primal Chaos Reveals in Form] grand formation was slowly being filled in.

As if it had gained a core, an indescribable wondrous power flowed forth like a gentle stream, constantly pouring out from the formation.

It nourished the dilapidated body of the Boundless Mirror.

This was also why, in the eyes of outsiders, the immortal artifact appeared brand new in the blink of an eye.

Even Li Fan's clone benefited in the process. It advanced from a mere clone toward becoming a true soul fragment.

Li Fan even had the feeling that if he continued to absorb the energy from the Boundless Mirror in this way, given enough time, this clone might grow even stronger than his main body's divine soul!

One must know that Li Fan had reached his current realm step by step, with an extremely solid foundation.

Leaving aside the theoretical accumulation from multiple reincarnations, just the Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven and the Xuanhuang Life-Death Transformative Phenomenon alone represented the essence of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Yet on such a foundation, the peak of Dao Integration realm could actually be caught up to by the energy within this mere remnant mirror!

Even if it was only in terms of the divine soul, it still shook Li Fan profoundly.

He let out the same sigh as Sun Luyuan: "So this is the might of an immortal artifact?"

The energy surging within the Boundless Mirror resembled order, yet also vitality.

However, just when Li Fan thought it would completely repair the Boundless Mirror, the mirror suddenly fell to the ground out of control.

The mirror surface projected a mass of light and shadow, enveloping the underground secret chamber.

Scenes flashed by rapidly.

The prophetic images within caused both Sun Luyuan and Li Fan's hearts to tremble violently.

The peaceful Xuanhuang land was engulfed in the flames of war.

Cultivators of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance clashed fiercely with another unknown force.

Arrows of light streaked across the sky in endless flight. Wherever they struck, earth-shattering explosions erupted.

A towering giant radiating golden light clashed fiercely with a black-haired, bare-chested man, locked in a battle impossible to separate.

The shockwaves from their power even caused the white mist barrier between heaven and earth to twist and fracture.

What made hearts tremble even more was that, amid this battlefield, there stood another figure clad in black imperial robes, his face impossible to discern.

He towered above everyone else.

Opposite him loomed yet another indistinct silhouette.

Boom!

As if the deduction had reached its extreme limit, the scenes projected by the Boundless Mirror suddenly warped and converged into a single point.

Then they erupted in a violent burst of white light.

When the radiance faded, only a dead, pitch-black void remained.

Crack!

Just as the Boundless Mirror's surface had begun to show signs of recovery, another fissure appeared.

Sun Luyao let out a miserable scream. His black shadow was forcibly drawn back into the mirror.

"Brother!"

Sun Luyuan snapped out of his shock in an instant. His expression changed drastically as he reached for the Boundless Mirror.

Yet the moment he drew near, his hand recoiled as if scorched by blazing flames.

Sun Luyuan stared gravely at his outstretched right hand.

In that brief span, it seemed to have endured thousands of years. The flesh had withered, skin shriveled, and deep wrinkles surged forth.

It was not only his physical body.

Sun Luyuan felt that a portion of his divine soul had genuinely experienced the relentless passage of time, forever altered beyond restoration.

If even an accidental touch produced such an effect, one could imagine what had happened to Li Fan's clone inside the Boundless Mirror.

Earlier, when he witnessed the Boundless Mirror spontaneously deducing the future of the Xuanhuang Realm, Li Fan—aside from his shock, had wanted to examine it more closely.

But then a sudden, lethal sense of crisis welled up within him, leaving him no time to dwell on anything else.

This obliterating power lurked in the deepest core of the Boundless Mirror. Even Li Fan's clone, who had largely mastered the mirror, had detected not the slightest trace of it before it erupted.

And when it came, it arrived with such ferocious, explosive force.

Like the erosion of endless ages, the Boundless Mirror which had only just regained some vitality after absorbing the fragment—suddenly endured the vicissitudes of countless calamities in an instant, decaying once more.

Even the core grand formation, [Boundless, Primal Chaos Reveals in Form], was ravaged as if swept by a violent gale.

Parts of its once-complete structure became fragmented again.

Li Fan's clone naturally could not escape unscathed.

Already merely a wisp of remnant soul, it quickly flickered like a candle in the wind, on the verge of complete dissipation.

First, he had previously refined the Boundless Mirror through formation techniques, essentially claiming most of it as his own. Dwelling within the mirror, he could borrow its power and gain a measure of resistance.

Second, at the critical moment when he sensed his clone was about to perish, Li Fan instinctively recited the Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Mantra.

Though it could not recover what had already been worn away, it greatly slowed the rate at which his clone was being erased in this storm.

After enduring bitterly for a long time, the terrifying calamity raging within the Boundless Mirror finally began to subside.

Li Fan realized it had not truly vanished.

Instead, it had retreated once more into the depths of the Boundless Mirror, lying dormant again.

Only then did Sun Luyuan's anxious voice slowly reach him.

"Brother, what happened to you?"

Sun Luyao, however, had suffered such severe damage that he was too weak to respond.

Seeing this, Sun Luyuan reacted swiftly.

He immediately carried the Boundless Mirror into the Sun family's essence blood pool.

Though called an essence blood pool, it carried no trace of blood stench. Instead, it brimmed with the fragrant aroma of heavenly materials and earthly treasures.

The pool water was not crimson but a dreamy, iridescent seven-colored radiance, like flowing celestial light.

Soaking within it, Li Fan's now-aged clone finally felt a measure of relief.

"Why is the consumption so rapid?" Sun Luyuan was startled to see how quickly the essence blood pool water changed color.

Nevertheless, he gritted his teeth and left, clearly going to gather more essence blood.

Chapter 1208: Hidden Dangers In The Xuanhuang Realm

Li Fan, having narrowly survived the ordeal, began to recall the terrifying power he had just sensed within the Boundless Mirror.

"Is this the reason the Boundless Mirror was damaged?"

"It gives me the feeling that it even surpasses those True Immortal Seal Scripts."

"Although everything I have seen so far are mere imitations of True Immortal Seal Scripts, what remains in the Boundless Mirror is only a lingering aftershock."

"Could the calamity that befell the immortal realm be related to this?"

Despite the myriad questions swirling in his mind, Li Fan had no intention of delving deeper into it for the time being.

An existence capable of destroying the immortal realm was absolutely beyond what he could confront at present.

"It only lingers somewhat within the Boundless Mirror. Even if induced to release, it would at most envelop the area of a single city."

"It might have some use in a critical moment."

Li Fan pondered.

"No wonder a majestic immortal artifact ended up occupied by the remnant soul of a mere Foundation Establishment cultivator. That annihilating storm is truly too terrifying."

"An immortal artifact originally belonging to the Boundless Sect and located in the Xuanhuang Realm underwent such a change..."

"Does that not mean this calamity once occurred in the Xuanhuang Realm?"

"A mere aftershock was enough to damage an immortal artifact. If it truly happened, it is impossible for no traces to remain."

Li Fan's thoughts paused. Suddenly, two major events that had altered the course of Xuanhuang Realm history came to mind, and he wondered if they might be connected.

The first was the inexplicable disappearance of Xuantian King Xuanyuan Hong.

The second was the disappearance of those six top-tier Longevity Immortal Realm cultivators who had besieged the Immortal Ancestor—back when he was only at the Dao Integration stage.

"These people's cultivations stood at the pinnacle of their era."

"Ordinary things within the Xuanhuang Realm could not possibly harm them. Unless..."

A stir arose in Li Fan's heart.

Li Fan also recalled the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage, who had arrived in the Xuanhuang Realm after the great calamity and had remained stationed in Daoyuan Prefecture ever since his injury, never moving.

"Was his arrival back then truly coincidental?"

At this moment, thick doubt rose uncontrollably in Li Fan's heart.

This realization also served as a stark warning to him.

Even though his perspective had now extended beyond the Xuanhuang Realm and reached the far end of the Star Sea, there were still existences within the Xuanhuang Realm that he could not see through and had yet to reach.

No matter what he did, he must exercise utmost caution.

Amid these surging thoughts, Sun Luyuan hurriedly returned.

He refined a large mass of essence blood along with several materials and added them to the essence blood pool.

The pool water, which had grown somewhat dim, once again shimmered with flowing radiance.

Li Fan also turned his attention to the condition of the Boundless Mirror.

It had almost reverted to the state it was in before absorbing the fragments.

Still, it was slightly more complete than before. Li Fan believed that once Sun Luyao awoke, the situation would improve to some extent.

Moreover, Li Fan had briefly glimpsed a more complete version of the Grand Formation of the Primordial Mystic Spirit. Even if perfect restoration was out of reach, making some repairs and patches was still within his capabilities.

Three days later, Sun Luyao, the spirit of the Boundless Mirror, finally awoke briefly from his deep slumber.

"Brother, I need some things."

After transmitting the list of materials required for repairing the formation, he fell unconscious once more.

Sun Luyuan looked at the detailed list his younger brother had sent and hurriedly set off to gather them.

During this period, the Chen family of the Medicine Hall had been closely monitoring Sun Luyuan's movements, fearing he might lash out in desperation if pushed too far.

Yet Sun Luyuan seemed to have forgotten the matter entirely. He came and went busily, apparently preoccupied with some urgent affair.

This left the Chen family somewhat perplexed.

Moreover, their backer at the Immortal Alliance headquarters, Dharma-Transmitter Chang, remained unreachable.

Thus, the Chen family chose to hold back and observe quietly.

Under Sun Luyuan's relentless, cost-no-object efforts, the required materials were soon fully assembled.

On the surface, it appeared as though Sun Luyao was absorbing them, but in reality, it was Li Fan's clone who took action.

As the shattered core of the formation was gradually restored, the condition of the Boundless Mirror finally began to stabilize.

After hearing about what his brother had endured, Sun Luyuan's brows furrowed tightly.

"Little brother, do you still remember the ancient ruin where we first found this mirror?"

Sun Luyao was slightly stunned.

"Before this, I thought that ruined site was merely an ordinary ancient relic eroded by the passage of time. But now that you mention it..."

Sun Luyuan's expression turned somewhat grim: "That place seems to have been destroyed by the obliterating storm within the mirror..."

"Perhaps it was precisely because of that earlier release that we brothers were able to seize the opportunity. Otherwise, with our cultivation at the time, how could we possibly have subdued this mirror?"

Sun Luyao recalled the scene from back then and slowly came to the same realization.

"There is such a possibility. I still remember that almost nothing of value could be found in that ruin. The only thing the Tianxuan Mirror acknowledged gave a very low contribution value for recovery..."

"Focus on recovering from your injuries. In a while, I will return to that old ruin to investigate. The entrance was extremely well hidden, and since only ruins remain, after so many years, it likely has not changed much. There may be some discoveries." Sun Luyuan said gravely.

"Be careful, Brother." Sun Luyao did not try to dissuade him, only offering a word of caution.

"And the Chen family..." Sun Luyuan suddenly gave a cold snort.

He recalled the scene that the Boundless Mirror had inexplicably deduced earlier.

"That force locked in a fierce struggle with the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance seems to be the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi that my brother previously discovered."

"In that case, the Tianxuan Mirror's prediction does have some basis."

"Both supreme artifacts have made such forecasts..."

"It seems the time has come to attempt contact with them."

Of course, Sun Luyuan would not go straight to their door himself from the outset.

After all, the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi had not yet openly declared enmity with the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. He would first send a trusted confidant to probe the situation before deciding on the next concrete steps.

By comparison, the Boundless Mirror—which he had once thought was a supreme treasure but now realized could erupt into a catastrophic anomaly at any moment—worried him far more.

Since his younger brother had already become the mirror spirit, abandoning it was absolutely impossible.

Sun Luyuan made some preparations and planned to first return to that ancient ruin to take a look.

The incident at Hundred Stones Cave-Heaven had made Sun Luyuan aware that the Chen family was surely still watching him from the shadows.

To avoid unnecessary trouble, Sun Luyuan directly contacted an old friend.

Ji Hongdao!

"Old Ji, there is something I would like to ask for your help with..." Sun Luyuan reached out through a spiritual talisman.

Soon, Ji Hongdao arrived at the Sun family gates.

"Hahaha, Old Sun, the favor I owed you from back then can finally be repaid." Ji Hongdao laughed heartily.

"I heard you recently clashed with the Chen family of the Medicine Hall?" Once inside the room, Ji Hongdao suddenly lowered his voice.

"Yes. The treasure in which Luyao resides was somehow discovered by the Chen family." Sun Luyuan stated plainly.

Ji Hongdao understood immediately.

"Rest assured. Though they have a Dharma-Transmitter as their backer, you also have a Mirror Holder supporting you from behind."

"Since their operation was exposed this time, they definitely will not dare act rashly again in the short term."

Sun Luyuan said solemnly: "A cornered beast is the most dangerous. For this trip, I will have to trouble you, brother."
