Chapter 1209: Hidden Immortal Sect Beneath The Earth

Ji Hongdao said proudly: "Rest assured. With me here, those people will absolutely not dare to act recklessly again."

"Even the Chen family would not dare to offend both the Strategy Formation Hall and the Guardian Hall at the same time."

Sun Luyuan cupped his hands: "Then I must thank Brother Ji in advance."

"The destination of my journey this time is Fuguang Prefecture, the Great Rift Valley."

Upon hearing this, a trace of surprise flashed in Ji Hongdao's eyes. He reminded: "I'm afraid this trip may encounter some complications."

Seeing Sun Luyuan's somewhat puzzled expression, he paused: "What? You didn't know? Due to the previous great upheaval in Xuanhuang, the Fuguang Great Rift Valley has already been compressed and resealed."

"The terrain has long since changed beyond recognition. Trying to enter beneath the original rift..."

Sun Luyuan furrowed his brows tightly.

After a long while, he let out a deep sigh: "Since that is the case, I can only do my utmost and leave the rest to fate."

Carrying the Boundless Mirror, which had slightly recovered its vitality, Sun Luyuan followed Ji Hongdao and flew toward the destination in a streak of light.

The two evidently shared an uncommon relationship. On the way to the Fuguang Great Rift Valley, he explained the purpose of this journey to Ji Hongdao in detail.

Of course, he somewhat downplayed and concealed the specific destructive power of the obliterating wind disaster that the Boundless Mirror had suddenly unleashed.

"You suspect that the ruin where the Boundless Mirror was originally discovered was actually created by the Boundless Mirror itself?" Having not witnessed that terrifying calamity with his own eyes, Ji Hongdao did not dwell on it too much. He simply regarded it as something capable of threatening a Dao Integration cultivator.

"Exactly. Since it is the origin of the Boundless Mirror, perhaps we can find a way to counter the obliteration calamity of the Boundless Mirror. Even if we only uncover some records—even failed ones, they can serve as lessons and experience..."

As the two were conversing, their expressions suddenly darkened at the same time.

They had clearly detected a Dao Integration cultivator from the Chen family following behind them, as expected.

"It's Chen Ruoyu of the Chen family."

"Do not get entangled with him. Shake him off."

Ji Hongdao transmitted.

A layer of white light enveloped them. With the enhancement of the formation, the two's speed suddenly surged, becoming several times faster in an instant.

In the blink of an eye, they had already vanished at the edge of the sky.

Seeing this, Chen Ruoyu hurriedly accelerated to pursue.

Moments later.

"Brother Ji's golden cicada shedding its shell technique is truly impressive."

"Hmph. Dealing with these idiots who only know how to refine pills is naturally no problem."

Two illusory figures suddenly appeared out of thin air and then flew off in another direction.

After shaking off the Chen family's pursuit and spending most of the day, the two finally arrived at the so-called Fuguang Prefecture Great Rift Valley.

Sun Luyuan looked at the long, uplifted dragon-like mountain range on the ground, and his face could not help twitching.

"I wonder if Brother Sun still remembers the exact location from back then?" Ji Hongdao asked.

Sun Luyuan carefully recalled: "I can only confirm the general direction. The rest will depend on luck."

At that moment, Sun Luyuan suddenly sensed a message from his younger brother.

"Brother, get a bit closer downward. I seem to have a faint sensation."

Sun Luyuan was overjoyed.

He skimmed low and circled the mountain range once, quickly locking onto the source of the sensation.

"Wait."

Just as Sun Luyuan prepared to forcefully dive underground, Ji Hongdao stopped him.

He took out a flying boat: "Let's go in with this."

"Dust-Crossing Boat?!" Sun Luyuan was somewhat surprised, but he did not hesitate and immediately entered it.

The Dust-Crossing Boat transformed into a streak of yellow light. Like ice melting into water, it slowly sank into the ground.

Watching the surrounding soil layers constantly shifting and the many residual restriction auras left in the ruins, Sun Luyuan sighed: "It's fortunate to have Brother Ji's treasure. Otherwise, it would have taken quite some effort."

Ji Hongdao nodded: "It was also a reward from Chief Lu. She has been at the Immortal Alliance headquarters recently and cannot get away. So she ordered me to run this errand for her."

The Dust-Crossing Boat continued descending all the way down. The boat's hull constantly collided with buried objects in the strata.

A red-orange glow emanated from the boat's surface, neutralizing the impacts.

Ji Hongdao, meanwhile, formed hand seals from time to time to set up formations, bolstering the Dust-Crossing Boat's defenses.

"This rift valley—no one knows what kind of place it was in ancient times. So many ruins are buried here."

It was not just architectural remnants. The deeper they went, the more frequently the two encountered massive piles of bones.

Some were human; others belonged to unknown gigantic creatures.

Even the two, who were well-traveled and experienced, could not help feeling a quiet chill in their hearts.

"Brother, it seems to be right here!"

When they had descended nearly three thousand zhang underground, Sun Luyao finally spoke up.

The Dust-Crossing Boat abruptly came to a halt.

The pressure here was already immense. The red halo around the boat swirled continuously, countering the external compression.

Sun Luyuan and Ji Hongdao looked around but could not locate any ruins.

"The changes are too great; not a single trace from back then remains. But the depth is indeed about right," Sun Luyuan said.

Ji Hongdao also surveyed the surroundings: "This place..."

"It seems a bit unusual!"

A circular formation disk flew out from his chest, hovering above his head and casting white light in all directions.

Under the illumination of the white light, Sun Luyuan vaguely caught glimpses of dark golden characters flashing by and vanishing.

"There are actually formation remnants left," Sun Luyuan said in surprise.

Although he was not proficient in formations, he still understood some basic principles.

"Brother, it seems we can enter from here."

Just as Ji Hongdao was using the formation to scan the surroundings in search of an entrance, his younger brother Sun Luyao had already found it ahead of him.

Ji Hongdao immediately directed the disk's white light to verify.

Sure enough, in a spot that appeared to be nothing but solid rock, the golden characters appeared with noticeably greater frequency.

Ji Hongdao closed his eyes and deduced.

After the time it takes to drink a cup of tea, he let out a low shout and pointed forward.

It was as if a sheet of talisman paper had been torn apart. Countless golden characters shattered and scattered.

The Dust-Crossing Boat seized the moment and flew inside.

The scattered characters showed signs of rejoining and fusing once more.

"No need to worry. This is a concealed formation that can absorb underground energy and operate automatically."

"Looking at its style, it is completely different from formations today. It must be quite ancient. I have already fully grasped its structure and can enter or exit at will," Ji Hongdao said proudly.

Sun Luyuan offered repeated praise: "It was absolutely right to invite Brother Ji this time! Otherwise, just this formation alone would have troubled me for who knows how long. But what is strange is that when my brother and I came here before, this formation did not exist..."

Sun Luyuan suddenly became alert.

Inside the Boundless Mirror, listening to the conversation between his brother and Ji Hongdao, Sun Luyao secretly glanced at the faint figure not far away. The awe in his heart grew even deeper.

Ji Hongdao had taken the time of a cup of tea to break the formation.

Yet that senior...had merely glanced once and already located the entrance.

Indeed, he had not discovered this place through any personal sensing.

It was entirely due to the senior's guidance.

Feeling Sun Luyao's gaze filled with both respect and fear, Li Fan's clone paid it no mind.

While Sun Luyuan and the others were still cautiously exploring here, he had already used the Boundless Mirror to complete a full scan of the ruins the instant they arrived.

"The First Origin Sect?"

Li Fan's tone carried an inexplicable note.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1210: The Bones Of The Grand Mage

The First Origin Sect was once one of the Ten Great Immortal Sects, on par with the Heavenly Sword Sect and the Grand Dao Sect.

Yet its presence was relatively faint in comparison.

It had almost no contact in the Fallen Immortal Realm, and in the current era, records related to it were scarcely found.

It was as though it had suddenly vanished from the world.

Apart from what he had incidentally learned from Senior Stone Tablet earlier—that the Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven, which he had used to achieve Nascent Soul, was the handiwork of the First Origin Sect, Li Fan had never gained much further understanding of it.

Unexpectedly, today, three thousand zhang beneath this Rift Valley, he had discovered traces of the First Origin Sect.

But traces were all they were.

The entire ruin was empty, devoid of any living soul.

The only remnant was a stone stele inside a collapsed building at the center of the ruin, upon which the two characters [First Origin] could still faintly be made out.

Beyond that, nothing of the former grandeur of one of the ancient Ten Great Immortal Sects could be found here.

"It seems the First Origin Sect did not fare well at all."

After inquiring with Sun Luyuan, he confirmed that this was indeed the ruin where they had originally discovered the Boundless Mirror.

However, back then they had only intruded upon a small portion of it.

For some unknown reason now, the formations that once separated the different areas had vanished, revealing the true appearance of the entire First Origin Sect.

"Boundless, Primal Chaos Reveals in Form!"

Li Fan's clone murmured softly.

In utter silence, the Boundless Mirror once again emitted a faint glow imperceptible to ordinary people.

In an instant, it enveloped the entire First Origin Sect ruin.

Thus, a complete structural map of the ruin immediately appeared synchronized within the Boundless Mirror.

Even details such as which areas still retained spiritual energy fluctuations, which locations concealed hidden secret chambers, the degree of danger in each place...

All such information was present and accounted for.

And all of this had been accomplished by the Boundless Mirror right under Ji Hongdao's very nose.

Yet this Formation Master, who already ranked among the absolute pinnacle of formation experts in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance—had not sensed the slightest thing.

Li Fan could not help but recall the scene from his 114th life: when he had traveled from the Congyun Sea to Tianyu Prefecture to study formations, and Ji Hongdao had personally lectured to over a thousand formation prodigies of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

Back then, in his eyes, let alone Ji Hongdao—even Huangfusong's level of attainment in formations had seemed utterly unreachable.

Yet after just over a dozen cycles of reincarnation, his own mastery of formations had reached a level that Ji Hongdao could no longer comprehend.

"With each life, I keep moving forward, while they remain stuck in place..."

"The gap between us will only grow wider in the future."

Li Fan watched Sun Luyuan and the other as they continued to advance step by step with utmost caution, and he shook his head slightly.

No longer patient enough to wait, he directly ordered Sun Luyao to guide the two men toward the only place in the current First Origin Sect that still held any value.

"Brother Sun, be careful. The formation aura here is the most intact..." Ji Hongdao warned as he took the lead to clear the path.

Sun Luyao felt somewhat speechless inside but could not afford to reveal it.

The two proceeded with extreme caution, passing through a wall concealed by formations, and entered a secret chamber.

At the innermost part of the chamber stood two statues—one large and one small.

The larger one sat cross-legged on a rush cushion, holding a whisk in hand.

The smaller one stood behind it, a smile upon its face.

This peculiar pair of statues instantly drew the attention of both men.

They did not act rashly but observed carefully.

"It seems to be nothing more than ordinary stone statues with no hidden mechanisms." After a long while, Ji Hongdao rendered his judgment.

Sun Luyuan breathed a sigh of relief upon hearing this.

At the same time, within the Boundless Mirror.

Li Fan glanced at the scene previously scanned and rendered by the Primal Chaos Reveals in Form Formation, then looked again at the secret chamber.

His heart sank.

"Can you see those two statues?" Li Fan asked Sun Luyao.

"Statues? What statues?" Sun Luyao was stunned, utterly bewildered.

"Tell your brother not to touch them." Li Fan offered no explanation and simply issued the command.

Having received Sun Luyao's warning, Sun Luyuan, out of trust in his younger brother—still stopped Ji Hongdao from probing further.

"Little brother, what exactly is dangerous about these things?"

Yet no matter how long he scrutinized the stone statues, Sun Luyuan could not discern anything unusual about them.

"I once came across a record in an ancient text that somewhat resembled these two statues. They seem to be the source of the Ten Sects' inheritance—the Great Mage of Heavenly Capital..." Sun Luyao relayed Li Fan's judgment verbatim.

"The Great Mage of Heavenly Capital?" Both Sun Luyuan and Ji Hongdao involuntarily took a step back.

They had heard of this legendary figure.

It was said he stood at the half-step True Immortal realm.

And anything even remotely connected to a True Immortal inevitably inspired dread.

The higher one's cultivation, the more one understood the vast chasm between ordinary cultivators and True Immortals, and thus the greater the fear.

Within the Boundless Mirror, Li Fan's clone thought at lightning speed.

He had once suffered greatly at the hands of the Faceless True Immortal statue.

It had taken six lifetimes of reincarnation to finally eliminate the Faceless True Immortal's influence over him.

Although the Great Mage of Heavenly Capital was not on the same level as the Faceless True Immortal, he too had once left a mark upon Li Fan.

Caution was imperative.

"The First Origin Sect's inheritance comes from the Great Mage of Heavenly Capital, and statues of the Great Mage are placed within the sect. That seems reasonable at first glance."

"But..."

"The other nine of the Ten Immortal Sects appear to have no such statues of the Great Mage."

"Moreover, even if they wished to pay respects, such statues should be placed in a grand, solemn central hall. Why would they be kept in a dim, underground secret chamber?"

Li Fan's intuition told him something was deeply wrong here.

"The First Origin Sect does not seem like a sect that reveres teachers and values the Dao. According to Senior Stone Tablet's description, as early as when the great calamity first appeared in the Xuanhuang Realm, they had already begun cultivating the Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven with the intent of directly devouring the Xuanhuang Realm to create an entirely new immortal cultivation world. They were only stopped later."

"The one who thwarted the First Origin Sect was undoubtedly Mr. Bai."

"Before the Xuanhuang heavens and earth had fully turned against cultivators, cultivators still revered the Heavenly Dao. Heaven and earth gave birth to them, like parents to cultivators."

"Yet the First Origin Sect harvested the essence of heaven and earth without the slightest mercy, little different from the new method cultivators."

"The Great Mage of Heavenly Capital..."

Once more, Li Fan employed the Primal Chaos Reveals in Form formation to scan the statues of the Great Mage of Heavenly Capital.

There were two—one large and one small.

According to legend, it was the seated one who had imparted countless techniques and secret arts to the Ten Sects without reservation.

But an indistinct intuition deep within told Li Fan that the standing child figure might be the far more extraordinary one.

Thus, the Primal Chaos Reveals in Form formation focused its attention primarily on the smiling child statue.

The Boundless Mirror's faint glow swept over the area containing the statue again and again.

With each pass of the scan, more lines gradually began to emerge in the images displayed within the Boundless Mirror.

Yet at the same time, these lines slowly faded away over time.

Li Fan narrowed his eyes and increased the speed of the Boundless Mirror's scanning.

The lines began to accumulate once more.

"Brother, help me."

Unable to withstand the excessive strain any longer, Sun Luyao appealed to his elder brother for aid.

Only after receiving support from Sun Luyuan's essence blood was the Boundless Mirror finally able to clearly reveal the true appearance of the statues in the secret chamber.

They were two bones.

As though every shred of flesh and blood had been stripped from some creature, leaving behind only these two remaining bones.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1211: The Body Is The Source Of The Immortal Calamity

His heart sank.

Under normal circumstances, if they were merely ordinary remains, Li Fan should not have sensed any living aura from them at all.

Yet these two bones before him...

They caused Li Fan's heart to tighten abruptly, as if he were being watched by the gaze of some entity.

The stone statues formed from the bones also seemed ready to come alive at any moment, with strands of eerie aura seeping out from them.

Li Fan decided instantly. Without another word, he directly ended the Boundless Mirror's probe.

The scene before him changed abruptly.

The bones vanished, and what met his eyes were once again the two seemingly ordinary stone statues.

Only then did the sense of crisis in Li Fan's heart slowly fade away.

"Senior..." Sun Luyao was frightened by the scene just now and felt somewhat at a loss.

"Tell your brother to stay far away from those two statues." Li Fan instructed with a calm exterior, but in reality massive waves had already surged in his heart.

Were those two bones truly the Grand Mage's?

Judging from their outward appearance, they most likely belonged to the Grand Mage of Heavenly Capital.

But why would he, with a half-Immortal body, have two bones left in the First Origin Sect?

"Could it be that he was so severely injured that he was powerless to retrieve his own bones even after they were seized by cultivators?"

This thought flashed through Li Fan's mind in an instant.

But soon Li Fan himself placed a huge question mark over it.

Was it possible?

The power that enveloped the entire Yuandao Prefecture and could devour ten years of time indicated that the Grand Mage should not have fallen yet.

The might of a half-Immortal, no matter how heavy the injury, was not something cultivators below the mortal realm could contend with.

Not to mention that it was an ancient immortal cultivator from the past.

"Unless..."

"Boundless Mirror."

Li Fan looked toward the timid and shrinking Sun Luyao, the new Boundless Mirror spirit.

When the Sun brothers had first picked up the damaged Boundless Mirror, the immortal artifact's spirit had already disappeared. That was what had allowed the two mortals to take advantage of the opportunity.

Judging from the obliterating wind calamity that had erupted within the Boundless Mirror not long ago, it seemed that during the confrontation with this calamity the artifact spirit had dissipated and the Boundless Mirror had been damaged.

"The Boundless Mirror is an ancient immortal artifact of the Xuanhuang Realm, bestowed upon the Boundless Sect by the Immortal Realm. Even if the Immortal Realm was destroyed by an unspeakable calamity, it should not have affected the Boundless Mirror. Yet strangely this obliterating wind disaster, which should never have appeared in the mortal world, unfolded right inside the Boundless Mirror..."

In that instant Li Fan recalled the event at the far end of the Star Sea: after his Holy Embryo clone infiltrated the Immortal Ark, it had synchronously brought the invisible calamity from the Darkest Star Sea into it, causing several cultivators to die tragically.

"Transmission..."

"Outside the Xuanhuang Realm..."

In the history of the Xuanhuang Realm the only known entity that had possibly come into contact with the Immortal Realm and originated from outside the Xuanhuang Realm was the Grand Mage of Heavenly Capital.

"From this perspective both the Boundless Mirror and even the First Origin Sect may have been affected by the Grand Mage."

The past events of this place were too murky and confusing, and the clues currently available were truly too few. Li Fan could only make such a guess.

"However if the guess is roughly accurate then perhaps the trouble with the Boundless Mirror can be resolved." Li Fan's eyes flickered.

After learning of the risks in this place, Ji Hongdao and Sun Luyuan still made the relatively wise choice to temporarily leave the secret chamber.

However, when it came to what they should do next, the two developed some disagreement.

"The remains of the Grand Mage of Tiandu are no trivial matter. We should report this to the Immortal Alliance as soon as possible." Ji Hongdao said seriously.

Sun Luyuan clearly expressed his opposition: "Brother Ji, you are being foolish! The Grand Mage is said to be the master of the Ten Immortal Sects. If the news reaches the Five Elders Association, they will surely stop at nothing to seize it back. A great war will be unavoidable, and countless lives will be lost! Given the current state of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, do you dare guarantee that this matter will not leak?"

Ji Hongdao had no words to reply.

Sun Luyuan continued: "Given the Dharma-Transmitters' attitude that only the Immortal Ancestor matters in their eyes..."

"Even the bones of a half-immortal would likely inspire no reverence in them. Once inside the Immortal Alliance, the outcome would be either research or placement in the Museum of Divine Treasures for public viewing and commentary."

"Moreover, the Grand Mage is a legendary half-immortal; whether he has truly perished remains uncertain. If we profane it today, we may well face his retaliation in the future." Sun Luyuan said all this with a face full of sincerity.

Ji Hongdao felt somewhat helpless: "Then what do you suggest we do?"

"Leave it untouched." Sun Luyuan declared firmly.

"Brother Ji, think about it: even the bones of a half-immortal offer us little benefit. With the new method before us, achieving longevity is already nearly hopeless, let alone becoming an immortal?"

"Taking it away might bring catastrophic disaster later, with no tangible benefit. Why bother?"

...

In the end, Ji Hongdao was still persuaded.

The fundamental reason was simply that these were only two bones after all.

And moreover, the bones of a half-immortal who had not succeeded in ascending.

Sun Luyuan was right: for Dao Integration cultivators like them, the use was minimal.

Of course, if they were the remains of a True Immortal, the choice would certainly be different.

"Although we will not take it for now, we cannot let others take it either. Brother Ji, your attainment in formation arts is profound and matches creation itself. Why not use formations to completely seal this ruin? Combined with the original protective formations here, it will ensure absolute safety." Sun Luyuan suggested again.

This was to show his own stance and attitude.

Sun Luyuan knew almost nothing about formations; as long as Ji Hongdao set up formations here, he himself could not secretly come alone to take the bones.

Ji Hongdao did not stand on ceremony and immediately began setting up the formations.

Once the ruin was resealed, on the way back, Sun Luyuan spoke in a faint tone: "If either of us manages to achieve longevity immortal one day, we can bring out these bones to study them. Who knows, it might allow us to advance further."

Ji Hongdao smiled bitterly and shook his head, not taking it to heart at all.

If before reaching Dao Integration, in their youth, they had still harbored grand ambitions and ideals of proving the Dao for longevity...

Then now, having reached Dao Integration, they had long since abandoned any thought of achieving longevity immortality.

The four steps of Dao Integration were each as difficult as ascending to the heavens.

The more they understood, the deeper their despair.

The two fell silent one after the other.

After a long while, Sun Luyuan let out a long sigh: "I truly wonder what kind of world-shocking genius could succeed in defying reason."

"In all these years, our Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance has had only one person besides the Immortal Ancestor who succeeded, and that person's whereabouts are unknown."

"If there were one more, our Immortal Alliance's situation would be vastly different."

"Tell me, why does the Five Elders Association have five Longevity Immortals? Could it be that they are truly more talented than the people of our Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and achieved what we could not?"

This was also a doubt that countless cultivators of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance could not let go of.

Hearing Sun Luyuan's lament, Ji Hongdao seemed to recall something and hesitated to speak.

"Between us brothers, is there anything we cannot say?" Sun Luyuan teased.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1212: Diverting Calamity To Invite Immortal Disaster

“Actually...”

Ji Hongdao hesitated for a moment before transmitting his voice: “I only heard this by chance and do not know if it is true or false. Brother Sun, just take it as entertainment. However, it is best not to spread this matter.”

After making sufficient disclaimers, he then spoke solemnly: “Actually, among the five Heavenly Sovereigns of the Five Elders Association, two were originally from our Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.”

“But for some unknown reason...” Ji Hongdao shrugged his shoulders.

Sun Luyuan was hearing this rumor for the first time and was instantly shocked beyond words.

He wanted to ask more, but seeing the other make a gesture, he wisely stopped.

Thus he did not bring it up again.

After all, this matter was truly too unbelievable.

Lost in wild thoughts, the two had already quietly returned to the Sun family.

“This trip was in vain. The problem of your younger brother being possessed by that mirror has not been resolved. What do you plan to do?” Ji Hongdao asked.

“We will take it one step at a time.” Sun Luyuan said somewhat helplessly.

“However, Brother Ji, you also said that the formations in that ruin are quite old. This means my previous guess was likely wrong. After the calamity erupts in the Boundless Mirror, it may subside for a long time. At least for now, my younger brother is safe in the short term.”

Ji Hongdao nodded upon hearing this: “I still have official duties to attend to, so I will take my leave first. If anything comes up, contact me anytime.”

“Thank you, Brother Ji!” Sun Luyuan said gratefully.

“Eh, no need to be polite.” Ji Hongdao waved his hand.

After exchanging a few more pleasantries, he transformed into a streak of escape light and departed.

The expression on Sun Luyuan’s face gradually calmed.

In truth, the reason he had stopped Ji Hongdao from reporting the discovery of the Grand Mage’s remains, besides the earlier excuses, was his own plan.

“If the news of the Immortal Alliance discovering the Grand Mage’s remains can truly spark a war between both sides...”

Dangerous light flashed in Sun Luyuan’s eyes.

“Perhaps this is an opportunity.”

“In the Boundless Mirror’s deductions, even that Holy Dynasty of Great Qi could fight the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance at its peak to a standstill. If they encountered one that had just finished a great war with heavy losses...”

Sun Luyuan absolutely could not imagine that he had actually thought of the same thing as someone else.

After the initial shock had subsided, this exact scheme had appeared in Li Fan’s mind.

However, after careful consideration, he had still abandoned the idea of using the Grand Mage’s remains as a chess piece.

Drawing this Half-immortal into the game carried far too high a risk.

And to achieve the same effect, Li Fan still held many other substitutes.

For example, the matter of Eastern Extremity being refined into a puppet.

Or fabricating that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance was still hiding a Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign who was about to prove the Dao.

As long as Li Fan wished, he could stir up a great war in the Xuanhuang Realm at any time.

However...

Recalling the rumor Ji Hongdao had mentioned earlier on the Dust-Crossing Boat, Li Fan frowned slightly.

“Two of the Five Elders Association’s five Heavenly Sovereigns were originally members of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance?”

“Yet they stood on the opposing side of the Immortal Alliance...”

“Could it be because of the Eastern Extremity matter, the rabbit dies and the fox grieves?”

“Or was it because they learned of the Immortal Ancestor dissecting and dividing the Xuanhuang Realm?”

For such matters, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance naturally wanted to cover them up by every means possible.

Apart from those who personally experienced it back then, finding any related written records would be extremely difficult.

After all, even after so many reincarnations, Li Fan had known nothing about it.

But fortunately, now that he knew, investigating would be much easier.

While Li Fan was pondering, Sun Luyuan and Sun Luyao’s conversation had already ended.

“Little brother, is this method truly effective?”

“Brother, rest assured, it is more than likely. This idea naturally surfaced in my mind after seeing the Grand Mage of Heavenly Capital. It seems the Boundless Mirror has some connection with the Grand Mage.” Sun Luyao said confidently.

“Alright, I will think of a way. But these You Clan people—I have never heard of them before. They are probably an absolute secret of the Immortal Alliance. It will not be easy to obtain some.” Sun Luyuan frowned tightly.

“Brother, do not worry. I should be able to hold on a little longer.” Sun Luyao smiled and comforted him.

Sun Luyuan let out a soft sigh and directly left the secret chamber.

Indeed, the solution Li Fan had provided to resolve the Boundless Mirror’s obliterating wind drew its inspiration from the earlier guess.

Diverting disaster eastward!

Using an immortal artifact, or something close to one, to draw out the hidden calamity within the Boundless Mirror.

Li Fan guessed that the obliterating calamity had transferred and spread from the Grand Mage of Heavenly Capital to the Boundless Mirror. This likely indicated that it was particularly sensitive toward existences at the immortal level.

Perhaps as soon as it sensed the aura, it would actively launch an attack.

At that time, most of its power could be drawn out.

Li Fan naturally could not use a true half-immortal artifact as bait, but creating some props that could possess an immortal aura was still feasible.

It relied on the refined source power essence provided by the You Clan people.

“With golden source power essence, supplemented by the Boundless Primal Chaos Reveals in Form formation—which is nearly immortal-grade, it should be possible to create a half-immortal artifact.”

“Even if it is only an empty shell on the surface, it will suffice.”

“Even if it fails, it is no matter. Consider it a test.”

Aside from the branch captured by the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance still controlled a large number of You Clan people.

As long as he thought of a way, relying on the Sun family’s status and connections, he should be able to obtain some.

However, what truly worried Li Fan now was the situation inside the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters.

Ever since receiving the message from the Immortal Ancestor ordering sixteen Dao Integration cultivators to leave for the Xuanhuang Realm, the Immortal Alliance headquarters had remained in a closed state.

It was clearly brewing something.

“With the foundation of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, accelerating the growth of sixteen Dao Integration cultivators would be effortless.”

“There is no need to keep it closed for such a long time.”

“The only thing I can think of that deserves such cautious treatment and fear of leakage...”

“Is a method to escape the Immortal Ruins and leave the Xuanhuang Realm!”

A sharp light flashed in Li Fan’s eyes.

The reason the various forces in the Xuanhuang Realm were now obediently lying low was largely due to the existence of the Immortal Ruins.

There was no way to escape.

If, without knowing about the High Wall, they learned of a safe way to leave the Xuanhuang Realm.

A great many would undoubtedly do so.

Without hesitation.

Just as had happened in the Xuanhuang Realm thousands of years ago.

When disaster strikes, each flies their own way!

“Even the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance itself must weigh whether anyone within the alliance would seize the chance to flee.”

“After all, they cannot truly publicize the existence of the High Wall. Otherwise, the world would surely fall into chaos.”

Thinking this, Li Fan once again activated the Boundless Mirror to spy upon the Tianxuan Mirror.

He had originally thought it would be fruitless as before.

Unexpectedly this time, he actually found some faint clues.

“Acquire Voidbright Flow Crystals immediately. Do not make a big fuss. Use covert methods. Once obtained, destroy them.”

“Voidbright Flow Crystals.”

...

Three or four such messages spread from the Immortal Alliance headquarters through the Tianxuan Mirror.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1213: The Immortal Alliance’S High-Pressure State

"Voidbright Flow Crystals?"

In an instant, relevant information surfaced in Li Fan's mind.

The Voidbright Flow Crystals was a silk-like solid crystal.

It could float in the air and drift like flowing water. It also emitted a dreamy, multicolored glow.

It was often mixed into magical treasures by female cultivators of the Xuanhuang Realm during forging, causing the treasures to release brilliant, dazzling radiance whether in their normal state or when activated.

But...

Aside from looking beautiful, it seemed to have no other uses.

Therefore, although its production was not particularly high, its price remained at a modest level of eight thousand contribution points per piece—neither too expensive nor too cheap.

Yet now, such an apparently flashy but useless item had people in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters risking future reckoning to hoard it in advance.

Clearly, this Voidbright Flow Crystals was connected to the Immortal Ancestor signs involving the sixteen Dao Integration cultivators departing the Xuanhuang Realm.

Inside the Sun family secret chamber, Li Fan used the Tianxuan Mirror's origin power and the Boundless Mirror's spying capabilities in tandem. He quickly detected several obscure related messages and transaction records.

These people operated very secretly and cautiously.

Most of them arranged deals through the Tianxuan Mirror and then conducted face-to-face transactions using standard spirit stones.

Occasionally, when the Tianxuan Mirror showed acquisitions, they would still post the information afterward as if offering it for sale. This ensured that a certain quantity of Voidbright Flow Crystals continued circulating in the Tianxuan Mirror market, preventing any major price fluctuations.

However, through repeated transfers, the vast majority of Voidbright Flow Crystals on the market gradually came under the control of the same group of people. The so-called transactions were merely moving goods from one hand to another.

"A rather clever method. It is far more pleasing to the eye than my own crude market manipulation."

"It seems they have done this kind of thing more than once."

Li Fan clicked his tongue in praise and, using the power of the Tianxuan Mirror's origin, joined in as well.

He possessed the ability to erase traces and information from the Tianxuan Mirror at any time. Naturally, he would not act as timidly as these shadowy rats.

He swept the market without restraint, buying frantically.

He cared nothing for price and purchased at rates no one could refuse.

Like a giant whale, he stirred up massive waves in the Tianxuan Mirror trading market.

Soon, the price of Voidbright Flow Crystals rose steadily and shot upward in a straight line.

This anomaly naturally drew the attention of more cultivators.

Although they still did not know the true use of the Voidbright Flow Crystals at this point, it did not stop them from following the trend and buying.

Li Fan's clone, using a fabricated Tianxuan Mirror identity, soon received several messages from strangers.

"Which family are you from? Acting so recklessly—do you want to die?"

"Brother, restrain yourself. Don't make it so that none of us can eat."

"Hmph, this old man advises you: greed brings no benefit. Spit out what you have swallowed so that we can all survive."

...

Li Fan replied to all these noisy messages with a single sentence: "Very good. I have lured you all out. Wait to be purged!"

The other cultivators fell completely silent in an instant.

Upon checking, quite a few had already died mysteriously within that short time.

"They are even so ruthless toward their own people."

"But they are still a bunch of fools."

Li Fan did not take these people seriously. While toying with them, he did not stop his acquisition of Voidbright Flow Crystals.

Unfortunately, this time the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's response was unusually fast.

"Senior, withdraw quickly!"

Sun Luyao sent a warning.

Li Fan had already begun his tail-cutting operation.

One Voidbright Flow Crystals after another was taken out from the mini Tianxuan Mirror, after which he directly erased the fabricated identity information.

Li Fan's clone then slashed out a black light of annihilation and destroyed the mini Tianxuan Mirror outright.

Only then did the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's announcement belatedly appear in the Tianxuan Mirror.

It directly classified the Voidbright Flow Crystals as a prohibited circulating item.

All crystals currently held by cultivators would be recovered by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance urged all parties to cooperate fully with the relevant work. Anyone who deliberately obstructed it would be charged with betraying the alliance.

...

As soon as the news spread, it caused shock in all directions.

This could be considered the harshest measure announced by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance since its founding.

And it was all for a small, previously neglected item like the Voidbright Flow Crystals.

What profound mystery did this thing possess that warranted such a massive mobilization from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance?

Those cultivators who possessed Voidbright Flow Crystals but were unwilling to hand them over simply disassembled and studied them instead.

As before, they still found nothing.

Suspicion and discussion brewed like a storm.

Yet the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance showed no intention of providing any explanation from beginning to end. It even employed even harsher measures to enforce silence.

No information related to the Voidbright Flow Crystals could be found in the Tianxuan Mirror anymore.

Any member within the Immortal Alliance system who dared to discuss the Voidbright Flow Crystal privately was directly removed from their post.

Even gatherings of non-system cultivators discussing it would be targeted by the Martial Halls of various prefectures, leading to summons and warnings.

Such a high-pressure atmosphere made cultivators across the world realize that this time was different: the Voidbright Flow Crystal had truly become a forbidden topic.

Xuanhuang cultivators still valued their lives.

For a time, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance fell deathly silent.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's control had not yet reached every single cultivator.

Thus, in deep mountain caves far from the immortal cities of various prefectures, and in heavily restricted secret chambers, discussions about the Voidbright Flow Crystal could not be avoided.

However, all the Voidbright Flow Crystals in the world had already been confiscated.

They could only speculate based on imagination.

Not to mention mere speculation—even if they had physical samples, it would still be difficult to figure out the true method of using the Voidbright Flow Crystal.

In the Sun family secret chamber, Li Fan even used the Boundless Mirror to conduct a detailed analysis of the Voidbright Flow Crystal, yet he still could not identify its special qualities.

"It seems the Voidbright Flow Crystal is only one relatively important link among the related materials. There should be other steps involved."

After consuming more than a hundred Voidbright Flow Crystals, Li Fan helplessly paused his research for the time being.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1214: Purple Qi Enters The Star Sea

“This divided soul was originally meant to be saved for the most critical moment.”

“Now it seems it must be deployed.”

Having lurked beside Wu Ninglu and gained full knowledge of her every action and habit, Li Fan’s clone took control of her body. He found an excuse to leave the Sun family without arousing anyone’s suspicion.

After shaking off the pursuit from the Chen family of the Medicine Hall, he reached a remote, uninhabited underground spot in the wilderness. Using whatever materials were at hand, he began constructing stone puppets.

He needed to deliver these stone puppets to the border of the Xuanhuang Realm before the sixteen Dao Integration cultivators departed.

This would allow him to observe their escape method up close.

With Li Fan’s current mastery of formations, he had no worry of detection by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

“They say one person cannot be in two places at once. I have already split off several clones, yet it still feels insufficient.”

Li Fan’s divided soul felt somewhat helpless.

A careful count showed the main body hidden within the Derivation Law Jade space of the Guardian Hall.

The Holy Embryo had gone far across the Star Sea to peer into the secrets of the Echoing Star Power.

The Boundless Mirror clone was too weak and lacked the strength.

As for the Holy Emperor clone, it had unfortunately been dragged by Yin Shangren into the Nether Realm.

Thus, only this hidden divided soul could act in person.

The situation in the Dark Sea could be set aside for now.

The day the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance completed their Immortal Ancestor mission arrived sooner than Li Fan had anticipated.

It was the thirty-third day after the Voidbright Flow Crystal had been listed as a prohibited circulating item.

A magnificent rainbow suddenly appeared between the heavens and earth of the Xuanhuang Realm.

It seemed to blend every color in the world, twisting and dreamlike.

Like a meteor flying backward, it pierced the dome of heaven and surged onward.

Before countless watchful eyes, the rainbow tore open the sky of the Xuanhuang Realm.

The countless afterglows it left behind lingered for a long time, spreading across the firmament and refusing to fade for several days.

For the next half month, the sky of the Xuanhuang Realm remained mottled with seven colors, strange and illusory.

Curious cultivators attempted to capture these scattered rainbow lights.

The colors proved to have physical substance and could be collected and refined.

They also possessed the property of making objects emit the same seven-colored glow.

Combined with the earlier strict prohibition by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance against any discussion of the Voidbright Flow Crystal...

The cultivators instantly understood.

This was the Voidbright Flow Crystal, used to construct the vessel for leaving the realm.

The revelation of the answer caused no chaos.

Most cultivators knew nothing of the Immortal Ruins’ existence, nor did they realize the Xuanhuang Realm could not be escaped at all.

They only knew that contact between the Xuanhuang Realm and the outside had been severed for a long time. They guessed the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s action was meant to reestablish communication.

But these people were unimportant.

The important cultivators, upon realizing the truth, began to harbor different thoughts.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance started to stir with undercurrents.

Time returned to the moment the colorful rainbow left the Xuanhuang Realm.

Through the eyes of roughly a hundred stone puppets, Li Fan gained a clearer view of the entire process by which the rainbow departed the Xuanhuang Realm.

The instant it left the Xuanhuang Realm, its flight trajectory changed exactly as expected: the originally straight line suddenly bent.

It veered toward the direction of the Immortal Ruins.

But not entirely.

It was like a mass of dough being slowly stretched.

Only a portion of the colorful rainbow was forcibly pulled off course by the attraction of the Immortal Ruins.

The remainder continued unwaveringly, speeding deep into the Star Sea.

As the colorful rainbow advanced, more and more hues were drawn away, falling toward the Immortal Ruins.

Red, orange, yellow, green, blue, indigo...

The disappearance of these colors caused the rainbow’s hue to gradually become simpler.

In the end, only a single strand of purple qi remained.

It flew alone into the unknown depths of the Star Sea.

From the perspective of the Xuanhuang Realm below, it looked like a beam of light shot toward the Star Sea, dispersed into various different colors.

Merely a somewhat astonishing phenomenon that would not spark much discussion.

Yet in Li Fan’s eyes, the scene was almost no different from a miracle.

“It actually resisted the suction of the Immortal Ruins?!”

“No, rather than resist, it would be more accurate to say it delayed and resolved it!”

“It released a portion of the suction force acting upon itself, offsetting the pull to a certain degree. Then, through layer after layer of shedding, it rapidly distanced itself from the Immortal Ruins, causing the attraction to grow ever weaker.”

“Countless precise calculations were required to accomplish this feat!”

“It was not a victory of pure power, but a triumph of skill.”

Li Fan’s mind replayed the dynamic curves of the color separation again and again.

They seemed to contain infinite profundities.

The Dao Deviation Stone crystal spun rapidly, attempting to deduce and recreate the scene within his sea of consciousness.

Yet after the yellow light separated, he already felt dizzy and unable to continue.

Though regretful, he could only stop.

“Is this the method devised by the Immortal Ancestor?”

“Elegant.”

Li Fan could only offer these two words in evaluation.

“The Voidbright Flow Crystal should only be used for separating the different hues.”

“As for the original driving force that propelled it out of the Xuanhuang Realm, it must still be those sixteen Dao Integration cultivators.”

“While bound to the heavens and earth of the Xuanhuang Realm, cultivators at the Longevity Immortal realm cannot easily leave unless they reach the level of the Immortal Ancestor. Dao Integration is already the highest combat force the Xuanhuang Realm can deploy.𝙡.𝒄𝓸𝒎

“Although they were forcibly cultivated by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, I can sense that the auras of those sixteen Dao Integration cultivators are not weak.”

Li Fan’s thoughts surged.

The scene of the colorful rainbow leaving the Xuanhuang Realm had given him a profound shock.

Only when someone easily accomplishes a goal you have pursued for a long time without success do people truly feel the existence of the gap.

“The Immortal Ancestor Heavenly Sovereign is indeed beyond ordinary reach.”

“But it does not matter. Once I master this method, and return to [Truth]...”

“Once again I will be ahead of him.”

...

Holy Dynasty of Great Qi.

Sun Erlang looked at Yi Shu with some puzzlement.

This senior had recently been speaking normally one moment, then suddenly falling into a daze the next.

If he had not personally witnessed the terrifying power of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother and known this must be the Stone Mother drawing on computational power, Sun Erlang might have suspected the man had fallen into some senile condition.

Yet what exactly was the Stone Mother doing that it affected even these divided bodies of the Taiyan Sect?

Having spent some time with Yi Shu and gained an understanding of the Taiyan Sect collective’s temperament, Sun Erlang did not guess blindly. He asked directly.

Only after a long while did Yi Shu regain his senses.

“It is nothing. I merely thought of a method to escape in certain extreme situations without abandoning the Stone Mother’s main body.”

“Before achieving complete information immortality, losing the Dao Deviation Stone Mother would still have a very great impact on us.” Yi Shu explained to Sun Erlang.

“Extreme situations?” Having personally witnessed the Stone Mother’s might and having reincarnated within the simulated worlds, Sun Erlang found it hard to imagine what kind of disaster could force the Stone Mother to flee in such a sorry state.

Chapter 1215: Scouring The Dark Sea

“If that day truly comes, Senior, please don’t forget to bring me along.” A moment later, Sun Erlang laughed and teased.

“No problem.” Yi Shu glanced at Sun Erlang and nodded in agreement.

Sun Erlang was immediately stunned.

“Your master has not been in the Great Qi Small World recently? I can no longer sense his presence.” Yi Shu bypassed the topic and asked instead.

Sun Erlang frowned upon hearing this: “Yes. I just don’t know where he has gone. From what I remember, this is the first time he has left the Holy Dynasty.”

Yi Shu looked toward the Holy Emperor’s Throne, which seemed to grow even taller, and countless lights and shadows flashed in his eyes for an instant.

Beneath the Holy Emperor’s Throne, inside the secret chamber where the You Clan people were kept.

Over a thousand You Clan people formed circles, dancing and performing a mysterious ritual.

At the center of their encirclement stood a pitch-black door that seemed capable of devouring all light.

Passing through this mysterious dark door led to the other side of the Xuanhuang Realm, the Dark Sea.

Holy Emperor Li Ping was currently inside it.

With him was Yin Shangren.

In the Dark Sea, vision, sound, and even divine sense lost their ability to communicate.

Yet Holy Emperor Li Ping could still feel Yin Shangren’s whispers in his ear.

It was as though some profound force directly adhered to the surface of his skin, allowing intimate exchange.

Being in the Dark Sea severed his connection to the Holy Dynasty’s fortune.

At this moment, Li Ping’s strength could be described as extremely frail. Any random Soul Transformation cultivator from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance could probably kill him easily.

However, as the destined Holy Emperor of the Holy Dynasty in this alien dark realm, Li Ping felt no fear whatsoever in his heart.

Because within his body flowed a power that ordinary cultivators could not detect.

An almost transcendent golden, vast force.

The You Clan cultivation plan was progressing very smoothly.

It even skipped the physical growth process entirely; as soon as they were born, they were mature individuals with all bodily functions at their peak.

Although the drawback was a further reduction in lifespan, expected to be only three to five years.

But as long as the flesh factories continued producing without end, the scale of You Clan people kept and loyally praying for the Holy Emperor in the Holy Dynasty could be guaranteed.

After the You Clan population exceeded four digits, the daily generated essence of vow power had already reached a considerable level.

Under Li Ping’s deliberate manipulation and weaving, a giant golden net formed within his body, centered on the heart.

The source power essence surged like an endless river. Now, Holy Emperor Li Ping could display all kinds of inconceivable divine abilities with a mere gesture, even without using his own cultivation.

For example, with a single thought, he could trace the source of the vow power essence and instantly return to the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi no matter where he was in the Xuanhuang Realm. Even if other forces interfered, at most it would slow his return speed; it could not completely prevent the process.

Moreover, when he was attacked, the giant net of source power essence would automatically protect and counterattack.

With the current scale of the golden giant net within his body, Holy Emperor Li Ping was very confident that even a Longevity Immortal from the Xuanhuang Realm could not keep him.

Of course, excluding the Immortal Ancestor.

This was also the source of his confidence in personally venturing into danger and coming to the Dark Sea this time.

Through the power of Yin Shangren’s world specter, the two were communicating back and forth.

“Have you not sensed the strangeness here?” Yin Shangren’s voice came intermittently; his main body was nowhere to be found.

“It is pitch black; besides that, there is nothing.” Holy Emperor Li Ping said in a deep voice.

“Ah, right. You are different from me, so it is normal that you cannot hear these sounds. The low murmurs of people and events from every era of the Xuanhuang Realm rise and fall within this Dark Sea...”

Under Yin Shangren’s explanation, Li Ping finally understood what he meant by the strangeness.

The Nether Realm was the collection of forces in the Xuanhuang Realm that deviated from the Immortal Ruins’ power.

These were originally part of the Xuanhuang Realm. Attracted to the Nether Realm for unknown reasons, they also synchronously brought some “information.” It could be a segment of sound or several images.

It might even be a complete divine thought.

Over time, suppressed by the power here, they all sank into the Dark Sea.

“So the Dark Sea is actually quite lively.” Li Ping said thoughtfully.

“It cannot be said that way. The characteristics of this place inevitably cause all information to fall silent. I can only access these fragments of time through absorption. Weren’t you asking about the reason for the You Clan race’s special constitution? I think I know the answer now.”

With these words, Li Ping felt a surge in the darkness behind him.

It was Yin Shangren who had suddenly appeared.

Incongruous with the darkness here, the small vortex-like state around him allowed Li Ping to clearly “see” him.

“If we regard the Immortal Ruins’ power as attraction, then the force maintaining the existence of the Nether Realm can be seen as repulsion. This repulsive force does not exist constantly like the Immortal Ruins’ attraction. Instead, it emerges and scours regularly like ocean tides. The accumulated scouring on the timescale has formed such a peculiar existence in the Xuanhuang Realm.”

Yin Shangren’s tone was filled with amazement.

Previously, although he had vaguely known of this region’s existence, he had never studied it systematically.

This in-depth investigation had deeply impressed him with the marvels of the Dark Sea.

“The attraction comes from forces outside the Xuanhuang Realm. The repulsion originates from the Xuanhuang Realm itself. Yet it is not from the Xuanhuang heavens and earth themselves...”

Yin Shangren fell silent for a moment, seemingly calculating something.

“If my guess is correct, this periodic spontaneous repulsive force should come from the ancient Xuantian Sect’s [Floating Across the Starry Sky Grand Formation]!”

Speaking of this formation, Yin Shangren’s tone suddenly became somewhat subtle.

It seemed some unpleasant memories had flashed through his mind.

But he quickly suppressed this emotion stemming from his own nature and continued the analysis.

“So that’s how it is. Floating across, floating across—if it cannot resist the Immortal Ruins’ attraction that gave birth to the Darkest Star Sea, how could it float in the boundless starry sea?”

“By comparison, the golden chains’ method of capturing other worlds is more like salvaging resources to strengthen itself. It is merely incidental.”

“It is indeed the Xuanhuang Realm’s greatest formation, unprecedented and unmatched in history. It is a pity that the grand formation has now been damaged, and if I am not mistaken, each activation of the Dark Sea’s repulsive force also consumes the Xuanhuang heavens and earth as its price. In the past, the Xuanhuang Realm could capture other worlds to offset this consumption.”

“But times have changed; let alone supplying the dark repulsion, the Xuanhuang Realm itself can barely be preserved.”

Yin Shangren actually revealed a rare tone of schadenfreude.

Chapter 1216: The Truth Of The Floating Across Formation

"Hmm." Sensing that Holy Emperor Li Ping's gaze seemed to lock onto him, Yin Shangren gave a light cough.

"Very well, back to the main topic. Creating dark repulsion is an instinctive behavior of the formation. Even if the formation is damaged, it will continue to operate according to its original logic. This Floating Across the Starry Sky Grand Formation is truly remarkable. It must have begun autonomously seeking substitutes after losing its Xuanhuang energy supply."

"The You Clan were born because of this."

"I believe you have already seen it. The power generated through the You Clan's prayers can partially replace the supply from the Xuanhuang Realm's heavens and earth..."

"I wonder how much you understand about formation arts. With what I have said, do you comprehend?"

As Yin Shangren spoke, he noticed that Holy Emperor Li Ping's expression seemed somewhat strange. He thought perhaps his explanation had been too abstruse and unclear.

Thus he asked directly.

"I have some slight understanding. The Floating Across the Starry Sky Grand Formation was built by the Xuantian King. If we follow your explanation, then this Dark Sea here is the same as that formation module which captures worlds in the Star Sea. Both are important functional parts of the Floating Across the Starry Sky Grand Formation." Li Ping said thoughtfully.

"Correct." Yin Shangren nodded.

"Moreover, according to my judgment, besides these two parts and the central control of the formation, the Floating Across the Starry Sky Grand Formation should have other functions as well..."

"The principles of formation arts involve endless circulation. The golden chains and the Dark Sea alone cannot form a complete formation cycle. There should indeed be two additional subsidiary formation modules to allow this grand formation to operate at peak efficiency."

Before Yin Shangren could speak, he realized that Holy Emperor Li Ping had already voiced his own judgment.

"It seems your attainment in formation arts far surpasses mine." Yin Shangren said in a faint tone.

Li Ping did not respond but continued pondering the missing parts of the Floating Across the Starry Sky Grand Formation.

At the same time, he did not stop communicating with Yin Shangren.

"If we view the Xuanhuang Realm as a flying boat struggling near a Star Sea vortex, then in a sense, this place serves as the boat's power source?"

After thinking for a moment, Yin Shangren expressed agreement.

"Then if... I mean hypothetically, we gave the Dark Sea sufficient energy supply, would it be possible to gradually struggle free from the vortex once more?" Holy Emperor Li Ping asked slowly, his tone enigmatic.

"...In theory, it is possible. However, we do not know the Dark Sea's energy conversion efficiency. It is more likely that even if we burned the entire current Xuanhuang Realm to exhaustion, we still could not move the Xuanhuang Realm even an inch. Moreover, I have a feeling that due to the recent decline in the You population, the Dark Sea's energy supply has once again encountered problems. It may have begun to stimulate new evolutionary energy-absorbing methods." Yin Shangren shook his head.

"A vague sense of crisis keeps surfacing in my heart. We cannot afford to be careless." Yin Shangren's tone grew solemn.

"However, have you not recently produced a large number of You people? If we can restore the Dark Sea's energy supply, it might prevent this evolution from occurring." Elder Yin suddenly said this.

Holy Emperor Li Ping of Great Qi remained silent.

...

Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters.

Li Fan, who had just failed in his attempt to crack the colorful rainbow light not long ago, now had no time to rest or adjust. He immediately began deducing the Xuanhuang Realm's [Floating Across the Starry Sky Grand Formation].

Li Fan recalled the scene from his 119th life when Mo Rubin had mysteriously vanished inside the Dark Sea, leaving behind only an empty shell.

"So the Dark Sea is actually part of the Floating Across the Starry Sky Grand Formation."

"Mo Rubin had successfully learned information about the Xuantian King through the Tianxuan Mirror and ultimately headed to the Dark Sea. It is uncertain whether he truly perished, but his entire cultivation was very likely directly devoured by the Dark Sea and converted into energy powering the Xuanhuang Realm."

"Whether he acted out of great love and willingly sacrificed himself for the Xuanhuang Realm, or faked his death to escape like a cicada shedding its shell, it is almost certain that the Xuantian King or at the very least matters closely related to the Xuantian King—is hidden within the Dark Sea."

"Previously I thought the Dark Sea was purely a manifestation of foreign power, so I could do nothing about it. But now that I know it is a manifestation of formation power..."

"I may not be unable to crack it!"

All of Holy Emperor Li Ping's various sensations inside the Dark Sea, along with the scenes he witnessed there in his 119th life, emerged in Li Fan's mind.

The Final Dissociation Disc, empowered by the Dao Deviation Stone, hummed faintly with unprecedented intensity.

It had actually been pushed to its absolute limit.

"No wonder when I first asked the Tianxuan Mirror for the Floating Across Starry Sky Grand Formation, it hesitated and even offered an immortal-grade formation diagram to temporarily placate me. Now it seems this Floating Across Grand Formation is fundamentally the ultimate transformation formation for the Xuanhuang Realm. It changed the Xuanhuang Realm from a pure immortal cultivation world into an eternal immortal boat capable of crossing the Immortal Ruins."

"If the formation had not been damaged, and if there were not still a High Wall outside the Immortal Ruins..."

"Perhaps the Xuanhuang Realm would have long since broken free of its shackles and begun roaming the Star Sea."

"Perhaps it was precisely when I demanded the Floating Across the Starry Sky Grand Formation diagram from the Tianxuan Mirror that I revealed a flaw. After all, given the importance of this formation, the Twelve Dharma Kings of the Xuantian Sect had no reason not to know about it. Yet my attitude at the time showed no corresponding importance. I treated it merely as an ordinary formation..."

Countless thoughts flashed through Li Fan's mind simultaneously.

Time passed slowly as the Dissociation Disc and the Dao Deviation Stone continued to flicker with light.

Feeling the long-familiar, increasingly intense stabbing pain between his brows, Li Fan frowned slightly: "It seems the complexity of this Floating Across Grand Formation exceeds my imagination. With too little known information, even with three immortal-grade formations as a foundation, forcing a deduction is far too difficult. Even attempting to deduce the vulnerabilities of the Dark Sea alone is somewhat challenging."

"Moreover, this formation appears to possess the ability to prevent others from deducing and cracking it?"

Li Fan felt a wave of shock inside.

Each time the Final Dissociation Disc reached a critical point in its deduction, the majority of the formation data would suddenly collapse and vanish into thin air.

It was as if some power, spanning time and space, was actively interfering.

Li Fan even suspected that if his main body had not been hiding inside the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance's Derivation Law Jade space, he might have been directly locked onto by this power rather than merely interfered with.

"Fortunately, each attempt at deducing and restoring the formation still leaves behind some retained data. It may be slow, but it works."

"Of course, it would be best to maintain the current state of the Dark Sea without any changes."

...

As Li Fan's main body thoughts surged, Holy Emperor Li Ping's decision suddenly shifted as well.

"I intends to contend with the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance for the Xuanhuang Realm and will natural

Chapter 1217: Another Layer In The Floating Across

"The Xuanhuang Realm is the only place we have to reside. Naturally we must protect it completely." Holy Emperor Li Ping spoke as a matter of course.

"However..."

Li Ping gazed at the deep, pitch-black ocean before him.

"Although the essence of source power is vast, its quantity is ultimately limited. I wonder how many You Clan members praying day and night would be needed to restore the Dark Sea."

Yin Shangren was somewhat moved by Li Ping's attitude. After pondering for a moment, he replied: "According to the You Clan leader, at the peak of their clan, their members' footprints covered the entire underground cave world. It is estimated there were at least five hundred thousand..."

Li Ping said slowly: "It seems our approach toward the You Clan people needs to be improved bit by bit."

Yin Shangren nodded in agreement as well.

"Let us end this inspection here for now. I have a feeling that the two of us seem to be gradually being watched by this darkness."

"Very well."

A burst of golden light enveloped Li Ping's body, making him appear incomparably sacred and majestic.

In the fluctuating light and shadow, it seemed as though space and time were twisting.

The next moment, Holy Emperor Li Ping's figure vanished into thin air and returned to the Great Qi small world.

Without using the teleportation gate produced by the You Clan people's sacrifices and prayers, but directly utilizing the power of the giant net of source power essence.

"At present, the source of the net of source power is only in Great Qi. If one day You Clan everywhere in the Xuanhuang Realm pray to me, then I can rely on this connection to appear anywhere at will." Holy Emperor Li Ping said thoughtfully.

After understanding the significance of the You Clan, Li Ping summoned Wang Xuanba.

He conveyed to him the latest strategy for dealing with the You Clan.

In addition to continuing to increase the production of You Clan in the flesh factories, they must also simultaneously expand the normal You Clan population for sustainable development.

"For these other You Clan people, you must instead find ways to increase their lifespan."

Wang Xuanba, kneeling on one knee, listened to the Holy Emperor's instructions. Although he did not know the reason for this sudden huge shift in attitude, he loyally accepted the task.

After Wang Xuanba left, Li Ping next summoned Sun Erlang.

He assigned him the task of leading troops to conquer several underground worlds.

Sun Erlang, who had been idle for a long time, gladly accepted the order.

However, unlike with Wang Xuanba, he did not simply send him away but continued to inquire.

"How has the Taiyan Sect's Yi Shu been performing lately?"

The fortune of the Holy Dynasty was entirely tied to Li Ping alone. As the destined Holy Emperor of Great Qi, he naturally knew everything that happened within the Great Qi small world like the back of his hand. Yet he still asked knowingly.

Sun Erlang also dared not hide anything and reported everything truthfully.

"Unexpectedly, the Taiyan Sect actually agreed to your request." Li Ping's icy voice carried no emotion as it echoed within the Holy Emperor's throne hall.

"But that is fine too. If one day the Holy Dynasty falls, going to the Taiyan Sect for refuge would also be a decent outcome."

Sun Erlang was instantly stunned. His expression changed at once: "Master, how could the Holy Dynasty..."

Li Ping actually smiled, which was extremely rare: "In this world, where is there any absolute certainty of victory? Having more escape routes, what harm is there? However, this is limited to you alone. Do not mention this matter to your junior brothers and sisters."

"You may go now."

Sun Erlang left in a state of panic and unease.

Li Ping sat upright above. The empty Holy Emperor's throne hall contained only him.

Although this exploration of the Nether Realm had lasted only a short while.

It had nonetheless caused no small impact on his mind.

"Floating Across Starry Sky Grand Formation, Fishing Pond..."

"My strength can already be considered half a step at the peak of the Xuanhuang Realm. Yet within the Xuanhuang Realm, there are far too many hidden oddities. The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance has ruled half of the Xuanhuang Realm for so many years; their foundation must be even deeper than what is outwardly revealed."

"Moreover, the war against the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance must not only be won but won quickly. Otherwise, years of prolonged cultivator warfare will inevitably inflict irreparable damage upon this heaven and earth once more. This is already a patched-up broken ship; how can it withstand further torment?"

As the destined Holy Emperor, there were still many things he needed to consider.

After pondering for a long time, Li Ping's figure flickered and vanished from the Great Qi small world.

When he reappeared, he was already within Nine Mountains Prefecture of the Xuanhuang Realm.

With the source power essence protecting his body, he now qualified to venture out occasionally without excessive worry for his personal safety.

"Heavenly Beast."

Hovering in mid-air, Li Ping sensed the chaotic and disordered space around him.

"If my guess is correct, the Heavenly Beast should also be part of the Floating Across Starry Sky Grand Formation."

"In the Star Sea, dangers lurk everywhere. To successfully cross, thorough scouting of the path ahead and avoidance of dangers are indispensable."

"The Eye of the Heavenly Beast..."

Vague images flashed through his mind.

Outside the Xuanhuang Realm, in an independent space called the Eye of the Heavenly Beast, it seemed possible to observe even deeper into the distant Star Sea compared to within the Xuanhuang Realm.

"It is a pity that the Heavenly Beast is already dead. Finding that unique space can only rely entirely on chance. The Star Sea is vast and boundless, with the immortal ruins' suction constantly affecting it, making it even harder than finding a needle in the ocean."

"Three of the four parts of the Floating Across Grand Formation have already shown traces. The only remaining one still has no clues whatsoever."

"..."

Shaking his head slightly, he temporarily set aside these thoughts. In utter silence, Li Ping had already arrived at the headquarters of the Lingyuan Sect.

The Lingyuan Sect was the largest native power in this chaotic region of Nine Mountains Prefecture.

Its followers all cultivated the Vital Essence Technique of All Living Beings, seemingly breaking the rule that technique could not be jointly practiced. In reality, they were merely tools for the Lingyuan Sect's leader to refine and absorb source power.

Easily bypassing all kinds of restrictions and following the sensing of the source power aura, Li Ping arrived at the underground base of Qiao Gong.

The place was filled with all sorts of strange and bizarre components, and the one busily working among them was none other than Master Qiao Gong himself.

"Where exactly is the difference?"

"Source power and source power essence—two words apart, yet a chasm of difference!"

The puppet shaped from golden source power exploded into fragments with a bang, light scattering everywhere.

It seemed the experiment had failed, and Qiao Gong was somewhat irritated.

"Come here!"

He turned his head and snorted coldly at a female cultivator standing nearby. A leather whip suddenly appeared in his hand.

The female cultivator's face was expressionless, her eyes lacking the liveliness of a normal person.

"Not like it at all! It doesn't resemble the real Shangguan in the slightest!"

Just as the whip in his hand was about to swing down, he suddenly caught sight of the Faceless Holy Emperor's figure in the secret chamber.

As though his guilty deed had been discovered, Qiao Gong's hand trembled, and the whip nearly fell to the ground.

"Who is it?!"

Qiao Gong was both shocked and angry. Looking at Li Ping, he shouted furiously.

In the secret chamber, the puppets that had originally been like dead objects instantly all came to life, staring at Li Ping.
