Chapter 1218: The Holy Emperor Receives The Mandate Of Heaven

Surrounded by heavy encirclement, Li Ping showed not the slightest trace of panic.

These puppets were all refined through the Vital Essence Technique of All Living Beings and driven by extracted source power.

And the Enlightenment Spirit Origin Technique currently cultivated by the people of the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi was precisely the sublimated version of the Vital Essence Technique of All Living Beings.

With just one glance, he saw through the way source power influenced the puppets. The golden web of source power within Li Ping's body emitted waves of invisible fluctuations.

The purity of the energy had already approached the limit of source power essence, forming a natural suppression against the puppets controlled by the Qiao Gong.

In the span of a few breaths, the dozens of puppets in the secret chamber that had been glaring fiercely now became unsteady and could no longer stand properly.

This included the female puppet of the Shangguan.

Qiao Gong's expression instantly turned extremely ugly. Having spent most of his life dealing with source power, how could he fail to recognize the method this mysterious person before him used to influence the puppets?

He had already abandoned any thought of fighting to the death with the other party and began plotting how to escape, or simply abandon this body and resurrect through a memory backup.

Li Ping observed the ever-changing expression on Qiao Gong's face and naturally saw through his little schemes.

Unhurriedly, he pointed out one by one the hidden locations of the Qiao Gong's several memory backups.

Qiao Gong's mind shook violently, and the horror he could no longer conceal appeared on his face.

"Who exactly are you!" His voice even cracked slightly from fear.

He could neither win in a fight nor escape.

Since fleeing to the Xuanhuang Realm, Qiao Gong had never felt such utter despair for even a moment.

"Fellow Daoist Qiao Gong, do not misunderstand. I have come here precisely to present you with a great fortune." Li Ping said slowly.

However, his slightly gloomy tone, combined with his face that could not be clearly seen, carried no persuasiveness at all.

Qiao Gong naturally did not believe it; in fact, the panic in his heart grew even stronger.

Because he had never mentioned the name "Qiao Gong" to anyone else in the Xuanhuang Realm.

The world only knew him as the mysterious and unfathomable Master of the Lingyuan Sect.

Yet now, a faceless mysterious person had appeared who could see through his origins with a single sentence and seemed to know everything about him!

Qiao Gong even began to suspect whether there had been other survivors from the Heavenly Mechanism Realm besides himself back then.

For a moment, Qiao Gong's mind was in utter chaos. However, his disordered thoughts all vanished the instant a cluster of bright golden light rose from Li Ping's hand.

"Source power... essence?!"

"How is this possible!"

Qiao Gong cried out again, his face full of disbelief.

Just as he wanted to look closer, the golden light sphere was already put away by Li Ping.

"Fellow Daoist, were you not seeking source power essence without success? I can teach you." Li Ping's words once again stunned the Qiao Gong.

"Fellow Daoist, were you not trying to recreate the immortal-grade puppet [Ancestor]? I can help you with that as well." Li Ping continued in his calm and unhurried manner.

At this moment, Qiao Gong had already become somewhat numb from shock. He was not even surprised that Li Ping knew about the existence of the Ancestor.

While feeling an overwhelming sense of absurdity in his heart, a faint trace of hope quietly rose.

What if what the other party said was true?

"What are your conditions?" Qiao Gong asked directly.

Li Ping chuckled lightly: "Hearing is false, seeing is believing. Fellow Daoist, please follow me first."

Qiao Gong followed behind Li Ping. From Jiushan Prefecture, they traveled eastward all the way to the depths of the Congyun Sea.

Near a submarine volcano.

"Hmm?"

Qiao Gong gazed at the terrain. He vaguely sensed that within the earth-vein fire below, a living being seemed to be slumbering.

And Li Ping’s next words confirmed his guess.

“You mean, a puppet with complete self-awareness?” Qiao Gong asked, somewhat skeptical.

“It is not a directly crafted puppet, but one transformed into a puppet through mysterious and wondrous methods. It has been absorbing the power of the earth veins for several thousand years and has still not completed its full transformation. If we rashly awaken it, I fear the puppet body will suffer from innate deficiencies. I brought fellow Daoist here precisely to see if you have any solutions.” Li Ping said calmly.

“Transforming the embodiment into an object?” Qiao Gong repeatedly pondered the meaning of these four words.

Then a golden light shot from his eyes, sweeping across the continuous earth veins below.

After a long while, the golden light faded. Qiao Gong nodded: “The puppet below has been continuously tempered by the power of earth fire for a thousand years. Its strength has indeed swelled to a level that was previously unattainable. However, the intensity of the earth fire is inherently limited. Even if it absorbs for another thousand years, its strength is destined to stop at the peak of Soul Transformation. Advancing further is absolutely impossible.”

“Moreover, this earth fire formation was set up by him before he fell into slumber. After such a long time, the two have already begun to show signs of merging into one. If we rashly modify it, we might awaken him as well.”

“Unless…”

Qiao Gong pondered for a moment before speaking thoughtfully: “Unless the Earth Fire itself that tempers this puppet undergoes a change.”

“As you know, the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm is treacherous and often brings about drastic changes in heaven and earth. And earth fire is a manifestation of the power of the Heavenly Dao’s laws…”

“However, this opportunity is something that can only be encountered, not sought.” Qiao Gong stated honestly.

Li Ping spoke slowly: “I understand.”

“As expected of a successor from the Heavenly Mechanism Realm. My original plan was to use heavenly materials and earthly treasures to modify and upgrade the Earth Fire Return to Origin Formation. But now that I think about it, fellow Daoist’s suggestion is better. Borrowing the power of heaven and earth to recreate the universe…”

Qiao Gong was slightly stunned, then emphasized again: “I said, this opportunity can only be encountered, not sought.”

“Is that so?” Li Ping gave a light smile.

“Fellow Daoist, watch my methods.”

Qiao Gong thought the other party was accumulating power to release some divine ability and immediately focused his attention to observe.

However, after waiting a full half day, there was still no movement at all.

If not for the faint yet persistent aura of source power essence still locking onto him, Qiao Gong would have considered slipping away at this chance.

But the situation was stronger than the man. Since the other party remained silent, he could only continue waiting along with him.

This wait lasted for nearly half a month.

Only because Qiao Gong was a master artificer with extraordinary mental fortitude could he barely endure it.

In a place invisible to Qiao Gong, inside the small world of the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi.

The large numbers of You Clan people who had been captured and those produced from the flesh factories began to pray once more toward the Dark Sea

Perhaps because they were originally created to provide power to the Sea of Dark.

They hardly needed any teaching; the efficiency of their prayers was several times higher than when praying to the Faceless Holy Emperor.

Subtle and gradual changes began to occur in the Xuanhuang Realm.

And the Faceless Holy Emperor of Great Qi, standing at the bottom of the Congyun Sea, silently sensed these accumulating shifts in the world.

Some things did not happen overnight but accumulated slowly.

Half a month later, when Li Ping felt that this accumulation had finally exceeded a certain critical threshold…

“Twist and reverse the principles of heaven and earth!”

The Faceless Holy Emperor murmured softly. His majestic figure pointed gently toward the continuous earth-vein fire below.

It seemed like nothing more than a playful gesture—but a moment later, Qiao Gong, who had been watching from the sidelines, was suddenly struck with shock and alarm.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1219: Ink Flame Merges With Tianyang

The earth-vein fire, which had already reached its theoretical limit, unexpectedly began to burn vigorously once more.

The fierce flames roared and surged outward.

Cracks appeared across the seabed, and countless bubbles churned without stopping.

What shocked Qiao Gong was not this strange phenomenon.

Instead, it was another peculiar living being that quietly took shape before his eyes within the earth fire.

After so many years in the Xuanhuang Realm, he was no longer unfamiliar with such an existence.

It had a name that almost everyone knew: Spirit of Heaven and Earth!

As the manifested embodiment of the Xuanhuang Realm’s heavenly laws, any cultivator who absorbed it could ascend in a single step and advance to Dao Integration.

Spirit of Heaven and Earth were extremely rare cultivation resources, and their appearance followed no predictable pattern.

Yet now, a Spirit of Heaven and Earth had taken form under a single point of the finger from this faceless mysterious person!

“This…”

In Qiao Gong’s perception, filled with incomprehension and extreme shock, an even more mysterious event occurred.

The Spirit of Heaven and Earth born within the earth fire did not behave in any unusual way.

Instead, it quietly catalyzed the earth fire. Once the fire shifted from orange-yellow to pitch black, it actually took the initiative to throw itself into the Earth Fire Return to Origin Formation!

It was clearly sacrificing itself to exchange for the possibility of further advancement for the puppet slumbering within the formation!

The black fire flickered silently. As the Earth Fire Return to Origin Formation continued to operate, it merged into the massive puppet body underground.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Continuous sounds of collapse and explosion came from beneath the ground.

These were signs that impurities within the puppet’s body were being refined away.

“The birth of this Spirit of Heaven and Earth not only did not actively attack cultivators, but instead followed that person’s will and lent a helping hand to this seabed puppet?”

“How is that possible?”

It was common knowledge that the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao and Xuanhuang cultivators held irreconcilable contradictions.

When cultivators saw a Spirit of Heaven and Earth, they could not resist the desire to devour it.

And Spirit of Heaven and Earth showed no mercy toward any cultivator who dared appear before them; they would kill without hesitation.

Yet the scene unfolding before him now completely shattered Qiao Gong’s understanding.

“Even the Spirit of Heaven and Earth obeys him so readily… Could it be that this person before me is actually the incarnation of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao?” Qiao Gong thought with a sense of absurdity.

“Spirit of Heaven and Earth are born to carry out heavenly duties. The black flame before us is called Ink Flame. Its duty is to assist in the birth of a Dao Integration puppet…” Li Ping explained in a calm tone.

The thought that followed in his mind, however, he did not voice directly: “Twisting and reversing the principles of heaven and earth can forge will of Heavenly Dao and create Spirit of Heaven and Earth out of thin air. However, that is the limit. Under normal circumstances, making a Spirit of Heaven and Earth, which stands opposed to cultivators, sacrifice itself to help a cultivator advance…”

“This completely violates the commands of will of the Heavenly Dao and is normally impossible to achieve.”

“Yet…”

“I am the incarnation of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao. Everything I do is for the sake of the Xuanhuang heavens and earth!”

“The heavens and earth will naturally assist me!”

Li Ping sensed the subtle changes in the world’s will that had occurred on him ever since the You Clan people resumed their faith in the Dark Sea under his command.

It was precisely because of the combined effects of multiple factors that the Spirit of Heaven and Earth that appeared this time was an extremely powerful Ink Flame.

The unsettling black flame even made Li Ping feel traces of danger.

Under the refinement of the Ink Flame, the seabed puppet also advanced at an unimaginable speed.

“The birth of this puppet should not be far off.”

“Although its realm is only Dao Integration, if we really fought, I am afraid I would be no match for it. And this is merely the result of that mysterious person pointing a single finger…” Qiao Gong secretly glanced at Li Ping once more.

While feeling both shock and fear in his heart, his anticipation for the two offers of help that the other party had mentioned grew even stronger.

“It seems he was not exaggerating…”

Li Ping and Qiao Gong waited for the puppet’s birth.

As the Ink Flame continued to merge with the puppet, the disturbances in the surrounding sea region grew increasingly violent.

However, Li Ping concealed them with a formation.

Otherwise, such an unusual phenomenon would have long attracted a large number of cultivators to come and watch.

Another half month later.

As if the oil had run out and the lamp had gone dry, the earth-vein fire at the seabed had completely extinguished.

The lava had condensed into black crystals.

Only the seabed still diffused thick mist due to the high temperature.

The aura of the Spirit of Heaven and Earth Ink Flame had thoroughly vanished. In its place, a newborn yet powerful aura emerged from the seabed.

The sense of danger transmitted to his heart made Qiao Gong unconsciously move a little closer to Li Ping’s position.

Suddenly, that aura disappeared from Qiao Gong’s perception.

Qiao Gong’s expression changed dramatically. A layer of silver-white armor emerged and protected him.

A pitch-black figure quietly appeared beside them.

Its target, however, was not Qiao Gong, but Li Ping, the Faceless Holy Emperor of Great Qi!

The newly awakened Tianyang had extremely high vigilance. After detecting two powerful auras nearby, he launched an attack on them without a word.

Facing the incoming crisis and Tianyang’s attack, Li Ping did not dodge.

He did not even call upon the power of source power essence for defense.

With just a faint voice transmission, he awakened the somewhat dazed Tianyang and stopped his reckless behavior.

“Tianyang, do you want to avenge your master and senior brother?”

Tianyang’s body trembled violently. He revealed his form and stared fixedly at Li Ping.

His appearance remained the same as before he fell into slumber, looking like a child of just over ten years old.

Only his eyes occasionally revealed a vicissitude that did not match his age at all.

After calming down slightly, the scenes from his slumber, when Li Ping helped him absorb the Spirit of Heaven and Earth also surfaced in his mind.

Knowing that his current strength was entirely thanks to the other party’s assistance, the hostility in Tianyang’s heart slowly faded.

Yet he still stared at Li Ping without letting go, wanting the answer to the question from earlier.

“The true culprit behind the great war with the Xuanhuang Realm back then, which caused the tragic deaths of countless sect cultivators, is still present…”

“I wish to…”

Li Ping slowly transmitted his explanation.

Tianyang listened quietly to the other party’s plan, his aura rising and falling.

Then he restrained it and flew to Li Ping’s side.

“This place is not suitable to stay for long. Let us return first.” Li Ping said.

He then looked toward Qiao Gong: “Fellow Daoist, will you come along?”

Ever since Tianyang appeared, Qiao Gong’s gaze had never left him.

“Do I dare not to come along…” Qiao Gong secretly grumbled to himself, but nodded in agreement.

Only after the three of them traversed the void and arrived inside the Great Qi small world did Qiao Gong’s line of sight finally shift away from Tianyang.

The Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven took the lead.

A total of nine Cave-Heavens surrounded the Great Qi small world.

They provided traction power for it to drift through the void.

And in this mysterious small world, the concentration of spiritual energy was actually much higher than that of the Xuanhuang Realm itself.

Moreover, it seemed to be veiled by an invisible force. Even Qiao Gong could not use his divine sense to clearly probe the details inside.

…

Just as Qiao Gong fell into shock once again.

A streak of aura transformed into a rainbow and approached, causing Qiao Gong’s heart to suddenly jump.

Even the previously silent Tianyang became alert.

A Longevity Immortal realm cultivator?

How is that possible?

Next

Chapter 1220: The Fishing Finally Yields A Treasure

Daoist Hundred Flowers came to report her work to Li Ping.

Of course, the main reason was to request more source power essence.

After absorbing it several times and experiencing rapid strength increases, Hundred Flowers had already tasted the sweetness and found it hard to let go.

In truth, the golden source power created through the You Clan’s prayer rituals still had some distance from true source power essence.

However, for an ancient method Longevity Immortal cultivator like Hundred Flowers, being able to advance further was already no different from the real thing.

While greeting Li Ping, Hundred Flowers also noticed Qiao Gong and Tianyang beside him.

Although Tianyang appeared as a teenager on the outside, the power contained within his body made Hundred Flowers secretly alarmed.

As for Qiao Gong, he gave her a familiar feeling.

It was the aura of a cultivator from outside the Xuanhuang Realm.

After carefully observing the two for a moment, Hundred Flowers did not say much.

The Holy Dynasty of Great Qi had gathered far too many strange and unusual characters. She had long since grown accustomed to it.

For her, this was actually a good thing.

After receiving the source power essence from Li Ping, Hundred Flowers happily returned.

“Fellow Daoist, who was that?” Qiao Gong asked somewhat reservedly.

“Daoist Hundred Flowers, former sect master of the Heavenly Lotus Sect in the Wansheng Realm.” Li Ping introduced briefly.

This simple sentence shocked Qiao Gong and also stirred his imagination.

She was actually like him, a survivor from another immortal cultivation world. Did that mean there were even more such people in this small world?

Carrying this question, Qiao Gong and Tianyang followed Li Ping’s lead and toured the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi roughly.

Whether it was the Immortal Spirit Pagodas that released spiritual energy day and night without rest, or the patrolling arrows that swept across the skies on alert, both left the two deeply amazed.

During the tour, the occasionally encountered unfathomable individuals further made them realize: within the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi, there were many hidden dragons and crouching tigers, many existences like themselves.

In the end, they arrived inside the Holy Emperor’s throne hall.

Li Ping returned to his rightful position, looked down at the two, and his voice echoed in the empty building.

“Fellow Daoist Qiao Gong, I know your artifact refining skills are superb.”

“I have some ideas here for refining new puppets. You may as well take a look and draw inspiration from them.”

Qiao Gong was quickly drawn in by the plans Li Ping displayed.

“Source power essence, combined with body transforming into the Dao, paired with the artifact refining techniques from the Heavenly Mechanism Realm…”

“To create existences with their own consciousness that can rival immortal-grade puppets.”

The more Qiao Gong looked, the more excited he became.

In his view, this plan was completely feasible. Many of the ideas inside even coincided with his own long-held concepts.

“I will specially open up a space within the Great Qi small world as your research area.”

“Tianyang will also assist you.”

Li Ping continued: “First refine some puppets to practice. Once there are no issues, then help transform Tianyang.”

Upon hearing this, Qiao Gong glanced at Tianyang and suddenly understood.

“With Ink Flame as the foundation and source power essence to assist in sublimation…”

“Once completed, his strength will probably not be below that of a Xuanhuang Longevity Immortal cultivator!”

Thinking that in the future he could truly birth Longevity Immortal cultivators, or even immortal-grade existences in a certain sense, with his own hands, Qiao Gong could not help but feel secretly thrilled.

Shortly after completing the handover with the relevant personnel of Great Qi, Qiao Gong impatiently began his work.

“With everything prepared, next is to wait for the people from the Medicine King Sect to return.” Li Ping thought.

He had long laid a dragnet. Once the Medicine King Cauldron returned to the Xuanhuang Realm, it would be directly drawn into the Great Qi small world.

Although pills did not have much effect on improving the overall strength of Great Qi, Li Ping possessed quite a few vicious pill recipes that could severely weaken the cultivators of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

The Faceless Holy Emperor sat upright on the Holy Emperor’s throne, his mind flashing through all recent affairs of the Holy Dynasty.

There was no need for him to handle everything personally; everything was proceeding in an orderly manner and developing steadily for the better.

Everyone knew that the Holy Emperor was watching at all times, so indeed no one dared to slack off.

After reviewing these minor matters, the Holy Emperor’s thoughts slowly calmed.

His figure flickered, and the next moment he arrived in the dark secret chamber beneath the Holy Emperor’s throne.

This place resembled an endless starry sky. Dozens of treasures floated quietly like stars, emitting brilliant light.

The Holy Dynasty’s fishing competition had never stopped for even a moment.

The treasures fished out from the fishing pond were countless.

Li Ping had not broken the promise he made back then.

All these treasures belonged entirely to the individual citizens of the Holy Dynasty.

Of course, for those who did not need the treasures due to personal temperament or because they could not figure out their specific uses, they could choose to hand them over in exchange for other rewards.

Inside this treasury were treasures from the fishing pond that still had no owners.

Most of them, Li Fan did not care much about.

Only one among them…

Li Ping extended his right hand, and golden source power essence suddenly appeared.

One of the light orbs in the treasury reacted the fastest. Before the other light orbs could make any move, it had already come to Li Ping’s side.

It rotated continuously in a flattering manner.

Inside the light orb, a small kitten could be vaguely seen, looking at Li Ping with eager eyes.

Li Ping condensed the source power essence at his fingertip, and the little kitten instantly understood his intention.

It cheered and approached, licking Li Ping’s finger.

“It is like a beast yet not a beast, like an artifact yet not an artifact. The vast Star Sea is truly full of wonders. Such a creation actually exists.” Li Ping could not help but sigh inwardly.

This little kitten looked harmless.

Yet the moment it approached, the golden source power web inside Li Fan’s body sensed danger and activated protection on its own.

Although it seemed like a living creature, it had no trace of life aura at all.

In fact, when it was first fished out, it was merely a small cat-shaped statue.

No matter how the lucky angler tried various methods to test it, he could not find any use for the cat-shaped statue. In the end, he reluctantly handed it over to the Holy Dynasty in exchange for other rewards.

At the beginning, even Li Ping had not noticed anything unusual about it.

It was not until one day that he suddenly realized some treasures stored in the treasury had inexplicably decreased. Only after using source power essence for a fishing enforcement operation did he catch the culprit.

The cat was merely one of its forms.

After eating its fill, it lazily prepared to sleep, but then sensed the intention transmitted by Li Ping.

Although still somewhat unwilling, considering its future meals, the little kitten let out a weak and powerless low roar.

After a flash of cyan light, the little kitten transformed into the shape of a small square box.

Around the box were engraved some strange scenes.

Celestial stars falling, countless beasts proliferating across the earth.

In the Star Sea, countless giant ships stretched endlessly.

Two colossal human figures confronted each other, separated by a river of light and shadow in the middle.

…

Li Ping held the small square box and aimed it at one of the light orbs in the treasury.

At one end of the box, a faint cyan light was instantly released.

It enveloped the light orb within.

Chapter 1221: Divine Object Beyond The High Wall

Moments later, a beam of light also shot out from the other end of the square small box.

The small box shook violently, as if it had consumed a great deal of energy.

Seeing this, Li Ping infused it with a bit more golden source power.

With this nourishment, the square small box slowly stabilized.

In the end, the light at both ends completely disappeared, and an item identical to the previous light orb appeared inside the Holy Dynasty treasury.

Li Ping drew both light orbs into his hands and carefully compared them.

Inside the two light orbs were two identical swords.

They seemed forged from myriad-year profound ice, and the blades emitted an ice-blue glow.

"The Icebound Myriad Years Sword is crafted from the Exotic Core of Profound Cold, an otherworldly object capable of causing an entire realm to freeze completely."

"If its cold energy is fully released, it can instantly freeze an entire prefecture of the Xuanhuang Realm."

"Even within the current Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, this sword should be considered a treasure of considerable value."

"However..."

Li Ping then looked at the other Icebound Myriad Years Sword in his hand.

There was no discernible difference between them.

"It was actually replicated so easily by this square small box." Li Ping's thoughts surged.

While Li Ping was pondering, the square small box wriggled and transformed back into the form of a small milk cat.

It seemed to have consumed a lot of energy and appeared somewhat listless.

It opened its mouth in a yawn, then refused to leave. Instead, it directly lay down on Li Ping's shoulder and fell into a deep sleep.

It was clearly not pretending. Even the golden source power Li Ping offered held no interest for it.

Li Ping did not drive it away either.

Such a treasure was better kept close at hand.

"From what I see now, when this treasure transforms into the square small box, its function is replication."

"It can achieve perfect duplication of objects. This treasure seems to still be in its growth phase. Some things cannot be replicated yet, and each replication consumes a great deal of energy. It requires a period of rest and recovery."

"Moreover, this treasure is not limited to only the square box and cat forms. It should have other transformations as well..."

Li Ping gently stroked the sleeping kitten, which responded with an unconscious meow.

"Interesting."

"It was worth all the effort I put into the setup. The Fishing Pond has finally given me a pleasant surprise."

"Since it appeared in the world in the form of a cat statue, I shall call it [Cat Treasure]."

When Cat Treasure was fished out, it was merely the most ordinary white light orb.

But clearly, a Cat Treasure with replication ability could not possibly be of only white quality.

"Although it may be because it had not been activated at the beginning. Regardless, even the immortal treasure Fishing Pond was deceived."

"The origin of this [Cat Treasure] is quite extraordinary."

"It may even come from beyond the High Wall..."

Li Ping's figure flashed and returned to the Holy Emperor's throne.

"This [Cat Treasure] will be a major asset in my struggle against the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance."

...

In the Sun family secret chamber.

Li Fan's main body and other clones had long known of Cat Treasure's existence.

However, in this life, leaving Cat Treasure in the Holy Emperor's hands would allow it to exert the greatest value, so there was no need to exchange it using an anchor point just yet.

"Besides, it is still in its juvenile stage. Its exact upper limit still requires further observation."

"Besides man-made magic treasures, can it also replicate the rare treasures of heaven and earth in the Xuanhuang Realm, or even existences like the Final Dissociation Disc?"

Li Fan thought for a moment. At most, it would only speed up his collection progress.

After all, each of his instances of [Truth] was essentially equivalent to replicating the entire world.

With this thought, Li Fan's previously slightly fluctuating state of mind instantly calmed down.

His thoughts returned to the Sun family side.

The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters had lifted its lockdown. Sun Luyuan had been summoned by Mirror Holder Fu for careful questioning.

However, the seven-colored rainbow light capable of escaping the Xuanhuang Realm remained under the strictest level of secrecy.

Even with the dual surveillance of the Tianxuan Mirror's origin and the Boundless Mirror, nothing could be detected.

Li Fan was not anxious.

There are no walls in the world that do not let wind through. No matter how strong the protective measures, they will eventually lose effectiveness with the passage of time.

What he needed to do was wait patiently.

Moreover, he was not the only one in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance interested in the seven-colored rainbow light.

Using the Tianxuan Mirror to constantly monitor the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

As long as they learned of this secret through any method, it meant Li Fan would also know about it.

"Senior, is the method you mentioned to resolve the Obliteration Wind Calamity truly feasible?"

Sun Luyuan, who had always been extremely fearful of Li Fan, suddenly spoke up and asked.

"It is like this." Before Li Fan could reply, Sun Luyuan hurriedly explained.

"Recently, for some unknown reason, strange scenes often flash through my mind. They are all things I have never experienced before. After thinking it over, the most likely source is the remnants left by that obliteration wind calamity..."

Under the indication of Li Fan's clone, Sun Luyuan projected the scenes he had seen.

The images were blurry and changed extremely rapidly.

Li Fan could only make out scenes where countless living beings turned into flying ash in an instant.

And the methods those beings displayed while struggling on the edge of life and death...

They did not seem like something ordinary cultivators could perform.

"Scenes of the great catastrophe in the Immortal Realm."

Li Fan pondered inwardly, yet his face showed no trace of heaviness.

He snorted coldly: "Stop scaring yourself. Do you think what you suffered was the true obliteration wind calamity? It is merely the remnants that have been passed down who knows how many times."

"Rest assured. As long as your elder brother can find information related to the You Clan, I have a ninety percent chance of helping you expel it completely."

Such a confident tone immediately put Sun Luyuan somewhat at ease.

In his view, the various methods displayed by this mysterious senior were probably not much inferior to the legendary True Immortals.

Sun Luyuan did not make them wait long.

Three days later, he quietly returned to the secret chamber.

Unlike the heavy expression he had before, this time his face was filled with irrepressible joy.

"Brother, did you find clues about the You Clan?" Sun Luyuan asked.

Sun Luyuan shook his head: "That is still being inquired about. I believe clues will appear soon."

"Then you..."

Sun Luyuan lowered his voice, yet could not hide his excitement: "This time, the Immortal Ancestor communicated with the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and also took the opportunity to sweep a circle over the Immortal Alliance headquarters. He issued relevant instructions and made certain personnel adjustments."

"That old thing Fu, because his strength has shown almost no progress over the years, seems to have been named and criticized by the Immortal Ancestor himself. He is extremely terrified and will be in closed-door cultivation for a long time. He will not be able to concern himself with us for the time being."

Sun Luyuan was also delighted upon hearing this.

"By the way, when the Immortal Ancestor inspected the Immortal Alliance, did he discover anything about the Tianxuan Mirror's human clone?" Sun Luyuan asked.

"That is still unclear. However, from the current situation, it seems he did not discover it."

"I have also been continuously monitoring the Tianxuan Mirror's human clone side, and there are no abnormalities for now."

Next

Chapter 1222: The Sun Family Welcomes The Fetus

Sun Luyuan remained silent for a moment, then said quietly: “Regarding that Holy Dynasty of Great Qi, I plan to make personal contact.”

“Hmm?” Sun Luyao was taken aback.

He knew his elder brother had been considering contact with the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi, but previously he intended to send loyal dead warriors first to probe the situation.

“Why the sudden change of mind?”

“Times have changed.” Sun Luyuan’s expression turned serious. “Who knows how long that old thing Fu will remain in seclusion? I must use this rare period of freedom to make proper arrangements for our brothers and for the future of the Sun family.”

Sun Luyuan’s eyes turned dark and cold: “I actually had some hesitation before. But that seven-colored rainbow light that left the realm directly made me give up all hope. In the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, we will forever be nothing more than tools to be used. If one day a great calamity strikes the Xuanhuang Realm, those Mirror Holders and Dharma-Transmitters will surely flee without taking us along.”

“The situation with Great Qi is different. As the saying goes, no merit is greater than following the dragon at its rise. If we pledge allegiance before they have made their name, once they succeed, we will become the Dharma-Transmitters of Great Qi.”

“Of course, it ultimately depends on the true strength of this Holy Dynasty of Great Qi.”

Sun Luyao nodded slightly.

“However, this small world of Great Qi is extremely mysterious. Even the Tianxuan Mirror has very few clues about it. Brother, how exactly do you plan to find them?”

Sun Luyuan gave a mysterious smile: “I asked a friend to help with the divination. Although we could not lock down the exact location, we did find the entrance to the small world. We will know once we see it in person.”

“Brother, will there be any danger?” A wave of unease suddenly rose in Sun Luyao’s heart.

Sun Luyuan, however, was full of confidence: “Rest assured. With my strength, even if Great Qi is a dragon’s lair or tiger’s den, I can still retreat unscathed.”

After comforting his younger brother for a few words, Sun Luyuan continued: “Oh right, there is also good news. Ninglu has broken through. She is now at the late stage of Soul Transformation, only one step away from Dao Integration.”

“When she broke through, her vital energy was in chaos. That is how I detected another strand of life force hidden within her body.”

“It turns out she was already carrying Lu’er’s child, but she had some private thoughts and kept sealing its vitality with a secret technique.” Sun Luyuan said with a half-smile.

Seeing his younger brother’s somewhat strange gaze, Sun Luyuan spoke seriously: “I have already verified it. It is undoubtedly our Sun family bloodline. No matter what identity Ninglu has, as long as she can give birth to offspring for our Sun family, nothing else matters.”

“I plan to have Ninglu continue sealing the vitality of the fetus in her womb for now. We will let her give birth only after she reaches Dao Integration.” Sun Luyuan suddenly added.

“Lu’er’s talent was already extraordinary, and Ninglu’s aptitude is also acceptable. Their offspring will definitely be the most promising among the younger generation of the Sun family.”

“During the process of a cultivator devouring the Spirit of Heaven and Earth, the laws of heaven and earth descend upon the body. If the fetus in the mother’s womb can sense this simultaneously…”

There was a trace of excitement in Sun Luyuan’s tone: “I am afraid that once this little one is born, his talent will not be inferior to any heaven’s prodigy of the Immortal Alliance. Our Sun family will finally have a true successor.”

“Most importantly, we now have the strength to plan for him to obtain a Spirit of Heaven and Earth.”

Whenever he spoke about the future of the Sun family, Sun Luyuan seemed to have endless words.

His younger brother Sun Luyao also rarely quieted down and listened with a smile.

“What a touching scene of deep brotherly affection.” Only after Sun Luyuan left did the mysterious shadowy senior’s strange, cackling laughter startle him awake.

Only then did Sun Luyao realize that he had already done many things betraying his elder brother.

“You Sun family’s little baby… is quite interesting.”

Li Fan’s clone’s words suddenly made Sun Luyao’s heart leap to his throat.

“Relax, I am not so low as to covet a child’s body.” Li Fan scoffed.

“I have a technique called the Creation Furnace Technique, which can continuously unearth the potential of the physical body. Most importantly, it has no cultivation threshold. One can even practice it while still in the womb. I also have the Limitless Fullness and Emptiness Method, which can continuously strengthen one’s divine soul through the mutual generation and restraint of the five elements…”

Li Fan’s clone spoke just a few words, yet they shook Sun Luyao’s heart deeply.

“Although the Limitless Fullness and Emptiness Method requires the assistance of five elements treasures during cultivation, with the current foundation of your Sun family, it should not be difficult to obtain them.”

“From the innate fetal stage, one can constantly temper both the divine soul and the physical body. Moreover, one can borrow the mother’s body to sense the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao at close range. Tsk tsk… I am somewhat looking forward to what kind of monster this little baby will become once he is born.” Li Fan’s clone let out a gloomy laugh.

“Senior… what are the conditions?” Sun Luyao was clearly tempted as well.

However, he understood very clearly that there was no free lunch in this world. He asked in a deep voice.

“Conditions?” Unexpectedly, Li Fan’s clone gave a cold laugh. “Does your Sun family even qualify to discuss conditions with me?”

“With your entire clan having only three Dao Integration cultivators, I can wipe you out with a flick of a finger.”

Sun Luyao fell silent.

“It is merely a whim, nothing more than an experiment.” Li Fan’s clone spoke in a faint, distant tone.

Li Fan was not lying.

In truth, he understood the situation of the fetus in Wu Ninglu’s womb even better than the two Sun brothers.

Previously, in order to observe the seven-colored rainbow light that had left the Xuanhuang Realm, his split soul had used Wu Ninglu’s body to sneak out and create some puppets.

During the time he was possessing her, Li Fan discovered that the fetus had faintly sensed his presence.

It instinctively sent out waves of “expulsion” thoughts toward him.

This had piqued Li Fan’s interest.

He had a premonition that if this fetus could be born smoothly, its future achievements would surely not be simple.

Under normal circumstances, the fetus had been sealed and suppressed by its mother for too long, making it very difficult for it to survive properly.

Even if it could barely be born, it would be destined to be congenitally deficient.

Not to mention cultivation.

But with Li Fan intervening in this life, the fetus would welcome an entirely new destiny.

While Li Fan’s clone was acting as a prenatal education teacher, Sun Luyuan had already found the entrance to the Great Qi small world.

The formation-breaking disc in his hand flashed continuously as he prepared to deduce and find the flaws in the formation here.

This was a formation-breaking treasure he had specially borrowed from Ji Hongdao. Although it was certainly not as effective as Ji Hongdao coming in person, it should still have no problem breaking ordinary protective formations.

Unexpectedly, the formation-breaking disc seemed to have malfunctioned. It only kept flashing without producing any result.

“That shouldn’t be the case?”

Sun Luyuan felt somewhat puzzled.

He held the formation-breaking disc and changed his position.

“Why not just announce yourself directly? Our Holy Dynasty of Great Qi would never turn away guests.”

Just as Sun Luyuan was still probing, a voice suddenly rang out beside his ear.

Sun Luyuan’s heart jolted violently. He quickly put away the formation-breaking disc and looked warily toward the direction the voice had come from.

He saw a rather handsome young man standing there with his arms crossed, looking at him with a half-smile.

“This little friend, may I ask…?”

Although the other party only had a Nascent Soul aura, the faint evil nature that inadvertently leaked from him made Sun Luyuan dare not underestimate him.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1223: The Shock Of Witnessing Holy Dynasty

The sinister-looking youth chuckled and said, “Just a nobody. However, if you wish to visit our Holy Dynasty of Great Qi, I can introduce you.”

Sun Luyuan pondered for a moment, then cupped his hands and replied, “In that case, I will trouble you, little friend.”

The sinister-looking youth, Ou Shangtian, revealed a meaningful smile.

With a cold shout, his figure instantly closed in and placed a hand on Sun Luyuan’s shoulder.

Sun Luyuan only felt his vision blur. In the next moment, he found himself in the endless void.

Ou Shangtian was already speeding ahead to lead the way.

Sun Luyuan hurriedly followed.

“This young man’s flying speed is truly fast.” Sun Luyuan thought in secret alarm.

The faint, colorful and brilliant glow emanating around his body made Sun Luyuan feel a greedy desire similar to the one he had when facing the Spirit of Heaven and Earth in the past.

Originally confident about this trip, Sun Luyuan’s state of mind was now somewhat disturbed. After flying aimlessly in the void for a while, a magnificent scene hidden like scenery beneath the water surface suddenly appeared ahead.

A massive sphere of light, its interior details impossible to discern.

Around the light sphere, numerous stars revolved, sparkling brilliantly.

Each star seemed to be a Cave-Heaven world.

Even from a great distance, Sun Luyuan was deeply shaken by the sight before him.

“Is this the small world of Great Qi?”

“Where could there be such a large small world?!”

While Sun Luyuan felt immense shock in his heart, he suddenly came back to his senses.

The young man who had been leading the way had disappeared without a trace at some point!

“Senior Hundred Flowers, I caught a Dao Integration cultivator from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. He was sneaking around, clearly up to no good…”

He vaguely heard the young man’s voice coming from an unknown direction.

“Wait!”

Sun Luyuan felt uneasy and was about to shout an explanation.

In the next moment, the scene before his eyes changed abruptly.

The previous vast world and Cave-Heavens vanished. What suddenly appeared before him was a magnificent palace shaped like a spire.

His body had been shrunk dozens of times, like an ant.

Every step in front of the palace looked like a mountain peak.

The majestic and enormous structures filled his vision, causing Sun Luyuan’s breathing to momentarily stop.

What he found even harder to understand was that all his cultivation had somehow completely disappeared.

He was now exactly like an ordinary mortal.

“An illusion?”

Sun Luyuan held his breath and concentrated, trying to clear all distracting thoughts from his mind.

After a long time, his emotions calmed and he opened his eyes again.

The scene before him remained the same magnificent palace, without the slightest change.

Furthermore…

Sun Luyuan discovered with some alarm that his body seemed to have become even smaller.

“Is this… a formation?”

This thought flashed through Sun Luyuan’s mind. He quickly took out Ji Hongdao’s formation-breaking disc once more.

Sure enough, the formation-breaking disc emitted a strong white light.

It flashed continuously as it automatically deduced and worked to break the formation before him.

Just as Sun Luyuan waited full of hope, the flashing frequency of the formation-breaking disc grew faster and faster.

Finally, it could no longer endure. As if reaching its limit, cracks appeared across its surface.

Then it exploded with a boom, leaving nothing behind.

Sun Luyuan stood there in a daze.

When he borrowed this formation-breaking disc, Ji Hongdao had never mentioned that such a situation could occur.

However, Sun Luyuan quickly came to his senses.

It was because this formation was far too complex, well beyond the range that the formation-breaking disc could handle.

“In that case, I can only try to break it with brute force.”

He waited a little longer. Apart from him, no other changes appeared within the formation.

Sun Luyuan took a deep breath. A dark red phantom floated out above his head.

The strongest method of a Dao Integration cultivator came from the Spirit of Heaven and Earth he had refined.

The Spirit of Heaven and Earth that Sun Luyuan had refined back then was named [Blood Shadow].

The instant the Blood Shadow appeared, Sun Luyuan’s body seemed to dissolve under its influence. It melted away strangely, leaving only a layer of skin.

Then it completely merged into the blood color.

Like gurgling flowing water, it moved against the steps, surging rapidly upward along the tall stairs of the formation.

Wherever the blood water flowed, the ground corroded with a hissing sound and left behind scorched traces.

But soon, the palace steps were restored.

The speed of the blood water’s flow grew faster and faster, gradually turning into a streak of red light. It no longer needed to climb along the stairs but shot straight toward the highest point of the palace.

However, the palace itself also underwent corresponding changes.

The stairs increased endlessly on their own, continuously extending in both directions with no end in sight.

Just as the two sides reached a stalemate, the wildly advancing red shadow suddenly burst apart.

It transformed into a sky full of blood rain. Using the dispersing force of the explosion, it no longer stubbornly climbed upward but instead fled in different directions across the space.

Those drops of blood water were instead like extremely scorching lava, capable of burning even space itself.

Wherever they scattered, holes appeared one after another in the formation space.

The towering palace scene trembled slightly.

As the phantom gradually shattered, the blood water that Sun Luyuan had transformed into slowly disappeared through these holes.

“I escaped!”

Sun Luyuan felt wild joy in his heart.

Killing intent surged within him as he prepared to settle accounts with the sinister youth who had led the way earlier. But the voice that reached his ears made him feel as if he had fallen into an ice cave.

“It was actually broken?”

“Master is going to punish me now. I worked hard on it for more than half a year, yet I couldn’t even trap a mere Dao Integration cultivator…”

The extremely annoyed tone of the sinister youth did not sound fake.

“What does this mean? Was that terrifying formation just now arranged by that youth? Not the protective grand formation of the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi?”

Sun Luyuan strongly suspected that he had misheard.

“What does ‘worked hard for more than half a year’ mean? Could it be that he is still a beginner? A formation that even the formation-breaking disc could not find flaws in after deducing to its limit is the result of just half a year of his work?”

Sun Luyuan stood there stunned.

“Your formation is actually quite good. It is just that your strength is still too low. When he fully unleashed that blood fire, I estimate that even a Dao Integration cultivator from the Xuanhuang Realm would not be able to withstand it head-on. It is only natural that your formation could not endure it.”

“Focus on your cultivation. In the Xuanhuang Realm, realm still reigns supreme. Strange beasts, strange abilities, and formations are all merely supplementary.”

A gentle female voice sounded beside the youth.

“Understood, Senior Hundred Flowers.” The youth responded somewhat dejectedly.

Amid the shining golden light, Sun Luyuan sensed that he had been locked onto by a gaze.

The aura coming from the other party made him feel as if he had fallen into an ice cave.

“This is…”

“A Longevity Immortal cultivator?”

Sun Luyuan’s mind went completely blank.

He had never expected that, right after arriving at the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi, he would immediately encounter a Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign.

“Hundred Flowers? What kind of Dao is that? Why have I never heard of it before?”

“You are from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance? What is your purpose in coming to our Holy Dynasty?” Daoist Hundred Flowers asked calmly.

Invisible golden light had already enveloped Sun Luyuan.

With just a single thought from her, this Dao Integration cultivator from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance could be turned into colored glass and easily crushed.

After all, so much source power essence had not been consumed for nothing.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1224: Heaven-Defying Formless Net

Faced with Daoist Hundred Flowers’ questioning, Sun Luyuan found himself unable to answer.

Dark golden glazed light swirled around him. The power that surpassed Dao Integration made Sun Luyuan’s spiritual sense scream warnings frantically, yet he dared not make the slightest movement.

“Junior… this junior…”

Before Daoist Hundred Flowers’ killing intent that felt almost tangible, Sun Luyuan, who was usually eloquent, could not even utter a complete sentence.

Indeed, for cultivators of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, the term Longevity Immortal Realm felt far too distant.

“Hmph, just as little Tian said. Sneaky and clearly up to no good.” A cold gleam flashed in Daoist Hundred Flowers’ eyes.

The dark golden light surrounding Sun Luyuan came alive and began to erode him.

“Rest assured. With my strength, even if Great Qi is a dragon’s lair or tiger’s den, I can still retreat unscathed.”

For some reason, Sun Luyuan suddenly recalled the bold words he had spoken to his younger brother before leaving.

He could not help feeling an overwhelming sense of absurdity.

“Could it be that I am really going to die here?”

Just as Sun Luyuan despaired and waited for death, a voice like heavenly music rang out and stopped Daoist Hundred Flowers’ actions.

“Bring him before me.”

Though the tone was calm, it carried boundless authority.

The wicked-looking youth’s expression changed upon hearing the voice. The playful look on his face vanished instantly, and he respectfully saluted from afar.

Daoist Hundred Flowers also moved at the sound.

The originally aggressive glazed light transformed into several ropes that firmly bound Sun Luyuan.

Following the two, Sun Luyuan was led into the tallest spire palace in the Great Qi small world.

Inside the grand hall, it was empty and solemn.

A figure sat upright at the top.

Enveloped in mysterious light, Sun Luyuan found it difficult to see the person’s face clearly.

“Kneel!”

The wicked-looking youth said coldly.

Sun Luyuan was a Dao Integration cultivator who held an important position in the Guardian Hall. Apart from existences like the Dharma-Transmitters and Mirror Holders, he already stood at the peak of power in the Immortal Alliance.

Yet before the ruler of this small world, he showed no hesitation and knelt decisively.

Even a Longevity Immortal Realm cultivator could be commanded here. What was a mere Dao Integration like him?

“Greetings… Holy Emperor.”

Prompted by the wicked-looking youth, Sun Luyuan spoke with a trembling voice.

“Sun Luyuan.” The Holy Emperor spoke his name directly.

Then, in just a few words, the Holy Emperor revealed almost all of Sun Luyuan’s background.

This left Sun Luyuan feeling ice-cold all over and shivering uncontrollably.

But the Holy Emperor’s next sentence swept away the chill in his heart and filled him with great joy: “I already know your purpose in coming. The Holy Dynasty does not oppose those from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance who wish to pledge allegiance.”

“Fewer killings are always better.”

The Holy Emperor’s voice, filled with immense authority, echoed throughout the palace.

“As the first person to defect to the Holy Dynasty, you indeed deserve some reward.”

A cold, clear gleam appeared beside the Holy Emperor and flew toward Sun Luyuan in an instant.

It stopped steadily in front of him.

“Hiss…”

An intense chill swept over him, causing a layer of frost to form on Sun Luyuan’s face.

Only by rapidly circulating his spiritual energy did he manage to dispel the icy aura.

Sun Luyuan looked closely and saw that what had stopped before him was a pale blue sword.

“This is the Icebound Myriad Years Sword. When its internal cold energy is activated, it can instantly freeze an entire prefecture of the Xuanhuang Realm. Although you possess the realm, your combat strength is truly too weak. Escaping is fine, but if you truly fight with your life on the line, I am afraid you cannot even defeat a few of my disciples.” The Holy Emperor commented.

Sun Luyuan did not dare to refute. He could only nod repeatedly in agreement.

“This sword is given to you for self-protection.”

Only after the Holy Emperor spoke these words did Sun Luyuan, under the somewhat unfriendly gaze of the wicked-looking youth, feel as if he were in a dream. He refined and stored away the Icebound Myriad Years Sword.

“Thank you for your grace, Holy Emperor.” Sun Luyuan was overjoyed.

“Since you have joined my Holy Dynasty, you must not be half-hearted from now on. Tian’er, he is now in your hands.”

The wicked-looking youth bowed and said: “Disciple understands.”

“You may both leave.” The Holy Emperor gave the order to dismiss them in a calm tone.

Daoist Hundred Flowers turned into a streak of escaping light and flew away first.

Sun Luyuan followed behind Ou Shangtian and left slowly.

“You, come with me.” Ou Shangtian glanced at Sun Luyuan and snorted coldly.

“May I know how I should address you?” Sun Luyuan, having received benefits, did not mind the other party’s attitude and asked with a thick-skinned smile.

“Just call me General Ou.”

Ou Shangtian said little else. He first brought Sun Luyuan into the Holy Capital of the Holy Dynasty, to the Holy Heart Hall.

The hall was only slightly lower than the Holy Emperor’s throne. Inside, over ten thousand people had gathered.

There were both the old and the young. All of them had devout expressions as they prayed toward a statue in the center of the hall.

The statue’s specific features could not be seen clearly, but its figure was identical to the Holy Emperor Sun Luyuan had just met.

The citizens of the Holy Dynasty praying here all possessed cultivation.

However, Sun Luyuan’s Dao Integration aura, which he had not yet deliberately concealed, had no effect on these people at all.

They all gazed firmly at the Holy Emperor’s statue, fully immersed in their devotion.

Their minds seemed connected into a large net. Mere Dao Integration power could not shake it in the slightest.

Inside the room, dozens of the Holy Emperor’s believers were kneeling.

All of them had reached the Golden Core realm, and their faith in the Holy Emperor was even firmer than that of those outside.

In the secret chamber, the Faceless Statue of the Holy Emperor still stood.

“You should also make your vow and pray.” After transmitting a segment of the oath, Ou Shangtian instructed.

Sun Luyuan naturally would not refuse.

He imitated the believers, knelt before the Holy Emperor’s statue, and chanted the words.

As Sun Luyuan did this, it seemed as if the majestic and sacred figure of the Holy Emperor truly appeared before his eyes.

What made him even more alarmed was that an invisible force emerged from the void.

It slowly attached itself to him, linking him with the Holy Emperor.

Sun Luyuan had the feeling that he was now like a flying insect that had accidentally fallen into a spider’s web. Although he did not struggle, he was sinking deeper and deeper.

“What kind of method is this?”

Sun Luyuan was horrified in his heart. Even Mirror Holders needed secret techniques to control their subordinates.

To achieve such deep control with just a few simple words…

It was simply unheard of!

But Sun Luyuan also knew he could no longer turn back.

After he finished reciting the oath, Ou Shangtian’s attitude improved noticeably.

He tilted his head slightly, as if listening to something.

Then he said to Sun Luyuan: “There is a restriction left by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance in your body. If you rashly remove it, it may alert the other side. Let it remain for now.”

“But rest assured. With Master’s power protecting you, this restriction cannot harm you.”

Ou Shangtian’s words put Sun Luyuan much more at ease.

“Since you have already joined our Holy Dynasty, you cannot only take benefits without doing anything. Here is a list…”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1225: Immortal Ark Trapped, Seeking Survival

Sun Luyuan took the list and glanced at it. He was greatly shocked.

On it, besides the exceptionally talented disciples from the various major families of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, there were also many cultivators holding key positions within the alliance.

Some were familiar to Sun Luyuan, but others held secret posts that even he knew little about.

“Try your best to win over the people on this list.”

“You don’t need to go to too much trouble. Just find an excuse to lure them…”

“Bring them over here. Once they witness the majestic grandeur of our Holy Dynasty, they will surely, like you, immediately realize their mistake and return to the right path!”

“Don’t be impatient. Take it slow to avoid revealing any flaws.” Ou Shangtian said.

“Every successful case will count as your contribution. There are plenty of good things in our Holy Dynasty’s treasury. That Myriad Ice Sword of yours is not even considered top-tier.”

Ou Shangtian’s words made Sun Luyuan’s heart race with excitement.

In the Tianxuan Mirror, although it claimed that anything could be exchanged, everyone with clear eyes knew that the real treasures could not be obtained with contribution points.

They either required extremely high merit ranks or came with various strict exchange conditions.

A divine sword like the one in his hand, capable of easily endangering an entire prefecture, would in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance only be possessed by the core members of institutions such as the Martial Practice Hall or the Garrison Court.

“Do a good job.” Ou Shangtian patted Sun Luyuan’s shoulder in a senior-like manner.

Sun Luyuan nodded repeatedly.

He did not stay long in the Holy Dynasty. Soon after, Sun Luyuan was sent away.

Upon returning to the Xuanhuang Realm, Sun Luyuan felt as if he had experienced a bizarre dream.

Only the extremely cold sword in his dantian reminded him that none of it was false.

“Brother, you’re back so soon? How did it go?”

In the Sun family secret chamber, his younger brother Sun Luyao asked impatiently.

Sun Luyuan did not mention the embarrassing situation he had encountered. He simply took out the Icebound Myriad Years Sword.

“The foundation of the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi is even stronger than we imagined. They actually have a Longevity Immortal in charge. As for the Holy Emperor’s cultivation, it is unfathomable and impossible to see through.”

“Such a treasure was given away just like that.”

“Compared to them, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance really amounts to nothing.”

…

As Sun Luyao listened to his brother’s words, the voice of the shadowy senior rang in his ears.

“How was it, as I said? Heh heh.”

“Brother, no matter what decision you make, I will always support you.” Sun Luyao’s expression was somewhat complicated as he stated his stance.

Sun Luyuan, however, thought his younger brother was worried about their uncertain future and comforted him warmly for a while.

After Sun Luyuan left the secret chamber, Sun Luyao finally could not hold back and asked, “Senior, how much do you know about this Holy Dynasty of Great Qi?”

This mysterious shadow had made an accurate prediction about Sun Luyuan’s trip to the Holy Dynasty. Clearly, he knew the true situation of the Holy Dynasty very well.

Li Fan’s clone put on an act and pondered for a moment.

Then, his tone grew heavy: “The choice made by your Sun family is a wise one. That Holy Emperor of Great Qi has strength no weaker than mine.”

“With no Immortal Ancestor appearing in the Xuanhuang Realm, he is an invincible existence.”

The meaning behind these words made Sun Luyao’s heart pound wildly.

Although he had already guessed at the mysterious shadow’s strength, hearing the other party confirm it personally still shocked him deeply.

“Do you know that the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm also possesses its own desires, just like cultivators?”

“This Holy Emperor is very likely the embodiment of the Xuanhuang Realm’s good thoughts. His purpose is to restore order to the world that has been distorted by the Immortal Ancestor.”

Upon hearing this, Sun Luyao’s heart shook violently once more.

The embodiment of the Xuanhuang Realm’s Heavenly Dao?

This origin was simply too astonishing.

His thoughts fell into brief chaos. Soon after, as if recalling something, Sun Luyao asked again: “The embodiment of good thoughts? Does Senior mean that the Xuanhuang Realm also has an embodiment of evil thoughts?”

Li Fan’s clone remained silent and did not respond.

…

At the distant edge of the star sea.

Deep within the endless sea of relics, inside the Xuanshang Ark.

Relying on the Spirit-Soul Harmonization Art, Zhao Xian’s cultivation speed surged forward at an astonishing rate, already breaking through the shackles of the Foundation Establishment stage.

He had successfully formed a Golden Core.

The commotion caused by breaking through to the Golden Core realm finally disturbed Zhao Xian’s master, Zhao Wuji.

Upon discovering Zhao Xian’s terrifying rate of progress, Zhao Wuji was not pleased but alarmed instead.

“Master, why?” Zhao Xian asked, full of resentment.

Zhao Wuji had not reported Zhao Xian’s successful breakthrough to the academy. Instead, he had actively suppressed and concealed it.

This naturally left Zhao Xian very dissatisfied. In a moment of impulse, he even wanted to reveal everything he had seen in his dreams.

However, having experienced many things in those dreams, he knew that this master of his was trustworthy.

He took a deep breath, suppressed the anger in his heart, and asked in a deep voice.

“It is too fast. Xian’er, your cultivation is advancing far too quickly. If it were only late Foundation Establishment, that would be fine, but you jumped straight to the Golden Core realm…” Zhao Wuji said with a face full of worry.

Under Zhao Xian’s repeated pressing, Zhao Wuji finally explained the matter.

“Geniuses like you, though extremely rare in our academy in the past, have still appeared before. Back in my day, I had a senior sister.”

“She was truly exceptionally talented. She even cultivated faster than you, without any process of gradual accumulation. In merely three years, she went straight from Qi Condensation to the perfection of the Golden Core realm.”

“This naturally caused a sensation in the academy, and then…”

Upon hearing this, Zhao Xian’s expression also became somewhat grave.

Because he had never heard of such a person existing within the academy.

“The senior sister was conscripted. From then on, there was no news of her. Her life or death remains unknown.” Zhao Wuji sighed deeply.

“Conscripted? Conscripted where?” Zhao Xian was slightly stunned.

The Immortal Ark had not experienced any real battles for a very long time.

He could not understand the word “conscripted” that his master mentioned.

Zhao Wuji hesitated, wanting to speak but stopping.

Finally, under Zhao Xian’s burning gaze, he first set up multiple restrictions to seal the area.

Only then did he transmit his voice: “Xian’er, do you know why our Dao Attainment Academy holds such a transcendent status? Even the Celestial Pivot Court does not dare to offend us excessively?”

Zhao Xian answered according to what he had learned before: “Naturally because our Dao Attainment Academy is the source of the Immortal Ark’s Dao techniques.”

“Then, why exactly is the academy called the source of Dao techniques?”

Zhao Wuji’s question left Zhao Xian stumped.

“Because our academy provided the most important theoretical support for the Immortal Ark’s survival after the great calamity descended.” Zhao Wuji said seriously.

“The power of the True Immortal that caused the boundless catastrophe back then cannot be comprehended by ordinary cultivators without absolute talent. Our academy…”

“Possesses the most outstanding scholars in the entire Immortal Ark.”

“Talent is also an extremely important resource in the Immortal Ark. No matter whether these geniuses are willing or not, once they display it, they will be firmly controlled.”

“Even stripped away and no longer belonging to themselves.”

Zhao Wuji’s words sent a chill through Zhao Xian’s heart.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1226: New Law Of The Darkest Star Sea

Although Zhao Wuji had not explicitly described the specific method of stripping away talent, the fact that those so-called geniuses from the Academy of Dao Attainment on the Immortal Ark in the past had never appeared in public view again suggested their fates were probably not good.

More critically, in his own dreams Zhao Xian had encountered nothing related to it.

This caused a momentary, irrepressible panic in him, who believed he could foresee events in advance.

However, he was no longer the simple and ignorant youth.

Soon a cold energy surged from his heart, allowing Zhao Xian to calm down quickly.

He pondered rapidly: “Indeed, exposing one’s talent would invite the covetous attention of the academy’s upper echelons. It might even cost me my life. But crisis often comes hand in hand with opportunity.”

“Now is not the time when the academy could cover the sky with one hand. The Immortal Ark has undergone major changes. The Severed Immortal Tower and the Celestial Pivot Court are fiercely contending for control of the Immortal Ark’s future. Even the Academy of Dao Attainment must consider the impact this struggle between the two sides will have on the Immortal Ark.”

“Talent is merely talent. As long as one can continue to survive, there will never be a shortage of geniuses.”

Zhao Xian felt his thinking had never been clearer. It was as if something in the dark was guiding him, allowing him to sift through the numerous chaotic clues and deduce the correct future.

“If I act timidly, the Immortal Ark will still face a doomed end. Only by confronting the three major forces of the Immortal Ark and seeking life amid death can I lead the Immortal Ark to find a way to survive.”

At this thought the fear in Zhao Xian’s eyes vanished completely. His previously chaotic aura became steady once more.

Zhao Wuji looked at his disciple’s change with some astonishment, not understanding the reason.

Zhao Xian took a deep breath and replied in a deep voice: “A petty man sacrifices himself for profit, while a sage sacrifices himself for the world. I have received favors from the academy, which is why I have achieved what I have today. If my talent is useful to the academy, then take it! Why should I begrudge it?”

“On the contrary, if I deliberately hide while cultivating with the academy’s resources, would that not be ungrateful and disloyal?”

These words were spoken with great righteousness. Standing on the moral high ground, Zhao Wuji found himself completely unable to refute for a moment.

He looked at this young disciple as if truly seeing him for the first time.

Zhao Xian’s sincere and resolute gaze even made Zhao Wuji feel a trace of shame in his heart.

The two confronted each other in silence.

In the end it was Zhao Wuji who yielded.

“Fine. Since you hold such convictions, it makes me seem petty instead.” Zhao Wuji sighed deeply and left in a daze.

Zhao Xian watched his master’s departing figure. A complex expression flashed across his face before it disappeared.

“I must save the Immortal Ark.”

In his mind this thought became even more resolute.

The master and disciple pair had not realized at all the reason for Zhao Xian’s stubbornness.

It was not because Zhao Xian possessed such noble character or firm faith.

It was purely because...

The clone hidden in his sea of consciousness was at work.

“Interesting. I did not expect that the Academy of Dao Attainment still has a method to seize others’ talents for themselves. As expected of the civilization that has survived in the Darkest Star Sea until now. It indeed has its merits.”

“Whether it is the secrets of the Academy of Dao Attainment or the control over True Immortal Seal Script by the Celestial Pivot Court and the Severed Immortal Tower, they all hold value for me. How could I let this kid Zhao Xian back down? Moreover, with me here, it is not that easy for anyone to harm him.”

The news that Zhao Xian had broken through to the Golden Core stage in such a short time spread throughout the entire academy three days later.

The academy had gone far too long without such a genius appearing. Now, with the storm about to break, under the deliberate fueling by certain interested parties, Zhao Xian’s fame quickly spread beyond the academy and grew increasingly exaggerated.

From barely breaking through to the Golden Core, it became directly entering the Golden Core and reaching perfection in seven days.

In the end, it turned into the exaggerated level of half-step Nascent Soul.

By this point, even if those who knew the inside story stepped forward to clarify, it was of no use.

Outsiders would only take it as the academy trying to conceal such a peerless genius.

The Severed Immortal Tower and the Celestial Pivot Court both sent people to inquire about Zhao Xian’s background.

Within the academy, opinions diverged on how to handle this rare cultivation genius who appeared once in several centuries.

Amid the surging tides of great momentum, Zhao Xian eliminated all distracting thoughts even more thoroughly.

He peacefully hid in the secret chamber specially opened for him by the academy and accelerated his cultivation as much as possible.

“No need to worry about whether spiritual energy is sufficient. So this is what it feels like. Is this the true treatment for the academy’s direct-line disciples?”

“Moreover, before the final decision on how to handle me is made, I can freely enjoy the vast majority of the academy’s resources.”

His divine sense intensity soared all the way, converting into his own cultivation.

After his realm advanced, it in turn nourished his divine sense to continue growing stronger.

Forming a perfect cycle, Zhao Xian’s cultivation speed had reached an unimaginable level.

Especially since there were essentially no bottlenecks or obstacles.

Zhao Xian felt his heart was as firm as a rock.

No distracting thoughts or inner demons could affect him in the slightest.

“I have never cultivated this rapidly even in my dreams. Could it be that I am truly a genius?”

“Was my previously slow cultivation progress simply due to scarce resources and not finding the right techniques?”

Such thoughts could not help but arise in Zhao Xian’s mind.

At this moment, however, Li Fan’s clone, which had blocked all the inner demons for him, was somewhat overexerted.

Even with the Immortal Heart Curse enhancing it, dealing with these breakthrough inner demons was no easy task.

The various extremely realistic illusions he experienced, although Li Fan could maintain his self-awareness, each time he escaped them it still took considerable effort. Especially since the process of confronting the inner demons seemed to be recorded, making the next encounter’s inner demons even stronger.

After all, it was only a clone; a bit of wear and tear was perfectly normal.

“One can imagine how difficult it was for cultivators to advance when the laws of the Darkest Star Sea had not yet shattered.”

“No wonder the new law of the Xuanhuang Realm could replace it.”

At this thought, an idea suddenly arose in Li Fan’s mind without reason.

The Darkest Star Sea could be seen as an enlarged version of the Xuanhuang Realm. The upper limit of its laws was higher here.

It was simply that they had been smashed to pieces by the power of the True Immortal Seal Script.

Since the Immortal Ancestor's new law worked in the Xuanhuang Realm, did that mean that under normal circumstances, the new law could also be cultivated normally in this star sea?

“The corresponding requirements would all need to be scaled up to the level of the star sea…”

“Foundation Establishment wonder treasure, Golden Core methods, Nascent Soul Cave-Heaven, and so on; when you get down to it, they are all manifestations of the world’s laws. The laws of the Darkest Star Sea are shattered and filled with the power of the True Immortal Seal Script.”

“Thinking in reverse, the True Immortal Seal Script is precisely the current manifestation of the law power in the Darkest Star Sea.”

A flash of insight struck Li Fan’s mind, as if he had captured some crucial clue.

“The Severed Immortal Tower cultivates by relying on the True Immortal Seal Script. Perhaps its principle is the same as the new law of the Xuanhuang Realm.”
