Chapter 1227: I Enter The True Immortal Path

“Is the sudden rise of Immortal Ancestor related to this Severed Immortal Tower?”

“What is his purpose in summoning sixteen Dao Integration cultivators to the Darkest Star Sea?”

“And the cultivator I encountered when I first arrived in this place full of relic fragments was not from the Xuan Immortal Ark. There should be another immortal cultivation civilization here. Is it the Shang Immortal Ark, or is it related to the Immortal Ancestor?”

...

Li Fan’s thoughts were interrupted by the intense energy fluctuations within Zhao Xian’s body.

Above Zhao Xian’s head, spiritual energy formed a vortex and was madly poured into his body.

At his dantian, cracks appeared on the somewhat purplish golden core.

As if something was being nurtured within it.

After two months of frenzied closed-door cultivation, Zhao Xian stepped into the Nascent Soul realm!

However, this Nascent Soul was different from what Li Fan had seen in the ancient texts of the Xuanhuang Realm.

It was not the image of a vibrant infant full of Dao charm, but directly a miniature version of Zhao Xian himself.

It even looked older than Zhao Xian himself.

Upon close observation, Li Fan discovered that the apparent age manifested by this Nascent Soul seemed to hold some profound mystery.

“Is this a predetermined fate? The innate limitation on Zhao Xian’s ability to break through to the next realm. In other words, before reaching the age manifested by the Nascent Soul, Zhao Xian cannot break through to the Soul Transformation realm?”

After a long time, Li Fan had a vague guess.

“Is this a different kind of bottleneck? It has never been mentioned in the records of the Xuanhuang Realm. It is just unknown whether this predetermined age limit can be changed.”

Zhao Xian, who had finally awakened from closed-door cultivation, also noticed the abnormality of his own Nascent Soul.

His progress had been too rapid, causing his mentality to be slightly off. It took him quite a while to accept this fact.

He privately sought out Zhao Wuji to inquire.

“What? You have formed your Nascent Soul?”

This time Zhao Wuji was not shocked but rather frightened.

The Academy of Dao Attainment had not yet finished discussing the handling of Golden Core Zhao Xian, yet he had already become a Nascent Soul cultivator!

Wouldn’t all the previous discussions be rendered void?

“How could this be?”

Only after seeing the slightly older miniature figure that emerged from Zhao Xian’s body did Zhao Wuji fully believe it.

“Looking at your situation, you have hope of reaching Soul Transformation within ten years. This...”

Zhao Wuji stared in a daze for a while, momentarily at a loss for words.

“No, I cannot make the decision on this matter. I must notify the Academy Master directly.” Zhao Wuji fled as if escaping, his figure looking extremely disheveled.

Meanwhile, Li Fan’s clone took the opportunity to temporarily transfer onto Zhao Wuji.

Zhao Xian would surely undergo strict examination soon.

Not knowing the strength of the Academy of Dao Attainment’s master, it was better to avoid the sharp edge for now.

Zhao Wuji went all the way to the top floor of the academy and pushed open the door.

There were three white-haired elders present, all looking at the flustered Zhao Wuji with some surprise.

“Zhao Xian has formed his Nascent Soul.”

These words caused all three elders to change expression slightly.

Just as Li Fan had expected, they no longer paid any attention to Zhao Wuji who had delivered the message.

Their figures flickered and appeared directly beside Zhao Xian.

By the time Zhao Wuji saw Zhao Xian again, seven days had passed.

Although he had only recently formed his Nascent Soul, his aura had already completely stabilized.

“The Academy Master retrieved the recorded image from the day you and I met. Because of my impassioned words, the Academy Master ultimately abandoned the original plan and instead decided to truly nurture me.”

“People with talent are not rare. But people with talent and absolute loyalty are very rare.”

Zhao Xian said to Zhao Wuji.

Recalling what had happened these past days, Zhao Wuji felt an extremely unreal sensation.

“However, Master, what you said was not wrong. If I were still at the Golden Core realm, I would probably have been refined into a Dao pill and swallowed by the Academy Master by now. Fortunately, I was one step faster and sought life from death. After all, the Academy Master’s age has almost reached its limit. The academy also needs a successor.” Zhao Xian transmitted quietly.

“This is also the last time I will call you Master.”

Zhao Wuji watched Zhao Xian’s departing figure with a sense of loss.

Li Fan’s clone then transferred back onto Zhao Xian.

As the academy’s successor, Zhao Xian’s treatment underwent a heaven-shaking change compared to before.

He not only resided on the secondary top floor of the academy but also gained access to many secrets that were previously completely unattainable.

For example, the true power system of the Immortal Ark.

One of the three elders of the academy, a cultivator named Yu Shuo, personally instructed Zhao Xian.

“Nascent Soul, Soul Transformation, Dao Integration. These realms are actually only surface appearances.”

“In the face of the scattered power of True Immortals that permeates the Star Sea, they are essentially no different at their core.”

“The so-called higher strength is merely allowing one to linger a bit longer.”

“Yet in the end, death is inevitable.”

Seeing Zhao Xian, whose worldview had been severely shaken and whose expression was somewhat dazed, Yu Shuo smiled and continued: “The reason our Academy of Dao Attainment is qualified to bear the name ‘Dao Attainment’ is that we were the first to find a method to avoid the harm of True Immortal power and even make use of it.”

“We call it the ‘Entering the Dao Method.’”

“Entering the Dao Method?” Zhao Xian was still full of confusion.

“It is said that every True Immortal has their own unique ‘Dao.’ The Darkest Star Sea we are in is filled with the unique powers of True Immortals. For mortal bodies to avoid being influenced by True Immortals, one must enter the Dao with one’s body.” As Yu Shuo spoke, his body underwent a strange change.

His appearance flickered inexplicably.

From the original white-haired, youthful-faced elder, he turned into an innocent young child. Then suddenly into an ambitious youth.

Not only his appearance, but also his body and aura were constantly changing.

“Once you enter the Dao of a True Immortal, not only can you avoid its harm. Even the power of other True Immortals in the Star Sea can mostly be eliminated.” Yu Shuo returned to his original appearance and said with some pride.

“This is also the method adopted by the Celestial Pivot Court and the vast majority of upper-level cultivators on the Immortal Ark.”

After some thought, Zhao Xian keenly pointed out the key issue.

“Does the Entering the Dao Method have any flaws? Has the Severed Immortal Tower found a new way to utilize True Immortal power?”

Yu Shuo nodded, showing an expression as if the youth was teachable.

“The power of True Immortals is not something anyone can touch. For ordinary cultivators to master it, there are great requirements on physique and aptitude, and they must use special materials and methods to progress gradually. If ordinary people come into contact with too much at once, the only outcome is death and the Dao dissipating.”

“Elder Yu, are you referring to the world extinction relics?” Suddenly recalling something, Zhao Xian quickly asked.

“Correct. And only the relics where the remaining True Immortal power is about to dissipate and is at its weakest have the best effect.”

“As for why the Severed Immortal Tower could stand against the Celestial Pivot Court in such a short time, it is said that they have found a method to harmlessly and perfectly absorb the world extinction relics.” There was some disbelief in Yu Shuo’s tone.

Chapter 1228: Outsider Of The Immortal Ark

“I have also seen the cultivator from the Severed Immortal Tower who is said to have entered the Dao through the new method. It is said that he achieved his Dao in only three years. Moreover, his control over True Immortal power is not inferior to mine.” Yu Shuo’s tone was strange.

“If that is the case, why does Elder Yu still seem to have quite a bit of doubt in your heart?”

“Because all the cultivators in the Severed Immortal Tower who are currently said to have entered the Dao and have appeared in public, the True Immortal power they control is all of the same kind.” Yu Shuo’s tone was faint.

“What does this explain?” Zhao Xian was somewhat puzzled.

Yu Shuo glanced at Zhao Xian with an inscrutable expression. After a long silence, he finally spoke calmly: “Zhao Xian, you must remember this. When dealing with True Immortal power, never let your guard down.”

“The deeper you enter the Dao, the more dangerous you become to yourself.”

Yu Shuo did not explain directly, but instead uttered this sentence for no apparent reason.

“Let us end today’s lesson here. Next time, it will be Nangong’s turn.” Yu Shuo’s figure disappeared along with his voice.

Zhao Xian remained in place, pondering Yu Shuo’s last words.

A flash of insight suddenly appeared in his mind, instantly revealing the meaning behind those words.

“True Immortal power is like a net. Although borrowing the net of a True Immortal allows one to draw upon its power, the result is only sinking deeper and deeper…”

Zhao Xian, who had suddenly understood this principle, felt as if a basin of cold water had been poured over him in winter, sending chills throughout his body.

“If this is truly the case, then our Academy of Dao Attainment and even the entire Immortal Ark…”

But soon, Zhao Xian gave a bitter smile.

“It is simply a necessary measure for survival. After all, survival is the first priority that the Immortal Ark must maintain. Moreover, we have been wandering in the Star Sea for so many years and have only seen the residual power of True Immortals, never their actual shadows. There is indeed risk, but it can be temporarily set aside from consideration.”

At the same time, Zhao Xian also understood what Yu Shuo meant about the strangeness of the Severed Immortal Tower.

In the Darkest Star Sea, various residual powers of True Immortals are mixed together.

If they had truly mastered a method to harmlessly and perfectly absorb world extinction relics, then the Dao-entering manifestations of the Severed Immortal Tower’s cultivators should also show various different states simultaneously.

After all, each person can adapt to different residual True Immortal powers.

In the Darkest Star Sea, the Immortal Ark knows of no fewer than five kinds of True Immortal powers. The three leaders of the Academy of Dao Attainment each have different directions of entering the Dao.

Yet the six Dao-entering cultivators from the Severed Immortal Tower who have appeared so far all display the exact same entering-Dao power.

Such a probability exists, but it is very small.

“Either their technique is only effective against a single type of True Immortal power.”

“Or else…”

A chill ran through Zhao Xian’s heart, and he dared not think further.

The current task assigned to him by the academy was to first stabilize his Nascent Soul cultivation, then attempt to break through to the Soul Transformation realm once more.

After successfully reaching Soul Transformation, he would slowly begin to attempt entering the Dao.

“Entering the Dao cannot be done too early, nor too late.”

“If one comes into contact with True Immortal power too early, a weak divine soul and will have almost no possibility of resisting the vast and matchless power of a True Immortal. There is a high chance of directly mutating into a mad, deformed monster.”

“And if it is too late…”

“The traditional Dao Integration realm, when merging with the remnant heavenly laws of this Star Sea, will produce intense rejection against the Dao of True Immortals. The difficulty of entering the Dao would increase by more than several times.”

The teaching voice of Nangong Lie, the master of the Academy of Dao Attainment, echoed in Zhao Xian’s mind.

“The academy will not push too hard for now. The urgent task is still to improve my own strength as quickly as possible.”

“Although the Nascent Soul tribulation shows that I can only break through after the age of fifty, the Academy Master said that the restrictions of the broken heavenly laws in this fragmented Star Sea are not absolute. With the foundation of the academy and the accumulations from my dreams, reversing fate and changing destiny is by no means difficult.”

Zhao Xian was very confident about this.

In fact, many details of the future dream that Zhao Xian had initially experienced had already differed greatly from reality. Under normal circumstances, as long as Zhao Xian was a bit more meticulous, he should have noticed the improprieties long ago.

However, under the continuous hidden influence of a certain existence, Zhao Xian had intentionally or unintentionally ignored all those unreasonable points.

Not to mention a mere Nascent Soul cultivator—even the three leaders of the Academy of Dao Attainment on this Immortal Ark would likely be far from a match for Li Fan when it came to manipulating people’s hearts.

At this moment, Li Fan’s clone was taking full advantage of Zhao Xian’s permissions to extensively browse all kinds of secret knowledge within the Academy of Dao Attainment.

All records related to True Immortal power had been hidden and could not be accessed.

However, there was not much concealment regarding the history of the Immortal Ark.

Consistent with what he had learned before, the Immortal Ark had split from the original immortal ark [Xuanshang].

What was described in more detail was that the main cause of the internal chaos on the immortal ark back then was an outsider.

Despite being an outsider, this person quickly won the trust of more than half the people on the immortal ark.

He even incited them to attempt crossing the invisible High Wall that sealed off the Darkest Star Sea.

At the critical moment, an immortal ark cultivator named Cai Zhengjun—who later became the first head of the Celestial Pivot Court of the Immortal Ark—timely led the crowd to stop this suicidal behavior.

However, it ultimately led to a war between the two sides, and the immortal ark was fractured.

Records of that outsider were also extremely brief. His true identity had already become a mystery.

Regarding the situation in the Darkest Star Sea, the Academy of Dao Attainment had many detailed descriptions.

In addition to the spatiotemporal maps recorded by the Immortal Ark during its years of drifting and fleeing, the Immortal Ark also had unique discussions about the Sea of Relics and the High Wall.

“Deeper in the Sea of Relics, near the location of the invisible High Wall, lies the graveyard of all realms and the burial ground of the Star Sea. The terrifying suction force from the center of the Star Sea spans across the Star Sea but is blocked by the High Wall, accumulating day by day. Coupled with the mysterious power of the High Wall, the suction force is fixed like tides, forming a reverse force field.”

“The closer one gets to the High Wall, the stronger the suction becomes. After dozens of explorations that all ended without results, the immortal ark has currently given up on the idea of further investigation.”

...

Li Fan was overjoyed upon unexpectedly finding this report.

Perhaps due to its special position, the Immortal Ark’s understanding of the overall picture of the Darkest Star Sea was far superior to that of the Xuanhuang Realm.

“Under the High Wall, tidal locking, world fragments?”

In Li Fan’s mind seemed to emerge a dead-silent scene of endless stretches of broken world fragments.

“So, the existence in the depths of the Star Sea that I originally thought could rival the suction force of the Immortal Ruins is merely the High Wall reflecting the suction force of the Darkest Star Sea?𝚕.𝚌𝗼𝗺

Although there was a slight disappointment in his heart, Li Fan instead felt relieved.

After all, as long as it was not another True Immortal, everything could be discussed.

“The invisible High Wall can actually block all the power of the [Hunger] character and even cause it to converge in reverse.”

“Just what kind of inconceivable mighty force is this?”

Chapter 1229: All Heavenly Prodigies Become Pills

Li Fan had personally witnessed the true power of the [Hunger] character art in the Immortal Ruins.

Therefore, a sense of awe naturally arose in his heart toward the High Wall that firmly suppressed the suction force of the Star Sea.

This was followed by an irrepressible curiosity.

“It is a pity that the Immortal Ark’s exploration of the other side of the Star Sea stopped more than two thousand years ago. With the changes of time and space, the star maps recorded back then can only serve as reference for me and actually hold little practical value.”

Li Fan recalled the great upheaval in the Xuanhuang Realm not long ago.

“It seems that the main area where the Immortal Ancestor and Heavenly Doctor appear in the Star Sea is precisely the graveyard of world fragments.”

“Something must have gone wrong at the foot of the High Wall, causing the usually stable tidal suction force to erupt in reverse toward the interior of the Darkest Star Sea.”

Various thoughts surged in Li Fan’s mind.

“The Immortal Ancestor was able to notify the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance in advance, which shows that this matter is definitely related to Him. And His summoning of those sixteen Dao Integration cultivators here is very likely connected to this as well.”

Thinking of this, Li Fan’s heart stirred: “If I wait here like a rabbit by a stump, I might actually encounter that seven-colored rainbow light.”

“Based on the time I previously spent crossing the Star Sea and the flying speed of the seven-colored rainbow light…”

“If I am truly fated to meet it, it should happen within the next few days.”

...

After finishing reading the parts he was most interested in, Li Fan began organizing other important gains.

This life could not only help Great Qi launch its uprising more smoothly, but it would also provide enormous assistance for Li Fan’s future reincarnations.

Among them, the most important to Li Fan was the refinement of [Dao Pills].

In the initial difficult years after the Immortal Ark split, the Immortal Ark cultivators, facing extreme material scarcity, had accumulated quite rich experience in cannibalism.

The prototype of the Dao Pill originated from that period.

Under conditions of limited resources, how to maximize absorption efficiency became what the Immortal Ark cultivators valued most at the time.

The Dao Pill, this secret art of refining cultivators into pills, was the ultimate technique summarized and perfected on the basis of the experiences from the dark period of the Immortal Ark’s history.

It not only refined the target cultivator’s memories and divine soul completely and made them one’s own.

Even their talent, temperament, and innate divine abilities could all be integrated into oneself!

Of course, the side effects were unavoidable.

The stronger the raw material used to refine the Dao Pill, the more pronounced the side effects would be.

If one’s own strength was insufficient and one forcibly consumed it, the body might not be able to handle the nourishing, and one could very likely suffer backlash, becoming neither human nor ghost.

In madness, one would no longer recognize even close relatives and engage in wanton slaughter.

Therefore, after the Immortal Ark gradually stabilized later, this Dao Pill secret art also became one of the forbidden techniques of the Immortal Ark.

Only the true core of the Immortal Ark was qualified to know it.

“It is quite complicated.”

Li Fan studied the entire refinement process in detail and frowned slightly.

The root of this secret art was still alchemy.

Alchemy was not Li Fan’s strength, but his formation skills had already reached the pinnacle.

The Final Dissociation Disc synchronously analyzed, disassembled, and converted each step into corresponding formations.

“As long as this formation is set up, fully automatic Dao Pill refinement can be achieved. It saves both effort and worry.”

“Although I no longer care much about innate divine abilities now, I have still mastered another special method.”

“It is also extremely useful for intimidating those so-called heaven’s chosen.”

“The only concern is whether this method will work at all in the Xuanhuang Realm.”

The two sections of the Star Sea not only have different time flows.

The Immortal Ark is exposed to the chaotic and disorderly Darkest Star Sea without the protection of a native world’s power.

Li Fan could not guarantee that the rules here would also apply in the Xuanhuang Realm.

“If there is an opportunity, I will just test it and find out.”

Besides the Dao Pill, the other thing that still caught Li Fan’s attention was a communication method that could reduce the interference from the remaining True Immortal power in the Star Sea to the lowest level.

It was created by the second head of the Celestial Pivot Court.

Its name was [Unbounded].

Its principle is to create a power with “resistance” attributes against True Immortal remnant forces, based on the Heavenly Dao attributes that still linger in the Star Sea.

However, the drawbacks are also obvious.

It requires knowing the other party’s exact space-time coordinates.

Moreover, due to the pervasive suction force in the Darkest Star Sea, this communication can often only be transmitted in one direction.

That is, following the direction of the suction force.

The Immortal Ark has almost no records of other survivors in the Darkest Star Sea.

Therefore, this Unbounded communication method is not valued at all.

But in Li Fan’s hands, it could serve some different purposes.

Outside the Immortal Ark, in the Sea of Relics.

Holy Embryo Li Fan, who was comprehending the Heavenly Dao of the Darkest Star Sea, suddenly opened his eyes.

A trace of playful expression flashed in his eyes. He immediately shot a silver light toward the direction of the Xuanhuang Realm that he sensed.

However, this was merely a momentary whim on Li Fan’s part.

The location of the Immortal Ark was simply too far from the Xuanhuang Realm, and it was also very close to the Immortal Ruins.

Li Fan actually had no confidence that this Unbounded message could successfully reach the Xuanhuang Realm.

He was just doing it for fun.

The Holy Embryo had gained quite a lot from the Immortal Ark. After all, it was the accumulation of an entire civilization.

Even if it was not the most top-tier part, it was still extremely precious to Li Fan.

The perspective returned to the Xuanhuang Realm.

Ever since the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi returned, Sun Luyuan had become exceptionally busy.

Although he had not visited every heaven’s chosen on the list, he had used his own connections to investigate their general situations.

Although Ou Shangtian had not urged him regarding the task of winning them over, Sun Luyuan deeply understood that this was his first assignment after joining Great Qi.

He not only had to succeed, but he had to do it beautifully, without any flaws that others could pick on, and in a way that would earn praise.

After careful selection, Sun Luyuan quickly decided on the target for his meritorious sacrifice.

That was the renowned Fairy Kongyou of the Immortal Alliance!

Fairy Kongyou’s cultivation was also at the Dao Integration realm.

But in the eyes of ordinary cultivators in the Immortal Alliance, Fairy Kongyou was far more important than Sun Luyuan.

Besides Fairy Kongyou herself being peerlessly beautiful in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, it was also because of her companion treasure, the [Yin-Yang Jade].

Fairy Kongyou could use the dual cultivation method of Yin and Yang to fuse the Yang half of the [Yin-Yang Jade] with her Dao companion. This would upgrade the Dao companion’s Foundation Establishment treasure, Golden Core, Nascent Soul Cave-Heaven, and even the life transformation phenomena itself.

It was enough to turn a mediocre person into a heaven’s prodigy in one leap.

It could also allow an unparalleled heaven’s prodigy to advance further and become an unprecedented supreme existence that shook the Xuanhuang Realm!

As early as when the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi existed, Sun Luyuan had heard that none of the several disciples under the Holy Emperor currently had Dao companions.

If he could deceive Fairy Kongyou over and arrange a match, not only would it make future recruitment efforts easier by borrowing her fame, but it would also create a favor owed to the Holy Emperor’s disciples.

Wouldn’t that achieve two goals with one move?

Sun Luyuan narrowed his eyes, and a plan had already formed in his mind.

Chapter 1230: Incorporating The Realm’S Evil Intent Into One’S Body

Fairy Kongyou had an extremely beautiful appearance, and there were many suitors pursuing her within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. The heaven-defying effects of the Yin-Yang Jade were enough to drive cultivators across the world mad.

In addition to her own special nature, Kongyou seemed to have another hidden background.

Among the upper echelons of the Immortal Alliance, it was secretly circulated that Kongyou was the exclusive favorite of a certain Dharma-Transmitter. Not only did she originate from a small world exclusive to Dharma-Transmitters, but even the half piece of the Heavenly Wonder within her body had been implanted, all for the purpose of nurturing and later harvesting it.

As the saying goes, there is no smoke without fire. Such rumors were not without basis.

Kongyou’s Spirit of Heaven and Earth for Dao Integration, Duskhue, was one of those raised and reserved by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. Kongyou had quietly taken it for her Dao Integration breakthrough, and the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had never made any statement about it.

Furthermore, more than thirty years ago, a Qin-named cultivator who had just advanced to Dao Integration fell deeply in love with Kongyou at first sight. After failing to win her through persistent pursuit, he actually plotted to use schemes and forcibly possess her. In the end, not only did he disappear, but even the once-prominent Qin family behind him completely vanished from the world, as if they had never existed.

Sun Luyuan did not feel any fear because of Kongyou’s possible special identity. On the contrary, it strengthened his determination to choose her as a sacrifice. At this moment, he came to his younger brother Sun Luyao’s place, wanting to use the power of the Tianxuan Mirror to lock onto her current location.

“Brother, if you do this, aren’t you afraid…” Sun Luyao was somewhat puzzled.

“The current Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance is still completely unaware of the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi. I am merely inviting the world-famous Fairy Kongyou to appreciate the scenery in a rather unique small world within the Immortal Alliance’s territory. What problem could there be?” Sun Luyuan smiled.

“A person like the Fairy Kongyou will absolutely not face any life-threatening danger within the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi. You can rest assured on this point.”

In fact, Sun Luyuan had some other small thoughts that he did not tell his younger brother.

Besides currying favor with the Holy Dynasty, he also wanted to use the power behind the Fairy Kongyou to conduct a probe into the strength of Great Qi. Although his previous trip to Great Qi had shown and told him of the Holy Dynasty’s power in every aspect, ultimately one still needed to see the real thing with one’s own hands.

“If we cannot even deal with a single Dharma-Transmitter, then how can we talk about contending with the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance?” Sun Luyuan said silently in his heart.

What Sun Luyuan did not know was that every one of his actions was under the watchful gaze of the Holy Emperor of Great Qi.

He thought he had acted covertly, but how could he possibly hide it from Li Fan, who had experienced countless cycles of reincarnation?

“Fairy Kongyou.” An extremely beautiful face instantly appeared before Holy Emperor Li Ping.

“Good. Several of my disciples are indeed no longer young. If she can give birth to some heavenly prodigies for my Great Qi, that would also be a joyous matter.”

As for the existence behind Kongyou, Li Ping indeed did not care much.

However, Sun Luyuan’s self-proclaimed clever behavior caused Li Ping to give him a special mark.

“Today’s actions will all receive their due retribution.”

Such a small matter was merely one of the countless scenes that flashed through the mind of the Faceless Holy Emperor seated upon the Holy Throne every day.

It could not stir even the slightest ripple in his heart.

Li Ping’s current main focus was still placed on the Nether Realm and the deduction of the new method.

Ever since he split off a portion of the You Clan people to fill the energy shortage of the Dark Sea, Li Ping felt that the degree of favor he received from the Xuanhuang Heaven was becoming higher and higher.

Not long after helping Tianyang achieve Dao Integration, the power of heaven and earth that he could borrow and mobilize had increased by dozens of times compared to before.

While grasping more authority, a certain inexplicable urging from the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao also appeared more and more frequently in Li Ping’s mind.

This body was tied to the future of billions upon billions of citizens of Great Qi, so Li Ping naturally would not act rashly.

Scenes from the Heavenly Dao’s perception flashed before his eyes.

A desolate Gobi desert landscape. Everything in sight was a hazy gray.

A towering stone mountain that seemed to connect heaven and earth stood in the center of the scene.

At the top of the stone mountain sat an old man.

Behind the old man was a coffin. Although it was only an perception scene, Li Ping could still sense an aura of decay and evil from that coffin.

It even made Li Ping feel slightly uncomfortable.

“The sealed evil thoughts of the Xuanhuang Realm.”

Li Ping naturally knew the reason why the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao kept urging him.

A part of itself had been sealed away, causing the Heavenly Dao’s strength to be greatly reduced. As a result, after the great calamity of “Techniques Cannot Be Cultivated Together” several thousand years ago, it had never again released any means targeted at cultivators.

If this lost portion of power could be recovered, perhaps the situation would take a turn.

But…

Even across a distance of billions of miles, Li Ping seemed able to sense the unsettling existence beneath the coffin lid.

“If this portion of evil thoughts is allowed to return, after being sealed by cultivators for so many years and suppressed for so long, the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao will definitely undergo extreme changes. This may not be a good thing for human cultivators or for the Xuanhuang Realm itself.”

“However, replenishing the strength of the Xuanhuang Realm’s Heavenly Dao does indeed hold such value.”

Almost in an instant, Holy Emperor Li Ping made his decision in his heart.

He would first absorb this portion of evil thoughts.

When he later reshaped the universe and returned to the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, this portion of power would naturally return to the Xuanhuang Realm as well.

Before that, whether this power was held in his hands or in the hands of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao…

In Li Ping’s view, there was no difference.

With Li Ping’s current strength, there was still some risk in trying to tame the Xuanhuang evil thoughts. Especially the cultivator named Xu Ke who guarded the Xuanhuang evil thoughts could not be judged by ordinary reasoning.

Therefore, before making full preparations, Li Ping would not act lightly.

He simply turned a blind eye to the urging from the Xuanhuang Realm.

When dealing with the power of the world, there was actually a worthy example to learn from within the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi.

Yin Shangren.

As a remnant soul incarnation from another immortal cultivation world, Yin Shangren had rich experience in struggling against world consciousness.

However, there seemed to be some taboo involved in this.

Li Ping had sent inquiries several times, but Yin Shangren was unusually tight-lipped and gave no response.

Fortunately, he was indeed busy researching possible breakthrough directions for the new method, so Li Ping did not hold it against him.

“The will of the world…”

Without Yin Shangren’s help, if Li Ping wanted to find a way to deal with it, he could only rely on himself.

In his memory, the Xuanhuang Realm still had quite a few fragments from other immortal cultivation worlds that retained their own consciousness.

Perhaps contacting them could yield some gains.

Thinking this, Li Ping’s figure flashed and arrived outside the entrance to the Great Qi small world, in the Congyun Sea.

At first glance, he saw the Spirit of Heaven and Earth that was about to be nurtured between heaven and earth — Crimson Flame.

It seemed to have also sensed Li Ping’s presence. Crimson Flame, still in the process of forming, transmitted a feeling of doubt.

Li Ping did not interfere with Crimson Flame’s process of burning the sea.

He knew this was an indispensable process that fragments from other worlds needed to undergo in order to truly integrate into the Xuanhuang Realm.

Next

Chapter 1231: Formation Intent Devours Congyun

Li Ping stood quietly, feeling the unyielding will of the vast ocean beneath his feet.

Facing a life-and-death crisis, any existence would struggle with all its might.

Li Ping also sensed the intense will to survive from this ocean.

But its struggle for survival was, for the Xuanhuang Realm, nothing but naked “evil intent.”

Li Ping regarded himself as the incarnation of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, so he naturally could not stand on the side of the Congyun Sea.

“Since you have come, submit obediently.”

“There is no need to continue with pointless resistance.”

With a single thought, his divine sense enveloped the entire Congyun Ocean.

The scenes on the tens of thousands of scattered islands, as well as the countless living beings in the seawater, instantly appeared in Li Ping’s mind.

Li Ping remained silent for a moment.

“This is a necessary sacrifice.”

A trace of determination flashed in his eyes. Strands of golden light surged from his body and scattered across the Congyun Sea.

The power that approached the essence of source power was undetectable by anyone in the Congyun Sea, where the strongest cultivators were only at the Soul Transformation stage.

In just a few breaths, the golden light spread throughout every region of the Congyun Sea. It pierced deep into the ocean depths, reaching all the way to the seabed.

Intense tremors, unknown to others, began to occur at the bottom of the Congyun Sea.

If viewed from the sky, the entire ocean surface appeared to form a giant net.

It completely enveloped this blue, vibrant sea.

Dark clouds suddenly gathered in the sky.

The originally calm sea surface suddenly surged with terrifying waves.

The waves were even more violent than during previous wind disasters.

Some islands capsized without warning, and even the spiritual islands guarded by cultivators were in imminent danger amid the fierce winds and heavy rain.

The cultivators of the Congyun Sea all watched the abnormal changes between heaven and earth with uncertainty.

Some cultivators with sharp spiritual senses vaguely felt a great calamity approaching. Their expressions changed at once, and without caring about anything else, they directly escaped through the teleportation formation on Ten Thousand Immortal Island.

Their departure drew the attention of a considerable number of people. A small portion of cultivators chose to follow and leave.

However, the vast majority looked down on these “panicky, timid as mice” escapees with disdain.

They stayed comfortably on Ten Thousand Immortal Island, watching the scene of the changing winds and clouds over the Congyun Sea.

Li Ping paid no attention to those fleeing cultivators, nor did he care about these onlookers.

His target, from beginning to end, was only the remnant will of the Congyun Sea.

The golden giant net, invisible to ordinary cultivators, had already sealed off the Congyun Sea.

The energy of source power essence accumulated and evolved within it. A grand formation was about to reveal its might.

The consciousness of the Congyun Sea clearly felt the increasingly tight binding of the golden giant net, causing the wind and waves it stirred to become even more violent.

Yet in Li Ping’s view, it was nothing but impotent rage.

“How exactly does one erase a world consciousness?”

Li Ping continued constructing the formation while pondering this question.

“I refine the gods from all living beings.”

He quickly thought of this peerless vicious formation.

“A formation capable of blocking even True Immortals for a moment or two should have no major problem dealing with a remnant world will.”

“Countless meaningless distracting thoughts will impact it…”

As Li Ping set up the formation, strange and bizarre scenes began to appear inexplicably in the sky above the Congyun Sea.

They were twisted, despairing faces.

They howled and waved their arms.

As if begging for help, or perhaps merely screaming unconsciously.

More and more eerie faces emerged on the sky curtain.

They were densely packed, almost filling every corner of one’s field of vision.

The ears of all living beings within the Congyun Sea gradually heard nothing but the wailing of these faces.

“Ah!”

Those with weak wills were quickly influenced by these demonic faces and became equally despairing and mad.

Those who barely held on soon fell into boundless terrifying nightmares.

They vaguely seemed to see a black sun rising in the sky and the great terror of the entire world on the verge of destruction.

Just like the despairing demonic faces in the sky, they experienced this unimaginable catastrophe together.

The teleportation formation on Ten Thousand Immortal Island remained open.

But all cultivators who saw the thousands upon thousands of faces in the sky lost the ability to control their own thoughts.

Regardless of cultivation level, from minor Qi Condensation cultivators to Soul Transformation island masters.

All were the same.

Although their minds were filled with boundless despair, they could not even generate the thought of wanting to escape.

It should be noted that the formation Li Ping had laid down was not targeted at these cultivators.

They were merely affected by the spillover, yet they had already reached such a state. One could imagine how cruel the scene facing the Congyun Sea’s consciousness was at this moment.

The remnant World Will of a single prefecture fundamentally could not resist it.

Amid the continuous howls of hundreds of millions of demonic faces, Li Ping sensed that the Will of the Congyun Sea was dissipating.

The towering giant waves were slowly calming.

It seemed as though everything would pass and the sky would clear after the rain.

But Li Fan knew that from now on, the Congyun Sea would no longer be the same as before.

The process of erasing the Congyun Sea’s consciousness lasted a full day.

During this period, any cultivator who entered the range of the Congyun Sea was affected and fell into terrifying illusions.

However, there was no mass simultaneous fall of living beings, so the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had not yet had time to react.

The dark clouds in the sky dispersed, and the Congyun Sea became clear and blue once more.

The silence was somewhat terrifying.

Li Ping sensed the scattered will of the Congyun Sea between heaven and earth.

The golden giant net slowly retracted, carrying the world remnant thoughts that had already been torn into thousands of pieces back into his body.

“Ugh…”

As Li Ping absorbed the consciousness of Congyun, countless images began flashing continuously in his mind.

Even though he was already accustomed to constantly monitoring every single event within the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi.

The total volume of information between the two was simply not on the same level.

The vast ocean did not contain only human existence.

Beneath the calm sea surface, billions upon billions of living beings were breeding and breathing.

From Li Ping’s subjective perspective, he would certainly not pay much attention to these creatures that lacked even wisdom.

But at this moment, he was passively absorbing them.

In the view of World Will, these fish were actually no different in essence from human cultivators.

Li Ping endured the intense pain in his sea of consciousness and accepted all this information.

As if he had swallowed a great tonic, the strength of his divine soul also inflated like a balloon.

Li Ping’s divine soul was already sufficiently tough among cultivators.

But in this rapid expansion, cracks inevitably appeared.

Ordinary cultivators facing such a situation would have no choice but to despair and wait for their soul to scatter.

But Li Ping was different.

Bearing the Heavenly Emperor Qi Canon, he not only used the hundreds of millions of citizens of the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi as anchors.

He also possessed transcendent source power essence within his body.

Golden energy surged, repairing the wounds in his divine soul.

At the same time, it served as a supplement for the expansion of his divine soul.

After the initial dangerous phase passed, Li Ping slowly adapted.

Chapter 1232: Qingfeng Has No Place

When the pain subsided, what followed was the rapid expansion of the divine soul.

The will of the world, even if only the remnants of a single prefecture, was absolutely a great tonic for cultivators.

In the past, when Holy Emperor Li Ping supervised and monitored various affairs within Great Qi, he still received and handled countless trivial matters from a “human” perspective. But from now on, Li Ping, who had absorbed the consciousness of Congyun Sea, might also be able to take in every detail of the Holy Dynasty from the overlooking perspective of “the world itself.”

After the consciousness of Congyun Sea perished, there was no wind for seven days.

Li Ping stood motionless between sea and sky, waiting for the fusion to complete fully.

Even without deliberate effort, Li Ping, who had devoured the consciousness of Congyun Sea, could still effortlessly perceive every event occurring within Congyun Sea.

He saw those cultivators affected by the demonic faces. After he withdrew his divine ability, the vast majority still had not recovered from their madness. After this calamity, not one in a hundred cultivators remained normal.

He saw the Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Crimson Flame, brewing in the sky. Because of the demise of Congyun Sea’s consciousness, there was no longer any need for it to descend and burn the sea. The condensed power of laws quietly dissipated.

He saw Cloudwater Heavenly Palace in the center of Congyun Sea, whose restrictions had loosened and was about to reemerge from its seal. After flashing with a mysterious blue glow, it fell into silence once more.

He also saw that the anomaly in Congyun Sea had drawn the attention of certain existences in the Xuanhuang Realm. Several massive divine senses descended instantly and swept over this ocean. But at this moment, Li Ping had replaced Congyun Sea’s consciousness and perfectly blended into the heavens and earth. Combined with his own formations and the concealment of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, Li Ping was not discovered.

……

Although he had temporarily concealed it, the vague sense of crisis in Li Ping’s heart did not disappear.

“It seems this is not a place to linger for long.” Li Ping thought inwardly after completely absorbing and digesting the consciousness of Congyun Sea.

However, before returning to the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi, he still had one thing to do.

His figure flickered, and in an instant Li Ping arrived at the seabed near Liuyun Island in Congyun Sea.

In the deep crevice of the seabed here, there existed a peculiar vortex.

At the center of the vortex, a pale blue bead emitted a faint glow.

“The Canghai Pearl.”

As part of Congyun Sea’s consciousness hedging its bets, the Canghai Pearl was the final path of survival that Congyun Sea had prepared for itself.

Li Ping reached out, and the pale blue bead flew out from the seabed vortex into his palm.

“Since it has already become part of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, it should be completely severed from the past.”

Golden energy enveloped the Canghai Pearl, then poured in fiercely.

After the light dissipated, the bead in Li Ping’s hand had changed from pale blue to a deep azure.

Yet it no longer possessed its previous liveliness.

It was because Li Ping had completely erased the last faint remnant of Congyun Sea’s will within the Canghai Pearl.

“From now on, the Canghai Pearl is simply the Canghai Pearl and has no connection to Congyun Sea.”

Li Ping did not store the pearl away. Instead, he flicked his finger and shot it randomly toward a corner of Congyun Sea, to await one with destiny.

All cultivators of an entire prefecture had turned into idiots.

When the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance finally noticed the anomaly in Congyun Sea, they were furious.

While sending people to investigate, how to handle these dangerous mad cultivators also became a difficult problem.

In the end, it took five Dao Integration cultivators working together to barely resolve the chaos in Congyun Sea.

Among these five was none other than Sun Luyuan, the head of the Sun family.

Previously, he had exhausted various methods and finally contacted the Fairy Kongyou.

Sun Luyuan had thoroughly investigated Fairy Kongyou’s background beforehand. He knew her temperament was simple, with emotions clearly shown on her face, and she was particularly curious about novel things. Therefore, Sun Luyuan fabricated a half-true, half-false lie.

“I heard before that Fairy, you once turned an ungrateful male cultivator into a dog and tormented him day and night?”

“That scene was truly unbearable to witness. As it happens, when I was traveling in a certain small world before, I encountered a similar scene. It also involved transforming criminals into the form of exotic beasts as a means of punishment...”

Sun Luyuan selectively recounted some of what he had seen and heard within the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi.

“The emperor of this small world actually had the same idea as me? Interesting.”

“And from your description, the level of development in this small world is quite extraordinary...”

After mentioning it a few times casually, Fairy Kongyou was indeed intrigued.

She agreed that if the opportunity arose, she would go sightseeing together with a few other familiar people from the alliance.

Seeing that the matter was settled, Sun Luyuan was overjoyed.

He even thought about using Fairy Kongyou’s name this time to lure more outstanding talents from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance over.

Who could have expected that before the plan could be implemented, a major tragedy struck the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. The deception plan had to be temporarily suspended.

Originally, the messy situation in Congyun Sea had nothing to do with him.

However, the chief of the Guardian Hall, Xu Yuanxian, had privately given him a task.

Only then did Sun Luyuan have no choice but to make the long and arduous trip.

“Clear Wind Hall. This is the place.”

After subduing and gathering all the mad cultivators who attacked anyone they saw, Sun Luyuan arrived at this building on Ten Thousand Immortal Island.

He did not sense the aura of any living beings inside.

But when Sun Luyuan truly walked in and saw the internal conditions clearly, his pupils suddenly shrank.

An old man lying on a wooden chair had tree roots growing from all over his body, tightly entwining and fixing him to the ground.

Although the old man had not completely died and still had a trace of breath left, in Sun Luyuan’s eyes, he was no different from a dead man.

It was as if his divine soul had been completely devoured by these strange roots, leaving only an empty shell.

“This is the guard of Clear Wind Hall that Elder Xu mentioned. He had at least Nascent Soul cultivation, yet he died so unclearly.”

Sun Luyuan shook his head slightly.

His gaze left the old man and swept through the interior of Clear Wind Hall.

Moments later, he discovered a hidden entrance.

Raising his vigilance, Sun Luyuan strode through the faintly visible green gap.

A somewhat spacious area.

“Thump! Thump! Thump...”

The sound of the ground trembling continued to come.

Following the source of the sound, Sun Luyuan looked over and saw a large tree that had grown human-like feet.

It was running across the ground.

In the center of the tree trunk, there was clearly an extremely aged face.

At this moment, that tree face was filled with endless terror.

This large tree was running frantically at high speed. Even its two feet were not enough to support its body, causing it to fall and get up repeatedly. Yet it never stopped its running.

It muttered to itself: “Once again, a calamity has come. This terrifying scene was never even seen in my father’s most vicious curses!”

“But it has never happened at all!”

“It is not fake, not fake!”

……

The tree man rambled incoherently, its words contradictory and completely impossible to understand.

Yet it remained deeply trapped in its own delusions. Even when Sun Luyuan tried to communicate with it, it was of no use.

“Ah Liu has gone mad.”

At this moment, a crisp female voice suddenly sounded beside Sun Luyuan’s ear.

“Hmm?” Sun Luyuan was startled.

Because he had already searched thoroughly earlier, and besides the tree person, there seemed to be no one else here.

But immediately, Sun Luyuan thought of the task entrusted by Elder Xu this time.

He promptly asked: “Is it Fellow Daoist Qingfeng?”

The female voice did not respond.

“I was entrusted by Elder Xu to bring you back.”

“According to him, this spirit wood has gone mad, so the passage within its body cannot be used, and Fellow Daoist Qingfeng is trapped here.”

As Sun Luyuan spoke, he carefully observed the surroundings.

He wanted to confirm the location of this Qingfeng.

But even with his Dao Integration realm observation, he still gained no clues at all.

“Ah Liu has gone mad.” The female voice still did not respond directly, only repeating this several times.

Sun Luyuan could not help but frown slightly. Could it be that this female cultivator had also been affected by the demonic faces and gone mad?

“Ah Liu and I had the best relationship. Now that it has gone mad, who will play with me in the future?”

The faint voice echoed in this not-too-large space.

Overlapping with the tree person Ah Liu’s occasional wails, it sent chills down one’s spine.

For some reason, Sun Luyuan felt the same eerie sensation in his heart as when he had wandered in the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi before.

“Fellow Daoist Qingfeng...” Sun Luyuan forced himself to cough lightly.

Although he did not know the relationship between this female cultivator named Qingfeng and Elder Xu.

But for the usually rigid and merciless Xu Yuanxian to make an exception and privately seek him out was enough to show that their relationship must be extraordinary.

Fortunately, things did not develop as Sun Luyuan had anticipated.

Qingfeng seemed to sob softly for a while, then finally emerged from her grief.

“So you were sent by little Xu. So he has not forgotten me.”

Qingfeng’s simple words instantly made Sun Luyuan’s hair stand on end.

“Little Xu? Xu Yuanxian?”

Sun Luyuan almost suspected he had misheard.

Elder Xu had served as chief of the Guardian Hall for over three thousand years; he was a genuine old monster.

Yet this girl, whose voice sounded very young, called him little Xu...

If she had not gone mad, then how old could she possibly be?

What exactly was the identity of this one called Qingfeng?

Countless thoughts flashed through Sun Luyuan’s mind, but he did not dare think deeply. His expression became restrained.

He no longer dared to address her as “Fellow Daoist Qingfeng” and cautiously changed his tone to ask: “Senior, why do you not show yourself? Is there any place where this junior has done something wrong?”

After a moment of silence in the space.

Qingfeng’s somewhat strange voice sounded behind Sun Luyuan.

“Have I not always been here?”

“Oh, right. I forgot. Ordinary people cannot see me.”

Qingfeng’s words made Sun Luyuan break out in even more cold sweat.

“Elder Xu said that he will send someone later to handle this mad spiritwood. Please, Senior, come with me for now.” Sun Luyuan said hastily.

“Ah! That is wonderful. I knew Ah Liu could still be saved.” Qingfeng, like a simple little girl, suddenly became cheerful.

“It is all because that cataclysmic wailing scene was too terrifying and scared it silly for the time being. If you had not come, it would have been a long time before anyone spoke to me again.”

Qingfeng’s voice drew closer and closer to Sun Luyuan.

Finally, it seemed to stop on his back.

“Fine then. I will listen to little Xu and leave here for the time being.”

“The aura here has changed; it does not feel very comfortable anymore.”

Feeling the seemingly real weight on his back, Sun Luyuan’s heart pounded wildly as he wiped the cold sweat from his forehead.

“Can this junior just leave directly?” he asked tentatively.

“Yes, I am already lying on your back.”

Qingfeng’s words confirmed Sun Luyuan’s guess.

Sun Luyuan did not dare linger and strode out of this eerie space.

The sound of the mad spiritwood continued to echo here without stopping.

“Shall we return to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters?” In Congyun Sea, Sun Luyuan, transformed into a streak of light and flying rapidly, asked again.

“Not there. That place does not feel comfortable. We are going to Luoyan Prefecture.”

Qingfeng’s mood seemed to have improved; she spoke in a somewhat cheerful tone.

“Luoyan Prefecture?” Sun Luyuan nodded.

Elder Xu’s task was merely to bring Qingfeng out and settle her in the Clear Wind Hall of another prefecture.

Sun Luyuan did not dwell on it and headed toward Luoyan Prefecture.

Along the way, he also pondered the true identity of the one on his back.

With the Immortal Artifact Boundless Mirror in his family, Sun Luyuan considered himself quite experienced and knowledgeable.

Yet he had never heard of any similar existence to this bizarre Qingfeng.

He could not even gauge Qingfeng’s strength.

Had Elder Xu made him travel thousands of miles just because he was worried she was trapped?

From this perspective, Qingfeng’s strength could not be very high.

But Qingfeng’s strange wording and various eerie behaviors made Sun Luyuan’s heart numb with unease.

Clearly, this formless and shapeless existence called Qingfeng was by no means simple.

Sun Luyuan complained bitterly to himself and quickened his speed.

He only wanted to complete the task quickly and get rid of her.

Qingfeng remained silent the whole way.

And he drew closer and closer to Luoyan Prefecture.

Just as relief seemed in sight, the next words from Qingfeng suddenly made Sun Luyuan feel as if he had fallen into an ice cave.

“You seem a bit impatient.”

“Indeed, there are not many in the world who are good to me.”

Sun Luyuan felt a chill rise from his heart.

Although Qingfeng said nothing more afterward, his sense of unease grew stronger and stronger.

“Something feels off.”

Sun Luyuan knew some change was happening to his body.

But he could not detect the specific details of the change.

It was not until Sun Luyuan entered Luoyan Prefecture and casually glanced downward that he suddenly realized with alarm.

At this moment, he was very close to the ground.

A blazing sun shone fiercely overhead.

Yet strangely, there was no shadow of his on the ground.

Sun Luyuan knew very clearly that he had not cast any spell to conceal his figure.

But his shadow had disappeared.

Somewhat dazed, he extended both arms.

He seemed to be becoming illusory.

His existence was fading away.

He was about to become like Qingfeng: formless and shapeless.

The change came too suddenly for Sun Luyuan to even feel fear in his heart.

His mind was simply blank.

Just as Sun Luyuan felt his connection to this world growing fainter and fainter...

A blazing golden light suddenly surged out from within his body.

Next

Chapter 1233: The Heavenly Dao Bestows Great Power

The perspective returned to a short while earlier.

After devouring the remaining world's will of the Congyun Sea and returning to the Great Qi small world, the Faceless Holy Emperor finally gained a clearer understanding of the changes occurring within himself.

In the past, he needed to focus his full attention on the numerous trivial matters within the Holy Dynasty. The spare time left for his own thoughts was not much.

But now, without even using one-third of his strength, Li Ping could already handle all matters, big and small, within the Holy Dynasty with great ease.

Besides a pure increase in the quantity of his divine soul, Li Ping’s senses also seemed to have become sharper.

He looked down upon this world and discovered the nascent, ignorant consciousness of the small world that was being nurtured due to the world’s ascension. Previously, the Great Qi small world had only operated spontaneously according to instinct and did not show the obvious “personalized” expressions like the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao. However, because Li Ping had continuously transformed the Great Qi small world, it had already met the basic conditions for birthing self-awareness.

The birth of world's will is a rather lengthy process. According to Li Ping’s estimate, at the current normal speed, it would likely require at least a thousand years.

However, if Li Ping continued to promote the ascension of Great Qi, this time would be shortened accordingly.

The Great Qi small world had reached its current state entirely due to Li Ping’s transformations. Moreover, the fortune of the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi had merged with Li Ping into one, and the rising, unstoppable powerful fortune also exerted various subtle influences on the small world.

All were cultivators, born and raised by heaven and earth. But in Li Ping’s case, the current Great Qi small world seemed more like his own creation. The grace of recreation was like that of heaven and earth as parents…

Sitting upright on the Holy Emperor’s throne, all kinds of profound insights continuously emerged in his mind.

These were not only from his own thoughts but also aided by this world.

Li Ping felt that he seemed to have grasped some extremely critical information.

However, at this moment, his thoughts were suddenly disrupted by a warning in his heart.

“Hmm?”

After a quick check, Li Ping found that within the web of source power essence he had laid out, one of the prey actually showed signs of breaking free.

Strands of peculiar fluctuations emanated from that person, as if trying to escape the restraint of the golden giant net.

“Sun Luyuan?”

Li Ping still had some impression of this Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance Dao Integration cultivator who had recently expressed his intention to pledge allegiance to the Holy Dynasty.

“Could it be that the people from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance have discovered and taken action?” Li Ping could not help but think.

But soon, he sensed what was happening to Sun Luyuan.

“Huh?”

Li Ping’s heart instantly became somewhat grave.

Using the source power essence within Sun Luyuan’s body, Li Ping instantly arrived by his side.

He stretched out his hand and placed it on Sun Luyuan’s shoulder. Golden, surging power continuously poured into his body.

Not long after, Sun Luyuan’s body, which had been slowly turning somewhat transparent, solidified once more.

“Thank you, Holy Emperor, for saving my life.” Having realized what had just happened, Sun Luyuan spoke with lingering fear, his face full of lingering terror and gratitude.

Li Ping slowly withdrew his hand, but his perception still did not leave Sun Luyuan’s back.

He could not directly observe the existence named Qingfeng.

Even the influence of Qingfeng’s insights on the outside world and heavens could not be used.

“You are quite interesting.”

If not for Qingfeng’s voice happening to sound at that moment, Li Ping would have almost thought she was Sun Luyuan’s illusion.

It was hard to believe that there were still things in the Xuanhuang Realm that he, Li Ping, could not observe.

“What exactly are you?”

There was no insult in his words; Li Ping instead asked somewhat solemnly.

Sun Luyuan lowered his head and completely hid the expression on his face.

Unexpectedly, Qingfeng did not get angry: “I want to know that myself.”

“It is just a pity that I have forgotten.” She said with some regret.

“My memory is a bit poor. Except for Little Xu, Ah Liu, and a few others, my impressions of the rest are not very deep…”

“What is your name?” Qingfeng asked.

“Li Ping.” Li Ping replied concisely.

He sensed an aura on Qingfeng similar to that of Yin Shangren, yet subtly different.

“Perhaps I can help you recall it.” Intuition told him that the secrets on Qingfeng might prove useful in the future war against the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. Thus, he offered proactively.

“Hee hee, I feel like you don’t have good intentions.”

Unexpectedly, Qingfeng saw through his thoughts with a single sentence.

“Sure enough, there are not many people in this world who truly mean me well.” Qingfeng’s tone once again turned resentful and low.

The moment Li Ping heard these words, golden source power surged forth spontaneously within his body.

This was a sign that his passive body protection had been triggered.

Since Qingfeng had chosen to act, Li Ping naturally would not be polite.

Behind him, the majestic phantom of the Holy Dynasty suddenly emerged.

Endless golden light flew out from the Holy Dynasty’s image.

It formed a giant hand that directly seized the position where Qingfeng was located.

Li Ping thought that since he could not observe the other party, he might as well directly seal and take away the space where Qingfeng resided, then bring it back for slow study.

But this extremely unpredictable Qingfeng was not something that could be subdued by such a simple method.

The golden giant hand returned to the Holy Dynasty phantom, leaving behind only a black void. That was proof that a small piece of space had completely disappeared.

Yet Qingfeng’s voice still came from that void.

“You want to take me back with you?”

“Absurd.” In response to Qingfeng, the Faceless Holy Emperor let out a cold snort.

On Li Ping’s originally featureless face, faint and blurry facial features vaguely manifested.

But they did not look like those of an ordinary human. They resembled the evolution of different energies.

Especially those two eyes, silver-white vortices as brilliant as the starry river in the sky.

“The power of heaven and earth, descend upon my body.”

Li Ping’s majestic voice resounded like thunder.

The sky above Luoyan Prefecture instantly changed color because of these words.

“If you want to drag me down, it depends on whether you have the weight to do so!”

Sensing his extremely close connection with the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, Li Ping then released the protection of his golden source power and allowed Qingfeng’s strange power to swallow him.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Bolts of lightning flashed within the dense dark clouds, and the earth trembled slightly.

The color of Li Ping’s body faded for a brief moment.

But it quickly returned to normal.

On the contrary, something was actually pulled out from Qingfeng’s side: some blurry images!

“Found you!”

Two beams of light shot out from Li Ping’s vortex-like eyes.

They were like chains that locked Qingfeng in place.

“Come out for me!”

Li Ping shouted violently. The golden giant hand appeared once more.

It grabbed the silver chains and pulled fiercely.

The small black spatial cavity that had been slowly healing suddenly showed signs of violently collapsing outward.

Chapter 1234: Ink Death Erases Traces Of Shadow

As the space in Luoyan Prefecture of the Xuanhuang Realm continued to collapse, Qingfeng was gradually pulled out and revealed her true form.

It was clearly a willow tree with branches and leaves fluttering in the wind!

However, Qingfeng was obviously not transformed from an ordinary tree. At first glance it looked like a willow, but upon closer inspection, it had fused the characteristics of many other trees.

After pulling out most of Qingfeng’s true body, the silver chains stopped advancing and remained suspended in mid-air.

It was as if they had encountered enormous resistance.

Despite appearing so disheveled on the outside, Qingfeng showed no sign of pain for some reason.

She simply kept murmuring to herself, as if Li Ping’s actions had truly stirred up some memories.

“This place is…”

“What exactly has happened all these years?”

“Am I Qingfeng? No, no, that is the name Xuyuan gave me. My original name was…”

“It was…”

“Tian…”

“Tianyin?!”

Another silver glow flew out from within the willow tree.

It shot straight toward Li Ping.

The silver glow was extremely fast. Almost the instant it appeared, it had already reached Li Ping’s face.

It aimed directly at his brain, at the location of his sea of consciousness.

The automatic protection of the golden source power essence was equally unimaginably fast.

A layer of protective shield appeared in front of Li Fan and collided violently with the silver glow.

There was no earth-shattering explosion, only the intense mutual erasure of their lights.

“Run?”

At the same time, Li Ping vaguely sensed a message transmitted by the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao.

It was actually a warning, urging him to escape quickly.

Li Ping’s heart sank. He glanced forward.

Under the grinding of the golden source power essence, the brilliant silver glow gradually revealed its true appearance.

It was an ordinary-looking silver needle.

Although the light covering it was slowly fading, the sense of threat it brought to Li Ping did not weaken in the slightest.

“At this moment, with the power of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao upon me, I can already be considered half a step into the Longevity Immortal realm.”

“Yet I am actually locked in a stalemate with a tiny silver needle…”

There was no frustration in Li Ping’s heart, only a trace of anger.

Along with this silver needle, an old and kindly face appeared in Li Ping’s mind.

“It is indeed inseparable from the people of the Heavenly Law Realm.”

“Causing chaos in the Xuanhuang Realm, such a crime deserves death!”

Killing intent surged continuously in Li Ping’s heart.

However, he also knew that he was not yet a match for Heavenly Doctor. He could only withdraw for the time being.

The silver chains held tightly in his hand suddenly loosened. Qingfeng, or rather Tianyin, slowly became transparent again.

“Go.”

At this moment, Li Ping could no longer concern himself with her.

While resisting the silver needle’s attack, he prepared to leave Luoyan Prefecture.

Even in this critical moment, he did not forget to take the completely stunned Sun Luyuan with him.

Wrapped in golden source power, Li Ping’s figure also slowly faded.

The Heavenly Doctor silver needle did not pursue. Instead, it returned to Tianyin’s body and suppressed her into the formless and shapeless state.

Before escaping, Li Ping vaguely heard a sigh coming from the air.

“Tianyin drifts faintly, to whose home does it fall…”

“Tianyin drifts faintly, to whose home does it fall.”

After Li Ping completely disappeared from Luoyan Prefecture, the abnormal movements between heaven and earth did not diminish.

Instead, they grew even more intense.

Bolts of lightning struck down madly. The raging thunderstorms resembled the end of the world.

Or rather, the true end of the world had arrived.

Because a pitch-black figure like ink had already descended upon Luoyan Prefecture.

If Li Fan were here, he would definitely recognize this familiar existence at a glance.

It was the Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death!

As the ultimate world-destroying method of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, it decisively descended after Li Ping left.

Ink Death first glanced in the direction where Li Ping had departed.

Then it split from one into ten thousand, and from ten thousand into billions.

Without any hesitation, it skillfully transformed into endless ink.

It intended to completely erase Luoyan Prefecture.

Ink Death had descended, and this time the speed of its spread was much faster than the Ink Death Sea Destruction that Li Fan had experienced before.

The cultivators of Luoyan Prefecture had no time to resist or escape.

In just the time it takes to drink a cup of tea, Luoyan Prefecture, which had been somewhat desolate yet still possessed signs of life, had completely turned into a void.

The sudden appearance of Ink Death naturally alarmed many hidden experts within the Xuanhuang Realm.

But at this moment, Luoyan Prefecture no longer existed.

Even if they wanted to search for any clues, they were powerless to do so.

The impact of Ink Death abruptly extinguishing an entire prefecture had only just begun.

Holy Dynasty Great Qi, within the Holy Emperor’s seat.

Li Ping sat upright. The remaining power of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao on his body had already informed him of what had happened in Luoyan Prefecture.

He knew this was an action taken by the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao to prevent him from being probed or tracked by Heavenly Doctor.

Moreover, Luoyan Prefecture had originally been a fragment of the Heavenly Law Realm, so the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao had acted very decisively in cleansing it.

“That woman is named Tianyin?”

“She possesses the ability to peer into people’s hearts, just like Heavenly Doctor.”

“She is formless and shapeless, hidden within the Xuanhuang Realm. Besides her own abilities, it is also likely due to the support of Heavenly Doctor’s silver needle.”

“Although the exact purpose is unknown, for the Xuanhuang Realm itself, it is definitely not a good thing.”

Although this contact with Heavenly Doctor had ended in failure, it had at least allowed Li Ping to understand the gap between himself and Heavenly Doctor.

“The Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor is even stronger.”

“It seems the road I must walk is still very long.”

“However, the power of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao I borrowed this time was limited. After all, I had only intended to subdue that woman. If it had truly come to a life-and-death struggle, the support from the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao could have increased dozens of times more.”

“My Holy Dynasty Great Qi still has unlimited prospects.”

“Moreover, the Fishing Pond is a supreme treasure. It may bring me some surprises from beyond the High Walls.”

Self-pity was not Li Ping’s style.

After a brief failure, he immediately began to summarize and seek solutions.

“But this alone is still not enough.”

Li Ping naturally thought again of the sealed evil thoughts of the Xuanhuang Realm.

This time, after devouring the remnants in the Congyun Sea, he had gained a clearer understanding of the power of the world’s will.

“If I can devour the Xuanhuang evil thoughts…”

“Once the power of Xuanhuang returns to completeness, I will undergo another qualitative change.”

“It seems this matter must be put on the agenda.”

...

While Li Ping was deep in thought, Sun Luyuan was trembling all over, hiding beneath the Holy Emperor’s seat and not daring to move.

Everything he had just witnessed, whether it was the Holy Emperor before him, Tianyin, or that silver needle had left Sun Luyuan deeply shocked.

He was clearly also a Dao Integration cultivator, yet in front of them he was like a child, without the slightest power to resist.

“What do you think of that woman named Tianyin?”

After a long time, the voice of the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor entered Sun Luyuan’s ears.

Sun Luyuan shivered and showed a bitter expression.
