Chapter 1235: Qing And Hong Meets Xu Ke

“This… this…” Sun Luyuan stammered for a long time, yet could not utter a complete sentence.

Fortunately, Li Ping did not make things difficult for him.

With a wave of his hand, he sent a beam of golden light flying into Sun Luyuan’s body. Li Ping said indifferently: “From now on, you probably cannot return to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. Stay in our Holy Dynasty for the time being.”

“After some time, I will arrange for you to return to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance under a different identity.”

Sun Luyuan raised his head in a daze and looked at Li Ping.

At this moment, Li Ping showed him the scene of Ink Death destroying the land in Luoyan Prefecture.

While endless fear surged in Sun Luyuan’s heart once more, he immediately understood the purpose of that golden light.

It had clearly erased the imprint left by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance in his body.

“That Tianyin involves too many secrets. If we do not completely erase an entire prefecture, it will inevitably allow others to trace the clues and track us down.” Li Ping explained.

“Then our Sun family…” Sun Luyuan suddenly thought of something, and his expression changed drastically.

“That will depend on your luck. If Tianyin tells the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance what happened to her, the Sun family will definitely be implicated. The entire clan will vanish from the world without a trace, and no one will be spared.”

Li Ping’s words turned Sun Luyuan’s face deathly pale.

But then his tone shifted: “However, you need not worry too much. Tianyin is formless and intangible. If she herself is unwilling to appear, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance probably cannot find her. Tianyin and the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance are not on the same side.”

“Moreover, the attack I launched before leaving, although it did not destroy her, still severely injured her. Tianyin’s condition was already unstable. When she appears and regains clarity again, she may not even remember us.”

Although Li Ping said this, it still could not put Sun Luyuan at ease.

The hidden meaning in Li Ping’s words was very clear: there was an even more terrifying existence behind Tianyin, one that even the unfathomable Holy Emperor of Great Qi before him had to be wary of.

“Damn Xu Yuanxian, saying it was just a simple errand. I actually believed your nonsense…”

Thinking of how he had inexplicably been dragged into this matter, with all his original plans disrupted and even his entire family facing implication, a nameless anger rose in Sun Luyuan’s heart.

“That old bastard is indeed no different from the other high-level members of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. None of them are decent!” he cursed inwardly.

Recalling the various crises his family would face after his disappearance, Sun Luyuan felt his mind in complete chaos.

At this moment, Li Ping spoke again.

“Actually, there is a way for you to return without arousing suspicion. As long as you inform the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance of our existence, you will probably not only avoid punishment but also earn a great merit.”

Li Ping’s voice echoed in the Holy Emperor’s seat.

Sun Luyuan’s heart jumped. He hurriedly expressed his loyalty: “How could this lowly one betray the Holy Emperor?”

Unexpectedly, Li Ping smiled: “I think you misunderstood my meaning.”

“Return to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and tell Xu Yuanxian everything about Luoyan Prefecture and our Holy Dynasty in complete detail. This is an order.”

“Ah?” Sun Luyuan was instantly stunned. He did not understand why the Holy Emperor would suddenly make this abrupt decision that completely contradicted his previous stance.

“Could it be that he is already prepared to wage war against the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance?” Sun Luyuan could not help thinking.

“After you meet Xu Yuanxian, say this…” Li Ping transmitted slowly.

Sun Luyuan’s expression gradually became colorful.

Not long after, Sun Luyuan quietly returned to the Xuanhuang Realm properly under the protection of Daoist Hundred Flowers.

He hurried to the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

At this moment, the Immortal Alliance headquarters was somewhat chaotic because of the destruction of Luoyan Prefecture.

But now, no one would imagine that this matter had anything to do with Sun Luyuan, this Dao Integration cultivator from the Guardian Hall.

Thus, Sun Luyuan smoothly arrived at the Guardian Hall headquarters and met Xu Yuanxian once again.

“You have returned? How did the task I assigned you go?” Xu Yuanxian did not even open his eyes.

“Elder Xu, something major has happened.”

Sun Luyuan appeared extremely terrified, looking as if he wanted to speak but hesitated.

Xu Yuanxian abruptly opened his eyes.

With a wave of his hand, he set up multiple restrictions before saying in a deep voice: “Do not panic. Speak slowly and carefully.”

Sun Luyuan took a deep breath: “I did follow your instructions, Elder, and met Senior Qingfeng at the Clear Wind Hall on Ten Thousand Immortal Island.”

“The tree person named A-Liu has gone completely mad. Senior Qingfeng was willing to leave with me. However, she was unwilling to come to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters and insisted on going to Luoyan Prefecture…”

Hearing the three words “Luoyan Prefecture” from Sun Luyuan’s mouth and connecting them to the recent calamity in Luoyan Prefecture, Xu Yuanxian could not help but change expression slightly.

“What exactly happened in Luoyan Prefecture?!” He abandoned his usual composure and asked coldly.

“When I was about to bring Senior Qingfeng to the destination, she suddenly became dazed and said something like, ‘Sure enough, there are not many people in the world who treat me well…’ Then I felt my body become ethereal, as if I was about to disappear from this world completely.” Sun Luyuan said with lingering fear.

Xu Yuanxian clearly knew of Qingfeng’s habit and showed no surprise.

“Just when I felt I was about to die, a golden figure suddenly descended. Then…”

“He started fighting with Senior Qingfeng.”

Sun Luyuan projected the image of Li Ping’s earlier struggle with Qingfeng.

“The power of heaven and earth, descend upon my body.”

In the image, that majestic voice spoke in a low shout.

Xu Yuanhan stared closely at the image, his expression gradually becoming grave.

Only when the silver needle appeared did he finally lose his composure.

The image came to an abrupt end.

“The other senior took me away. When I returned, I learned that Luoyan Prefecture had already been destroyed. And Senior Qingfeng’s whereabouts are unknown…” Sun Luyuan put on an extremely terrified appearance.

“Elder Xu, what exactly is going on?! What is the identity of Senior Qingfeng?” He asked in return.

Xu Yuanxian did not answer.

He frowned and pondered for a moment before saying: “Do you know the name of the cultivator who saved you?”

Sun Luyuan nodded: “He called himself the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor. However, he did not speak much with me and soon sent me back.”

“By the way, Elder Xu, the small world where the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor resides seems a bit strange…”

Xu Yuanxian did not pay attention to the second half of Sun Luyuan’s sentence. Instead, he murmured to himself: “Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor.”

Sun Luyuan did not disturb him and simply waited patiently for the other party’s reaction.

“You do not need to ask more about this matter. Just pretend it never happened.” Xu Yuanxian suddenly said this.

Sun Luyuan was stunned.

It was not an act.

“Elder Xu…”

Xu Yuanxian raised his hand to interrupt Sun Luyuan: “It was my misjudgment. We have not met for many years; I did not expect things to have developed to the point of irreconcilable opposition.”

“I will find an opportunity to compensate you later. But this matter must be kept secret.” Xu Yuanhan’s cold gaze locked onto Sun Luyuan.

Sun Luyuan shivered and hurriedly swore to comply.

“You may return first. If there are any issues, I will contact you again.”

“Remember what I said.”

Before leaving, Xu Yuanxian did not forget to remind him once more.

Sun Luyuan had expected the matter to be extremely dangerous, yet it had been resolved so smoothly.

While feeling greatly relieved, he was also filled with an unreal sensation.

“However, that old thing’s reaction was basically within the Holy Emperor’s expectations.”

“Irreconcilable opposition? What exactly does that mean?”

Sun Luyuan could not figure it out no matter how he thought.

But this unexpected turn not only preserved his life but also earned him a favor from Xu Yuanxian. It could truly be called an unexpected delight.

Sun Luyuan shook his head slightly and temporarily suppressed the gloom in his heart.

“Since I am already at headquarters, I might as well go see Lu’er.”

With this thought, Sun Luyuan soon arrived in the Derivation Law Jade space.

Sun Ang seemed very pleasantly surprised by his arrival and kept asking questions.

Sun Luyuan answered in great detail.

“You have really suffered, Lu’er.”

“As long as the family is safe, that is all that matters.”

Sun Ang’s single sentence suddenly brought a trace of guilt to Sun Luyuan’s heart.

Feeling embarrassed to stay longer, he left hurriedly.

Sun Ang, that is, Li Fan, watched Sun Luyuan’s departing figure with an inexplicable expression.

The conversation between Sun Luyuan and Xu Yuanxian had naturally fallen into his ears without missing a single word.

The Holy Emperor’s sudden change of mind had also been influenced by him.

“As expected, there are people in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance who know the true situation of the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“The Xuanhuang Realm is being dismembered, transformed, and fused.”

“Xu Yuanxian knows this, and so does the Dharma-Transmitter Jiang. However, as subordinates of the Dharma-Transmitter, they undoubtedly stand on the side of the Dharma-Transmitter.”

“They may have already anticipated the actions against the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao. But this has already exceeded their ability to handle, so they chose to watch from the sidelines…”

“After all, not everyone has the courage to stand up and oppose the Dharma-Transmitter. Even the few Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns in the Five Elders Association simply allow it to happen.”

Li Fan snorted coldly in his heart.

“However, I believe there are still hot-blooded individuals in the Xuanhuang Realm whose passion has not cooled.”

The figure of Shang Shaojun flashed before Li Fan’s eyes.

“What is lacking now is an opportunity to unite them.”

“The Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor…”

“It has now vaguely become somewhat worthy of the name.”

This time, the Xuanhuang Realm actually took the initiative to release Ink Death to help the Holy Emperor’s clone conceal his tracks. This was something Li Fan had not expected before.

“This is certainly related to the clone helping and feeding back the Heavenly Dao through the Dark Sea, but more so because the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm has been in a state of isolation and helplessness for too long. Now that such a Holy Emperor has suddenly appeared, it clings to him as if he were the last life-saving straw.”

“With all kinds of coincidences and opportunities converging on him, the evolution of this clone has truly become somewhat interesting.”

Li Fan narrowed his eyes as his thoughts raced rapidly.

Although the clone was still under his absolute control, during the period when Li Ping wrestled with the Tianyin, with the support of the golden source power and the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, the clone’s strength had faintly surpassed the current Li Fan.

This caused some deviation in the path the clone was advancing on.

It was like driving a car on the road. Li Fan could still firmly control all the functions of the car, but the road itself had changed due to the vehicle’s own condition.

“However, for me, this may not be a bad thing.”

“The more assistance received from the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, the more it will force out the full details of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and even the Dharma-Transmitter.”

“And that Xuanhuang evil thought. It is also time to probe its depths.”

The same thought flashed through the minds of Li Fan’s main body and the Holy Emperor clone at the same time.

Before long, Sun Erlang arrived at the Holy Emperor’s throne together with Wang Xuanba.

“How is the current situation in the beast small world?” Li Ping first asked Sun Erlang.

“Reporting to Master. After personally witnessing the strength of our Holy Dynasty, especially after seeing Xuanba’s more advanced beast technology, the Imperial Princess has agreed to submit unconditionally.”

“At present, her forces are being slowly incorporated. Although the army there consists of mortals, after three to five years of transformation, they will become combat strength for our Holy Dynasty. In particular, the large number of beasts will somewhat alleviate the shortage of resources in the Holy Dynasty.” Sun Erlang said in a deep voice.

“Not bad. The efficiency of our flesh factories is expected to increase more than fivefold within three years. Moreover, the Imperial Princess indeed has her own unique insights into beast development technology. She is even better than me!” Wang Xuanba spoke with praise.

After listening to the reports from the two, Li Ping nodded slightly.

He then informed them of the location of the small world where the Xuanhuang evil thought, that is, Xu Ke, was located.

“You two, go together with Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong to this place. See if you can invite Xu Ke back.”

“Be respectful in your attitude.”

Sun Erlang and Wang Xuanba exchanged a glance.

They had run errands before, but this was the first time the Holy Emperor had been so solemn about it.

The two brothers did not dare to slack off. They immediately took leave of the Holy Emperor and hurried first to the empire’s small world.

They found the Holy Masters Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong.

“What? Xu Ke?”

“Are you sure you did not mishear?”

Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong were stunned on the spot after hearing the two’s explanation.

Then Xiao Qing lost her composure and directly grabbed Sun Erlang tightly, repeatedly confirming and asking.

“Since Master said so, it definitely will not be wrong. Will the two of you come with us?”

Although Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong had countless doubts in their hearts, they could not overcome thousands of years of longing.

They immediately agreed.

The four of them traveled together and arrived at the entrance of that small world.

“This is…”

“The formation aura of the Beast Taming Sect.” Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong said excitedly.

Their auras became somewhat disordered. Clearly the person they wanted to see was right ahead, yet at this moment they suddenly lacked the courage to take that step.

Only under Sun Erlang’s urging did they enter the interior of the small world.

In the lonely heaven and earth, there was only a white-haired old man sitting alone on the mountain peak.

“Xu Ke!”

Xiao Qing’s trembling voice sounded.

The old man’s body stiffened slightly, then he slowly raised his head.

Two figures, one in green and one in red, were flying rapidly toward him.

Sealed memories from the past instantly surged into his mind.

Scenes from thousands of years ago reappeared before Xu Ke’s eyes.

Only Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong still looked exactly as they did back then.

While he, Xu Ke, had already become old and frail.

The three faced one another.

There were a thousand words in their hearts, yet they did not know where to begin.

“Sun Erlang.”

“Wang Xuanba.”

“We greet Senior.”

Two untimely voices interrupted the scene of the three reuniting.

Chapter 1236: Good And Evil Finally Meet

“Who are you?”

Sun and Wang arrived together with Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong. For a moment, Xu Ke felt dazed and thought they were his descendants.

But he quickly reacted.

Given the conditions of the empire’s small world, which lacked spiritual energy, it was impossible for cultivators of such a realm to be born there.

Xu Ke’s profound gaze immediately fell upon the two of them.

Sun Erlang saluted with neither arrogance nor humility and said, “By the order of my master, we have come to invite Senior to meet him.”

“Hmm?”

Xu Ke was somewhat surprised.

He had not expected that there were still people in this world who remembered him.

After Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong transmitted the recent encounters of the empire, Xu Ke’s expression grew increasingly grave.

“Someone who knows about me and possesses such capability? Who is it?”

Vague figures flashed through his mind, but he excluded them one by one.

He glanced almost imperцепtibly at the decayed wooden coffin behind him. Xu Ke originally wanted to refuse their invitation.

But…

Xu Ke’s gaze lingered for a moment on Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong.

He then let out a soft sigh.

They had clearly known his location yet still brought Qing and Hong along. The implied threat was self-evident. Although he had avoided them for a thousand years without meeting them, it had been out of necessity. How could Xu Ke be a heartless and unrighteous person who could watch them suffer helplessly?

Thinking of this, Xu Ke shook his head slightly. “I can go with you for a trip. But I have a few conditions.”

Sun Erlang quickly replied, “Senior, please speak freely.”

“First, one of you two must stay in my place on this mountain peak. Before I return, no matter what happens, you must not leave early. To guard against any unforeseen events, I will set up restrictions.” Xu Ke said slowly.

“This…” Upon hearing this strange request, Sun Erlang hesitated.

“No problem. I will sit here in Senior’s place.” Wang Xuanba, however, patted his chest and agreed.

Sun Erlang knew that his brother, after cultivating the technique passed down by their master, could transform into countless forms like a strange beast. Even if the body were completely destroyed, it would not have much impact. He therefore did not stop him.

Xu Ke nodded and continued, “Second, after I leave this small world with you, no one is allowed to approach within ten zhang. Otherwise, do not blame this old man for being ruthless.”

Xu Ke glanced at Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong emphasizing his tone.

Clearly, this “anyone” included Qing and Hong as well.

Sun Erlang felt a chill in his heart but still nodded in agreement.

“As for the last point…”

Xu Ke said to Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong, “If I show any signs of abnormality, kill me. Do not hesitate for even a moment.”

As he spoke, a wooden sword no larger than a palm appeared in Xu Ke’s hand at some point.

He extended his hand, and the wooden sword flew to Xiao Qing on its own.

Xiao Qing was completely stunned.

But seeing Xu Ke’s aged yet extremely resolute face, which looked exactly like the expression he had when he insisted on leaving the empire back then.

Tears shimmered in her eyes, yet Xiao Qing said nothing more and softly agreed.

“Alright.”

Xiao Qing gripped the wooden sword tightly in her palm while carefully listening to Xu Ke’s voice transmission explaining how to use the wooden sword.

Once everything was settled, Xu Ke looked toward Sun and Wang.

Seeing Xu Ke so solemn, Sun Erlang and Wang Xuanba did not dare to be negligent. “Does Senior have any other instructions?”

“Wait here for a moment and stay farther away.”

The two men and two beasts did not understand why. They exchanged a glance but obediently flew far away from the abrupt stone mountain where Xu Ke stood.

Xu Ke pressed his right hand on the mountain peak and exerted slight force.

The stone mountain, the heavens and earth, and even the entire world trembled slightly in that instant.

It seemed as if sharp, venomous curses rang out, or perhaps it was merely an illusion.

After the shaking subsided, the small world seemed somewhat different from before.

Yet Sun Erlang and the others could not see exactly where the difference lay.

Xu Ke slowly stood up, looked at Wang Xuanba, and stamped his right foot.

Wang Xuanba signaled Sun Erlang to rest assured, then flew over to Xu Ke’s side.

“Sit down.”

Xu Ke moved his foot away, revealing a deeply sunken area on the ground at the mountain peak.

Wang Xuanba sat down with a plop.

“It might hurt a little, but you won’t die.”

Xu Ke flew into the air at this moment and said slowly.

The instant he flew out of the range of that towering stone mountain, Wang Xuanba let out a miserable cry.

It was as if some heavy object had suddenly pressed down on him. Caught off guard, his back was directly flattened against the ground.

The continuous sounds of bones being crushed and ground rang out without pause.

“Xuanba?!” Sun Erlang cried out in alarm.

“I… I’m fine!” A hand stretched out from the body that had almost become a flattened meat patty.

It waved weakly toward Sun Er’lang.

“It’s just… it really hurts a bit.”

Xu Ke pointed lightly at Wang Xuanba. “Let’s go. I said he won’t die.”

Wang Xuanba’s pressure seemed to ease slightly, and he barely managed to raise his head again.

Seeing this, Sun Er’lang took a deep breath. Without hesitation, he led the way forward together with Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong.

Xu Ke followed closely behind.

Like Xiao Qing and Xiao Hong, Sun Erlang secretly observed Xu Ke from behind.

When Xu Ke had been sitting, the seemingly decayed coffin on his back had not been very noticeable.

But whether it was Sun Erlang’s illusion or not, ever since they left the small world, that coffin seemed to become increasingly intact.

Its shape was also slowly growing larger.

It was as if…

Something could emerge from inside at any moment.

Combined with the three strange conditions Xu Ke had mentioned earlier, an uneasy feeling quietly rose in Sun Erlang’s heart.

Even more so, indistinct low murmuring sounds reached his ears.

Just as Sun Erlang could not help but listen, a cool, icy sensation suddenly arose in his heart on its own.

The noise in his ears vanished instantly, replaced by Xu Ke’s low warning.

“Do not pay attention to those strange voices.”

Sun Erlang immediately broke out in a cold sweat.

He prided himself on having a strong will and had even experienced another real lifetime in the simulated world of the Dao Deviation Stone in the Taiyan Sect.

Yet centuries of experience proved so fragile before those eerie sounds and nearly collapsed in an instant.

“If not for the Immortal Heart Mantra personally taught by Master, I fear I would have suffered.”

“This Xu Ke…”

Sun Erlang dared not look at Xu Ke or the coffin on his back any longer. He simply focused his mind and quickened his pace.

Before long, the group traversed the long void path and arrived outside the Great Qi small world.

“Huh?”

This time, the one who let out a sound of surprise and uncertainty was Xu Ke.

Sun Erlang turned back and saw that, for some unknown reason, the coffin on Xu Ke’s back had once again become decayed and smaller.

It was almost no different from the one in the world where Xu Ke had originally been.

All citizens of Great Qi in the Holy Capital had already been notified in advance and had returned to their homes.

The vast city was empty, with only a straight, void path specially opened for Xu Ke leading directly to the Holy Emperor’s throne.

“Master is already waiting for Senior.” Sun Erlang said respectfully.

No further guidance was needed.

Ever since arriving here, Xu Ke had already sensed that omnipresent, powerful aura that enveloped the entire small world like the great sun in the sky.

It far surpassed ordinary Dao Integration realm.

It even exerted a certain degree of suppression on the Xuanhuang evil thought sealed within the coffin on his back.

What surprised Xu Ke even more was that he vaguely detected a trace of familiarity within this aura.

But…

“How is that possible?”

With three parts doubt, three parts unease, and a hint of hidden excitement in his heart, Xu Ke finally entered the Holy Emperor’s seat.

After clearly seeing the figure seated above, Xu Ke felt a slight relief in his heart.

At the same time, a few traces of disappointment flashed through him.

“I was overthinking. How could it be the already deceased Mister Bai?”

Forcing down the distracting thoughts in his mind, Xu Ke focused and carefully observed this legendary Faceless Holy Emperor of Great Qi.

Or rather, the Heavenly Mandate Holy Emperor.

At the same time, Li Ping was also observing Xu Ke.

And the coffin behind him.

Li Ping naturally saw far more than Sun Erlang.

What he saw was not decayed wood, but a faintly visible transparent pale figure.

The figure possessed the most handsome appearance in the world, yet it was paired with an expression full of venom and evil.

Li Ping sensed that the urging from the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao had descended once again.

And it had become even more intense.

Meanwhile, the pale Xuanhuang evil thought seemed to sense something as well.

It suddenly raised its head and stared fixedly at Li Ping.

In response, the coffin on Xu Ke’s back began to shake violently.

Loud banging sounds echoed continuously within the tall holy hall.

“You…”

Xu Ke suddenly became alert.

His figure retreated explosively as a rotating wheel illusion appeared above his head.

The golden wheel spun rapidly, and images of countless ferocious beasts along with roaring sounds emerged from it.

He attempted to suppress the vibrating coffin.

Yet the Xuanhuang evil thought’s struggle grew unprecedentedly fierce.

Only when Holy Emperor Li Ping’s heart stirred and the [Heaven and Earth, Myriad Spirits—All Living Beings Refine the Soul] in the Great Qi small world began to circulate slightly, concealing the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, did the Xuanhuang evil thought, having lost its connection to its other half, gradually quiet down.

At this moment, Xu Ke, who had tried for a while and discovered he could not fly out no matter what, also gave up his futile efforts.

He stared coldly at Li Ping. “Who exactly are you?”

By now, how could Xu Ke not realize that this Holy Emperor had come for the sealed Xuanhuang evil thought?

“Who am I…”

Li Ping paused slightly. “That is actually not important. What is important is that it is time to return things to their rightful owner.”

Xu Ke’s expression changed drastically. “Return things to their rightful owner?”

“Back then, Mister Bai failed to fulfill the Heavenly Dao’s entrustment and even sealed a portion of the Heavenly Dao’s power. Now I shall correct the chaos and return the Dao to Heaven.” Although Li Ping’s voice was not loud, it echoed continuously throughout the Holy Emperor’s seat.

These words struck Xu Ke’s ears like a thunderclap.

“What did you say?!”

Having lived so long and witnessed many bizarre events in the past, Xu Ke had never been this shocked before.

“Blocking is not as good as guiding. Sealing is only a temporary measure. The longer it is suppressed, the fiercer the backlash will be when it breaks free one day. Did Mister Bai not teach you this principle?”

“How much longer can you hold on?”

Li Ping did not give Xu Ke any time to react and continued to shake his mind without pause.

Cold sweat dripped from Xu Ke’s forehead.

“You are…” he asked with disbelief.

“The Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable?”

The moment these words were spoken, the air inside the Holy Emperor’s throne seemed to freeze.

After a brief silence, Li Ping gave a soft chuckle.

“No.”

This answer clearly exceeded Xu Ke’s expectations, and he immediately fell into confusion.

“After Mister Bai’s betrayal, do you think the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao would still trust cultivators?”

Li Ping’s tone was enigmatic.

“I am not the Xuanhuang Celestial Venerable.”

“I am the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao itself.”

As Li Ping spoke, he rose abruptly from the Holy Emperor’s seat.

In an instant, his figure seemed to expand infinitely.

Like a giant standing tall between heaven and earth, it filled Xu Ke’s entire field of vision.

The already overwhelmingly powerful aura on Li Ping erupted once more and climbed higher.

The concealment of the Myriad Spirits Great Formation vanished, and Li Ping borrowed the power of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao.

For the sake of its other sealed half, the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao was unprecedentedly generous this time.

A faint crack appeared in the sky above the Great Qi small world.

It was a sign that the entire world had been stretched to its limit.

If not for the fact that, over these years, Great Qi had been elevated considerably under Li Ping’s management and its upper limit far exceeded that of ordinary small worlds.

It would probably have been directly burst apart by this.

Although it appeared completely powerless before the Immortal Ancestor and the True Immortal of the Immortal Ruins.

In reality, with the current Xuanhuang Realm having fused with many remnants of immortal cultivation worlds.

The power of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao was actually not much weaker than when it had been complete in the beginning.

Strength and weakness are relative.

At this moment, in Xu Ke’s eyes, he seemed to see once again that graceful figure from back then, whose every movement carried the rhythm and will of heaven and earth.

The favor of heaven and earth was added entirely to his body.

But what was different was that Mister Bai had been gentle and refined.

While the figure before him now…

Was domineering and majestic.

It inspired fear and conviction.

In a trance, Xu Ke recalled Mister Bai’s instructions from back then.

“On the matter of whether to leave, I hold a different opinion from this consciousness.”

“I do not think I am wrong, nor do I consider it a betrayal.”

“I am merely seeking a better way to save the world.”

“Xu Ke, if possible, please help me watch over it.”

“I believe that one day, it will understand.”

...

At that time, Xu Ke had not understood Mister Bai’s words.

Over these several thousand years, the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao consciousness separated by Mister Bai had not only failed to understand but had instead developed in an increasingly extreme directioℴ𝓋ℯ𝘭.𝑐ℴ𝑚

Not only had the conveyed thoughts and curses grown more and more venomous.

It had even begun to influence any existence that approached it.

The small world where Xu Ke resided, which was used to seal the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao consciousness, had slowly lost its vitality and turned completely into a barren gobi desert.

His own body had also aged far more than under normal circumstances.

Right up until today, Xu Ke had thought that he would ultimately meet the fate of being devoured by the evil thought.

He had never expected…

“So this is what Mister Bai meant by ‘understanding’?”

“It is merely the other half, the main body, that has understood.”

Xu Ke could not help but think this way.

“Hand it over to me.”

Just as Xu Ke’s thoughts were in turmoil, Li Ping spoke again.

This jolted Xu Ke out of his deep contemplation.

He involuntarily took a step back. The golden wheel continued to suppress the decayed wooden coffin.

Chapter 1237: Borrowing Fortune To Devour The Heavenly Thought

“Words alone carry no proof. How can it be proven?”

Even though Xu Ke had already sensed the familiar aura on the other party, the Xuanhuang evil thought sealed behind him was far too important. If entrusted to the wrong person, it would undoubtedly cause unimaginable calamity.

This was also why Xu Ke had spent so many years sealing it alone.

He simply could not find anyone both capable and trustworthy.

Li Ping saw Xu Ke’s extreme caution and his outright refusal, yet he did not grow angry. His laughter echoed continuously.

“It is not for me to prove it to you, but for you to see the truth clearly!”

With Li Ping’s words, the ceiling of the Holy Emperor’s throne hall and even the sky of the Great Qi small world seemed to tear open.

Xu Ke’s vision instantly returned to the Xuanhuang Realm. Scene after scene unfolded before his eyes without pause.

In the endless night, specks of golden light emerged from nowhere. They stirred invisible waves across the dark sea.

Above the boundless ocean, golden chains crisscrossed like a giant net. Within the golden net echoed sharp, unwilling, hate-filled wails. A consciousness was slowly being ground away. At the center of the golden light, the Faceless Holy Emperor stood in the void, his aura cold and forbidding.

In a rather desolate prefecture, powerful heavenly might blessed the Faceless Holy Emperor. Beside him, a pitch-black shadowy figure flickered in and out of view. Though nothing stood before them, Xu Ke instinctively felt unease.

...

These successively flashing images all showed the close connection between the Faceless Holy Emperor before him and the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao.

Meanwhile, the Xuanhuang evil thought in the coffin behind Xu Ke struggled even more violently, as if desperate to return to its main body. This basically confirmed the judgment.

Yet for some unknown reason, Xu Ke still felt some hesitation in his heart.

“Perhaps I have stayed with this Xuanhuang evil thought for too long and have unknowingly been influenced?” Xu Ke suddenly felt a jolt in his heart.

The image of the Faceless Holy Emperor was simply too upright. As a result, Xu Ke, who stood on the opposing side, could not help but begin doubting himself.

“The Xuanhuang evil thought has been sealed for far too long. It has nearly exceeded the limit that ordinary cultivators can control. Since it is one with the current Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, if it suddenly returns, even the Heavenly Dao itself may undergo extreme and unpredictable changes.”

“Only by first suppressing it and then slowly grinding away the evil thought completely can the Dao be returned to Heaven. In this process, I will probably still need your assistance.” After a brief stalemate, Holy Emperor Li Ping spoke again.

These words elicited two completely different reactions in the Holy Emperor’s throne hall.

Xu Ke was overjoyed.

Yet the coffin lid behind him, after a brief quiet, shook even more frantically.

Li Ping even vaguely saw, on that pale figure’s face, a gaze a hundred times more venomous than before.

“Come with me for now.”

Li Ping did not press Xu Ke to hand over the Xuanhuang evil thought. Instead, he brought him to a certain location high above the Great Qi small world.

“Can you sense anything different here?”

“The fluctuations of the Xuanhuang evil thought are suppressed and almost imperceptible. Not only the Xuanhuang evil thought, but this small world itself and even the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao...” A trace of astonishment flashed in Xu Ke’s eyes.

“This is the eye of the Grand Formation of the Myriad Spirits of Heaven and Earth. This formation seals the heavens and cuts off the earth. Let alone a world consciousness, even a True Immortal descending could be resisted to some degree.” Li Ping said in a calm tone.

In his memory and understanding, this formation could indeed hinder a True Immortal’s descent to a certain extent.

Although Xu Ke had lived a very long time, he had never truly witnessed the might of a True Immortal. Thus he harbored no doubts about Li Ping’s words.

“With this formation present, even if it is released directly, it will have no way to escape.”

Xu Ke also possessed some understanding of formations.

The more he observed, the more alarmed he became.

This Grand Formation of the Myriad Spirits of Heaven and Earth was indeed as profound and wondrous as Li Ping had described.

“If I had possessed this formation back then, I would not have needed to suppress it personally for several thousand years.” Amid his shock, Xu Ke felt a momentary daze.

After touring the Myriad Spirits Grand Formation, Li Ping brought Xu Ke to Yin Shangren’s location.

It resembled a schoolroom filled with children studying earnestly. The innocent, pure faces pulled Xu Ke’s thoughts back a thousand years to the small, rundown temple in Ningyuan City. The scene from back then overlapped somewhat with the sight before him, leaving Xu Ke slightly stunned.

Yin Shangren’s reaction, however, was rather alarming.

While teaching the children, he suddenly froze completely.

After that instant of stillness, he shot to his feet and stared fixedly at the coffin behind Xu Ke.

His body trembled violently before it split open, revealing another self that had been hidden all along.

All four eyes were filled with unprecedented greed.

Xu Ke frowned slightly. That gaze, as if looking at prey, made him very uncomfortable.

What concerned him even more was the reaction of the Xuanhuang evil thought in the coffin behind him.

It was very strange.

It had suddenly fallen silent. The curses and temptations that had constantly echoed in Xu Ke’s ears vanished once more.

Yet unlike earlier in the Grand Formation of the Myriad Spirits of Heaven and Earth, where the Xuanhuang evil thought itself had remained unaffected, this time it seemed startled and had quieted down on its own.

Moments later, the Xuanhuang evil thought resumed pounding the coffin lid, asserting its presence.

But in Xu Ke’s eyes, it now looked more like bluster.

Only when Li Ping’s body stepped between Yin Shangren and the Xuanhuang evil thought did both sides return to normal.

Yin Shangren’s split body closed once more. He grinned and said, “I never expected that the mighty Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao would one day be reduced to this state.”

“If my brothers knew, who knows how delighted they would be.”

Yin Shangren smacked his lips. “I have eaten plenty of those worthless world specters. But I have never tasted a complete world consciousness. I wonder what it would be like.”

Xu Ke felt a chill of horror upon hearing this.

Instead, Li Ping transmitted a voice introduction of Yin Shangren’s origins.

“A world specter left behind after being devoured by the Xuanhuang Realm, and one that greatly admires Mr. Bai?”

At first Xu Ke still harbored some doubts. But after seeing the white rib bone that Yin Shangren treasured, no other thoughts remained in his mind.

Even though it looked like an ordinary rib bone no different from any other.

Xu Ke recognized at a single glance that it belonged to Mr. Bai.

Mr. Bai’s smiling face appeared once more before Xu Ke’s eyes, only to shatter abruptly in the next instant.

All that remained was this white rib bone.

A wave of grief suddenly surged in Xu Ke’s heart.

After learning of Xu Ke’s special connection to Mr. Bai, Yin Shangren put away his earlier ferocious expression and became gentle.

His eyes, however, still swept across the decaying coffin from time to time.

“With Yin Shangren present, even if it escapes the layers of restrictions, it will at most end up as food on a plate.” Li Ping said indifferently.

With his emotions somewhat calmed, Xu Ke finally believed in the identity and plan of this Faceless Holy Emperor.

The moment he let go of the obsession in his heart, Xu Ke felt an unprecedented sense of relief.

“I have persisted for so long. From now on, let the Xuanhuang Realm handle it itself.” Xu Ke thought this way.

“Why, are you also interested in it?” Yin Shangren looked at Li Ping with some surprise.

Li Ping briefly recounted what had happened in Luoyan Prefecture before adding, “It has helped me greatly.”

Yin Shangren shrugged. “Then I will not compete with you for it. Even if I eat it, at most it will satisfy my appetite before being expelled again.”

Li Ping remained silent and did not respond.

“That silver needle...” Yin Shangren suddenly displayed an image to Li Ping.

“Does it look like this?”

Li Ping nodded. “Why? Have you seen it before?”

“It seems somewhat familiar, but it was a very long time ago.” Yin Shangren narrowed his eyes, recalling something.

“Right, a long time ago I saw it in this location. But at that time it was not me; it was a remnant memory from a portion I once devoured. So the specific details are not very clear. If you are interested, you can go take a look. You might find something useful.”

Yin Shangren transmitted a location to Li Ping.

As a world specter, he still retained a faint sensing to his original body. The location Yin Shangren conveyed turned out to be the precise current position of the Xuanhuang Realm.

“I understand. Let us finish handling this matter first. With my current strength, even if I truly discover something, I would find it difficult to investigate deeply.” Li Ping spoke with complete honesty and no reservations.

“The Xuanhuang Realm truly has far too many evil spirits and monsters.” Yin Shangren snorted softly.

At this moment Li Ping turned to Xu Ke. “Yin Shangren and I previously planned to gather and bury Mr. Bai’s scattered remains so he may rest in peace. After this matter, if you are willing, you may assist us.”

Xu Ke agreed without the slightest hesitation. His voice even trembled slightly. “Good, good.”

Having successfully persuaded Xu Ke, the guardian, the next step was to release and absorb the Xuanhuang evil thought.

Although Li Ping possessed absolute confidence, he did not act carelessly.

Instead he made thorough preparations.

The following day, a piece of news spread throughout the entire Holy Dynasty.

Seven days later, the Supreme Holy Emperor would bestow grace.

All citizens who prayed sincerely within these seven days would receive the Holy Emperor’s reward.

The more sincere the heart, the more generous the reward.

Praying to the Holy Emperor was already a required practice for the people of Great Qi. Now that the Holy Emperor had explicitly promised rewards, how could anyone dare to slack off?

Thus, within merely half a day after the holy decree was issued, Li Ping sensed the fortune of the Holy Dynasty Great Qi condensing to an unprecedented degree.

Even the power of fortune formed within his body by the Qi of Heaven and Earth’s Myriad Spirits showed signs of transforming from purple toward gold.

“This method offers only temporary effect and cannot last long.”

“If the promise is broken, it will bring backlash, and even a drop in realm is not impossible.”

Li Ping felt his strength continuously climbing toward its peak, yet his heart remained perfectly calm.

Golden source power was drawn forth as the will of the masses focused into one.

The Grand Formation of the Myriad Spirits of Heaven and Earth began to operate slowly.

It severed all connections with the outside world.

At this moment, not only could the Xuanhuang Realm no longer observe the existence of the Great Qi small world, but even Daoist Hundred Flowers, who was right beside it within the Five Elements Great Cave-Heaven, suddenly lost all connections to the Great Qi small world.

Had Li Ping not notified her in advance, Daoist Hundred Flowers might have mistaken it for some terrifying calamity striking Great Qi.

The process of devouring the Xuanhuang evil thought still required blocking the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao outside.

Otherwise something unpredictable might occur.

Seven days passed in the blink of an eye.

Li Ping’s state, after seven days of brewing and preparation, had reached its peak.

At this moment, within the eye of the Myriad Spirits Grand Formation, Yin Shangren, Xu Ke, and Yi Shu stood in a triangular formation.

Li Ping sat cross-legged in the air at the center of the three.

As part of the Taiyan Dao Deviation Stone, Yi Shu had naturally sensed the unusual changes occurring in the Great Qi small world.

He had come of his own accord to inquire.

Li Ping hid nothing and directly informed him of the plan to devour the Xuanhuang evil thought.

After a brief daze, Yi Shu immediately offered to help.

He even proposed that the Taiyan Sect and the Holy Dynasty Great Qi could deepen their cooperation further in the future.

After pondering for a moment, Li Ping agreed to Yi Shu’s request.

He had invited these three merely for appearances.

Within the Grand Formation of the Myriad Spirits of Heaven and Earth, everything remained under his control.

As long as Yi Shu behaved himself it would be fine. If he made any unusual move, Li Ping could refine him with a single thought and devour him together with the Xuanhuang evil thought.

“Formation, activate!”

Li Ping shouted coldly.

The three inside the formation felt the sky suddenly turn chaotic.

Purple star points flickered within it from time to time.

It was as if they had entered the endless void and left the real world behind.

All three tensed.

At this moment Li Ping pointed gently at the decaying wooden coffin placed beside him.

There was no violent sound of destruction.

The wooden seal layer dissipated like a wisp of black smoke.

A figure gradually clarified from blur, appearing within the formation before Li Ping.

Unlike the venomous appearance Li Ping had seen earlier.

What stood before him now was no different from the Mr. Bai in his memories.

Smiling faintly, it asked, “Do you want to eat me?”

There was no hatred in its eyes, only comfort and relief.

“Mr. Bai?!” Xu Ke could no longer hold back and cried out in alarm.

It was not that Xu Ke lacked composure; this figure simply resembled Mr. Bai far too closely.

Li Ping remained unmoved and nodded calmly. “Correct.”

“Being able to merge with me can also be considered a fitting end for you.”

These simple words caused the originally calm and composed Xuanhuang evil thought’s expression to freeze abruptly.

“What are you?!”

The Xuanhuang evil thought could no longer maintain its disguise and screamed in panic.

It suddenly transformed into a wisp of black qi and lunged toward Li Ping.

Yet before Li Ping there seemed to be an infinite distance.

No matter how hard it tried, it could not draw near at all.

“Noisy.”

“Once you merge with me, you will know what I am.”

Li Ping glanced at the Xuanhuang evil thought.

A vast and mighty force descended from the sky.

It instantly bound the black smoke that the evil thought had become.

Li Ping chanted softly. A purple-gold vortex slowly formed above everyone’s heads.

Chapter 1238: The Great Celestial Venerable’S Immortal Body

Within the purplish-gold vortex, faint winds and thunder surged.

At the narrowest bottom of the funnel-shaped energy vortex was the somewhat panicked figure of the Xuanhuang Evil Thought.

Images of countless different life forms flashed rapidly through the wind-and-thunder vortex. Like a hurricane, they tore at the Xuanhuang Evil Thought.

As a result, its body gradually twisted and blurred.

“I am the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao! The father and mother of you cultivators! How dare you treat me like this!”

The Xuanhuang Evil Thought roared.

It refused to wait passively for death. The appearance that had resembled Mr. Bai finally revealed its true form.

Its body swelled like an inflated balloon. In the blink of an eye it formed a grotesque and terrifying monster covered in blue-purple lumps. Even more horrifying was that the Xuanhuang Evil Thought’s face had vanished. Or rather, the countless black shadows rapidly crawling across the surface of its shell were now its many faces!

Their expressions varied, yet none showed any positive emotion. Madness, hatred, despair, brutality...

Every dark and twisted aspect of living beings was fully displayed on the Xuanhuang Evil Thought.

Substantial black malevolent energy surged from its body and shot skyward, clashing head-on with the wind-and-thunder rotation of Li Ping’s heaven-and-earth great millstone.

In the silent collision, Li Ping’s heaven-and-earth great millstone actually paused for a moment.

Li Ping noticed that roughly one-third of the faces on the Xuanhuang Evil Thought had fixed their gazes squarely on him.

“Die! Die! Die! Die! Die! Die! Die!”

A sharp curse erupted and swept toward him.

Xu Ke and the other two, who were guarding the formation for Li Ping, all frowned slightly at this moment.

Even the empty face of Holy Emperor Li Ping was faintly impacted by the evil thought, revealing blurred facial features.

Countless tentacles covered in blue-purple tumors extended from the monster shell of the Xuanhuang Evil Thought. They whipped about madly, attempting to break free from the restraints of the Myriad Spirits Formation.

Yet how could this peerless grand formation, pushed to the limit by the Final Dissociation Disc, be broken so easily?

After a brief pause, the purplish-gold vortex slowly began turning once more.

The sound of howling wind and thunder grew even stronger than before.

Moreover, waves of human prayers rang out from within it, forming a unique rhythm in resonance that forcefully suppressed the earth-shaking roars of the Xuanhuang Evil Thought.

“Evil, after all, cannot defeat justice.”

“Look at the state you are in now. Do you still deserve to be called the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao?!”

Li Ping’s calm voice, carrying a hint of mockery, pierced through the numerous noises of the formation confrontation and reached the ears of the Xuanhuang Evil Thought with precision.

“Damn it! Who caused this?!”

After a moment of daze, it exploded with complete fury.

Clearly even the Xuanhuang Evil Thought itself found it hard to accept that it had fallen into such a plight.

Its body, suppressed by the heaven-and-earth great millstone, suddenly showed signs of rising once more to resist.

Li Ping merely nodded slightly. While continuously using words to provoke and incite the Xuanhuang Evil Thought, he focused his mind on directing the Heaven and Earth Myriad Spirits Great Formation.

He drew the fragments stripped from the Xuanhuang Evil Thought into his own body through the vortex.

Negative emotions flowed into Li Ping’s consciousness like trickling streams.

The three guarding the formation saw this and could not help feeling somewhat tense.

Because they were inside the formation, they could sense the terror of the Grand Formation of Myriad Spirits even more clearly.

Even though it had been sealed for several thousand years, it was still a part of the Heavenly Dao of this great world. Yet before this formation, it could be said to have not the slightest power to resist.

The three’s original preparations had become completely unnecessary. They only needed to watch Holy Emperor Li Ping perform.

The more terrifying the formation, the more uncontrollable their worry became.

If the Holy Emperor were affected by the evil thought and lost control...

It would likely be a disaster ten thousand times more dreadful than the Xuanhuang Evil Thought breaking free.

Even though they had great confidence in the Holy Emperor’s strength, they could not help feeling nervous at this moment.

After all, they were still inside the formation themselves.

“There is no need to worry. I am fine.” Li Ping seemed to sense their emotional fluctuations and calmly transmitted an explanation.

Not to mention the firmness of Li Ping’s own will; with the support of the Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Mantra alone, it was impossible for him to have his personality twisted by the Xuanhuang Evil Thought. Moreover, he was not devouring it violently in one gulp. Instead, he was using the Heaven and Earth Great Millstone to slowly grind the Xuanhuang Evil Thought into pieces bit by bit and absorb it gradually, thereby minimizing the side effects.

“Moreover, the wills of billions of Great Qi citizens are anchored to me.”

“Billions of living beings can refine the spirit. This is merely a part of the original Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao. How could it affect me?”

Li Ping’s mind remained clear.

Rather than confronting the “evil thought,” the greater pressure Li Ping faced at this moment was actually digesting the mottled and chaotic memories within the other party’s body.

Although most of them had already blurred after thousands of years of distortion and despair.

These fragmented memory pieces had long been deeply buried in its consciousness. At this moment, as its body was dismembered, they flowed into Li Ping’s mind together.

Scene after scene of broken images flashed rapidly through Li Ping’s mind.

Most were difficult to discern in detail.

Only one segment of the scene appeared repeatedly and continuously.

It was obviously a memory that had been deeply engraved in the Xuanhuang Evil Thought. During the years it was sealed, it had replayed in its mind more than once.

In an endless dark void.

A swiftly flying figure suddenly stopped.

“Why have you stopped?”

The voice from the memory asked.

The original emotion had been somewhat relieved and joyful.

But now it carried a trace of doubt.

The white-robed figure, that is, Mr. Bai, turned around and looked back into the distance.

His expression shifted constantly, extremely complex.

“What exactly is wrong?”

The perception from the memory gradually grew irritable in the deathly silence.

“I cannot leave.”

In the end, Mr. Bai took a deep breath and said slowly.

“Why not leave?”

“The calamity has already formed. The general trend is like this and cannot be reversed. Rather than staying there, it would be better to head for the High Wall and fight with all our might. There may still be a slim chance of survival.” The voice from the memory was first stunned, filled with incomprehension. Then it became somewhat anxious and hurriedly tried to persuade him.

“With the remains left by the first-generation Great Celestial Venerable as shelter, and detonating those few immortal artifacts as propulsion, the two of us working together have a very high chance of crossing the High Wall!”

“We have prepared for so long. How can you suddenly change your mind?!”

Even after so long, Li Ping could still feel the incomprehension and anger in the separated Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao consciousness.

Mr. Bai perhaps knew he was in the wrong and remained silent.

“Say something!”

Only after the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao had finally stopped did Mr. Bai slowly reply.

“That place is my homeland.”

“From birth until now, every person I have known, every person I have heard of, and those I loved or hated have all spent their lives there.”

“Now that it has encountered calamity, I can indeed leave. But what about them?”

Mr. Bai asked quietly.

The Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao could not understand Mr. Bai’s emotions at all. In its view, ensuring its own survival was the most important thing.

“As long as I am here, and we find another suitable world, we can soon give birth to another Xuanhuang Realm.”

“Even the people you know, I can recreate them all.” It could not help explaining.

Mr. Bai merely smiled lightly and shook his head.

Without further hesitation, he turned and flew back toward the Xuanhuang Realm.

Seeing the situation spiraling out of control, the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao grew increasingly anxious.

It continued to persuade him.

Mr. Bai simply turned a deaf ear; his resolve was firm.

“I know you have treated me well.”

“You always say I am the reincarnation of that person.”

“But I know I am myself.”

“Su Bai. An ordinary mortal.”

The Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao’s memory had already become somewhat unstable amid mounting rage.

Yet Mr. Bai’s words remained sporadic but exceptionally clear.

“I never wanted to cultivate or become some immortal.”

“I only want...”

“To save the people I care about.”

“I am sorry.”

“As for the things you gave me, the Great Celestial Venerable’s immortal body and those immortal artifacts...”

“Even this cultivation of mine, I will return them all to you.”

Mr. Bai said with extreme calm.

...

Boom!

The memory burst like a bubble.

Having inherited the power of the Xuanhuang Evil Thought, Li Ping now felt the same and personally experienced the extreme anger of being deceived and betrayed.

“Die! Die!”

It seemed as if the voice of the Xuanhuang Evil Thought began circling continuously in his mind.

Even Li Ping’s aura fluctuated slightly because of it.

Meanwhile, inside the formation, the grotesque evil-thought main body suppressed and ground by the heaven-and-earth great millstone also seemed to sense the change occurring in Li Ping.

It shrieked: “Have you felt it?! Can you understand me?!”

“This pain of being deceived and then sealed for thousands of years!”

......

Unexpectedly, its outpouring of words was abruptly cut off by a single sentence from Li Ping.

“At this moment, I am in human form.”

“Therefore, the one I can empathize with more is actually the one you hate so deeply.”

“I believe Mr. Bai did the right thing.”

“Some matters are things you will never be able to understand. So...”

“You may go in peace.”

Li Ping’s voice gradually turned icy.

The blurred facial features that had faintly appeared under the impact of the Xuanhuang Evil Thought’s memories vanished once more.

A cold and ruthless aura emanated from the Faceless Holy Emperor.

Without the slightest hesitation, he accelerated the rotation of the heaven-and-earth great millstone.

Like an indestructible towering dam, it completely blocked the Evil Thought’s assault.

The three guarding the formation saw this scene and could not help letting out a sigh of relief.

They had truly been somewhat afraid that the Holy Emperor would be influenced by the evil thought.

Especially Yin Shangren.

He joked to Xu Ke: “Actually, my essence is not all that different from the Xuanhuang Evil Thought.”

“If he can swallow it so easily, swallowing me would be just as effortless.”

“It seems that in the future, I should stay as far away from him as possible.”

Xu Ke remained silent.

Gazing at the figure inside the formation that appeared to grow ever taller, he felt a momentary unease in his heart.

He wondered whether handing over the Xuanhuang Evil Thought had been the right choice after all.

The purplish-gold vortex continued to spin without pause, and the sound of wind and thunder grew even louder.

The Xuanhuang Evil Thought’s struggles grew weaker and weaker.

It was only a matter of time before it was completely devoured.

As the power of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao was absorbed and refined, the majestic figure of the Faceless Holy Emperor grew even taller.

It seemed as if it would burst through the dome of the Great Qi small world and reach another realm.

Li Ping’s figure appeared on the sky curtain.

At this moment, the billions of citizens of Great Qi could clearly see the Holy Emperor’s visage.

The boiling public sentiment reached its peak at this instant.

The gathered fortune instantly broke through the purple level entirely, transforming into an incomparably dazzling, resplendent golden light.

Even though it seemed as if this moment would immediately fade back down.

Li Ping sensed something in his heart and seized this fleeting opportunity with extreme acuity.

With a gentle wave of his hand, he scattered the portion of Xuanhuang Evil Thought power absorbed and converted by the heaven-and-earth great millstone into the Great Qi small world.

Countless purplish-gold light dots drifted down like heavenly flowers scattering across the mortal realm.

From the perspective of the ordinary citizens of Great Qi below, they witnessed the Holy Emperor’s bestowal that had been rumored in the past few days.

Those purplish-gold flowers seemed to carry a natural enchanting power that beguiled the heart.

The citizens of Great Qi, disregarding the Holy Emperor’s majesty, all reached out to touch them.

After the purplish-gold flowers entered their bodies, the changes they brought caused those people to kneel on the spot and shout the Holy Emperor’s name in reverence.

The fortune of Great Qi, which had already reached its peak, underwent another transformation as if catalyzed.

This time, under the mountain-and-sea-like unified cheers of countless Great Qi citizens, the originally extremely unstable golden fortune became completely stable.

Billions of living beings roared like mountains and seas.

For a brief moment, even the three formation guardians above Great Qi fell into a daze.

“Is this the rumored ‘when one attains the Dao, even chickens and dogs ascend to heaven’?” Yin Shangren could not help sighing.

Xu Ke unconsciously studied the motionless body of Holy Emperor Li Ping. For some unknown reason, he seemed to see Mr. Bai’s figure on him.

“Perhaps what I did was not wrong.” Xu Ke thought to himself.

Meanwhile, Yi Shu of the Taiyan Sect narrowed his eyes slightly, as if calculating and deducing something. A look of astonishment appeared in his eyes.

The Heavenly Emperor Qi Canon had transformed to its final layer of realm and become completely stable.

With power waxing and waning in opposition, the Xuanhuang Evil Thought finally had not the slightest chance of resistance left.

“I will wait for you.”

“You will eventually share my fate.”

Before its consciousness completely dissipated, the Xuanhuang Evil Thought suddenly uttered such a vicious curse for some unknown reason.

Holy Emperor Li Ping turned a deaf ear to it and remained unmoved.

The heaven-and-earth great millstone pressed down with a boom.

Power from the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao flowed endlessly into his body.

Li Ping felt something in his heart, as if an invisible shackle had been broken.

“The Longevity Immortal realm?”

“Or rather, a different kind of Longevity Immortal realm.”

He mused.

....

At the same time.

Inside the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters, in the Derivation Jade Space.

The feedback from the clone’s breakthrough abruptly stopped at the moment the Holy Emperor reached Longevity Immortal realm power.

Therefore, it did not attract the attention of the others in the space.

Li Fan had already anticipated the Holy Emperor’s breakthrough.

What Li Fan truly cared about was the memories from the Xuanhuang Evil Thought.

“Immortal artifacts?”

“And the first-generation Great Celestial Venerable’s immortal body?”

Li Fan closed his eyes, hiding the shock in them.

“Mr. Bai’s words, ‘I will return them to you.’ Does that mean these things are still within the Xuanhuang Realm?”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1239: Source Power Seals The Immortal Calamity

In the 119th lifetime, Li Fan once fabricated rumors of so-called True Immortal remnants. Using them as bait, he lured cultivators from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance into the small world of the Great Reversal of Life and Death Formation, thereby exposing the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign to the world ahead of time.

Yet he never expected that a True Immortal remains would actually be hidden within the Xuanhuang Realm.

“Moreover, it was no ordinary immortal.”

“The immortal body of the first-generation Great Celestial Venerable?”

“Interesting.”

This single sentence concealed many secrets.

The Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable was not a specific cultivator but represented a certain position and title.

Li Fan had already learned this at the deepest part of the Fallen Immortal Realm.

“My guess back then was correct. As long as one passes the trials of the Fallen Immortal Realm, one should be able to succeed as the new Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable and, very likely, obtain the several immortal artifacts left behind by Mr. Bai at the same time.”

“Then where would Mr. Bai have hidden the immortal body of the first-generation Great Celestial Venerable? Would it still be inside the Fallen Immortal Realm?”

Li Fan’s thoughts turned rapidly.

This first-generation Great Celestial Venerable concerned him greatly.

The remains could be called an immortal body, so without doubt this Great Celestial Venerable had been at least a True Immortal in cultivation.

Why had this True Immortal died? And why, before his fall, had he chosen to leave his remains in the Xuanhuang Realm?

Could the birth of the Fallen Immortal Realm be related to this True Immortal Great Celestial Venerable?

The clone Holy Emperor now stood at the pinnacle of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Factoring in the realm’s own expansion over the years, the Holy Emperor’s strength could be said to be only a hair’s breadth away from that of Mr. Bai in his prime.

With a concrete reference, Li Fan was now certain that Mr. Bai alone could not have created the vast and illusory Fallen Immortal Realm.

Yet during the era of the Ten Great Immortal Sects, no one in the world knew of the Fallen Immortal Realm’s existence.

What could be confirmed was that even if it had not been created personally by Mr. Bai, the Fallen Immortal Realm was undoubtedly deeply connected to him.

The several layers of illusory trials had proven this beyond doubt.

“Although Mr. Bai ‘betrayed’ the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, he did not break his word. He returned the immortal artifacts and the immortal body to the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao. Even his own cultivation, in the process of reversing the principles of immortal and mortal, returned to the heavens along with his death.”

Li Fan pondered inwardly, yet he had no intention of searching everywhere for the Great Celestial Venerable’s immortal body.

Although the Great Celestial Venerable’s immortal body was an unparalleled treasure, it held little use for Li Fan at present. Even if he relied on the immortal body for protection and truly crossed the High Wall, he would never leave rashly while knowing nothing about what lay beyond it.

Not to mention that his current realm was still far from being able to wield immortal spiritual qi.

Simply figuring out how to detonate an immortal artifact and use its explosive power to cross the High Wall without harming himself was already beyond Li Fan’s knowledge.

“I must proceed step by step. After all, as long as I remain within the Xuanhuang Realm or the range of the Darkest Star Sea, everything will eventually be mine.”

Li Fan had a very clear understanding of his own advantages.

“The Holy Emperor clone in this life already possesses enough capital to challenge the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. With various enhancements, his current strength has even surpassed mine. Let us see how he stirs up the winds and rains.”

“This Xuanhuang Realm is like a small pond. Only by thoroughly muddying the waters will those old monsters hiding in every corner of the pond emerge one by one. I need only wait quietly here and watch the changes unfold.”

“However, the clone I left in the Sun family can be nurtured into a future Great Celestial Venerable.”

His attention shifted in an instant.

The clone had now completely taken control of Wu Ninglu’s body.

Yet she remained unaware.

He merely influenced every decision and action of hers in complete silence.

The most important part was for her to sacrifice her own cultivation without hesitation to nourish the fetus in her womb.

If it had been the former Wu Ninglu, she would never have done so.

She had only regarded the unborn child as a bargaining chip to control Sun Ang.

But now that Li Fan had intervened, matters were naturally different.

Ever since the clone had earlier detected the fetus’s unusual nature, he had begun meticulous prenatal education.

Having experienced the pre-birth cultivation phase with the Heavenly Fate black bird Xiao Hei in the Fallen Immortal Realm, Li Fan possessed considerable experience.

Under his personal guidance, the fetus absorbed the mother’s cultivation essence, gradually replenishing the congenital deficiencies caused by the previous seal.

Its body grew stronger day by day, and its consciousness slowly stabilized from weakness.

While still in a state of primordial chaos and knowing nothing of the world, it was led by Li Fan’s clone into one carefully crafted illusion after another.

“It is often said that a sage is born. Yet it is not impossible to create one after birth.”

“So-called judgment is based on deeds, not the heart. No matter what one thinks inwardly, as long as one maintains strict moral standards and behavioral principles toward oneself and never breaks them until death…”

“Then to outsiders, this is a true sage.”

Cultivating from a state of innate primordial chaos in the consciousness was clearly much easier than postnatal education.

What Li Fan was doing now was more like shaping a living being’s personality.

He had no particular prior experience and could only explore through practice. If this proved feasible, then not to mention producing a sage, mass-producing scholars would certainly pose no problem at all.

Li Fan had already noticed that although the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao had once been deceived by Mr. Bai, it still trusted people like Mr. Bai more easily.

Just as with the Faceless Holy Emperor of this life.

“And the fundamental reason likely traces back to Xuantian King Xuanyuan Hong and even the first Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable.” Li Fan mused thoughtfully.

The birth of the fetus within Wu Ninglu’s body had already been planned by Li Fan to occur at the exact moment she devoured the Spirit of heaven and earth and advanced to Dao Integration.

“If the method is handled properly, the power of the heavenly laws condensed within the mother’s body can perhaps be directly transferred to the child through childbirth.”

“It may even give birth to the first cultivator in the Xuanhuang Realm to achieve Dao Integration from birth…”

“Such a phenomenon—who would dare say he is not a sage?”

Li Fan thought this way and found himself somewhat expectant about the fetus’s future.

He had already written the script of its destiny in advance.

“Such a heaven-sent genius would undoubtedly be regarded as the future hope of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. Yet this very person, at the moment when the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi wages full-scale war against the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, would resolutely choose to turn his blade against them.”

“And he would firmly declare that he stood on the side of justice.”

“The scene of the entire Xuanhuang Realm forcing the Immortal Ancestor to reveal himself would surely be quite interesting.”

Vivid images flashed through Li Fan’s mind.

Without needing the Dao Deviation Stone for deduction, he could already roughly predict the future course of this life.

Of course, this was the most ideal state.

Whether it was cultivating an innate sage or whether the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi could truly develop to the point of forcing the Immortal Ancestor to appear would still require a long period of steady growth.

Meanwhile, in the Sun family.

The strand of divine sense hidden inside the Boundless Mirror lived far more leisurely than the clone.

Besides constantly spying on the secrets of the Tianxuan Mirror, the vast majority of its energy was devoted to attempting to lure out the obliterating wind calamity within the mirror.

Because Sun Luyuan had pledged allegiance to the Holy Emperor, he had been fortunate enough to borrow more than a hundred robust You Clan people who had only recently been born.

At this moment they were hidden in the Sun family’s underground secret chamber beside the family’s essence blood pool.

The treatment these You Clan people received was even better than that of their kin in the Holy Dynasty.

They were even granted two hours of rest each day.

Of course this was not out of Li Fan’s kindness; the Boundless Mirror simply could not withstand overly frequent experiments and required time to recover.

“My guess was correct. This obliterating wind calamity will indeed actively attack any power approaching the level of an immortal.”

At this time the Boundless Mirror emitted waves of faint glow as it floated in the air.

Its surface did not reflect the outside scene but instead rapidly flashed through images of numerous civilizations suffering destruction.

A sense of ancient desolation and immense age emerged from the mirror’s body.

The Boundless Mirror, which had only recently recovered by absorbing essence blood, once again became tattered and broken.

Black, formless annihilation wind was drawn out from the Boundless Mirror.

It flew toward a golden square not far away.

What formed this square was, naturally, pure source power.

Although it could not compare to the source power essence of the Holy Emperor clone, it was more than enough to lure the obliterating wind calamity.

The moment the obliterating wind calamity entered the range covered by the source power, its form instantly changed.

The power transcending the mortal world vanished in an instant, and the lowered purity of the source power caused a drop in energy level.

Having lost its target, the obliterating wind calamity paused briefly in place.

It was about to continue spreading outward.

But the source power shifted and formed a grand formation.

It sealed the power of the obliterating wind calamity within.

This grand formation had been designed by Li Fan with reference to the incomplete Grand Formation of the Primordial Mystic Spirit, simulating a relatively static space through formation patterns.

Inside the formation there was nothing at all—an absolute state of void.

It resembled the end-of-the-world scene after everything had been destroyed.

As expected, the obliterating wind calamity was enticed by it; its energy no longer surged violently but slowly went dormant.

The remaining power of the wind calamity inside the Boundless Mirror also subsided.

“Senior’s methods are truly divine. If we do this a few more times, the hidden danger within my body can be completely eliminated.” The Boundless Mirror spirit Sun Luyao said with unrestrained joy.

Li Fan paid him no attention. Instead he carefully stored away the small golden square that now contained the obliterating wind calamity.

He had already produced more than twenty such small squares.

Due to differences in technical proficiency, the strength of the wind calamity contained within each varied.

But without doubt, if the balance within any golden square was broken, it would unleash a terrifying calamity.

Sun Luyao paid no mind to Li Fan’s silence and continued describing his own changes.

“Senior, I have a premonition. Once the wind calamity is completely purged, my control over the Boundless Mirror will reach an entirely new level. At that time I may be able to sense the locations of other Boundless Mirror fragments and fully restore this immortal artifact.”

“Not bad.” Li Fan nodded faintly, his attention still focused on the golden squares.

Having witnessed Li Fan’s methods, the eager-to-claim-credit Sun Luyao hesitated for a moment before he could not help adding, “Senior. Once I am fully restored as an immortal artifact, I may even be able to simultaneously sense the locations of any other immortal artifacts that might still remain in the Xuanhuang Realm.”

Upon hearing Sun Luyao’s words, Li Fan finally turned his head slightly.

“Oh? You are so certain?”

Sun Luyao’s spirits rose. He hurriedly explained, “Beings like us can always sense one another to some degree. Especially since I, the Boundless Mirror, also possess the ability to measure the heavens and investigate the earth. If other immortal artifacts truly still exist in the Xuanhuang Realm, then after I am restored the probability of finding them would be quite high.”

Li Fan smiled. “I see you have been locked away too long and are simply eager to go out for a stroll.”

Sun Luyao gave an embarrassed laugh. “Of course that is one reason. But my desire to help Senior search for immortal artifacts is absolutely sincere.”

Li Fan glanced at him. “Let us first purge the wind calamity completely.”

Li Fan’s expression grew somewhat grave. “Do not be fooled by how weak it appears now; the root cause still shows no sign of true reduction.”

“After all, it is a power that transcends the mortal world. We cannot be careless.”

Sun Luyao nodded deeply in agreement.

Holy Dynasty of Great Qi.

More than ten days had passed since Li Ping had completely devoured the Xuanhuang evil thoughts.

Having gained such a massive amount of power, Li Ping, whose strength had surged, also needed time to digest and adapt.

He finally allowed himself a rare period of rest.

Yet inside the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi the atmosphere had never been more prosperous.

The fortune of the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi was now tightly bound to Holy Emperor Li Ping.

Under normal circumstances, cultivating the Heavenly Emperor Qi Canon caused the growth of imperial fortune to drive the emperor’s strength forward.

But at this moment in Great Qi, the rare reverse occurred: the Holy Emperor’s breakthrough in strength fed back into the dynasty’s fortune.

And once the people’s hearts and the dynasty’s fortune were further strengthened, they in turn provided another round of nourishment to the Holy Emperor.

Thus, in addition to the strength gained from devouring the Xuanhuang evil thoughts, Li Ping’s Heavenly Emperor Qi Canon had, shortly after the breakthrough, once again approached the point of transformation.

This time, however, the original texts contained no records of the path ahead.

There was no road left to follow.

“Something akin to the Longevity Immortal realm below True Immortal is already the limit of the fortune path.”

“After all, relying solely on imperial fortune to break the shackles and achieve True Immortal…”

“Is still too difficult.”

“Unless it is a Great Holy Dynasty capable of spanning the star sea.”

Li Ping shook his head slightly and suppressed the thought.

Given the premise of the Darkest Star Sea, even if the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi unified the Xuanhuang Realm it would still be insufficient to support him in breaking through to the True Immortal realm.

“Since this path is blocked, I must find another way.”

“Now that the Xuanhuang evil thoughts have been devoured by me, if I wish to grow even stronger next…”

“First, there is the supreme treasure Fishing Pond.”

“Second…”

The Holy Emperor sat upright upon the Holy Emperor’s throne. In his mind appeared the figures of native Xuanhuang Realm creations such as Roumu and the Stone Tablet.

“As parts of the Xuanhuang Realm’s laws, the Heavenly Dao has suffered great calamity while they live on comfortably.”

“Since the gifts of heaven and earth are useless to them, it is time to reclaim what heaven and earth bestowed.”

A vicious aura surged from the Faceless Holy Emperor.
