Next

Chapter 1240: Where The Rainbow Light Vanishes

As the Holy Emperor of Great Qi, he had to remain expressionless whether happy or angry. Even with no outsiders present, this was still necessary.

“Reclaiming the power of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao does not necessarily require killing them.”

“If they voluntarily return the power, I would rather not create more killing karma. But if...”

Li Ping’s heart turned icy cold.

Ruomu and the Stone Tablet were each alone, yet their combat strength was equivalent to that of a Longevity Immortal Realm cultivator. Fighting them would serve as a good test before the decisive battle against the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

“When a lion hunts a rabbit, it must use full strength. I cannot underestimate them.”

“Ruomu is weaker, so I will start with him. I must also avoid the Stone Tablet’s notice...”

A plan gradually took shape in the Holy Emperor’s mind.

As one of the three major branches of Ruomu that had fallen into the mortal world, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree would surely maintain a specific connection with the main body.

Since Ruomu had been severely wounded by cultivators, he had become deranged and no longer appeared in the world.

Perhaps he could first locate Ruomu’s true hiding place through the Earth Vein Ancient Tree and then weaken his power.

With a thought, Wang Xuanba soon arrived at the Holy Emperor’s seat.

The Xuanhuang evil thoughts had been completely devoured by Li Ping, so there was no need for him to remain at the peak of the sealed small world.

After receiving the Holy Emperor’s transmission not long ago, he had already returned to the Holy Capital.

The heavenly grace that descended during Li Ping’s breakthrough had also been partially enjoyed by the clone in the small world.

At this moment, Wang Xuanba’s realm had reached the late Nascent Soul stage and was not far from Soul Transformation.

However, Wang Xuanba’s strength was mainly reflected in his various exotic beast abilities. Realm level was not particularly important to him.

“The Dao One insect has a variant capable of eroding spirit wood. Cultivate some first; I will have great use for them soon.”

Wang Xuanba nodded and accepted the order.

...

Just as Holy Emperor Li Ping prepared to reveal his fangs to the Xuanhuang Realm for the first time.

At the far end of the distant Darkest Star Sea, outside the Xuan Immortal Ark, Holy Embryo Li Fan encountered a crisis.

The source of the danger was not the residual True Immortal power in the star sea or some unknown cultivator.

It was the Sea of Relics where he currently resided.

Not long ago, this originally stable ruin had begun to tremble faintly for some unknown reason.

The tremors in the Sea of Relics were secondary. More importantly, once the continuous ruins no longer felt like a single entity, the suction from the High Wall deeper in the star sea became even more pronounced.

In Holy Embryo Li Fan’s eyes, the entire star sea seemed to have suddenly risen like a tide, sweeping up countless gathered relics and dragging them toward the shore.

The Xuan Immortal Ark had activated a special contingency plan.

Even though the Ark remained invisible and hidden in the void, Holy Embryo Li Fan could still clearly sense its movement.

It was not advancing in a fixed direction but constantly shifting positions in a dodging and weaving manner.

Like a compass that never stopped rotating, it deflected the suction from the High Wall.

Holy Embryo Li Fan had initially thought of copying this method to resist the High Wall’s pull.

But it was not as easy as it appeared.

“The Immortal Ark’s rotation does not use itself as the sole pivot; it connects faintly with the entire Sea of Relics. Only by borrowing the near-infinite number of relics does it barely evade the force.”

Holy Embryo Li Fan felt a power dragging his body.

Even while flying his sword in the opposite direction, he was sinking bit by bit.

As he grew farther from the Immortal Ark and was about to be pulled deeper into the Sea of Relics, Holy Embryo Li Fan could not help but consider forcing his way onto the Xuan Immortal Ark for shelter.

Even if it led to a bitter battle, it would be better than crashing headfirst into the invisible High Wall.

Just as he prepared to act, a seven-colored rainbow light suddenly lit up in the Darkest Star Sea from the direction of the Xuanhuang Realm and the Immortal Ruins, instantly drawing his attention.

He was slightly stunned at first, then quickly realized it was the sixteen Dao Integration cultivators summoned by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance at the Immortal Ancestor’s call.

“They arrived so quickly?”

Holy Embryo Li Fan could not help feeling surprised.

Even accounting for the different time flows in various regions of the star sea, the rainbow light’s arrival was much earlier than Li Fan had expected.

“Wait, could this sudden change in suction be meant to guide the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s rainbow light?”

Watching the rainbow light streak across the star sea, this thought emerged in Li Fan’s mind.

After pondering for a moment, he gave up his futile struggle and used the suction to fly deeper into the star sea as well.

The rainbow light moved extremely fast, but within the ruin-filled Sea of Relics it inevitably slowed down.

Li Fan protected himself with a sword formation and quietly followed behind.

Li Fan had originally assumed the Sea of Relics had a limited range.

But after tracking for roughly three days by Xuanhuang Realm time and still not leaving the continuous ruins, Li Fan finally gained a rough understanding of the scale of the once-flourishing immortal cultivation world in the Darkest Star Sea.

Beyond the relics there was no visible end, and no distinction of east, west, south, or north.

Yet the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s rainbow light clearly had special guidance and maintained a fixed direction.

The route it took seemed meticulously calculated to avoid all dangerous zones within the ruins.

Thanks to them, Holy Embryo Li Fan followed smoothly behind.

Another four days passed.

The dense broken relics ahead suddenly became sparse.

Li Fan’s spirits lifted; he knew he was about to leave the Sea of Relics.

Without obstruction, the rainbow light gradually accelerated.

Only half a day later, a scene that Li Fan would never forget appeared before his eyes.

A great wall stood in the dark void ahead.

At first, Li Fan thought this was the so-called High Wall.

But after clearly seeing the details of the great wall, Li Fan suddenly understood.

The “bricks” that made up the endless stretching wall were clearly one ruined world after another.

Just like the wreckage in the Sea of Relics, what lay ahead was the ruins of worlds themselves.

Li Fan estimated in his heart that even after devouring many immortal cultivation world fragments and far surpassing the Xuanhuang Realm of ancient times in scale, it would still amount to nothing more than an insignificant section within this Remnant World Great Wall.

“Just how many immortal cultivation realms were destroyed in the ancient catastrophe?”

“And by what mighty force were they all gathered here?”

“The High Wall…”

Li Fan gazed into the distance with all his might, trying to locate the High Wall that was known by name but never seen in form.

Yet in the darkness, apart from the countless world fragments, no other anomaly could be seen.

After a brief moment of distraction, Li Fan realized he was still uncontrollably flying toward the ruined world wreckage.

And his speed was increasing faster and faster.

“If I crash into it like this, even with my Heavenly God Sword Body, I will perish in both body and soul.” Li Ping frowned inwardly.

But he quickly locked onto the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s rainbow light ahead of him.

For some unknown reason, that rainbow light showed no sign of slowing down at all.

Instead it actively accelerated once more, speeding toward the front.

“This…”

After pondering for a moment, Holy Embryo Li Fan decisively followed closely behind.

“The Immortal Ancestor went to such great lengths to summon these Dao Integration cultivators. He cannot possibly let them die in vain.”

As if to confirm Li Fan’s guess, after advancing a distance into the ruined realm sea the rainbow light suddenly ignored the High Wall’s suction, turned in a new direction, and flew toward one of the world fragments.

It was as if something there possessed an attraction even stronger than the High Wall’s echoing star power.

A trace of unease flashed inexplicably through Holy Embryo Li Fan’s heart.

“The Immortal Ancestor?”

After drawing near the spot where the rainbow light had turned, he too sensed that power.

Yet under the warning in his heart, Holy Embryo Li Fan did not follow it over.

Instead he continued flying deeper into the remnant world sea for some time. Thanks to the possible influence of the Immortal Ancestor’s power, Li Fan’s speed gradually slowed.

He finally found a stable point and came to a halt.

However, Li Ping knew this stability was only temporary.

As soon as that suspected Immortal Ancestor power disappeared, he would once again lose control and rush toward the High Wall.

Li Fan tried flying into the ruined world ahead to take shelter.

But these corpses of worlds clearly would not offer protection to cultivators the way the Xuanhuang Realm did.

“It seems my end is near.”

Just as Holy Embryo Li Fan was thinking this, not far away at the original destination of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s rainbow light, the sudden disappearance of an aura made him jolt sharply.

Before he could react, a second and then a third Dao Integration cultivator’s aura vanished in succession.

Dao Integration cultivators, even those forcibly accelerated to the realm, were still supreme experts beneath the Longevity Immortal Realm.

They were existences countless cultivators dreamed of becoming.

Yet in this dark void, within the Remnant World Great Wall, they died without the slightest sound of struggle.

“What is happening?”

“Did the Immortal Ancestor do this?”

“He went to such painstaking effort to call them here, only to slaughter them?”

While Holy Embryo Li Fan felt shocked and doubtful, the speed at which the Dao Integration cultivators’ auras vanished from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s rainbow light did not slow.

In the blink of an eye, all sixteen Dao Integration cultivators were dead.

“If they die here, will they return their Dao to heaven?” Holy Embryo Li Fan suddenly wondered.

“Wait, return the Dao to heaven…”

Li Fan thought of something and immediately focused, sensing the spot where the rainbow light had disappeared.

Although the cultivators’ life auras had vanished, the power of the heavenly laws still lingered faintly there.

Yet it was fading in a regular pattern, like ice slowly melting into water.

“This…” Li Fan’s heart shook. He now knew exactly what had just occurred.

And next, the originally turbulent ruined realm sea inexplicably stabilized once more.

This further confirmed Li Fan’s judgment.

“They actually used the Spirit of Heaven and Earth within the Dao Integration cultivators to repair the Remnant World Great Wall?”

Li Fan felt both shocked and furious.

“All this effort just to rob one wall to mend another?”

Li Fan found it utterly absurd.

Yet the complete stabilization of the Remnant World Great Wall and the gradual weakening of the High Wall’s echoing star power forced him to believe it.

Although he very much wanted to rush to the spot where the rainbow light had vanished to see for himself, Holy Embryo Li Ping was still not prepared to face the Immortal Ancestor directly. Especially since this Holy Embryo body of his was formed from the sword laws of the Xuanhuang Realm.

If he charged over rashly, his fate would likely be the same as those sixteen Dao Integration cultivators: reduced to material for repairing the Remnant World Great Wall.

Although Holy Embryo Li Fan did not fear death, dying in such a stifling way was truly too frustrating.

“The great upheaval in the Xuanhuang Realm originated from the High Wall’s echoing star power.”

“Judging from the scene before me, this echoing star power and the Remnant World Great Wall have reached a subtle balance. Under normal circumstances it would not be strong enough to reach the Xuanhuang Realm at all.”

“So the Remnant World Great Wall lost balance due to external force, which caused the star sea to fall into turmoil?”

“…”

“This continuous stretch of world ruins that has existed since ancient times can enter an unbalanced state.”

Holy Embryo Li Fan could not help recalling how, before—when he had rampaged through the Abyss of the Xuanhuang Realm and destroyed a fragment of a broken world.

Then a figure resembling the Immortal Ancestor had appeared and patched it with a new world fragment.

“Could it be that this very action triggered the subsequent chain of events?”

Li Fan suddenly felt a sense of wry helplessness.

“What is this? Tear down the east wall to mend the west, then tear down the west to mend the east?”

After some thought, Li Fan felt the possibility was actually quite high.

“After all, from the current real situation, he truly managed to reverse this catastrophe.”

“…”

Li Fan remained silent.

He carefully recalled and reviewed the details of the matter once more.

“There are still some strange points.”

“Since the state of the Remnant World Great Wall can be so easily disrupted, the Immortal Ancestor should have been extremely cautious when he took away the world fragment back then. Acting so carelessly does not seem like the Immortal Ancestor’s style at all.”

“Unless the operating mechanism of the Remnant World Great Wall is too profound, and even the Immortal Ancestor only half understands it. Or else…”

“The node inside the Chizha Abyss was critically important to the Xuanhuang Realm’s restoration. It could not be lost. Therefore, even if it would cause the Remnant World Great Wall to lose balance, the Immortal Ancestor had no choice but to do it.”

Holy Embryo Li Fan’s thoughts turned rapidly.

With the current conditions, he could not determine which guess was correct.

But he could now confirm that the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor was inside this Remnant World Great Wall.

Or perhaps it was the Heavenly Doctor.

Holy Embryo Li Fan carefully concealed his aura once more. Without acting rashly, he quietly hid himself.

Fortunately the energy here was extremely complex and chaotic.

Although the echoing star power was suppressed by the Remnant World Great Wall, it still rippled like waves through the void.

This perfectly masked the faint fluctuation Li Fan had exposed.

He patiently waited more than ten days until the warning in his heart completely disappeared.

Only then did Holy Embryo Li Fan slowly fly toward the spot where the rainbow light had vanished.

He flew very slowly. The distance that could have been covered in the blink of an eye took him nearly half a day.

After confirming no one was around, Li Fan began to examine the small world he was in.

It seemed no different from the other world fragments here.

Yet for some unknown reason, Li Fan felt a sense of familiarity with this place.

“What exactly is…”

After a while, Li Fan realized.

“The Congyun Sea…”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1241: Cat Treasure Second Transformation

“Congyun Sea was once part of it, or rather, a fragment with a very similar aura that also belonged to the Heavenly Law Realm...”

In the past, Li Fan had used the Canghai Pearl to establish his foundation and merged his consciousness with the Congyun Sea.

In this life, the Holy Emperor’s clone had directly devoured the remnant consciousness of the Congyun Sea. It could be said that Li Fan was extremely familiar with the Congyun region.

Therefore, even though this world fragment had long since become barren and deathly silent for ages, Li Fan only stood there for a moment before still sensing that trace of familiarity from it.

Li Fan suddenly realized in his heart: “It is probably because the Immortal Ancestor took action to repair it, and because this place is his homeland.”

Strolling through the ruins of the Heavenly Law Realm, even though thousands of years had passed, the terrifying traces of the world’s destruction could still be clearly seen.

No complete buildings could be found on the surface, only scattered remnants of broken walls and ruins dotted like stars. Occasionally, cultivator skeletons could be seen in the ruins. From their postures, it did not seem as if they had died in battle with others.

“It seems the catastrophe occurred extremely suddenly, without even a chance to struggle.”

Although he had already destroyed the Xuanhuang Realm in various ways, each time after the Xuanhuang Realm’s destruction, Li Fan had returned to the peak of Jieli Mountain through [Truth]. He had only personally witnessed a few extinct worlds.

A sense of oppression surged into Holy Embryo Li Fan’s heart without reason.

It was not that his state of mind had been shaken, but rather the instinctive reaction a living being would have in such an environment.

This world fragment was not very large, and Li Fan quickly searched through it. He did not find any valuable clues.

“However, this remnant world is relatively well preserved. At present, the Immortal Ancestor has only refrained from acting due to concerns about the balance of the Remnant World Great Wall. It is likely that before long, He will still take it away.”

“This place is not suitable for lingering.”

Li Fan was very decisive and quietly transformed into a streak of flowing light to leave the area.

After thinking for a moment, he did not return directly outside the Xuan Immortal Ark but instead chose to seclude himself here within the Remnant World Great Wall.

First, because there was already a strand of his consciousness lurking inside the Xuan Immortal Ark, possessing and controlling Zhao Xian. Under the current circumstances, he did not need to take action himself.

Second, because...

“The upper limit of the laws here is even higher.”

“Is it because it is closer to the High Wall? Or because of the remnants of the thousand worlds?”

“If environmental factors are disregarded, this is truly an excellent place for comprehending the Dao and cultivating.”

Holy Embryo Li Fan had achieved his Foundation Establishment and Cave-Heaven through the Xuanhuang Sword Dao, giving him exceptionally keen sensitivity to laws.

If the intact Xuanhuang Realm could be considered a low point in the height of the law upper limit within the Darkest Star Sea, then here, the Remnant World Great Wall, represented the highest peak of the law upper limit in the entire Star Sea.

Even though affected by the reverberating star power, making the rhythm of the laws difficult to grasp, and the laws themselves being incomplete and extremely chaotic.

But if one could seize those fleeting opportunities to comprehend them, then a single moment of enlightenment might surpass a hundred or even a thousand years of ordinary bitter cultivation.

“The supreme principles of the mortal realm were brutally torn into fragments by the power of the True Immortal Seal Script from the immortal realm, scattering throughout the Star Sea.”

“Later survivors like myself can instead more easily sense and comprehend them. Under normal circumstances...”

In Holy Embryo Li Fan’s mind, he instantly recalled the fragmented images from the 119th life, occasionally seen through the blood-colored long river, of the ancient Xuanhuang Realm’s era when it interacted with other immortal cultivation realms.

“At that time, all realms still existed and everything was in perfect order. For cultivators, not to mention comprehending them, even coming into contact with these Darkest principles was extremely difficult. Anyone who encountered such an opportunity by chance would likely have hope of treading the immortal path.”

Holy Embryo Li Fan’s thoughts flowed slowly as countless insights into the Dao surged into his mind.

Fearing that the sword light might attract the Immortal Ancestor and the Heavenly Doctor who might exist within the Remnant World Great Wall, even with thousands of inspirations, Li Fan did not actually slash out with his sword.

He only simulated it in his mind.

Even so, it still filled him with delight.

At the same time, a thought could not help but emerge: “Having stayed here year-round, it seems the cultivation of the Immortal Ancestor and the Heavenly Doctor has already reached the peak of the mortal realm.”

During his previous journey through the Star Sea, he had already harbored some suspicions. Now he was completely certain of this guess.

Li Fan had no doubts whatsoever about the talent of the Immortal Ancestor and the Heavenly Doctor.

Their ability to comprehend the supreme laws of the Star Sea was definitely no lower than that of the Holy Embryo, and likely higher.

But compared to before, the slight apprehension in Li Fan’s heart had already vanished.

“They have indeed reached the upper limit of the Darkest Star Sea, but that is all. The immortal path is severed, with no hope of becoming immortal...”

A faint gleam flashed in Li Fan’s eyes.

The reason the world regarded the Dharma-Transmitting Immortal Ancestor as unmatched was simply that no one knew his exact cultivation realm.

Although Li Fan had previously held a vague guess that the Ancestor was at the peak of the mortal realm below the True Immortal level, he could not precisely quantify or sense the difference between this peak and the Longevity Immortal realm.

Now, standing at the foot of the High Wall and personally experiencing the upper limit of power in this Darkest Star Sea, all of Li Fan’s doubts vanished.

Having a clear target made pursuit far easier.

At the same time, Li Fan felt fortunate. The reason the Immortal Ancestor Heavenly Sovereign and the Heavenly Doctor had remained at this realm for thousands of years was that the Star Sea’s upper limit was fixed at this level.

It was as if their cultivation had stalled for a long time, which in turn gave Li Fan the time he needed to catch up.

“Moreover, with the True Immortal from the Immortal Ruins present, even if they found a way to break through the mortal realm, they would still have to weigh the consequences for the safety of the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“In the race between the tortoise and the hare, the tortoise is the victor.”

Holy Embryo Li Fan set aside all distracting thoughts and calmly began to comprehend the supreme laws of the Star Sea drifting in this place.

...

Xuanhuang Realm.

After a period of cultivation, Wang Xuanba had successfully bred a sufficient number of the Dao One Insects that the Holy Emperor had specified.

“Heaven and earth spirit trees each have their own functions. My little bugs have one more wonderful trait: after they devour a tree, the spirit tree does not die immediately. Instead, it gradually transforms into a insect-infused spirit tree. The original consciousness can freely choose whether to remain, and it can even merge other consciousness sources into the Dao One swarm to achieve a clone-like existence.” Wang Xuanba said proudly while showing off to Ou Shangtian.

“Ah, I still feel that the path of beast taming is ever-changing and far more interesting. Formations are truly dull.” A trace of envy flashed in Ou Shangtian’s eyes.

Wang Xuanba smiled with a hint of schadenfreude. “Who told you to aim too high and want to learn everything you see? You’ve displeased Master.”

“Yes, until my formation skills reach Master’s requirements, I must not be half-hearted. Sigh, it feels so far away.” Ou Shangtian sighed.

“By the way, Master also mentioned earlier that he will have me act as bait in the future to lure out a hidden expert from the Xuanhuang Realm. That person’s strength is not weak. If my formations are not refined enough, I might even be in mortal danger. I wonder if it’s true or not.” Ou Shangtian said somewhat casually.

Upon hearing this, Wang Xuanba’s expression changed. “Junior Brother, do not speak recklessly! Since Master said so, it must be true. You have also met Senior Xu Ke before. The hidden experts in the Xuanhuang Realm truly cannot be underestimated. Junior Brother, you must take this seriously!”

Ou Shangtian nodded in apparent agreement on the surface, but in his heart he still felt somewhat dismissive.

He had seen that Xu Ke as well. The man was nothing more than a decaying old fellow on the verge of death.

Although his strength was far greater than Ou Shangtian’s, with the Seven-Colored Jade Void Flowing Light on his person, if he wanted to escape, even Xu Ke could not stop him!

“I want to see just how high this expert Master mentioned really is!”

Inside the Holy Dynasty’s treasure vault beneath the Holy Emperor’s seat.

Li Ping glanced faintly toward the direction of Ou Shangtian and Wang Xuanba and shook his head.

He had naturally sensed Ou Shangtian’s thoughts.

Youthful arrogance, combined with many divine treasures at his side, was understandable.

“But if he does not diligently study formations and relies only on the seven-colored divine light, escaping from the Stone Tablet’s hands will be rather difficult.”

Li Ping smiled inwardly but had no intention of reminding him again. It would be good for the boy to suffer some setbacks. Stone Tablet would not rashly strike a killing blow anyway.

As long as the Stone Tablet appeared, Li Ping would personally deal with it.

Setting aside the matter of retrieving the Stone Tablet and then handling Ruomu, Li Ping now turned his attention elsewhere. He reached out and gently stroked the orange kitten resting on his shoulder.

Compared to before, Cat Treasure had grown noticeably larger.

Its fur had changed from sparse to dense and was beginning to appear soft and smooth.

What concerned the Holy Emperor even more was Cat Treasure’s second transformation.

Previously, Cat Treasure could transform into a small square box. When the box emitted light, it could replicate anything it covered.

Its limit was still unclear, but at the very least, replicating a World Artifact refined from the power of an entire realm posed no problem, though it consumed a bit too much energy.

Not long ago, when Wang Xuanba had brought the spirit-tree-devouring Dao One Insects to report to the Holy Emperor, Li Ping had for the first time sensed Cat Treasure actively conveying its thoughts.

After Wang Xuanba left, Li Ping began conducting experiments.

He placed a swarm of roughly tens of thousands of Dao One Insects in front of Cat Treasure.

At first, Cat Treasure appeared somewhat fearful and observed from afar, yet its eyes revealed clear longing.

It advanced two steps, then retreated one, slowly approaching the Dao One swarm.

The Dao One Insects, which were usually extremely sensitive to foreign objects from the outside world, showed no reaction whatsoever to Cat Treasure’s approach.

Finally reaching the swarm, a faint blue glow emerged from Cat Treasure’s body. Under Li Ping’s watchful gaze, it transformed into a blue light sphere.

The sphere enveloped the Dao One Insects.

Inside the blue light sphere, the swarm seemed to freeze instantly and fell completely still.

On the surface of the sphere, various diagrams of the Dao One Insects’ bodily structures flickered from time to time.

Li Ping’s expression grew serious.

The light sphere did not last long; this form seemed to consume an enormous amount of energy.

Before long, Cat Treasure returned to its small kitten form and climbed back onto Li Fan’s shoulder.

It lazily lay down once more.

The previously motionless Dao One Insects resumed their movement.

Yet from beginning to end, they remained completely unaware of what had happened to them.

“It is not that the Dao One Insects’ intelligence is too low. Even if they were transformed into cultivators from the Xuanhuang Realm, the result would probably not be much better.”

“This blue light of Cat Treasure...”

It was a mysterious power that Li Ping had never encountered before.

His heart stirred. He patted Cat Treasure.

Although the little orange cat seemed somewhat reluctant, for the sake of the golden source power essence that Li Ping had fed it over a long period, it still perked up.

Blue light enveloped its body and shattered in an instant.

Moments later, Cat Treasure had astonishingly transformed into a swarm of Dao One Insects!

It was identical in every way to the swarm previously covered by the blue light sphere not far away!

The entire transformation had occurred right before Li Ping’s eyes, yet he could detect no difference whatsoever between the two.

“It is not a transformation technique, but a complete change in life form. That is why no distinction can be sensed.”

Even though he prided himself as the destined Holy Emperor who had transformed the Xuanhuang Realm, at this moment Li Ping could not help but let out a heartfelt sigh of admiration for this godlike technique.

“Moreover, the change is not limited to merely replicating the original body. It can also evolve completely according to the characteristics of the replicated entity.”

A realization dawned on Li Ping. A speck of golden light appeared at his fingertip.

Cat Treasure understood his meaning. The Dao One swarm suddenly swelled. In a visible change, the number of bugs grew from tens of thousands to hundreds of thousands, multiplying tenfold in an instant.

The Dao One Insects swarm that Cat Treasure had become returned to Li Ping’s side as if seeking praise.

After receiving permission, the blue light shattered once more, and it reverted to its orange kitten form.

It happily swallowed the source power essence.

“This is actually another form of replication. But it replicates living beings and can also bring about changes to Cat Treasure itself.”

“Such a technique...”

Li Ping fell silent.

In his memory, only treasures from the immortal realm could achieve something like this.

Yet Cat Treasure showed no trace whatsoever of immortal spiritual energy.

“Beyond the High Wall...”

An undeniable fact had become clear.

“It is just unknown what level of treasure Cat Treasure is outside the High Wall.”

Li Ping’s heart remained steadfast; he was not shaken by the possible gap between what lay inside and outside the High Wall.

“Item replication, life-form imitation... I wonder if Cat Treasure has any other functions?”
