Chapter 1242: Heavenly Dao As Nourishment

The power that originally served to protect the earth veins was devoured by the [Dao One] insects and instead transformed into nourishing sustenance.

At first only a fist-sized clump of black shadow appeared. In an instant it spread like thick ink dissolving in water, gradually dyeing the surging river of earth veins black.

What appeared even more eerie was that the black torrent eroded by the [Dao One] insects retained identical aura and energy fluctuations to the original yellow earth veins, differing only in color. Ordinary perception could not detect this mutation at all.

However, Wang Xuanba, who possessed his own clone within the [Dao One] swarm, could clearly sense the terrifying speed at which the swarm was strengthening and expanding.

Following the earth veins, it spread fiercely outward from Yueyang Prefecture as the center toward every region of the Xuanhuang Realm.

In addition to horizontal expansion, the [Dao One] insects also continued eroding downward along the dense, hanging roots of the massive tree in the vertical dimension.

“The wonders of creation truly reach this extent. If not for Master’s guidance, I would never have imagined that such a colossal tree was hidden beneath the Xuanhuang Realm.” Wang Xuanba sighed inwardly.

Through the images transmitted by the [Dao One] insects bodies, Wang Xuanba saw the originally dense but motionless roots begin to sway slightly, as if coming alive, once they were blackened.

A vast consciousness swept back and forth across the blackened sections. Compared with before, it now carried a trace of unease and uncertainty. Wang Xuanba knew this must be the consciousness of the Earth Vein Ancient Tree.

Yet it was destined to be futile. Before the mutated [Dao One] insects fully activated their complete insect transformation and merely lurked within, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree could not detect that its body had been infected.

“It clearly knows something is wrong with its body but cannot find the cause. As the condition worsens, the fear and despair in its heart accumulate. Attacking the mind first is indeed the superior strategy. Master’s plan is truly masterful.” A cold smile appeared on Wang Xuanba’s face. Sensing the moment was right, he issued a command in his mind.

The surging dark shadow swarm that had been rapidly expanding came to a complete halt in an instant. The [Dao One] insect swarm fell into deep slumber and lay dormant within the body of the Earth Vein Ancient Tree.

The yellow earth-vein power continued to wash over it, seemingly suppressing the shadow. Yet the black did not disappear; it simply settled at the bottom of the yellow.

Viewed from a macro perspective, it looked as though a dim spot had appeared within the Xuanhuang earth veins.

“Master said that the earth veins ultimately affect the countless living beings on the surface. Even the slightest change could cause mountains to collapse and the earth to crack. If handled improperly, it might even provoke hostility from the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao…”

Just moments ago, Wang Xuanba had indeed faintly sensed a disturbance in the will of heaven and earth. However, once the [Dao One] insect entered dormancy, that disturbance quickly vanished.

“Interesting.” Wang Xuanba narrowed his eyes and pondered in place for a moment.

Then, without pausing, he hurried toward the next earth-vein node. He intended to plant sixteen such black spots inside the body of the Xuanhuang Earth Vein Ancient Tree.

The plan proceeded smoothly.

Except for the final few instances, when the increasingly uneasy ancient tree instinctively triggered violent surges in the earth-vein currents and inflicted minor injuries on him, the rest of the swarm shadows were placed perfectly.

Even the Earth Vein Ancient Tree’s aware struggles proved completely useless. On the contrary, they caused the [Dao One] swarm to spread through the earth veins at an explosive rate, making those shadow spots noticeably larger than the others.

Half a month later, Wang Xuanba, having perfectly completed his mission, returned to the Holy Capital.

Inside the Holy Emperor’s hall, several of Li Ping’s disciples were already waiting in advance.

Wang Xuanba was somewhat surprised. These senior brothers and sisters had each been cultivating according to Master’s instructions and rarely had the chance to meet. Some he had never even seen in person and had only heard that Master had accepted such a disciple. He had not expected them all to gather together today.

“It seems Master is finally about to make a major move.” Wang Xuanba immediately grew excited.

He strode forward, knelt on one knee before the Holy Emperor, and reported the mission’s progress.

The Faceless Holy Emperor waved his hand lightly and helped Wang Xuanba to his feet.

“Well done.”

Rarely hearing words of praise from the Holy Emperor, Wang Xuanba could not help but raise his head proudly.

“With sixteen swarms of [Dao One] insects implanted, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree is doomed. Which of you will go on behalf of your master to subdue this tree?” Li Ping then asked calmly.

The six disciples below all responded in unison: “This disciple is willing to share Master’s burdens!”

Li Ping looked at these disciples who spoke from the heart and nodded in satisfaction.

The six disciples consisted of three males and three females.

They were Sun Erlang, Su Yuqing, Wang Xuanba, Lin Ling, Ou Shangtian, and Daoist Child Liuli.

The first five had been taken in and carefully trained by Li Ping for some time. Daoist Child Liuli, however, had been accepted on a sudden whim after Li Ping discovered the extraordinary nature of Cat Treasure.

Yet Li Ping harbored no ill intentions. He simply planned to nurture her and later exploit her special connection with the Fishing Pond to obtain more treasures from it. Li Ping’s thoughts were sincere, so he openly told her the truth. Daoist Child Liuli was also highly perceptive of people’s hearts. Once she sensed Li Ping’s honest intentions, she gradually accepted and grew accustomed to this master.

Zhong Shentong kept reminding her to obey the Holy Emperor’s commands faithfully. Coupled with occasional private lessons from Daoist Hundred Flowers, this little Daoist child’s progress became the fastest among all the disciples.

Incidentally, ever since arriving in the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi, Zhong Shentong had completely embraced the idle life he loved most. The continuously rising spiritual energy concentration in the Holy Dynasty allowed him to live comfortably for several more decades without breaking through his current realm, so he simply stopped cultivating altogether.

With the Holy Emperor’s special permission, he spent every day happily casting a line in the Fishing Pond. His days truly made others envious.

Returning to the scene inside the Holy Emperor’s hall, the disciples all looked eager upon receiving the Holy Emperor’s task.

Li Ping, however, had already made his decision.

“Lin Ling, you will go with Xuanba. If the ancient tree knows its place, then all is well. If it remains stubborn…”

Li Ping smiled faintly: “You may replace it.”

As the Spiritwood Realm incarnation, Lin Ling had been conquering other small worlds for the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi ever since Li Ping awakened her in this life. The tangible, almost solid killing intent that she did not conceal made her the most awe-inspiring among Li Ping’s six disciples.

The others raised no objections when Li Ping assigned the task to her.

The Faceless Holy Emperor passed a wheel-shaped treasure to Lin Ling through the air: “With this wheel, you can freely control the [Dao One] insects. Once the insects reach the critical stage, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree should have no room for resistance. Still, you must guard against it acting desperately…”

After giving a few final simple instructions, Li Ping watched Lin Ling and Wang Xuanba leave the Holy Emperor’s hall.

The hall fell quiet for a moment.

Ou Shangtian gathered his courage and asked: “Master, regarding the matter you mentioned earlier that you would entrust to me…”

Li Ping smiled slightly: “It will be soon. Just make your preparations.”

The remaining three disciples also turned their gazes toward him.

As Li Ping’s senior disciple, Sun Erlang had long assisted in managing the Holy Dynasty’s affairs both large and small. He knew his master had been planning for a long time, and this sudden action was certainly not limited to just this one matter.

“Erlang.” As expected, Li Ping spoke.

“This disciple is here.”

“Go on behalf of your master and make a trip to the Five Elders Association.”

Sun Erlang was instantly stunned.

He had imagined many possible conquests ahead, yet he had never anticipated that his master would send him to the Five Elders Association.

This name was extremely unfamiliar to the vast majority of people in the Holy Dynasty. Sun Erlang had only learned a little about it previously from Yi Shu of the Taiyan Sect.

“Ask them on my behalf whether they know about the events involving the Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign back then.”

Li Ping transmitted an image into Sun Erlang’s mind.

“Eastern Extremity?” Sun Erlang had never heard this name before. But anyone titled Heavenly Sovereign was undoubtedly at the Longevity Immortal realm.

Therefore, after clearly seeing the image in his mind, even with his resolute mindset forged across two lifetimes, Sun Erlang could not help but tremble all over.

Li Ping paid no attention to Sun Erlang’s reaction and continued with his instructions: “The Five Elders Association is different from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. The power of the Heavenly Sovereigns covers the entire region, with each governing a separate territory. On this trip, head to the domain of the Truth Heavenly Sovereign. As for the other Heavenly Sovereigns, do not provoke them…”

Sun Erlang committed his master’s instructions firmly to heart.

Just like Lin Ling, before departing Sun Erlang also received a bestowed treasure.

“You already carry the Dao Deviation Stone, which is the greatest treasure of all. Relying on other external objects would instead lead you astray.” As Li Ping spoke, he slowly passed a radiant, crystalline stone to Sun Erlang.

Inside Sun Erlang’s sea of consciousness, the Dao Deviation Stone suddenly erupted with unprecedented blue light, followed by waves of greedy, restless intent surging forth like a flood.

The craving from his soul left Sun Erlang unable and unwilling to suppress it.

“Another Dao Deviation Stone? And its quality is even better than the one in my sea of consciousness! At the very least, it is mid-grade or higher.” Sun Erlang was overjoyed beyond measure.

Having spent a long time accompanying Yi Shu of the Taiyan Sect in the Holy Dynasty, Sun Erlang fully understood the priceless value of Dao Deviation Stones in this world. Only a few remained in existence, and nearly all were already claimed by other cultivators with profound fortune. Outsiders could search endlessly without success. Yet now, his master was able to present him with a mid-grade one…

Sun Erlang immediately kowtowed in gratitude for his master’s kindness.

The joy in his heart directly washed away the fear that had just arisen from the current state of the Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign.

“Absorb it slowly. The sublimation of the Dao Deviation Stone will nourish and drive comprehensive improvement across all your abilities. Of course, if you wish to fully unleash the stone’s potential, you may need to pair it with another rare treasure.”

Li Ping’s majestic voice echoed through the hall: “The foundation of the Five Elders Association is the Ten Great Immortal Sects from back then. Though the name has perished, the substance remains, and it contains countless treasure vaults within. On this journey, you may invite Yi Shu of the Taiyan Sect to accompany you.”

“I shall obey Master’s command.” Sun Erlang nodded, stepped forward, and departed.

Among all the disciples, Li Ping held the deepest and most unique feelings toward Sun Erlang. This appointment was not only to borrow strength to strike at another but also because Li Ping had faintly sensed that Sun Erlang’s own opportunity lay there. This premonition was profoundly subtle, not derived from deduction based on sufficient known information, but more like the sudden intuitive guidance a cultivator receives right before a breakthrough.

Seeing that her senior brothers and sisters had all received treasures, Daoist Child Liuli could not help feeling full of anticipation. Her large eyes stared straight at Li Ping.

Su Yuqing, by contrast, showed the calmest reaction among everyone. Perhaps it was because the child in her womb had not yet been born.

Su Yuqing’s mind recalled the words her master had spoken to her earlier: “The fetus in your belly is in fact the incarnation of the Heavenly Dao of this small world.”

“This realm faces destruction, so it borrows your body to seek a slim chance of survival. However, even with the small world’s Heavenly Dao blessing you, you remain a mortal vessel whose body is far too frail. Even if you are fortunate enough to successfully give birth to this child, she will struggle to grow up, and you will die.”

“Unless you cultivate with me…”

From that day onward, Su Yuqing had followed Li Ping to the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi.

As her realm grew higher, the weakness in her original body gradually disappeared. The fetus in her womb also slept more peacefully.

Yet Master had said that the stronger her cultivation at the time of delivery, the greater her daughter’s future achievements would be. Maternal instinct drove Su Yuqing’s dedicated pursuit of cultivation to stand firmly first among the six disciples.

She harbored no particularly deep obsession with earning merits through warfare.

Li Ping’s divine sense locked onto the two disciples in the hall who had not yet received specific assignments and remained silent for a long time.

This made Daoist Child Liuli sense something. Her heart skipped a beat, and she quickly lowered her head, withdrawing her expectant gaze.

“You two are compatible in temperament. Being together will benefit each other. Moreover, because of your special constitutions, it is not advisable for you to come into frequent contact with killing intent…”

“I have recently opened a special small world. You two will come inside and help.”

Upon hearing this, Daoist Child Liuli immediately pulled a long face.

Perhaps because she had been raised by Zhong Shentong, her personality was extremely similar to his. Without external force compelling her, she tended to be quite lazy. Since Li Ping had accepted her as a disciple, he naturally intended to correct this bad habit.

After Ou Shangtian left, Liuli and Su Yuqing followed Li Ping to the newly opened small world.

“Master, it’s so dark here. Liuli is a little scared.” Liuli deliberately spoke with unclear enunciation, sounding pitiful.

This was not Liuli pretending to be miserable. This world truly differed from ordinary small worlds and contained not a trace of light. It was the special world that Li Ping, together with Tianyang and Xu Ke, had jointly created specifically for the You Clan people.

“Yuqing, aside from the darkness, have you sensed any other differences here?” Ignoring Liuli’s feigned pitiful act, Li Ping asked slowly.

Absolute pitch blackness and tranquil silence caused the activity of the fetus in Su Yuqing’s womb to decrease. It seems hypnotized and gradually fell into deep sleep.

Su Yuqing sensed the emotions transmitted by her daughter. After a moment she frowned and replied: “The Heavenly Dao of this small world… is not yet complete? Or rather, it simply does not exist?”

“It is merely a vast expanse of space. In this disciple’s view, it does not yet deserve to be called a world.”

Su Yuqing’s talent was indeed extraordinary; she sensed the anomaly here in mere moments.

Li Ping smiled: “You answered only half correctly. In the beginning, this place was not without a small world Heavenly Dao consciousness. Although extremely weak, it did exist.”

Su Yuqing was stunned. She was about to ask where the world’s Heavenly Dao had gone.

A golden cube suddenly appeared before her. Its dazzling radiance illuminated the entire dark small world.

Sensing the somewhat familiar aura within the cube, Su Yuqing instinctively felt an irrepressible trace of fear.

The fetus in her womb was startled awake and began to stir restlessly.

Because sealed inside this golden cube was a world Heavenly Dao consciousness identical in nature to her own!

“They all received gifts, so naturally you will not be left out.” Li Ping could naturally sense Su Yuqing’s fear.

“This place must remain under my absolute control and has no need for a world Heavenly Dao consciousness, so I stripped it away and sealed it.”

“I give it to you now.” Li Ping said calmly.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1243: Raising The Sword Toward Ruomu

The golden cube shone with dazzling light. Li Ping’s voice echoed for a long time in this absolutely pitch-black small world.

Once the instinctive fear subsided, what arose in Su Yuqing’s heart was an uncontrollable and intense craving for the sealed object inside the golden cube.

Even the fetus in her womb felt the same.

“You and this small world’s Heavenly Dao, which is still in the process of gestating and awakening, can be considered kindred in a certain sense. The method your master is imparting will allow you to slowly refine and absorb it. It may be equivalent to several centuries of bitter cultivation.”

As Li Ping’s voice rang out once more, a profound and wondrous technique also appeared in Su Yuqing’s mind.

It resembled a secret art, yet also a formation, or perhaps both. It was extremely arcane. Even with Su Yuqing’s comprehension, understanding it proved somewhat difficult.

“This realm holds great significance for your master and the entire Holy Dynasty of Great Qi. Once you devour and refine the Heavenly Dao consciousness of this realm, you can replace it and possess supreme authority within this small world. With you watching over this place, your master can rest assured.”

Only at this point did Su Yuqing suddenly understand.

Although it was a laborious duty, Su Yuqing had always possessed a calm and indifferent temperament with few worldly desires. Moreover, cultivating by embodying the Heavenly Dao here was likely the fastest path for her progress, and it would bring enormous benefits to her daughter as well.

“I shall obey Master’s command.” Su Yuqing took a deep breath.

“Master, I don’t want to stay here! It’s so dark!” Daoist Child Liuli shook her head frantically.

Li Ping clearly had no intention of indulging her.

He rapped Liuli’s head with his knuckle and spoke in a deep voice: “Light and darkness are two sides of the same coin. Without darkness, light would lose its meaning. The reverse is also true. As the Glazed Wisdom Fruit, cultivation is easy for you. Yet this is also your fatal flaw…”

Liuli listened with wide eyes, half understanding. Her intuition told her that this somewhat fearsome master of hers was not lying.

“Your progress is rapid, but compared with human cultivators, your upper limit is inherently restricted. To break through that limit, you must draw from the principle of [Glazed] and the principle of [Wisdom]. From this place, you can gain insights into both.”

“You are the youngest among your master’s disciples, so naturally the gift will not be lacking.”

Another sphere of light appeared in Li Ping’s hand. It was not a seal like the previous golden cube, but a pure aggregation of energy.

Daoist Child Liuli subconsciously licked her lips. The moment she saw the light sphere, she felt her stomach grow hungry.

She reached out to grab it, but the light sphere was illusory and slipped through her palm. Liuli grew extremely annoyed and waved her hands repeatedly, yet it was all in vain.

Such supreme delicacy lay before her, yet she could only look and not eat. She almost burst into tears on the spot from grievance.

“Maste…” Liuli looked up at Li Ping with eager, watery eyes.

Li Ping smiled: “This source power essence carries two layers of restrictions added by your master. Only when you achieve a breakthrough in either Wisdom or Glazed will the restrictions be lifted.”

“And once you break through the limit, this energy will help you fully stabilize your new transformed form.”

Daoist Child Liuli stared fixedly at the source power essence sphere and nodded vigorously.

“Heavenly Dao is heartless and treats all things as straw dogs. When you control this realm, take nature as your guiding principle. Remain high above. Unless absolutely necessary, it is best not to personally intervene in the affairs between ordinary living beings.”

Li Ping’s voice lingered for a long time as his figure suddenly shattered and vanished from the realm.

Su Yuqing recalled her master’s usual demeanor before the people of Great Qi and fell into thoughtful silence.

“The You Clan?” Su Yuqing began examining the various materials her master had transmitted to her mind.

Meanwhile, Daoist Child Liuli kept looking around, eager to find where the path to breaking her limit lay.

...

Xuanhuang Realm.

Wang Xuanba had now returned underground in Yueyang Prefecture.

Lin Ling accompanied him.

Lin Ling toyed with the wheel-shaped treasure Li Ping had bestowed upon her, appearing somewhat indifferent toward the mission of subduing the Earth Vein Ancient Tree.

“Junior Sister, can we begin?” Wang Xuanba felt a bit intimidated by this stern-faced junior sister and asked cautiously.

“No rush. Let us wait a little longer.” Lin Ling shook her head.

“Wait? Wait for what?” Wang Xuanba asked in surprise.

In his perception, quite a few of the sixteen dormant swarms of insects had already awakened from their seals and were stirring restlessly.

“Master said to give it a chance. If it remains stubborn, it will not be too late to act then.” Lin Ling frowned slightly but still explained patiently.

“Then we will follow Junior Sister’s lead.” Wang Xuanba said with a grin.

The two stood quietly in the earth veins for a little while. Suddenly Wang Xuanba once again felt that vast consciousness belonging to the Earth Vein Ancient Tree sweeping around him.

But unlike before, this time the Earth Vein Ancient Tree did not ignore them directly and lingered for a long time.

Wang Xuanba glanced over and saw Lin Ling put away the wheel in her hand. Her expression turned serious.

It seemed she was conversing with someone.

Wang Xuanba did not interrupt their conversation. In truth his nerves were already taut. As soon as Lin Ling transmitted a thought, he would cooperate by activating all the [Dao One] insect swarms.

At first Lin Ling appeared to be chatting normally with the Earth Vein Ancient Tree. She even showed occasional smiles on her face.

But without warning Lin Ling’s expression changed abruptly.

She scolded coldly: “I thought of you as my kin, which is why I opened my heart to communicate with you just now. Yet why do you spout such nonsense and urge me to rebel?!”

“How could the methods of my master be something a mere rotten old tree like you could fathom!”

Upon receiving Lin Ling’s transmission, Wang Xuanba also gave a cold laugh. He then awakened the dormant insect swarm beneath Yueyang Prefecture here.

The black shadow surged like a flood after a dam burst, unstoppable and rushing forth.

Visibly, the surrounding yellow earth-vein currents were instantly dyed black. A sharp explosive sound came faintly from underground but was drowned out by the surging army of bugs.

“How does it feel to lose one tentacle?” Killing intent flashed in Lin Ling’s eyes.

“Severing thousands or hundreds of your tentacles would be easy for me.”

Wang Xuanba understood and cooperated by awakening the remaining fifteen swarms of insect, but he still kept them under control as a deterrent.

The entire Xuanhuang land trembled imperceptibly. This was caused by the Earth Vein Ancient Tree’s fear leading to a loss of overall control over the earth-vein currents.

A hunched old man’s phantom appeared before the two.

He looked at Lin Ling with fear on his face. At the same time his tone carried some anger as he questioned: “I acted with sincere goodwill. Why do you harm me?!”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1244: Life And Death Hold No Fixed Conclusion

Wang Xuanba examined the somewhat sleazy-looking little old man before him and found it hard to connect him with the legendary ancient tree that controlled the Xuanhuang Realm’s earth veins.

Facing the Earth Vein Ancient Tree’s accusation, Lin Ling merely sneered: “Goodwill? I have followed Master for only a few years, yet I have not only successfully taken form from an ignorant and weak consciousness but also gained my current impressive cultivation.”

“How long have you cultivated? Killing you would take only a thought from me!” Lin Ling locked her cold gaze on the Earth Vein Ancient Tree. The wheel behind her suddenly appeared and spun rapidly.

The insect swarms within the earth veins seemed to sense something and responded in unison with agitation. Even though they had not collectively broken free from their seals, those fifteen shadow swarms suddenly grew noticeably larger.

“Let’s talk properly. Let’s talk properly.” The hunched old man grew extremely flustered.

But Lin Ling ignored him and continued pressing aggressively: “Your mother tree is not my mother tree. What you call turning from darkness to light looks to me like destroying my path!”

“Those who block the path deserve no rest until death!” Lin Ling shouted furiously.

Wang Xuanba chuckled and cooperated by loosening the restraint on one swarm of insects.

The Earth Vein Ancient Tree’s expression changed drastically. Before he could speak, one leg seemed to snap suddenly, causing his entire figure to lose balance and stagger to the ground.

“Misunderstanding! It’s truly a misunderstanding! The mother tree is the source of all life in the world. Not only for us Spiritwood beings but even for humans...”

Before the Earth Vein Ancient Tree could finish, another swarm of insects exploded and interrupted him. This time it was the other leg. The hunched old man looked up in embarrassment, panic written all over his face.

“The source of all living beings in the world? Where is it now?” Lin Ling’s words were full of disdain.

“Even if what you say is true, so what?”

Lin Ling stepped forward and stood before the Earth Vein Ancient Tree.

“Compared with my master...”

“You are nothing but a clay chicken or tile dog!”

Lin Ling stomped down hard on the Earth Vein Ancient Tree.

The phantom old man wailed. Although some of its body had been eroded by the [Dao One] insects, even with its remaining strength it should not have been completely powerless to resist. Yet the Earth Vein Ancient Tree did not struggle.

Because the old man knew that what this young kin before him said was not wrong.

“What exactly are these things...” On the surface the Earth Vein Ancient Tree had completely submitted, but in secret it was trying to dissolve this strange black energy.

But this power seemed different from any kind it had known before in the Xuanhuang Realm, leaving it completely helpless.

“These few disciples already possess such methods. What kind of divine being must their master be? Could it be some great expert from ancient times? We have no grudge or enmity. Why suddenly target me? Could it be to deal with...” The Earth Vein Ancient Tree’s mind was filled with uncertainty and countless thoughts flashed through it.

But Lin Ling did not give the Earth Vein Ancient Tree much time to think.

“Those who recognize the times are wise. Since you and I are both Spiritwood, I will give you a chance. Submit to our Holy Dynasty of Great Qi!”

“Otherwise, I would not mind reluctantly taking over your position as the Earth Vein Ancient Tree!” Lin Ling said coldly, her killing intent sharp.

As she spoke, emerald-green roots extended from beneath her feet, like countless tentacles surging into the black insect swarm torrent.

Pure green spread from Lin Ling’s lower limbs, quickly dyeing the black green.

Lin Ling, who controlled the imitation beast wheel, had now merged her body with the insect swarm. She was the swarm, and the swarm was her.

Wherever the [Dao One] black torrent passed, it became a place reached by her will and part of her body.

If it was the bodies previously eroded by the [Dao One] insects, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree might still have a chance to reclaim them. As long as it found a way to completely eradicate the [Dao One] insects and then continuously washed them with earth-vein power.

But now, the part occupied by Lin Ling...

The Earth Vein Ancient Tree’s face gradually turned deathly pale.

Because it had completely lost perception of part of its own body. It even sensed that right beside its original body, there was now a hostile existence.

It could swallow the rest of it at any moment!

This feeling was truly chilling and further confirmed that Lin Ling’s words were not empty threats.

Not much time was left for itself...

This thought flashed through the Earth Vein Ancient Tree’s mind.

But submitting to that so-called Holy Dynasty of Great Qi would mean betraying its father.

Ruomu’s sinister face suddenly appeared before the Earth Vein Ancient Tree, causing it to shiver involuntarily.

The intention to submit in its heart vanished once more.

Just as the Earth Vein Ancient Tree hesitated indecisively, it faintly sensed a special intent from Lin Ling.

“Hmm?”

“This old one...”

Lin Ling glanced at it slightly. The Earth Vein Ancient Tree paused, then corrected itself: “I am willing to submit to the Holy Dynasty.”

Wang Xuanba stared at the ancient tree with some suspicion. Lin Ling then ordered: “Then come with us to meet Master.”

There was no room for refusal at all.

Sensing the insect swarms still eyeing it menacingly within its body, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree could only agree helplessly.

Its body materialized from illusion, appearing quite disheveled.

It obediently followed behind Wang Xuanba and Lin Ling.

The journey was without incident, and they arrived inside the small world of Great Qi.

The various unfathomable auras coming from all directions made the ancient tree tread as if on thin ice, trembling with fear.

Since even two disciples could handle it so thoroughly, the Holy Emperor of Great Qi would be even more so.

Inside the Holy Emperor’s hall, the ancient tree’s submission needed no further mention.

Besides falling into the Holy Emperor’s golden source power net, losing freedom of body and mind forever, and becoming a servant of Great Qi, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree also confessed everything it knew about Ruomu.

In the end, it managed to preserve its own little life.

The Holy Dynasty thereby officially gained the method to locate Ruomu’s main body.

“Holy Emperor above, today I have leaked my father’s aura. Soon He will surely sense it. Therefore, the key to success lies in the word speed.” Having chosen to surrender, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree, now thoroughly committed, proactively offered strategies to Li Ping.

“You are right. That is why I am already on the way there.”

Li Ping’s voice came from above, but what left the Earth Vein Ancient Tree stunned was that the Faceless Holy Emperor’s figure was slowly fading and vanishing.

And it could not even detect when the other party had left.

“The Holy Emperor’s strength is truly unfathomable. It is even above fa...”

“Hmph, that old madman is in for some good fortune now.”

For some unknown reason, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree actually began to feel a bit of anticipation.

Meanwhile, outside the Holy Emperor’s hall,

Wang Xuanba looked at Lin Ling, wanting to speak but hesitating.

“If you have something to say, just say it. Staring at me like this all the time, I’m afraid I won’t be able to resist drawing my blade.”

Lin Ling’s sudden words startled Wang Xuanba.

He scratched his head and looked at this junior sister of his. No longer hesitating, he asked: “Just now, at the final moment of persuading the Earth Vein Ancient Tree to surrender, it seemed Junior Sister said something to it?”

Lin Ling showed a disgusted expression as if to say this was a boring question.

“I simply told it that if it surrendered, I could form an alliance with it.”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1245: Subduing Ruomu With Reason

“Indeed, the identity of being from the so-called same clan is truly useful. I never expected that a casual remark would make it agree on the spot.”

“What?” Upon hearing Lin Ling’s answer, Wang Xuanba’s expression changed.

“Ally with that old thing? Junior Sister, are you serious?”

The Earth Vein Ancient Tree’s shift from arrogance to deference truly left Wang Xuanba feeling disdainful.

“It was merely a deceptive remark to coax it. Ally…”

“Does it even deserve that?” Lin Ling’s tone remained calm, yet the profound indifference in her words was unmistakable.

“Now that it has joined the Holy Dynasty, could it possibly go back on its word? If it dares mention such a thing in front of me…” Lin Ling snorted coldly.

Wang Xuanba was momentarily stunned.

He had always placed some value on keeping promises, so Lin Ling’s open breach of word was somewhat difficult for him to accept.

Seeing his reaction, Lin Ling’s expression softened into a smile: “Senior Brother, the art of war never shuns deceit.”

“When dealing with enemies, if one must always keep one’s word and follow through, would that not be foolish?”

Wang Xuanba had no reply.

“Moreover, Senior Brother, do you remember what Master instructed us before we went to see the Earth Vein Ancient Tree?” Lin Ling asked again.

“Of course I remember. Master said that if the ancient tree remained stubborn, Junior Sister could replace it.” Wang Xuanba recalled and replied.

“The name Earth Vein Ancient Tree sounds quite domineering. At first I had some expectations, but after seeing its appearance with my own eyes, only disappointment remained.” Lin Ling’s answer left Wang Xuanba somewhat astonished.

“Too weak. Far too weak. The strongest point of the Earth Vein Ancient Tree is its unity with the Xuanhuang earth veins, allowing it to summon the surging power of earth veins across the entire Xuanhuang Realm. This is both its characteristic and its advantage, yet it also determines the upper limit of the Earth Vein Tree. Moreover, the body of the Earth Vein Ancient Tree is far too large. In the eyes of true experts, it is full of flaws.” Lin Ling shook her head slightly.

“My dream is to become a figure like Master. If I truly became some Earth Vein Tree, my strength might soar in the short term, but my future prospects would be completely severed. I am not foolish.”

“I can only continue deceiving that old thing and keep it on duty.”

Lin Ling gave Wang Xuanba a sideways glance: “Senior Brother, what kind of person do you take me for?”

Wang Xuanba gradually came to his senses. Feeling a bit embarrassed, he scratched his head and apologized on the spot: “I underestimated you, Junior Sister.”

He turned to look at the Earth Vein Ancient Tree in the Holy Emperor’s hall and nodded in agreement: “The ancient beings of Xuanhuang are strong, indeed. Yet they merely borrow the power of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao’s laws. When the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao flourishes, they naturally rise with it. But if the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao is trampled underfoot by a figure like the Immortal Ancestor, as it is today, they can only linger on in the shadows, reminiscing about their past glory.”

“I did not expect Senior Brother to be so cultured, speaking with such eloquence.” It was unclear whether Lin Ling was sincerely praising him or delivering a small jab of sarcasm.

“I did not think that far. I simply want to become stronger.”

“Enough talk. I am going to cultivate. Swallowing part of that old thing’s body this time truly yielded some gains. If I had known, I should have been more ruthless and devoured more.” Lin Ling’s slightly regretful voice echoed in Wang Xuanba’s ears.

Moments later, her figure gradually faded and her aura vanished.

Wang Xuanba smiled wryly and mocked himself for overthinking.

...

Xuanhuang Realm, Tianyu Prefecture.

A renowned scenic wonder of the Immortal Alliance.

A fissure several hundred li long snaked across the earth like a black scar.

Even from afar, one could faintly sense the endless chill emanating from within the fissure.

The closer one approached the fissure, the more rapidly the temperature plummeted.

Even Golden Core cultivators could not endure the low temperature here for long and could not linger.

At the bottom of the fissure abyss, bursts of deep blue light would occasionally erupt.

That was freezing cold air.

Yet if one stood beside the fissure and looked down, one would see countless lotus flowers composed of flames blooming within this icy abyss.

They were not cold flames, but truly scorching hot flame lotuses.

Cold and heat, completely contradictory yet intertwined, appeared simultaneously within this icy abyss.

The Faceless Holy Emperor of Great Qi, Li Ping’s figure, suddenly appeared outside this wondrous scene of ice abyss and fire lotuses.

Just like the countless tourists who came here, he stood quietly and gazed downward.

“Bell-like spirit and beautiful nurturing, the wonders of creation.”

After a long while, Li Ping could not help sighing inwardly. In his eyes, the scene here was actually different from what ordinary cultivators saw.

The blue ice abyss truly exuded a deep aura of death.

Pitch black and suffocating.

The blazing red fire lotuses, however, were pure white and radiated a vigorous life force.

What appeared to be cold and heat intertwining here was actually…“Black abyss, white flowers. Death abyss, life flowers.”

Li Ping stepped forward, trod on empty air, and his figure plummeted rapidly into the black abyss.

His disappearance drew no attention from the surrounding cultivators.

At this moment, from Li Ping’s perspective, the scenery around him suddenly stretched and twisted.

Although the white flowers of life did not seem far away, Li Ping had already been falling for a long time yet had not drawn any closer to them.

The entire black abyss space expanded and extended infinitely as he fell.

What came with it was a correspondingly thickening aura of death.

Sensing the death aura attempting to invade him, Li Ping sneered inwardly but did not pay it much heed. For an ordinary cultivator, even one at the Dao Integration realm, such black death aura would drastically reduce their lifespan upon contact.

Yet he, Li Ping, was the incarnation of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao and part of the Xuanhuang laws. Even if it was a portion that had gone out of control, how could it harm him?

Ever since he had forcibly devoured the Xuanhuang evil thoughts, Li Ping had indeed sensed that the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao seemed to have grown somewhat distant.

He was certain, however, that this was merely the Heavenly Dao’s instinctive reaction after the evil thoughts were devoured.

After some more time passed, the bond between him and the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao would eventually return to normal. Although he was currently in a period of weakened connection, it was still not something the death aura here could shake.

Sure enough, even without any protective measures, the Holy Emperor Li Ping remained impervious to all laws. The black death aura merely swirled around him in layers.

It could find no opening at all.

Li Ping’s gaze pierced through the thick death aura and fixed directly on the flowers of life below.

“The boundary between death and life, the origin of all spirits.”

“The Xuanhuang Realm has become like this, yet it merely hides in this secret place, lingering on…”

“What use are you!”

A killing intent suddenly surged from Li Ping’s body.

It forcefully pushed back the death aura around him.

“Retreat before me!”

Li Ping shouted in a low voice. The layers of death aura beneath him actually parted, opening a passage.

The space no longer extended. Li Ping’s figure drifted forward and passed through it in an instant. A dense surge of life force rushed toward him. The white flowers of life were now within reach.

Black clouds of death floated and swirled overhead, refusing to disperse.

Facing the thick life force, Li Ping’s heart suddenly grew somewhat grave.

Compared with pure death aura, a large amount of useless life force was more like a deadly poison.

Because life force was, by nature, a beneficial state.

As the incarnation of the Heavenly Dao, Li Ping could naturally enjoy this favor from heaven and earth. It was unknown how many years had passed since a cultivator not rejected by heaven and earth had come here.

The life force accumulated for several thousand years beside the black abyss and white flowers now seemed to have finally found an outlet. It surged madly toward Li Ping. Even though he intentionally tried to block it, he could not withstand the excessive abundance of vital energy here.

Inevitably, a portion still surged into Li Ping’s body.

Vital energy indeed brought certain benefits.

But Li Ping was not a pure Longevity Immortal cultivator who relied solely on his own cultivation.

Source power essence, the fortune of the Holy Dynasty, Heavenly Dao blessings...

Only through the combination of all these various forces had he achieved his current strength comparable to the Longevity Immortal realm.

By now, Li Ping’s condition had long reached a state of balance after his advancement.

At this moment, however, this balance was slowly being disrupted by the continuous influx of endless vital energy.

Li Ping’s hair, as if coming alive, grew wildly.

In the blink of an eye, it had already spread down to his ankles.

His body was also expanding in sync.

One zhang, two zhang...

Amid the creaking of his bones, in just a few breaths Li Ping had transformed into a three-zhang giant.

It certainly made him appear more majestic, yet for the current Li Ping this was not a good thing.

He furrowed his brows slightly and tried his best to convert and absorb the surging vital energy.

But the rate of shrinking was far slower than the rate of growth.

While maintaining the balance of the various forces within his body, Li Ping observed his surroundings and searched for a way to enter Ruomu’s hiding place.

After devouring the Xuanhuang evil thoughts, very few things in the Xuanhuang Realm could escape his sight. Even though some of the evil thoughts’ memories had grown blurry, as long as he saw it with his own eyes, he could quickly sense it again.

With a flicker of his figure, Holy Emperor Li Ping vanished from the bottom of the Black Abyss.

The vital energy that had lost its target for infusion suddenly became somewhat dazed. The white flowers swayed gently and could only draw the vital energy back.

A place filled with an eerie aura, like a world of faded, withered yellow leaves.

Li Ping’s nearly five-zhang-tall body suddenly appeared within it.

The boundless vitality he carried surged out from his body and spread toward the surroundings.

Yet in this strange environment, it seemed completely unable to propagate.

Soon, like autumn leaves withering, it transformed into withered yellow solid matter and drifted down from the air.

With an outlet for the unwanted vital energy, Li Ping gradually expelled the culprit causing the imbalance from his body.

His body slowly shrank.

However, because the internal balance had already shifted during that brief period earlier.

After Li Ping returned to normal, some details of his body did not revert; instead, they became part of his physical characteristics. For example, his height remained at three zhang and three chi, with ink-black hair hanging down to his waist.

His physique had also become fuller, with explosive power hidden beneath the golden-yellow robe.

Li Ping did not pay special attention to these changes.

They were merely external appearances. If he wished, he could revert to his original form at any time.

At this moment, his attention was focused on this eerie location.

The decaying scent that permeated here even made him feel somewhat uncomfortable.

What concerned him more was the center of this place.

A colossal tree that reached the heavens stood quietly, yet its entire body was withered yellow and covered in spots.

Even Li Ping’s three-zhang-tall giant form was no more than an ant at the base of this massive tree.

On the withered yellow branches, countless cultivators hung densely packed.

Like fruits filling the tree.

All of them had their eyes closed, their faces showing strange, satisfied expressions.

Li Ping’s gaze swept across the area. Apart from this withered yellow ancient tree, he found no other figures.

“So only the main body is here...”

Li Ping knew that Ruomu had a clone that frequently went out to lure cultivators here, turning them into Golden Core fruits to vent the hatred in its heart.

It seemed to have gone out at the moment.

Li Ping was not in a hurry and continued to carefully observe this space.

On the towering trunk, faint deep scars could be seen, as if it had suffered some fatal injury.

Li Ping narrowed his eyes and attempted to awaken the connection between this heavenly creation and the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao.

But perhaps due to being infected by the withered yellow, Li Ping’s call received no response whatsoever. Instead, it provoked the ancient tree’s instinctive defense.

Specks of withered yellow began eroding from the ground beneath Li Ping’s feet, following the giant tree’s veins.

Li Ping deliberately lowered his defenses and absorbed a trace of the withered yellow into his body.

He wrapped it with golden source power essence to prevent it from spreading and then observed it carefully.

Although the withered yellow power was eerie, it could not break through the seal of the source power essence. From this withered yellow power, Li Ping sensed not only ordinary aging. He also sensed what felt like the decay of the entire heaven and earth.

It was as if the heavens were about to collapse, with utter decline fully revealed and beyond redemption.

“This is...”

“The world is heading toward ruin.”

Countless scenes emerged in his mind, all depicting events in various prefectures of the Xuanhuang Realm caused by the decay of heaven and earth, with countless calamities descending.

Yet Li Ping knew that in reality, these events that should have occurred in the prefectures of the Xuanhuang Realm had not taken place.

“Ruomu is the source of all spirits in the Xuanhuang Realm. To a certain extent, it truly reflects the vital state of the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“Ruomu has now taken on this appearance, yet the Xuanhuang Realm still appears full of vitality...”

Li Ping frowned slightly.

Soon he understood the answer.

“If there were no external injection of vital energy, the Xuanhuang Realm would surely decay in sync with Ruomu.”

“The source of this vital energy is...”

“The remnants of other immortal cultivation realms that have been devoured.”

“Although they were dismembered, during the process of fusion, the remaining vital energy was drawn from those immortal realm remnants.”

“Xuanhuang has thus regained new life...”

Li Ping said thoughtfully.

“This surgery is truly exquisite beyond compare.”

As the incarnation of the Heavenly Dao and the nominal victim, after realizing what had truly happened over these thousands of years, he could not help but express heartfelt admiration.

“Life and death are impermanent. Death and life are impermanent.”

“This old one was once an existence that controlled life and death, yet now I have fallen to such a state.”

“The world seeks to abandon me. How could I not feel hatred...”

Just as Li Ping was pondering, a strange voice suddenly sounded from behind him without any warning.

Li Ping knew that the owner of the voice was Ruomu.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1246: Fruit Tree Inception

Sun Erlang remained hidden and observed the battle between the Five Elders Association cultivators and the giant ape. Gradually a sense of gravity arose in his heart.

“So strong…”

This was his evaluation of the team of cultivators before him.

There were no spiritual energy fluctuations, and the destructive range of both sides’ techniques was confined to a certain space, so Sun Erlang could not perceive their exact cultivation levels.

Yet merely from their coordination during combat, their fearlessness in battle, and their calm handling of every crisis and other combat qualities, the level of this team of cultivators was truly unprecedented in his experience.

Even when compared to the opponents he had encountered in the Dao Deviation Stone simulated worlds, this team remained an extremely top-tier existence.

What surprised Sun Erlang even more were their attack and healing methods.

Besides various artifacts, the team occasionally used some rather peculiar items, such as books, wine cups, and even necklaces. These provided buffs to their own side while imposing various negative effects on the giant ape.

Moreover, their healing speed was frighteningly fast.

New Method cultivators who housed Cave-Heavens in their bodies would require at least several breaths to rebirth from the Cave-Heaven after their physical body was destroyed.

But for these people, a flash of white light was all it took, and no matter how severe the injury, they could instantly recover. One moment they were struck by the giant ape’s attack and on the verge of death; the next second, under the illumination of white light, they were once again full of vigor.

No matter how tough and thick-skinned the giant ape was, it could not withstand such attrition.

Strangely, this demonic beast seemed stubbornly fixated and did not know to flee.

It fought until its last breath before collapsing with a boom.

The moment the giant ape died, it transformed into dazzling golden light and surged into the bodies of the team of cultivators that had slain it.

The group immediately burst into cheers.

“This boss we farmed this time was really damn tanky. Good thing we brought enough supplies; otherwise we would have been done for.”

“Unfortunately, its Snow Ape Sacred Armor didn’t drop in the illustrated handbook; otherwise we would have really struck it rich this time.”

These words drew agreement and sighs from the rest of the team.

Sun Erlang, however, listened to this completely incomprehensible speech with a somewhat subtle expression.

“Let’s go. There’s nothing good left around here. Head back to the [Paradise] first.”

“This Xuanhuang Realm really isn’t a place for people. If not for the mission, I wouldn’t want to come here at all.”

The team of cultivators filed into the warship in the sky, chatting animatedly.

The warship was enveloped in colorful light and vanished from the sky as if teleporting.

Sun Erlang stared at the spot where they had departed. In his mind, the Dao Deviation Stone continuously replayed the recorded scenes.

“Paradise…”

“It should be the territory of that Carefree Heavenly Sovereign. It seems somewhat different from the rest of the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“They actually call the Xuanhuang Realm ‘not a place for people’… Interesting.”

“I should report this matter to Master.”

Thinking this, Sun Erlang did not pursue the peculiar warship.

He remembered his master’s assigned mission and continued heading toward the Land of Truth.

Three days later, after crossing a white mist barrier, Sun Erlang clearly sensed that something in the world had changed.

“The prefecture I was in earlier belongs to the Human Dao Heavenly Land. This place should be the Land of Truth.”

Sun Erlang knew that within the Land of Truth, under the coverage of the Heavenly Sovereign’s principle of Truth, no cultivator could fabricate lies.

Thus he became even more cautious and careful.

As a newcomer, everything here was completely unfamiliar. Sun Erlang decided to first gather information before seeking a way to meet the Truth Heavenly Sovereign.

Compared to the Human Dao Heavenly Land, the Land of Truth appeared more peaceful. Patrols by Five Elders Association cultivators were rarely seen.

The boundary between mortals and cultivators within the realm was also not particularly distinct.

One could even see them living together in the same cities from afar.

“Those are indeed mortals without any cultivation. It seems the power of the immortal-mortal miasma is suppressed here.”

After careful observation, Sun Erlang reached this conclusion.

What felt even more novel to him was that status here was not determined by cultivation level.

He even witnessed a young Golden Core cultivator respectfully bowing to a mortal elder.

This made him click his tongue in wonder.

After secretly observing for three days, although he had gained a general understanding of the Land of Truth’s customs and people, he still had not found a suitable opening.

“I can’t just shout in the middle of the street that I’m here to seek an audience with the Truth Heavenly Sovereign, can I?”

Just as Sun Erlang was at a loss, on this day he suddenly noticed several outsiders like himself.

“People from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance?” Sun Erlang narrowed his eyes.

After tailing them and eavesdropping for a while, Sun Erlang immediately understood.

These four cultivators had been captured during the conflict between the Five Elders Association and the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. They were now assimilated individuals in their probation period.

Their freedom was not restricted, and they were permitted to travel through the three major lands of the Five Elders Association except for the Carefree Paradise Land.

“Brother Xu, what do you think of the Five Elders Association compared to the Immortal Alliance?”

All four wore the same conspicuous greenish uniforms resembling prison garb. One middle-aged man with a long beard asked his companion.

The Xu-named cultivator seemed not yet recovered from heavy injuries and had a pale face. He coughed lightly before replying: “Compared to the Immortal Alliance, it is indeed much freer and more relaxed.”

“Although we have to sign that divine soul contract, even without the contract binding us in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, we still couldn’t refuse the Alliance’s orders, right? In my opinion, there’s actually no difference.”

Another young cultivator chimed in: “Moreover, once the contract is signed here, there is no shortage of cultivation techniques or resources. As long as one’s talent is sufficient, there is unlimited room for development. No one will obstruct or suppress you. Unlike the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, humph…”

Although the long-bearded man’s expression looked somewhat unpleasant, he could not help but agree with the others’ views.

“It seems Brother Wang Yang still harbors illusions about the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance!” The Xu-surnamed man, seeing the expression on long-bearded Wang Yang’s face, could not help but sneer.

“You remember the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, but does the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance remember you? I have never heard of any cultivator captured by the Five Elders Association who successfully escaped back to the Immortal Alliance. Moreover, even if you were lucky enough to escape, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance would probably treat you as a spy and arrest you directly.”

Wang Yang’s expression shifted between gloom and uncertainty. In the end, he could only let out a long sigh, unable to refute.

The four of them fell into an awkward silence for a moment.

“By the way, when that lord previously introduced the situation of the Five Elders Association, he mentioned that cultivators from every prefecture here have the opportunity to directly meet a Heavenly Sovereign. I wonder if that is truly the case.” The young cultivator once again stepped forward to smooth things over.

“Brother Zhang, rest assured on this point. I have inquired beforehand, and it is absolutely true. However, the methods to meet a Heavenly Sovereign differ from place to place, and without exception they are all extremely difficult. Only peerless geniuses can achieve it.”

“For example, in the One Heart Immortal Sect, those who achieve twenty consecutive victories in arena battles with no equal have a high probability of being guided before the One Heart Heavenly Sovereign.”

“Another example is the Land of Truth here. It is said that one only needs to sit and comprehend the Dao beneath the Truth Fruit Tree. The longer one persists, the greater the chance of receiving guidance from the Heavenly Sovereign.” The Xu-surnamed man explained enthusiastically.

“I have also attempted the test of the Truth Fruit Tree before…” The Xu-surnamed man said, deliberately pausing.

Only when the gazes of the others had all focused on him did he continue: “Difficult! Difficult! Difficult!”

“You all know that no lies exist within the Land of Truth. Yet the test of the Truth Fruit Tree is to weave lies as much as possible!”

The Xu-surnamed cultivator’s expression turned somewhat strange: “Sitting beneath the fruit, one feels as if one has become an omnipotent creator god. One must create something from nothing and fabricate an illusory, alternate world. The richer and more realistic the details of the world, the longer one can persist under the fruit tree’s cleansing.”

“Wait…” Upon hearing this, Wang Yang could not help but interrupt the Xu-surnamed cultivator.

“Is there any restriction on weaving this world? Otherwise, wouldn’t it be easy if one simply used the unique small world customs from the Xuanhuang Realm as a substitute?”

The Xu-surnamed cultivator shook his head: “At the very least, small worlds that truly exist within the Xuanhuang Realm cannot be manifested in the fruit’s illusory realm. It is like…”

He pondered for a moment and said slowly: “It is somewhat like the principle that technique cannot be cultivated simultaneously. Any world copied by imitation will turn to ashes the instant it takes shape. Only completely fictional ones can exist stably and withstand the fruit’s test. Describing it in words is indeed somewhat profound. However, once you experience it personally, you will understand.”

“Even if one ultimately cannot meet the Truth Heavenly Sovereign, merely sitting in meditation beneath the Truth Fruit Tree is an enormous opportunity for us cultivators. Weaving a world involves countless great Daos. It is truly difficult to achieve relying solely on a cultivator’s own power. During the test, one mostly borrows the power of the fruit tree. It is like the Heavenly Sovereign lending a brush to help us splash ink and paint…” The Xu-surnamed cultivator’s expression became distant, as if reminiscing about that wondrous experience.

“What is the purpose of the Heavenly Sovereign doing this?” Wang Yang frowned slightly.

“Purpose? Why bother thinking about that? All I know is that completing the test brings no loss whatsoever and instead offers an extremely high chance of enlightenment and breakthrough. Such a good thing would only be enjoyed by a select few in the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. Yet here in the Five Elders Association, everyone has the opportunity to try.”

“The Truth Fruit Tree takes root and sprouts within illusion and blooms in every city. Anyone interested can sit cross-legged and meditate beneath the tree.”

“This time the fruit’s appearance in the world also seems not far off. You may all experience it personally. If it were not that each person only has one chance in a lifetime, I would definitely try the opportunity to create a world again.” The Xu-surnamed cultivator said with some regret.

The other three showed varying expressions.

Meanwhile, Sun Erlang, who had been eavesdropping from afar, narrowed his eyes.

“So coincidental? Just as I was dozing off, someone delivers a pillow? Could it be that I have been discovered, and this is some kind of scheme?”

This thought flashed through Sun Erlang’s mind first.

“No, it must be that Master already knew in advance that there are methods in every region of the Five Elders Association to directly meet the various Heavenly Sovereigns, which is why he sent me on this errand. Otherwise, under normal circumstances, what opportunity would an outsider cultivator have to meet a Longevity Immortal realm ruler at the pinnacle?” After pondering for a moment, Sun Erlang suddenly understood.

“The Truth Fruit, huh?”

Sun Erlang’s heart stirred.

He possessed the Dao Deviation Stone in his mind, as well as the experiences of another life in the simulated world. Participating in this so-called world-creation beneath the fruit tree should give him some advantage.

“First, I will give it a try. If it really doesn’t work, then I will think of another method.”

Having made his decision, Sun Erlang waited patiently.

More than twenty days passed. On this day, the peaceful city suddenly became somewhat restless.

A shadow rapidly spread across the ground from the center of the city.

It enveloped the entire city.

Then a lush, verdant giant tree rose from the shadow.

It seemed both real and illusory.

On its branches hung many emerald fruits that looked tender and juicy, drawing one’s gaze irresistibly at first sight.

“The Truth Fruit Tree has appeared in the world and will remain for seven days and seven nights. All living beings within the land may seek enlightenment beneath the tree.”

Ding!

A crisp chime of a bell rang out, spreading into the distance.

A resounding voice descended from the sky and appeared in everyone’s ears.

“Thank you for the Heavenly Sovereign’s grace!”

Countless living beings within the Land of Truth kowtowed in thanks together.

They repeated it three times, the echoes lingering.

After the ceremonial formalities concluded, cultivators began walking one after another to the shadow beneath the Truth Fruit Tree and sitting in meditation with closed eyes.

The city instantly fell very quiet, as if afraid of disturbing the cultivators undergoing the test.

The group of Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance defectors whom Sun Erlang had encountered earlier also walked slowly toward the Truth Fruit Tree.

Sun Erlang hurriedly followed.

Along the way, they encountered no one conducting inspections.

The Heavenly Sovereign’s words that all living beings could attempt it were indeed not empty talk.

Beneath the branches of the fruit tree, Sun Erlang even saw not only humans but also some flying insects and beasts occupying patches of ground in the tree’s shade.

This peculiar scene left Sun Erlang somewhat emotional.

“It is said that the ancient sages taught without discrimination. Not only humans, but all living beings of the world could receive their guidance. This Truth Heavenly Sovereign indeed carries a bit of that flavor.”

Sun Erlang also followed the others’ example and sat directly on the ground.

He closed his eyes.

In an instant, Sun Erlang felt the surrounding world vanish.

The faint rustling and small sounds that had existed in the city previously all disappeared.

Although his eyes were closed, Sun Erlang felt as if he had opened them.

Before him was a vast expanse of white nothingness.

“Create a world…”

Numerous rules and information surfaced one after another in Sun Erlang’s mind.

“First, the general situation of the world.”

Sun Erlang thus set it according to the simulated world he had experienced before in the Dao Deviation Stone.

The blank world gained color.

However, something unexpected happened. Just as the world was about to take shape, it collapsed with a rumble like a sand sculpture.

It returned once more to blankness.

“Even a world adapted from the Xuanhuang Realm as the prototype doesn’t work?”

“A completely fictional world?”

Sun Erlang could not help but frown slightly.

However, the next moment, inspiration struck. He recalled a world that his master had used as an example when instructing him previously.

He immediately had an idea.

“The immortal path does not manifest; only materialism endures.”
