Chapter 1247: Insisting On Exploring The Star Sea

“All transcendent power in the world has already vanished. Ordinary people’s lifespan is limited to a century at most.”

“Yet the eternal pursuit of the Dao by people in the world remains unchanged.”

“By researching, analyzing, and utilizing laws, even mortals can unleash astonishing power.”

...

In the blank world before Sun Erlang, a sphere suddenly appeared.

The sphere rotated on its axis, and countless black dots representing humans moved across its surface at an extremely rapid speed.

At first the buildings on the ground were only a little over one zhang tall, but as time flowed quickly, more and more high-rise buildings rose from the ground.

Before long, even flying vehicles appeared among the clouds.

While weaving the world conceived in his mind, Sun Erlang recalled his master’s teachings from that time.

“The predicament of the Xuanhuang Realm is essentially the same as that of these worlds without transcendent power. Ancient texts often say that the immortal path is eternal. Perhaps those words are not false.”

“The so-called [immortal] refers to those beings who exhaust the principles of the Dao and employ the Dao to their utmost. They stand at the far shore of all living beings, equal to the Dao itself, looking down upon the masses, undying and indestructible.”

“But what if the [Dao] itself undergoes a change?”

“The Dao remains the Dao. Yet those beings unable to adapt to the changed Dao will inevitably head toward destruction.”

“Unable to harmonize with the Dao, immortals would no longer deserve the name. They would fall.”

“Among the myriad living beings, new [users of the Dao] will always emerge. The power they wield will grow stronger and stronger.”

“They will become the new [immortals].”

...

At that time, the Faceless Holy Emperor’s words had seemed profoundly mysterious. Not only Sun Erlang, but none of the other direct disciples beneath the Holy Emperor had understood the reasoning within them.

Yet now, thanks to the power of the Truth Fruit, he had the fortune to experience the sensation of a creator.

Sun Erlang gazed at the world he had created before him and suddenly felt a stirring in his heart.

In that spherical world, after countless years of natural evolution, groups would inevitably arise that aligned with the Dao of the world he had made.

They could perceive the ultimate secrets of the world that other living beings could not detect and thereby become the so-called [Users of Dao] in this realm.

Boom!

Sun Erlang’s vision rapidly traversed space and descended onto the sphere he had created.

Inside an ordinary building, a young scholar sat frowning in thought beneath a lamp.

The blackboard before him was covered with complex formulas and diagrams.

“Wrong, wrong, wrong…”

“Where exactly is the problem? Why does my calculated result deviate so greatly from the actual trajectory of motion?”

“It feels as though some force is restraining us, preventing us from leaving this planet.”

The scholar painfully clutched his hair, then suddenly raised his head and looked out the window.

In the pitch-black night, his gaze seemed to meet Sun Erlang’s eyes directly.

...

Sun Erlang’s vision blurred, and the scene shattered with a boom.

When he came back to his senses, he discovered with surprise that spaceships had already begun appearing on the sphere, attempting to fly beyond the “boundary of the world.”

Yet outside the world, Sun Erlang had not yet created anything; it remained empty.

Naturally they would not succeed.

Sun Erlang’s thoughts turned rapidly as he began opening new maps.

The white space gradually darkened. In the starry sky, besides the original first planet, thousands upon thousands of other planets began to appear.

The map’s scope expanded more than a hundred thousand times in an instant, and Sun Erlang’s Dao Deviation Stone suddenly felt overburdened.

To reduce the workload, Sun Erlang set nearly all these planets as desolate, lifeless wastelands devoid of any living beings.

So vast that, without immortal power, the tiny people could scarcely cross between planets in an entire lifetime.

...

After expending tremendous effort, Sun Erlang barely managed to rescue the world that had been on the verge of collapse.

But soon, before he could perfect and expand the star-sea landscape, the world once again became precarious.

Upon investigation, Sun Erlang realized that the inhabitants had begun to doubt the reality of the world they inhabited.

Not only because they could not fly out of the world, but also because they had observed countless planets in the star sea that had not been there before.

These planets had appeared as if from nowhere, shattering their understanding.

“One must not crudely set rules. Instead, the appearances of all things must be rooted in the fundamental principles of the world.”

This sentence suddenly surfaced in Sun Erlang’s mind.

He quickly made the corresponding adjustments.

In the next attempt by the stubborn inhabitants, one spaceship actually succeeded in breaking free of the planet’s restraint and entered the star sea.

The humans aboard the spaceship discovered a blue ring of light encircling the planet.

It was precisely the existence of this ring that interfered with external observations from inside the planet and had prevented their earlier attempts to escape it.

This was a discovery of world-shaking greatness!

After surviving life-and-death crises, the spaceship finally returned to the surface and made the discovery public.

The scholars on the planet, once they incorporated the variable of the star ring into their calculations, finally achieved temporary consistency between theory and reality.

People cheered and welcomed the arrival of a new era. At the same time, the crew of the first ship to leave the planet were all regarded as heroes.

With the experience of success, the development speed of the planet’s long-suppressed inhabitants reached a peak.

Fortunately, by this time Sun Erlang had already sketched out most of the desolate star-sea landscape.

Moreover, thanks to the vastness of the created star sea, space and time temporarily held these inhabitants in check.

Yet Sun Erlang knew this was only a temporary measure.

Sooner or later they would detect the abnormality of this star sea.

“If one does not wish to be discovered, one must continue expanding this star sea.”

“And the speed of expansion must exceed the speed at which these tiny people explore.”

The macro view pulled back tens of thousands of times once more.

Endless nebulae and star rivers were born beyond the darkness.

The Dao Deviation Stone was already operating at its limit.

“So-called pulling one hair moves the entire body. Every object in the star sea is connected to the others.”

“It is not simply a matter of adding them afterward. One must also consider the influence their appearance has on the original planet.”

“...”

After pondering, Sun Erlang sensed that the Dao Deviation Stone was in danger of collapsing. With a resolute heart, he crudely used spatial spans to minimize these influences.

“By the time these tiny people notice something wrong, it should be a long time later.”

The inhabitants continued progressing at astonishing speed.

They studied the star ring and invented retrograde boats that could reverse-engineer the ring’s restraining power.

Like a slingshot, they hurled the spaceships outward at high speed.

Although there was no turning back, it greatly accelerated the inhabitants’ exploration of the star sea.

“This is too fast?”

Sun Erlang stared dumbfounded as the first reverse-sailing boat continuously accelerated by borrowing the power of the rings of passing planets.

It headed toward the edge of the Star Sea.

“No…”

Sun Erlang pondered for a moment. He guided a meteor, altering its original trajectory and sending it smashing fiercely toward that reverse-sailing boat.

Unexpectedly, the people aboard dodged it at the limit.

In a blur before his eyes, the scene on the reverse-sailing boat appeared before Sun Erlang.

“Sir, thanks to you! Otherwise we would have been completely wiped out this time!”

A middle-aged man with a long beard and a stern face did not relax his expression: “This piece of gravel came rather strangely. The starry sky is so vast, yet it happened to meet us…”

“Stay on full alert! Do not be careless!”

...

Hearing the little person’s words, Sun Erlang could not help feeling embarrassed. He had indeed acted somewhat crudely this time.

But he quickly thought of a way to remedy it.

Sun Erlang pointed lightly toward the planet closest to the reverse-sailing boat.

Before long the planet exploded with a boom.

It transformed into countless fragments that rained down like a torrential storm toward the reverse-sailing boat.

Luck could save them once but not twice.

Such a dense swarm of fragments, precisely guided.

The reverse-sailing boat naturally had no chance of survival.

In the collision it turned into a ball of flame and vanished from the Star Sea forever.

However, a single accident could not suppress the natives’ curiosity about the Star Sea.

Next, increasingly larger and more advanced reverse-sailing boats flew out from the original planet one after another.

They began exploring in all directions.

Although he really wanted to destroy all these ships again using the old method, Sun Erlang knew that his previous intervention had already shaken the rules of this creation game.

If he continued to intervene so directly, this world would collapse even faster.

So Sun Erlang could only think of another way.

He watched the countless ships bouncing off the star rings and soon had an idea.

With a thought, imperceptible subtle changes occurred in the star ring around the Primordial Star.

It took the native little people a full year to notice something was wrong.

The planet experienced record-breaking extreme weather across the board.

Cold currents and droughts swept through and ravaged everything.

Grain production dropped, and even the natives’ birth rate began to decline slightly.

Sensing the anomaly, the little people gathered the planet’s most elite scholars to study the cause of these disasters.

In the end they reached a conclusion.

Although the star ring bound them and prevented them from venturing into the Star Sea, it was also a protective umbrella.

It isolated the planet from the extreme environments in the Star Sea.

In recent years, excessive use of the star ring’s elastic force had caused a slight shrinkage in the star ring.

Even though this shrinkage was negligible for the star ring itself, the planet’s fragile ecosystem could not withstand the impact it brought.

After intense discussion, the native little people ultimately temporarily abandoned their plan to explore the Star Sea.

The reverse-sailing boats were sealed away from then on.

“We can hold on for a while longer.”

Sun Erlang could not help letting out a sigh of relief.

“I wonder how much time has passed in the real world outside.”

He looked at the galaxy he had personally shaped before him, and his expression grew somewhat dazed.

Time flew by.

In the blink of an eye another century passed on the Primordial Star.

Starting from the third decade, flying boats began approaching the star ring one after another to take samples and conduct research.

In the fifth decade, through the unremitting efforts of all the little people, they finally found a way to repair the star ring’s damage.

Sun Erlang personally witnessed these little people recreating the power of the star ring in the laboratory.

He could not help feeling deeply shaken, with countless thoughts surging in his mind.

“The power of the star ring originates from the foundational laws of the world I created.”

“But these little people are also manifestations of this world’s laws. As part of the world, they are capable of investigating the world’s essential origin…”

“Because they truly exist, they can study it and make use of it.”

...

Changes on the Primordial Star continued.

After multiple experiments confirmed the effectiveness of repairing the star ring, the little people, who had been pent up for so long, could no longer endure.

An unprecedented era of exploration arrived.

Countless reverse-sailing boats once again departed from the Primordial Star, exploring the endless Star Sea.

In the seventh decade the little people invented a method to voyage far without relying on the star ring’s elastic force, using only the star ring power furnace.

From then on the natives officially achieved round-trip exploration of the Star Sea.

From the eighth decade to the hundredth year the little people ushered in a new era of opening up the Star Sea and colonization.

At this point Sun Erlang knew that it was only a matter of time before the lie of this world he had woven was exposed.

Watching the countless little people who still sacrificed without hesitation, an inexplicable emotion arose in Sun Erlang’s heart for some reason.

He no longer tried to expand the boundaries of the Star Sea.

Instead he silently watched as the little people’s footsteps approached the edge of the world.

Five hundred years later.

When a strangely shaped reverse-sailing boat finally reached the edge of the Star Sea, the world before Sun Erlang suddenly shook violently.

Boom!

Like exploding stars, it burst forth with brilliant light.

And disappeared.

Yet Sun Erlang did not return to the real world.

Before his eyes remained a blank void.

“Hmm?”

Sun Erlang was somewhat surprised. He looked around, unsure what had gone wrong.

“Do you think there was any problem with that world just now?”

A voice suddenly sounded from behind Sun Erlang.

Sun Erlang’s heart jumped. He immediately turned his head.

He saw a Daoist with long eyebrows and white hair, holding a whisk, smiling as he observed him.

“This is…”

Sun Erlang vaguely guessed the identity of this person. Gathering his courage, he probed: “Greetings, Heaven

Previous

Next

Chapter 1248: Defying The Principles Of Xuanhuang Once Again

Hearing Sun Erlang’s words, the white-haired Daoist smiled faintly.

“Correct. Every person possesses their own independent personality and thoughts. It is the instinct of living beings to scatter when disaster strikes. Trying to make everyone share the same heart and mind is truly difficult. The various events that have occurred throughout the history of the Xuanhuang Realm illustrate this point.”

The white-haired Daoist lightly waved his whisk. In the pure white space before Sun Erlang, countless scenes instantly emerged.

A black shadow representing the end of the world floated above the Xuanhuang Realm, ready to descend at any moment. Yet regarding how to respond to this catastrophe, the various sects and factions in the Xuanhuang Realm still held differing attitudes.

Some were determined to abandon the realm and flee. Some chose to hide like turtles pulling their heads in. Some ran about desperately, calling for everyone to unite.

There were also those who took advantage of the chaos to loot and satisfy their own selfish desires.

The living beings in the scenes had no specific appearances and were all represented by faceless figures.

Yet the absurd farces playing out were incredibly realistic.

“But this is the Xuanhuang Realm of the past.”

The white-haired Daoist waved his whisk once more, and the scenes changed abruptly.

“Ten thousand hearts united as one, firm will defying heaven’s mandate, open alliance of divine power, no worries hence no fear, one word settles the nine cauldrons…”

Accompanying the words of the Truth Heavenly Sovereign, what appeared before Sun Erlang were detailed scenes of human life within the territories of the Five Elders Association.

“What do you think? How does this compare to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance?” the Truth Heavenly Sovereign asked.

“It is naturally far superior to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.” Sun Erlang replied without hesitation.

He then added: “It is just a pity that all of this is built upon the great divine abilities of the five Heavenly Sovereigns. Everything is as illusory and insubstantial as flowers in a mirror or the moon in water. If one day any of these five Heavenly Sovereigns suddenly meets with misfortune, this seemingly beautiful scene will collapse in an instant.”

Speaking openly in the territory of the Five Elders Association about the possible fall of a Heavenly Sovereign was an utterly rebellious statement. Yet the white-haired Daoist, suspected to be the Truth Heavenly Sovereign, showed no anger.

Instead, he nodded in agreement: “What you said does hold some truth, yet it also does not.”

Sun Erlang’s expression froze. He cupped his hands respectfully and said solemnly: “Please grant me your teaching, Heavenly Sovereign.”

“The reason it holds truth is that, from the perspective of an ordinary outsider cultivator, it is indeed so. The order here exists because of the five Heavenly Sovereigns. If the Heavenly Sovereigns perish, the order will also vanish completely.”

“The reason it does not hold truth is that you have not understood the true meaning of the Longevity Immortal realm in this world.”

The Truth Heavenly Sovereign stroked his beard and spoke slowly: “Defy the principles of heaven and earth to prove longevity immortality.”

“After a cultivator defies the principles, they naturally become a part of the world’s principles. Just like sunlight, water droplets, and air, they together constitute the [World] itself.”

“Humans are not isolated individuals. It is meaningless to deny the existence of the Longevity Immortal realm and then discuss the cultivators living under its cover.”

Sun Erlang did not fully understand the Truth Heavenly Sovereign’s words.

To him, they sounded more like a kind of sophistry.

But the Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign, standing at the pinnacle of this world, would obviously not argue with a junior like him over mere verbal advantage.

Sun Erlang thus frowned and pondered carefully.

After a long time, he said thoughtfully: “What we are discussing is the cultivators of the Xuanhuang Realm. This is the major premise. And the Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign has already become an inseparable part of the Xuanhuang Realm, so…”

“No, that is not right.” Sun Erlang suddenly became alert. “The key point is that your power does not seem to cover the entire Xuanhuang Realm. It is limited only to this area.”

The Truth Heavenly Sovereign asked: “Have you ever sensed the existence of the Carefree Heavenly Sovereign’s divine power?”

Sun Erlang nodded: “Of course I know of it.”

“As fellow Longevity Immortal realm cultivators, Carefree’s strength is not much greater than ours. What he can achieve…”

“We can naturally achieve as well.” The Truth Heavenly Sovereign said slowly.

Upon hearing this, Sun Erlang instinctively felt some disbelief at first.

Then, as he thought further, his expression gradually turned serious.

“Then why?” he could not help asking.

“Naturally because the time has not yet come.” The white-haired Daoist smiled lightly and waved his whisk once more.

The white space of the Truth Fruit Tree slowly began to fade away.

The figure of the Truth Heavenly Sovereign also gradually became blurry.

“Heavenly Sovereign!”

Only then did Sun Erlang suddenly recall his master’s instructions. He hurriedly called out: “Does the Heavenly Sovereign know about the matter concerning the Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign?”

“You will understand once you see.”

The white space exploded with a loud crash.

Countless lights and shadows burst forth at once and bloomed in Sun Erlang’s mind.

He returned to the real world, yet he remained immersed in the images transmitted by the Truth Heavenly Sovereign.

“Defying the principles of heaven and earth again? Are you mad? You have already reached this step. You are only one step away from defying the principles. As long as you proceed normally step by step, it is enough to prove Longevity. Why would you do this?” The young Daoist’s expression was full of confusion, his words intense.

“Longevity? It is merely transforming the body into the Dao.” The figure opposite him had an unclear face, but his tone was full of disdain toward the [Longevity Immortal realm].

He continued: “If not for that existence, transforming into the Dao to achieve Longevity would be fine. But the entire cultivation system is built upon the framework he created…”

“Xinghan, do you know what the consequences will be?”

The young Daoist remained silent.

“The Xuanhuang Realm is being reborn through destruction. This is the only thing he cares about right now. Someone proving Longevity is completely irrelevant to him. In fact, he even hopes for the birth of Longevity Immortal realm cultivators who suit his intentions. Do you know why?”

The young Daoist continued to lower his head and say nothing.

“Your talents are no less than mine. How could you not understand? You are merely harboring a bit of luck.”

“Let me say it directly. One day, when the Xuanhuang Realm welcomes its rebirth and completes its transformation, the so-called Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns will completely become part of the new world’s Heavenly Dao… living a fate worse than death, or rather, where life and death no longer matter.”

“They are merely tools for reshaping the world.”

The figure sneered coldly.

“It is nothing but your delusion and speculation…” After a long while, the young Daoist spoke feebly in rebuttal.

The blurry figure paid no heed and continued on its own: “I do not believe it! I insist on contending with him!”

“Reversing the principles of heaven and earth to prove longevity. This is the foundation of his Dao achievement, but it is also his greatest weakness. If we can reverse his principles once more, we can instantly steal all his fortune and replace him…”

“Eastern Extremity! You…” The young Daoist hurriedly looked around, fearing that this heaven-defying statement would be overheard.

Yet the blurry figure was filled with lofty ambition, eyes brimming with ambition.

“Xinghan, do not be nervous. On this path, I am not alone.” At this moment, Eastern Extremity suddenly spoke to comfort him.

“The Immortal-Mortal Miasma contains the profound mystery of reversing the principles of heaven and earth once more. It originates from the previous Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable who has already passed away.”

“I have recently gained some clues. As long as…”

Boom!

Before Eastern Extremity could finish speaking, the scene suddenly shattered.

When the view before his eyes became clear again, it was still Eastern Extremity’s figure.

However…

The originally smug and high-spirited Eastern Extremity now appeared extremely eerie.

Head hanging low, body completely naked.

Long hair floating, countless threads extending from within his body.

Stretching into the endless void beyond.

“Eastern Extremity!”

This horrifying scene, accompanied by the Truth Heavenly Sovereign’s cry of alarm, suddenly shattered and vanished.

…

“What is this?” Sun Erlang’s mind shook violently.

Although it was only a brief exchange of words, every secret revealed was world-shocking.

Sun Erlang first looked around and discovered that not much time had passed since he entered the creation illusion of the Truth Fruit Tree.

At this moment, there were still cultivators on the fruits with eyes closed undergoing the test.

But Sun Erlang had no intention of staying here any longer.

He had to return as quickly as possible and report everything he had seen and heard to his master.

Fortunately, the attention of the cultivators in the city was entirely focused on those prodigies still remaining in the illusion.

There was little attention paid to Sun Erlang, the failure.

He quietly left the city and headed straight for the entrance to the Great Qi small world.

While flying, Sun Erlang’s thoughts raced.

“Has the Truth Heavenly Sovereign already discovered my presence long ago?”

“So he appeared in the illusion to meet me.”

“They already knew about Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign’s situation. Since I came here on Master’s orders, they used me to convey these scenes to Master.”

“What deeper meaning lies behind this?”

Even though Sun Erlang prided himself on having seen much of the world, he still found it hard to remain calm before this truth involving the deepest secrets of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Sun Erlang could not help but increase his speed a little more.

…

In fact, there was no need for him to return to the Great Qi small world in person.

Through the golden giant net woven from source power essence, the Faceless Holy Emperor had already synchronously perceived everything that happened to him the instant he entered the Truth illusion.

Similarly, the Truth Heavenly Sovereign had not actually been speaking to Sun Erlang, this small cultivator who had not yet reached Dao Integration.

He had been communicating with the Faceless Holy Emperor.

Li Ping carefully replayed the scenes transmitted by the Truth Heavenly Sovereign.

At the same time, the other party’s seemingly meaningful words echoed repeatedly.

“A tool for the Immortal Ancestor to transform new worlds?”

“It seems they still have some self-awareness. Upon seeing me appear in the world and contend with the Immortal Ancestor, they subtly express goodwill…”

“However, it cannot be fully trusted.”

Li Ping snorted coldly.

The Five Heavenly Sovereigns had indeed embodied the Dao with their bodies, that was correct.

Yet facing the pressure from the Immortal Ancestor, Li Ping did not believe at all that after so many years they would simply sit and wait for death without doing anything.

“Carefree Paradise, self-formed heaven and earth.”

“Land of Truth, deriving countless worlds.”

“As for the other few, they mostly hold the attitude of watching tigers fight from the mountain.”

“And those truly driven to a dead end, such as today’s Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, will eagerly seize any chance of survival the moment it appears.”

Li Ping’s thoughts remained very calm and were not affected by the somewhat horrifying scene from the Truth Heavenly Sovereign.

“But Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign’s statement that the mystery of reversing the principles of heaven and earth once more is hidden within the Immortal-Mortal Miasma… may be true.”

“Eastern Extremity’s Purifying Body Spiritual Pool can dissolve the reversed principles of Su Bai just like the Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Curse.”

“Reversing the principles of heaven and earth once again…”

Li Ping pondered, contemplating these two statements.

“It cannot be relied upon entirely, but it is still a viable method to shake the Immortal Ancestor’s strength.”

Thinking this, Li Ping’s figure flickered and arrived near Yin Shangren.

Xu Ke was also present here.

They were observing a delicate and lovely little girl, both clicking their tongues in wonder.

Upon seeing Li Ping arrive, Yin Shangren spoke first: “You came at the right time. Come look at this little one.”

The majestic aura of the Holy Emperor and his faceless appearance made the little girl somewhat nervous.

Nevertheless, she summoned her courage and looked directly at Li Ping.

His divine sense instantly swept through the little girl’s body. Li Ping was slightly startled and transmitted: “Did you not say they all cultivate the new new method?”

“Why is this little girl cultivating the Immortal Ancestor’s method?”

Xu Ke answered with deep emotion: “Holy Emperor, please do not misunderstand. No one here taught her the cultivation method. She figured it out herself.”

“And among this batch of children, she is not the only one like this.”

Yin Shangren’s tone was also somewhat strange: “It is what they call being driven to a dead end and finding another path. They failed to establish their foundation with our method, saw their companions advancing continuously, and in desperation instinctively stumbled upon another cultivable path by chance.”

“To avoid affecting those little ones cultivating the new new method, we could only take them away.”

The Faceless Holy Emperor listened in silence.

This time the source power essence swept through, thoroughly examining the specific conditions within these young children’s bodies.

“It is not a coincidence.”

“The Immortal Ancestor’s method has already become part of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao.”

“It is like a naturally existing path in the world. Even if one’s eyes are covered, one will instinctively walk onto that path.”

Li Ping recalled the scene in the world created by Sun Erlang not long ago.

“This is the Dao of Xuanhuang. The supreme Dao. Even the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao itself cannot escape its influence.”

“Following its Dao, one can prove the supreme within the realm, which is longevity.”

“As for going against the Dao…”

“It is either you die or I perish.”

Before Li Ping’s eyes appeared Eastern Extremity’s eerie body that resembled a puppet.

Yet there was not the slightest fear or hesitation in Li Ping’s heart.

“Su Bai and Eastern Extremity will fail…”

“That does not mean I will!”

“Although the Immortal Ancestor is powerful, it is not without a solution.”

Li Ping’s thinking had not changed from beginning to end.

“To successfully go against the Dao, one way is to reverse its principles once more and disrupt its foundation.”

“The second is to use strength to suppress strength and break the situation with force. The Dao of the Immortal Ancestor is indeed supreme in the Xuanhuang Realm. However, the world is not limited to only the Xuanhuang Realm. Beyond the world lies the Star Sea. Beyond the Star Sea, there is still the High Wall.”

Li Ping’s thoughts turned as he simultaneously explained the purpose of his visit to Yin Shangren and Xu Ke.

“Immortal-Mortal Miasma?”

Yin Shangren found it somewhat strange that Li Ping suddenly brought up this previous research subject of his again.

“Mr. Bai succeeded in reversing the principles at the cost of his own life. Because he was deeply favored by the Heavenly Dao, the greater the entanglement, the greater the backlash. In the end, he could not escape and returned the Dao to heaven.”

Yin Shangren explained.

“Since it is already a reversal of principles, why can it be dispelled again?”

“Does that not mean that every process of expelling the Immortal-Mortal Miasma is equivalent to reversing the principles of heaven and earth once again?”

Li Ping asked calmly.

This was a question Yin Shangren had never considered before, and he was immediately stunned in place.

Chapter 1249: Mr. Bai’S Gift

“Reversing the principles, reversing the principles…”

“Could it truly be like this?”

Yin Shangren stood dazed in place for a long time before he began to tremble uncontrollably.

Xu Ke’s aged face was also filled with excitement. “No wonder Mr. Bai always told me back then that I only needed to wait.”

“Besides the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao descending to save itself, countless mortals continue to step onto the path of cultivation. The process of cultivation is precisely the process of reversing the principles of the immortal-mortal miasma. Over thousands of years, day after day and month after month.”

The Faceless Holy Emperor spoke in a quiet tone. “What defies intuition is that removing the immortal-mortal miasma, something so simple in the eyes of cultivators and merely the first step and prerequisite of cultivation, is in essence identical to the ultimate goal of cultivation: attaining longevity and proving the Dao.”

“Both are acts of reversing the principles of heaven and earth.”

These words caused complex expressions to appear in the eyes of both Yin Shangren and Xu Ke.

Yin Shangren said, “If the Reversal Principle Heavenly Sovereign were still alive, mortals of the Xuanhuang Realm would not find it so easy to reverse the principles of heaven and earth and step onto the path of cultivation. But precisely because Mr. Bai has already perished, the restrictions imposed by the principles of the immortal-mortal miasma have been greatly weakened.”

Li Ping spoke up at this moment. “Moreover, there is the Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign, who saw through the secrets here and further reduced the difficulty of reversal through the so-called Purifying Body Spiritual Pool Formation.”

Xu Ke’s lips moved slightly. It took him a long while to speak with difficulty. “When I last saw Mr. Bai, I already sensed the resolve to die in his heart. All these years I could not let it go, but now I may understand a little.”

Yin Shangren once more fell into a muttering trance. “Everyone in the world is reversing the principles of heaven and earth, yet when all is said and done, it still remains within the Immortal Ancestor’s cultivation system. Even if we know this, what use is it?”

“There must be something we have not yet thought of…”

Li Ping spoke with certainty. “The answer should lie within the immortal-mortal miasma. When it comes to research on the immortal-mortal miasma, no one in the current Xuanhuang Realm can compare with you. I fear I must trouble you, my friend, to work a little harder.”

“Although there are no longer any mortals within Great Qi who carry the immortal-mortal miasma, it is not difficult to find some from the small worlds we have conquered for research purposes. I will have my people fully assist you.” Li Ping said to Yin Shangren.

“Rest assured, Holy Emperor.” Yin Shangren suddenly grew excited.

...

At the same time.

Inside the Derivation Jade Space of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

After the Holy Embryo saw the Remnant World Great Wall, ripples once more stirred in the calm heart of Li Fan’s main body.

“Reversing the principles of heaven and earth once again? The immortal-mortal miasma.”

Li Fan suddenly recalled his own experience of removing the immortal-mortal miasma inside the Purifying Body Spiritual Pool Formation on Liuli Island.

The magma-like scorching pool water had ravaged his consciousness. Even with his firm willpower, he eventually fell into a state of complete ignorance and unawareness.

His mind went blank, and he acted purely on instinct.

Before entering the spiritual pool, Li Fan had been cultivating the Xuanhuang Heart-Cleansing Mantra day and night. Thus, by instinct, he once more activated this technique.

The moment the miasma in his body was completely purged, Li Fan inexplicably comprehended the advanced, sublimated version of the Xuanhuang Heart-Cleansing Incantation.

The Xuanhuang Heart-Refining Mantra.

Later it evolved into the current Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Mantra. This technique had provided no small help to Li Fan on his journey.

“The kind of pain inside the Purifying Body Spiritual Pool is not something ordinary people can endure. There is a high chance one would fall directly into unconsciousness. Even if one remained conscious, the extreme agony would leave no room for any other distracting thoughts.”

“Carrying the soul and embracing the One, cleansing the mysterious mirror, modeling oneself after nature.”

“Humans uniting with the Dao. Instinctive behavior in a state of ignorance and unawareness is precisely what comes closest to the Dao. This may be why so many mortals in the world can successfully remove the immortal-mortal miasma and then rearrange the principles of heaven and earth.”

“Yet in reality, this is not a complete reversal of the principles. The fact that one’s strength does not undergo a qualitative leap after removing the immortal-mortal miasma proves this point.”

Li Fan pondered deeply.

“First, because it is not achieved entirely through one’s own power. Second…”

The Dao Deviation Stone in his mind glowed. Li Fan retrieved the instantaneous comprehension from that moment and savored it repeatedly.

He fell into thought. “When the immortal-mortal miasma was finally removed.”

“It was as if it disappeared on its own.”

“It was as if it sensed the Xuanhuang Heart-Cleansing Mantra I was circulating and spontaneously transformed into the Heart-Refining Mantra.”

“It triggered some kind of condition.”

Li Fan recalled the pre-death image of the Eastern Extremity Heavenly Sovereign that Truth Heavenly Sovereign had transmitted to him.

“The secret to reversing the principles of heaven and earth again is hidden within the immortal-mortal miasma.”

Before Li Fan’s eyes, it was as if he saw countless mortals over thousands of years breaking the shackles between immortal and mortal and stepping onto the path of cultivation by defying the laws.

“The birth of every cultivator involves a process that approximates reversing the principles of heaven and earth again.”

“Even though they themselves are completely unaware of it, this is something that truly and genuinely occurs.”

“Reversing the principles of heaven and earth again is tantamount to confronting the Immortal Ancestor directly and completely overturning the cultivation system he personally shaped. Even heaven-gifted talents like Mr. Bai and the Eastern Extremity ultimately failed. From this, one can see how difficult it is. But…”

Li Fan suddenly saw the light.

He connected it to the process of his own repeated simulation reincarnations.

“The process of every mortal confronting the immortal-mortal miasma can be viewed as a simulation of reversing the principles of heaven and earth again.”

“The power of one person may have its limits. The power of thousands or tens of thousands also has its limits.”

“But the power of thousands upon thousands of people across countless generations is endless and inexhaustible.”

“Before such a grand force, it is only a matter of time before the Immortal Ancestor’s new method are broken.”

Li Fan’s heart shook. “The myriad spirits of heaven and earth, and the refining of the gods by all living beings, use pure quantity to shake the immortal power that transcends the mundane.”

“Infinite living beings defying the laws across eternal generations—this magnifies the advantage of quantity once more on the scale of time, achieving a qualitative change from quantitative accumulation.”

“As long as there still exist beings in the Xuanhuang Realm who refuse to accept the fate of mortals, this method will continue indefinitely.”

Li Fan’s thoughts raced. In his heart, his respect for Mr. Bai, who had created such a wondrous method, grew even deeper and more sincere.

Ignoring the limit of lifespan, his methods remained effective after his death, crossing the long river of time to influence generations yet to come.

Unlike Li Fan, who possessed the Truth treasure, this was already the limit that ordinary mortal cultivators could achieve.

Of course, this held little value for Li Fan to reference.

What Mr. Bai needed thousands of years of Xuanhuang Realm cultivators to accomplish, Li Fan could achieve simply by continuously using Truth.

But this did not prevent Li Fan from claiming Mr. Bai’s fruition.

“In my view, the so-called secret hidden within the Immortal-Mortal Miasma that allows one to reverse the principles of heaven and earth once again is actually the insights accumulated by countless cultivators over countless years while purging the Immortal-Mortal Miasma.”

“All of it has been recorded and preserved.”

“If one can absorb these repeated near-simulations of reversing the principles of heaven and earth.”

Li Fan prided himself that he could now remain unmoved by most treasures in the Xuanhuang Realm, even those like Cat Treasure from beyond the High Wall. Yet he had to admit that, upon sensing these countless experiences of reversing principles, his heart was stirred.

Unlike spiritual treasures, this package of experiential insights was something that could directly enhance the essence of Li Fan’s Divine Soul.

It was enough to sharply shorten the gap between him and the Immortal Ancestor and the Heavenly Doctor.

Although it was a one-time gain, it held tremendous significance for Li Fan. The sooner he obtained it, the better.

“At the very least, it can save me three lifetimes of reincarnation time.”

Li Fan began calculating in his mind.

“So where exactly has Mr. Bai hidden this great gift package?”

“In the Fallen Immortal, or within the power of the Immortal-Mortal Miasma itself? Does it require some special conditions to trigger?”

If not for the fact that this life he had already decided on the strategy of sitting on the mountain to watch the tigers fight, Li Fan would now wish he could send his main body to investigate personally.

However, years of experience allowed him to suppress this impulse.

“With Yin Shangren and Xu Ke working hard on the research, it will definitely be more efficient than me groping around blindly.”

“What they know is equivalent to what I know. In the end, it will all be mine anyway, so there is no need to rush.”

“I can also see just how substantial this gift package truly is. After the Holy Emperor clone absorbs it, will it really be able to shake the Immortal Ancestor?”

“Heh heh heh, it truly makes me somewhat expectant.”

Li Fan, who would inevitably feel bored after long periods of closed-door cultivation inside the Derivation Jade space, now felt his emotions surging.

...

The perspective returned once more to the Holy Emperor clone.

Uncovering the secrets of Mr. Bai’s legacy was not something that could be completed in a day or two.

Before that, the time Li Ping had agreed upon with Ruomu had already arrived.

The Holy Emperor once again came to the outside of the White Flower Black Abyss.

He did not become careless despite holding absolute advantage. To prevent Ruomu from acting desperately, he quietly laid down a layer of protection with source power essence around himself.

Only then did Li Ping step back into the lifespan refining space.

Perhaps because of Li Ping’s previous nourishment, or because Ruomu himself had seen hope.

In the originally deathly still and despair-filled withered yellow space, this time Li Ping actually sensed traces of vitality.

Although extremely weak, the appearance of this vitality that should not have existed already explained certain things.

Li Ping also noticed something different from before.

The cultivators hanging on Ruomu’s branches…

Had all disappeared.

“Greetings, Holy Emperor.”

The old Daoist appeared before Li Ping with a smiling face.

Ruomu noticed the direction of the other party’s gaze. Before Li Ping could ask, he took the initiative to explain: “Since I have already decided to submit to you, naturally I must make some changes. It is good to abandon those past acts of impotent rage.”

“To be honest, after looking at these human fruit for so long, I have indeed grown somewhat tired of them.”

Li Ping said calmly: “Where are they now?”

Ruomu chuckled: “Naturally, I have returned them to the heavens.”

As he spoke, Ruomu handed over a tree leaf.

The leaf showed both green and yellow hues. Upon closer inspection, it contained its own space, filled with a small mountain of various golden cores.

These golden cores appeared to be dissolving, incomplete and damaged.

Li Ping could vaguely see the shapes of heavenly materials and earthly treasures within the cores of the golden cores.

“I was originally a spiritual being of heaven and earth, holding authority over life and death in the Xuanhuang Realm. Although that authority has gradually been reclaimed, my connection with heaven and earth has never truly been severed.”

Ruomu glanced at Li Ping and continued: “Through my absorption and conversion, the heavenly power devoured by these cultivators can be returned to heaven and earth more quickly.”

“You can see clearly that I have not embezzled any of it.”

Li Ping withdrew his gaze from the leaf: “I believe you are not that foolish. Compared with the rewards I offer, these are simply insignificant.”

Ruomu nodded repeatedly in agreement.

“Come here.” Li Ping suddenly spoke.

Chapter 1250: Stone Tablet Falls Into The Pond

The old Daoist hesitated for a moment, but ultimately walked slowly to stand before the Faceless Holy Emperor.

Compared with Li Ping’s massive body of three zhang and three chi, the hunched Daoist appeared like an infant.

Li Ping gently placed his palm on Ruomu’s head.

Boom!

The scene before Ruomu shattered in an instant.

In a flash, he seemed to leave the lifespan refining space.

He arrived in an endless dark starry sky.

A golden vortex, like a galaxy, swirled majestically.

In the center of the galaxy, the towering figure of the Faceless Holy Emperor could be vaguely seen.

That powerful and sacred aura seemed even stronger than the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao that Ruomu was familiar with.

As one of the oldest existences in the Xuanhuang Realm, Ruomu naturally understood what this power represented.

The last trace of dissatisfaction in his heart slowly disappeared.

Ruomu revealed his original form. He was no longer the ugly old human Daoist, but a small, withered yellow sapling that looked malnourished.

Before the vast golden vortex galaxy, he appeared like an ant.

His branches and leaves swayed gently, expressing Ruomu’s submission.

The figure of the Faceless Holy Emperor rapidly expanded, instantly filling Ruomu’s entire field of vision.

The golden galaxy also exploded with a roar.

Endless starlight submerged Ruomu.

When Ruomu awakened from the illusion, Li Ping’s figure had already disappeared.

“Wait patiently.”

“When you are needed, you will be summoned.”

Only Li Ping’s voice continued to echo in his mind.

And Li Ping’s original promise was not broken.

Ruomu clearly felt that his bond with the Xuanhuang Realm was slowly recovering.

Although returning to his peak from back then was already impossible.

At least there was now something to look forward to.

Moreover…

Ruomu looked at his own palm.

In addition to the original green leaf, a small green bottle had somehow appeared.

It was clearly left behind by the Faceless Holy Emperor before he departed.

A method to manipulate the restrictions naturally emerged in his mind. Ruomu followed the instructions and opened the small green bottle.

A pure surge of vitality emerged from the green bottle.

Although it could not compare to direct nourishment from the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao, for Ruomu in his current state, it was an absolute great tonic.

Ruomu greedily swallowed all the vitality from the green bottle in one gulp.

Only when not a single drop remained did he reluctantly stop.

However, Ruomu noticed that the vitality inside the small green bottle had not completely disappeared.

It was slowly regenerating on its own.

What surprised Ruomu even more was that the generation of this vitality did not draw any energy from the outside world.

It was as if the vitality was completely derived by the green bottle itself.

“Truly a fine treasure.”

Ruomu smacked his lips rather ungracefully.

“It does not seem to be an object from the Xuanhuang Realm.”

Ruomu pondered for a moment, then smiled wryly.

“Why worry about so much? As long as there is food and drink, it is fine.”

“It cannot be worse than before.”

Only after losing something does one realize how precious it once was.

If anyone tried to seize these things again…

Then even if the other party was the Immortal Ancestor, Ruomu would dare to bite back twice.

The Earth Vein Ancient Tree was also within the net of source power essence cast by the Faceless Holy Emperor.

Therefore, after Ruomu surrendered, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree faintly sensed something. However, it could not be certain and could only feel growing unease day by day.

“Now I am a subordinate of the Holy Emperor and enjoy his protection. Surely He would not dare do anything to me?” The ancient tree could only comfort itself in this way.

The Earth Vein Ancient Tree clearly understood that in this world, probably only the Holy Emperor could preserve its life.

The more useful it was to the Holy Dynasty, the safer it would be.

Thus, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree was eager to prove its value to the Holy Emperor.

Unfortunately, ever since arriving in the Holy Dynasty of Great Qi, it seemed to have been forgotten. No one had come to seek it out.

Even the previously assigned task of organising the earth veins of the Great Qi small world had not been inspected by anyone.

This left the Earth Vein Ancient Tree quite depressed.

However, on this day, the opportunity for the performance it had been waiting for finally arrived.

The visitor was the Holy Emperor’s fifth disciple, Ou Shangtian.

“Is this the schematic diagram of the Xuanhuang Realm’s earth vein currents?”

Ou Shangtian pointed at the image ahead and asked.

It was densely packed, resembling a vast underground net that extended to every corner of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Like blood vessels within the human body, they continuously contracted and pulsed. The sight appeared rather eerie.

The Earth Vein Ancient Tree nodded and said: “Young Revered sees clearly.”

Ou Shangtian seemed quite pleased with the ancient tree’s flattery and readily accepted the title of “Young Revered.”

“I heard from Senior Sister Lin Ling that when traveling within the earth vein currents, the power of the Xuanhuang Realm’s Heavenly Dao naturally conceals them, making it difficult for ordinary people to detect. Is that correct?” Ou Shangtian asked again.

“Indeed.” The Earth Vein Ancient Tree extended a withered branch. “Young Revered, please examine it.”

Ou Shangtian felt the earth vein current power surging within the withered branch and frowned slightly: “I see nothing particularly remarkable about this. How can it shield others’ perception?”

The Earth Vein Ancient Tree smiled faintly: “Young Revered must understand that sand gathers to form a tall tower, and water droplets converge into rivers. A single point of earth vein power may seem insignificant. But when the earth vein currents of the entire Xuanhuang Realm converge, they become vast and mighty, carrying the force of thunder.”

“After all, the circulation of earth veins is also an important part of the normal operation of the Xuanhuang Realm. This old one merely holds temporary authority over it. Compared with the boundless principles of the entire Xuanhuang Realm, ordinary cultivators are no different from ants and are naturally overlooked.”

Ou Shangtian stared blankly at the schematic diagram of the earth vein currents and fell into deep thought.

After a long while, he came back to his senses, returned the earth vein withered branch, and instructed: “Perfect. This time I am going out to carry out the task assigned by Master, and I may need your help.”

The Earth Vein Ancient Tree had been waiting for exactly this. Upon hearing the words, it rejoiced greatly: “Young Revered may command freely. I will certainly assist with full sincerity.”

“There is a senior in the Xuanhuang Realm whose strength is unfathomable and who carries a stone tablet on his back… Do you know of him?” Ou Shangtian looked at the Earth Vein Ancient Tree and asked.

The Earth Vein Ancient Tree’s expression changed abruptly. Its previous eagerness vanished in an instant.

It stared at Ou Shangtian in disbelief and only after a long pause asked tentatively: “Young Revered, is the Holy Dynasty planning to make a move against Senior Stone Tablet?”

“Forgive my bluntness, but the Stone Tablet is a genuine Longevity Immortal realm expert who has attained the Dao for many years.”

Ou Shangtian waved his hand somewhat impatiently: “I naturally know the strength of that so-called Stone Tablet. Longevity Immortal realm!”

“Compared with Master, it counts for nothing!”

“Rest assured. I am merely responsible for luring him to a specific location.”

Hearing Ou Shangtian say this, the Earth Vein Ancient Tree finally relaxed slightly.

That mysterious and unfathomable Faceless Holy Emperor had already subdued its father. Dealing with this Stone Tablet should not be a big problem.

The ancient tree became invigorated: “Young Revered, may I ask how I should cooperate?”

Ou Shangtian explained the detailed plan. The ancient tree pondered and nodded, indicating that there was no major issue.

“Time is short. Once I have met with Master, I will set off immediately.”

The ancient tree replied: “My main body remains in the Xuanhuang Realm. As long as Young Revered summons me, I can manifest at any time.”

Ou Shangtian hurriedly entered the Holy Emperor’s hall.

When he emerged, the anxiety on his face had completely disappeared.

“I possess the Kun-Qian Bone Hand and the protection of the Seven-Colored Jade Void Glimmering Light. Moreover, I have received treasures bestowed by Master this time and have studied formations for such a long time.”

“I only need to lure him to the Fishing Pond, and the task will be complete. There should be no major problem.”

“However, isn’t the Fishing Pond a treasured land of our Holy Dynasty? Why does Master instead ask me to lead a great enemy there? Is he not afraid of any accidents?”

Ou Shangtian pondered but could not understand.

In the end, he could not be bothered to think about it further. He left the Great Qi small world and headed straight for Nine Mountain Prefecture of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

Relying on the surging earth vein currents, in just the time it takes to drink a cup of tea, he had already arrived within the borders of Nine Mountain Prefecture.

Ou Shangtian tested it and indeed found that the spatial laws here were somewhat chaotic and fragile.

If he rashly used instant movement techniques, he might directly tear space and be sucked into spatial turbulence, disappearing without a trace.

“However, flying poses no problem. These nine mountain peaks may trouble ordinary cultivators, but they cannot trouble me with the seven-colored divine light.”

Thinking this, the Seven-Colored Jade Void Glimmering Light, under his deliberate control, formed a protective ring around his body. Like a great sun in the high sky, it continuously radiated its own rhythm outward.

With a slight thought, a set of armor emitting the same seven-colored luster suddenly appeared on Ou Shangtian’s body.

Master’s words rang in Ou Shangtian’s ears at the same time: “This object is called the Flowing Light Armor. It is refined from nine heavens lightning and morning mist neon. Once worn, flying speed can rival lightning. It can also ignore the vast majority of attack methods.”

Ou Shangtian tested it.

Gazing at the peak of the mountain range ahead, in just a single moment of daze, the next instant he had already arrived at the summit.

“So fast!?” Even the instigator Ou Shangtian could not help feeling alarmed in his heart.
